===== 문제 1 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following procedures is correct when applying for a mobile driver’s license?
① Applications can be made using any app.
② Applications must be made using a smartphone under the applicant’s name with identity verification.
③ Applications can only be made at the traffic civil service office of a police station.
④ A mobile driver’s license cannot be issued at a driver’s license test center.
정답: 2
해설: Article 85-2 of the Road Traffic Act (Issuance of the Mobile Driver's License and Verification of the Driver's License, etc.), Article 78-2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Application for the Mobile Driver's License, etc.).

===== 문제 2 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT true about driver's license issuance?
① A driver’s license must be issued within 30 days of passing the driver's license test.
② A driver’s license cannot be issued in English.
③ A mobile driver’s license can be issued.
④ A lost driver’s license can be reissued.
정답: 2
해설: Articles 77 to 81 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act; Article 80 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act. When a driver's license is lost or is so damaged that it can no longer be used, a person may apply to the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency, as prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety, to be reissued a license.

===== 문제 3 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the validity period for an international driver's license issued by the commissioner of the City/Do  (Province) police agency from the date of issuance?
① 1 year
② 2 years
③ 3 years
④ 4 years
정답: 1
해설: Under Article 80 of the Road Traffic Act, a person who has obtained a driver's license and wishes to drive abroad must apply to the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency to be issued an international driving permit under the 'Convention on Road Traffic' referred to in Article 96(1)1; its period of validity is one year from the date of issuance.

===== 문제 4 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is a prerequisite for driving an emergency passenger  vehicle with prescribed seating capacity of no more than 15 persons?
① Class I driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles; 3 hours of traffic safety education
② Class I driver’s license for special motor vehicles (large towing vehicles); 2 hours of traffic safety education
③ Class I driver’s license for special motor vehicles (recovery vehicles); 2 hours of traffic safety education
④ Class II driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles; 3 hours of traffic safety education
정답: 1
해설: Under Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, a bus (van) with a seating capacity of 15 requires a Class 1 Large license or a Class 1 Ordinary license, and a person engaged in emergency-vehicle duties must receive new (3 hours) and regular (2 hours) traffic safety education under Article 38-2(2) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act.

===== 문제 5 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the period of validity of a learner’s permit according to the Road Traffic Act?
① 6 months from the date of issuance
② 1 year from the date of issuance
③ 2 years from the date of issuance
④ 3 years from the date of issuance
정답: 2
해설: Article 81 of the Road Traffic Act (Effect of a Provisional Driver's License): A provisional driver's license is valid for one year from the date it is issued. However, even before one year has passed since the provisional license was issued, if the person who received the provisional license obtains a Class 1 Ordinary license or a Class 2 Ordinary license, the provisional license loses its effect.

===== 문제 6 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following should NOT be indicated in a driver’s license regarding  the special conditions for allowance of driver’s license?
① A: Manual transmission
② E: Deaf sign and convex mirror
③ G: Specially produced and approved car
④ H: Right direction indicator
정답: 1
해설: Under Article 54(3) of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Conditions of a Driver's License, etc.), the criteria for imposing license conditions are set out in Table 20: A is automatic transmission, B is a prosthetic arm, C is a prosthetic leg, D is a hearing aid, E is a hearing-impaired sign and convex mirror, F is a hand-operated brake/accelerator, G is a specially manufactured and approved vehicle, H is a right-side turn signal, and I is a left-side accelerator. When a physically disabled person takes the driving test, the person must take and pass the test in a vehicle meeting the applicable conditions, and after passing, a license conforming to those conditions is issued.

===== 문제 7 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A vehicle with total weight of 780 kg is to be towed with an 11-seater passenger vehicle. Which of the following  driver’s license(s) must the driver hold?
① Class I driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles and for small towing vehicles
② Class II driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles and Class I driver’s license for small towing vehicles
③ Class I driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles and for recovery vehicles
④ Class II driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles and Class I driver’s license for recovery vehicles
정답: 1
해설: Under Note 3 of Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, to tow a trailer of 3 tons or less whose gross weight exceeds 750 kilograms, a person must hold a license permitting operation of the towing vehicle together with a Class 1 Small Trailer license or a Large Trailer license.

===== 문제 8 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO statements are correct regarding the types of vehicles that can be driven by specific types of driver's  license?
① An asphalt distributor may be driven by a driver holding a Class I driver’s license for large motor vehicles.
② A dump truck may be driven by a driver holding a Class I driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles.
③ A 250 cc two-wheeled vehicle may be driven by a person holding a Class II driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles.
④ A motorcycle may be driven by a person holding a Class II driver’s license for small motor vehicles.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Under Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Types of Vehicles That May Be Driven), a dump truck requires a Class 1 Large license, and a motorcycle with an engine displacement exceeding 125 cc requires a Class 2 Small license.

===== 문제 9 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: One intends to drive a 12-seater passenger vehicle. Which of the following driver’s license types must one obtain  to do so?
① Class I driver’s license for large towing vehicles
② Class I driver’s license for recovery vehicles
③ Class I driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles
④ Class II driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles
정답: 3
해설: Under Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, a Class 1 Ordinary license allows driving of a bus (van) with a seating capacity of 15 or fewer; (1), (2), and (4) allow driving of a bus (van) with a seating capacity of 10 or fewer.

===== 문제 10 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following people can obtain a Class II driver's license for ordinary motor vehicles?
① Those who are blind in one eye but possess visual acuity of 0.5 in the other eye
② Those who cannot differentiate red, green, and yellow colors (color blind)
③ Those who are 17 years old
④ Those who are deaf
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 45(1)1(b) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, a Class 2 driver's license requires the person to be at least 18 years old and to have a visual acuity, measured with both eyes open at the same time, of 0.5 or higher (however, a person who cannot see with one eye must have a visual acuity of 0.6 or higher in the other eye). The person must also be able to distinguish the colors red, green, and yellow, but may obtain the license even if unable to hear.

===== 문제 11 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a correct definition (standard) of a “motorcycle” under the Road Traffic Act?
① Displacement of up to 50 cc; maximum rated output power of up to 0.59 kW
② Displacement of less than 50 cc; maximum rated output power of less than 0.59 kW
③ Displacement of up to 125 cc; maximum rated output power of up to 11 kW
④ Displacement of less than 125 cc; maximum rated output power of less than 11 kW
정답: 3
해설: See the definition in Article 2, subparagraph 19(b) of the Road Traffic Act. A 'motorized bicycle' means a vehicle falling under any of the following: (a) among motorcycles under Article 3 of the 'Motor Vehicle Management Act', a motorcycle with an engine displacement of 125 cc or less (or, if powered by electricity, a maximum rated output of 11 kilowatts or less); (b) any other vehicle equipped with an engine of 125 cc or less (or, if powered by electricity, a maximum rated output of 11 kilowatts or less) (excluding electric bicycles under Article 2, subparagraph 1-2 of the 'Act on the Promotion of Bicycle Use' and outdoor mobile robots under subparagraph 21-3).

===== 문제 12 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following are the qualifications for taking a test for Class I  driver's license for a large motor vehicle? (Excluding experience in driving a two-wheel
① A person who has been driving a vehicle for at least 6 months and who is 18 years or older
② A person who has been driving a vehicle for at least 1 year and who is 18 years or older
③ A person who has been driving a vehicle for at least 6 months and who is 19 years or older
④ A person who has been driving a vehicle for at least 1 year and who is 19 years or older
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 82(1)6 of the Road Traffic Act, a Class 1 Large license may not be issued to a person under 19 years of age or to a person with less than one year of experience driving a motor vehicle (excluding motorcycles).

===== 문제 13 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an incorrect description of postponing the driver's license aptitude test under the Road  Traffic Act when traveling abroad?
① You must submit an application to postpone the aptitude test before leaving the country.
② You cannot have an agent apply for a postponement of the aptitude test on your behalf after you leave the country.
③ You must provide supporting documents of your departure such as an E-ticket to apply for a postponement of the  aptitude test.
④ If your application is approved, you must take the aptitude test within three months of your return.
정답: 2
해설: (1), (3): Article 55(1) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act (Postponement of Renewal Issuance of Driver's Licenses and Regular Aptitude Tests, etc.). (4): Article 55(3) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act. (2): Article 83(3) of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Postponement of Renewal Issuance of Driver's Licenses and Regular Aptitude Tests) and Form 59.

===== 문제 14 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A person who fails the road driving test may retake the test (  ) after the date of failure. Which of the following  belongs in (  )?
① 1 day
② 3 days
③ 5 days
④ 7 days
정답: 2
해설: Under Article 49(4) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, a person who fails the road driving test may retake it after 3 days have passed from the date of failure. 15. Which two of the following are NOT appropriate descriptions of the 'Good Driver Mileage' system? (1) It is a system that grants substantial incentives to drivers who observe and practice traffic laws well. (2) If a driver becomes subject to a suspension disposition, the mileage may be deducted from the accumulated points. (3) A person who has not paid a fine or an administrative penalty may still participate in the mileage system's pledge of no-violation and no-accident. (4) If a person causes a traffic accident or violates traffic laws during the pledge-performance period, the person may not make the pledge again.

===== 문제 15 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What are TWO inappropriate explanations for the "Good Driving Mileage" system?
① It is a system that provides practical incentives to those who comply with and practice traffic laws.
② If a driver receives a suspension order, the penalty can be deducted from the accumulated points.
③ Those who have not paid the traffic penalty or administrative fine can participate in the no-violation, no-accident  pledge of the mileage system.
④ In case of causing a traffic accident or violating traffic laws during the pledge period, the person cannot take the  pledge again.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Under Table 28 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, if a driver becomes subject to a suspension disposition, this may be deducted from the accumulated points. Anyone holding a driver's license may participate in the mileage system, but a person who has not paid a fine or an administrative penalty may not make the pledge, and even if a traffic accident occurs or a traffic law is violated during the pledge-performance period, the person may make the pledge again (per the public notice on standards for granting driver's-license preferential points).

===== 문제 16 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT the correct definition of personal mobility devices (PMDs) in motorcycle form?
① The ascent angle is less than 25 degrees.
② The device must weigh less than 30 kg.
③ The term "bicycles, etc." means bicycles and personal mobility devices.
④ The motor should not operate when driving at a speed of 25 km/h or more.
정답: 1
해설: Article 2, subparagraph 19-2 of the Road Traffic Act; Article 3, subparagraph 1 of the Act on the Promotion of Bicycle Use. A 'personal mobility device' means, among the motorized bicycles under subparagraph 19(b), one that does not have its motor operate when running at 25 km/h or faster and whose vehicle body weighs less than 30 kilograms, as prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety; a gradient (climbing) angle is not specified.

===== 문제 17 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Below are explanations of the standard for personal mobility devices (PMDs) according to the Road Traffic Act.  Which one is correct?
① For vehicles with a motor, the motor should stop when the device reaches or exceeds a speed of 20 km/h.
② It includes electric bicycles that cannot move solely by motor power (PAS: Pedal Assist System).
③ It is a vehicle with maximum rated output power of 11 kW or less, which weighs less than 35 kg.
④ The weight of the vehicle should be less than 30 kg.
정답: 4
해설: Article 2, subparagraph 19(b) of the Road Traffic Act: any other vehicle equipped with an engine of 125 cc or less (or, if powered by electricity, a maximum rated output of 11 kilowatts or less) (excluding electric bicycles under Article 2, subparagraph 1-2 of the Act on the Promotion of Bicycle Use and outdoor mobile robots under subparagraph 21-3). Subparagraph 19-2: a 'personal mobility device' means, among the motorized bicycles under subparagraph 19(b), one that does not have its motor operate when running at 25 km/h or faster and whose vehicle body weighs less than 30 kilograms, as prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety. * PAS (Pedal Assist System) electric bicycle: moves by the combined power of the pedals and the motor and cannot move by motor power alone. * Throttle-type electric bicycle: a bicycle that can move by motor power alone and is classified by law as a 'personal mobility device'.

===== 문제 18 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO actions would cause someone to be barred from acquiring a driver's license for 2 years? (If given a  penalty more severe than a criminal fine)
① Driving without a license three times
② When taking a driver’s license test on behalf of another person.
③ When charged for assaulting a police officer during an inspection, resulting in the revocation of one’s license.
④ When causing three or more traffic accidents while driving under the influence of alcohol.
정답: 1, 2
해설: Under the provisions of Article 82 of the Road Traffic Act (Grounds for Disqualification from a Driver's License), if a license is revoked after the person is booked for assaulting a police officer conducting enforcement, the disqualification period for obtaining a license is 1 year; if a person causes three or more traffic accidents while driving under the influence, the disqualification period is 3 years from the date the license is revoked.

===== 문제 19 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT true about an English driver’s license? (Limited  to countries that have ratified the Geneva Convention or the Vienna Convention)
① When driving in a country where an English driver’s license is recognized, it is possible to drive without a separate  notarized translation.
② The English driver's license can be used regardless of the length of stay in a country where an English driver’s license is  recognized.
③ You should bring your Korean driver's license, an international driver's license, and your passport in a country where an  English driver’s license is
④ It is a license wherein the content of the driver's license is written in English on the back of the driver's license.
정답: 2
해설: English Driver's License Guide (Korea Road Traffic Authority). The period during which one may drive differs by country, and most countries allow only a short period of about 3 months, so a person staying long-term must obtain a driver's license of the country concerned.

===== 문제 20 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Under the Road Traffic Act, a motorcycle is defined as a two-wheeled vehicle with maximum rated output of  (  ) or less when powered by electricity. Which of the following belongs in (  )?
① 11 kW
② 9 kW
③ 5 kW
④ 0.59 kW
정답: 1
해설: Article 2 (Definitions) of the Road Traffic Act. A motorized bicycle means, among motorcycles under the Motor Vehicle Management Act, a motorcycle with an engine displacement of 125 cc or less (or, if powered by electricity, a maximum rated output of 11 kilowatts or less), and any other vehicle equipped with an engine of 125 cc or less (or, if powered by electricity, a maximum rated output of 11 kilowatts or less).

===== 문제 21 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct definition of "line of curbstones" as stipulated in the Road Traffic Act?
① A line indicating the direction of the thoroughfare for vehicles and horses
② A line for guiding vehicles to drive in a single line through a set part of the road
③ A line consisting of rocks or other material for distinguishing roadways from sidewalks
④ A line indicating separate lanes
정답: 3
해설: Article 2 (Definitions) of the Road Traffic Act, subparagraph 4: 'curb line' means a line formed by stones or the like that separates the roadway from the sidewalk.

===== 문제 22 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding personal mobility devices under the Road Traffic Act is correct?
① It is possible to drive a PMD with more riders than the permitted capacity.
② A person over the age of 65 who has returned his/her driver’s license can drive a PMD.
③ A person over the age of 13 can drive a PMD without a driver’s license.
④ When using a crosswalk, you must get off the personal mobility device and push or carry it while walking.
정답: 4
해설: Article 13-2(6) of the Road Traffic Act (Special Provisions on Methods of Passage for Bicycles, etc.): when the driver of a bicycle, etc. crosses a road using a crosswalk, the driver must dismount and either push or carry the bicycle, etc. while walking.

===== 문제 23 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the age standard for individuals whose driver's  license renewal and aptitude test interval is 3 years?
① Individuals aged 65 or older
② Individuals aged 70 or older
③ Individuals aged 75 or older
④ Individuals aged 80 or older
정답: 3
해설: Article 87(1) of the Road Traffic Act.

===== 문제 24 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Below are descriptions of the verification process for identifying individuals wishing to obtain a driver’s license  according to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree. Which of the followin
① If the resident registration card has been lost, identification can be performed via the confirmation of application for  the issuance of a resident r
② An individual who cannot be verified by either identification certificate or fingerprint information is not permitted to  take the test.
③ Fingerprint information may be compared and verified using an electronic method without the applicant's consent.
④ If the applicant refuses to verify his/her own identity, the issuance of a driver’s license may be denied.
정답: 3
해설: Article 87-2 of the Road Traffic Act; Article 57 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Taking the Driver's License Test). A person who cannot present an identification document may, if the applicant so wishes, have their identity verified by comparing fingerprint information electronically.

===== 문제 25 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following government authorities provides special education to those intending to get a license for  fuel cell electric buses (FCEBs; seating capacity of 35 or more)?
① Korea Gas Safety Corporation
② Korea Occupational Safety and Health Agency
③ Korea Road Traffic Authority
④ Korea Expressway Corporation
정답: 1
해설: Article 51(1) of the Enforcement Rule of the High-Pressure Gas Safety Control Act, Table 31.

===== 문제 26 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Whom must the person likely to have his/her driver’s license suspended due to a violation of traffic laws  according to the Road Traffic Act request when wishing to receive the recommended special tra
① Chairperson of the Korea Road Traffic Authority
② Head of the local government having jurisdiction over one's domicile
③ Head of a driver licensing office
④ Commissioner of a City/Do police agency
정답: 4
해설: Article 73(3) of the Road Traffic Act (Traffic Safety Education): a person falling under any of the following who applies to the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency may receive special traffic safety recommendation education, as prescribed by Presidential Decree. In this case, this applies only to a person who has not received such education within the past year before receiving the recommendation education.

===== 문제 27 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, if a person who is blind in one eye intends to  obtain a Class I driver's license for ordinary motor vehicles, the visual acuity of his/he
① 0.5, 50
② 0.6, 80
③ 0.7, 100
④ 0.8, 120
정답: 4
해설: Article 45 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act (Standards of Aptitude Required for Driving Motor Vehicles, etc.): however, where a person who cannot see with one eye wishes to obtain a Class 1 Ordinary driver's license, under the standards of aptitude required for driving, the other eye must have a visual acuity of 0.8 or higher, a horizontal field of vision of 120 degrees or more, a vertical field of vision of 20 degrees or more, and no scotoma or hemianopsia within the central 20 degrees of vision.

===== 문제 28 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: How often is a person between 65 and 75 years of age with a Class I driver’s license (excluding persons who  have lost sight in only one eye) required to take a regular aptitude test?
① Every 3 years
② Every 5 years
③ Every 10 years
④ Every 15 years
정답: 2
해설: Article 87(1)1 of the Road Traffic Act: a person aged 65 or older but under 75 who has been issued a Class 1 driver's license must undergo a regular aptitude test every 5 years. However, for a person who cannot see with one eye and has obtained the Ordinary license among the Class 1 licenses, the interval is 3 years.

===== 문제 29 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When should drivers return their driver's license to the commissioner of the City/Do (Province) police agency?  (Select TWO)
① When they have their driver's license revoked
② When they have their driver's license suspended
③ When they are notified that they should take an occasional aptitude test
④ If they fail to take a regular aptitude test for 6 months
정답: 1, 2
해설: When a person receives a disposition revoking their driver's license, when a person receives a disposition suspending the effect of their driver's license, when a person who lost their driver's license and was reissued one then finds the lost license, or when a person who received a provisional license obtains a Class 1 Ordinary license or a Class 2 Ordinary license, the person must return the driver's license to the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency having jurisdiction over their place of residence within 7 days.

===== 문제 30 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following correctly describes the renewal period of a driver’s license under the Road Traffic Act (for drivers under 65 years of age only)?
① Within two months before or after one's birthday in the year that marks the tenth anniversary since passing the driver’s license test.
② Within four months before or after one's birthday in the year that marks the tenth anniversary since passing the driver’s license test.
③ Within six months before or after one's birthday in the year that marks the tenth anniversary since passing the driver’s license test.
④ Within eight months before or after one's birthday in the year that marks the tenth anniversary since passing the driver’s license test.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act [enforced Jan. 1, 2026], Article 87 (Renewal of Driver's License and Regular Aptitude Test): (1) A person who has obtained a driver's license must, within the periods classified as follows, have the license renewed and reissued by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency, as prescribed by Presidential Decree. 1. The first license renewal period is within 6 months before or after the birthday falling in the year in which 10 years have elapsed, counting from the date the driving test under Article 83(1) or (2) was passed (5 years for a person aged 65 or older but under 75 on the date of passing; 3 years for a person aged 75 or older; 3 years for a person who cannot see with one eye and has obtained the Ordinary license among the Class 1 licenses). 2. For license renewal periods other than those in subparagraph 1, within 6 months before or after the birthday falling in the year in which each 10 years have elapsed, counting from the immediately preceding license renewal date (5 years for a person aged 65 or older but under 75 on the immediately preceding renewal date; 3 years for a person aged 75 or older; 3 years for a person who cannot see with one eye and has obtained the Ordinary license among the Class 1 licenses).

===== 문제 31 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following cannot be issued an English driver's license?
① A person who has passed the driver’s license test and who applies for a driver’s license
② A person who has passed the driver’s license aptitude test and who applies for a driver’s license
③ A person who applies for exchanging a foreign driver’s license with a domestic driver’s license
④ A person who applies with a student license
정답: 4
해설: Article 78 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Application for an English Driver's License, etc.): a holder of a provisional driver's license is not eligible to be issued an English driver's license.

===== 문제 32 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is not allowed to be driven  with a Class II driver’s license for ordinary motor vehicles?
① A recovery vehicle
② A passenger vehicle with seating capacity of less than 10
③ A passenger car
④ A truck with loading weight of 2.5 tons
정답: 1
해설: Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Types of Vehicles That May Be Driven).

===== 문제 33 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A person whose driver’s license test result becomes invalidated due to cheating shall be prohibited from taking  the relevant examination for ( ) from the date of imposition. Which answer fits in ( )?
① 2 years
② 3 years
③ 4 years
④ 5 years
정답: 1
해설: Article 84-2 of the Road Traffic Act (Measures Against Persons Committing Cheating): a person whose test is invalidated due to cheating may not take that test for 2 years from the date of that disposition.

===== 문제 34 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT a valid reason for  postponing license renewal or the regular aptitude test?
① If the person is staying overseas
② If the person is unable to move due to an illness
③ If the person is serving as a noncommissioned officer or higher in the Army, Navy, or Air Force pursuant to the Military  Personnel Management Act
④ If the person falls victim to a disaster or a calamity
정답: 3
해설: Article 55(1) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act.

===== 문제 35 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, a person who has received an extension of the  period for renewal of a driver's license must renew and obtain a driver's license within (
① 1 month
② 3 months
③ 6 months
④ 12 months
정답: 2
해설: Article 55(3) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act: a person granted a postponement of the license renewal period must have the license renewed and reissued within 3 months from the date the ground for postponement ceases to exist.

① Drivers of fuel cell passenger vehicles
② Drivers of large FCEBs (seating capacity of 36 or more)
③ Drivers of fuel cell trucks
④ Drivers of special-purpose FCEVs
정답: 2

===== 문제 37 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding the obligations of a driver who has obtained a conditional driver’s  license upon installing an ignition interlock device under the Road Traffic Act?
① A person who has obtained a conditional driver’s license upon installing an ignition interlock device and who drives without  registering it with the
② A person who has driven a vehicle without an ignition interlock device or with such device that fails to meet the installation  standards shall be ord
③ A person who drives a motor vehicle with an ignition interlock device, etc. knowing that the device is less effective as it was  dismantled, tampered
④ After the period of license disqualification due to drunk driving, a person may only drive a vehicle with an ignition  interlock device from the time
정답: 4
해설: Article 50-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by Drivers, etc. Who Have Received a Conditional Driver's License with a Drunk-Driving Prevention Device Attached) and Article 93(1) of the same Act (Revocation and Suspension of a Driver's License). - The license is revoked if a person drives a motor vehicle, etc. equipped with a drunk-driving prevention device without registering it with the City/Provincial Police Agency (subparagraph 21). - The license is revoked if a person drives a motor vehicle, etc. on which no drunk-driving prevention device is installed, or on which a drunk-driving prevention device not meeting the installation standards is installed (subparagraph 22). - The license is revoked if a person, knowing that the device's effectiveness has been reduced through dismantling, manipulation, or other means for the purpose of servicing the drunk-driving prevention device, drives the motor vehicle, etc. on which that device is installed (subparagraph 23).

===== 문제 38 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the standard for penalties imposed on a driver who knowingly uses a fake petroleum product as vehicle  fuel?
① An administrative fine between KRW 50,000 and 100,000
② An administrative fine between KRW 500,000 and 1 million
③ An administrative fine between KRW 2 million and 20 million
④ No penalties
정답: 3
해설: Table 6 of the Enforcement Decree of the Petroleum and Alternative Fuel Business Act (Standards for Imposing Administrative Fines): a person who uses fake petroleum products as vehicle fuel while knowing they are fake may be subject to an administrative fine ranging from 2 million won to 20 million won, depending on the amount used.

===== 문제 40 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a problem that can occur in cars when refueling with fake petroleum products?
① Increased engine noise due to corrosion and damage of the fuel supply device
② Reduced power and fuel efficiency due to damage to the injector that injects fuel
③ Reduced power due to reduction of engine friction caused by increased lubricity
④ Power-downs due to damage to the high-pressure fuel pump supplying fuel
정답: 3
해설: If fake petroleum is used as motor vehicle fuel, the decrease in lubricity increases friction, which can cause damage to the high-pressure fuel pump and the injectors.

===== 문제 41 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO items should you check before getting in a vehicle?
① Condition of tire wear
② Whether there are obstacles in front of or behind your car
③ Whether the dashboard readings are working properly
④ Whether the brake pedal works normally
정답: 1, 2
해설: Checking whether the driver's-seat instrument panel and the brake pedal operate normally is an inspection item performed from the driver's seat after boarding.

===== 문제 42 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the color generally displayed on the inspection screen when the life of a maintenance-free (MF) battery  expires?
① Yellow
② White
③ Black
④ Green
정답: 2
해설: For a typical maintenance-free (MF) battery, green (blue) indicates a normal condition, black meaning the specific gravity of the electrolyte is low indicates a need for charging or replacement, and white (red) indicates that the battery has reached the end of its life.

===== 문제 43 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following CANNOT be considered a fake petroleum product for use as vehicle fuel?
① Gasoline mixed with methanol
② A product that contains less than 5% high-grade gasoline mixed with normal gasoline
③ Diesel oil mixed with kerosene
④ A product with less than 5% water mixed with diesel
정답: 4
해설: A fake petroleum product as defined in the Petroleum and Alternative Fuel Business Act means a product manufactured for use as vehicle fuel by mixing petroleum products with other petroleum products (including petroleum products of a different grade) or petrochemical products; since there is no separate applicable standard based on the amount mixed, even a small amount mixed in may be regarded as a fake petroleum product. Using a fake petroleum product or fuel outside its lawful use leads to vehicle malfunctions, which cause traffic accidents and environmental pollution from increased harmful emissions, etc. Where water or sediment enters gasoline, diesel, etc., the product is regarded as being of nonconforming quality.

===== 문제 44 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not the correct method of checking for hydrogen gas leaks?
① Measurement using a flammable gas detector
② Confirmation through soapy water
③ Confirmation by smelling gas
④ Confirmation using a hydrogen detector
정답: 3
해설: Hydrogen Vehicle Charging Station Construction Manual, p.12, Korea Gas Safety Corporation. Hydrogen is the lightest element on Earth and is colorless, tasteless, and non-toxic. It is also a gas whose leaks are hard to detect because there is no odorant with a diffusion rate similar to hydrogen's.

===== 문제 45 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT correct regarding the safety rules for hydrogen vehicles?
① Switch off the vehicle power before charging.
② Smoking should be done at a distance from the vehicle.
③ When hydrogen gas leaks, request the safety supervisor from the charging station for a safety check.
④ After a traffic accident involving the collision of a hydrogen vehicle, drive the vehicle after undergoing a gas safety  inspection.
정답: 2
해설: Hydrogen Vehicle Charging Facility Maintenance Manual, p.21. General precautions include: 1) absolutely no smoking around the charging station; 2) use of explosion-proof electrical equipment; 3) no use of mobile phones inside the gas facility room; 4) a vehicle speed limit of 10 km/h or less within the charging station; 5) no operation of equipment before mastering the manual; 6) the charging station must be used and maintained only by trained personnel; 7) operation in accordance with the matters stated in the manual and the High-Pressure Gas Safety Control Act; 8) no operation of equipment in an unsafe situation; 9) a prohibition on operating when safety-related equipment does not function properly, and a prohibition on operating while ignoring safety-related equipment; 10) compliance with operation and maintenance procedures; 11) operation only within the pressure range described in the manual.

===== 문제 46 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not necessary when checking for leaks in hydrogen vehicles?
① Connection between valve and container
② Regulator
③ Gas hose and pipe connection
④ Boost inverter of fuel cell
정답: 4
해설: The electrical devices consist of an inverter that converts the DC output from the fuel-cell stack into AC, and supercapacitors and secondary batteries for storing the electricity generated during braking. The power conversion device converts the DC power obtained from the stack to the voltage level for the motor, converts DC power into AC power to drive and control the high-current AC drive motor, and converts it to the voltage for driving the various electronic devices in the vehicle.

① When charging, use a charger and an adapter that are compatible with the specifications.
② During charging, do not disconnect the charging connector arbitrarily. End charging with the charging completion  button.
③ Be careful not to use the charger with wet hands; prevent water from entering the charging device.
④ When using a portable charger to charge, you can use a household power strip or an extension cord.

===== 문제 48 =====

① Reverse light – Amber
② License plate light – Blue
③ Taillight – White
④ Brake light – Red

===== 문제 49 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT correct regarding the characteristics of the fuel used in an LPG vehicle?
① It burns in a gaseous state, allowing the engine to operate smoothly.
② LPG for motor vehicles has a distinct odor.
③ It is generally lighter than air and less explosive.
④ It has a lower energy density compared to gasoline or diesel, resulting in lower output and lower fuel efficiency for   vehicles equipped with an engi
정답: 3
해설: It generally burns in a gaseous state, so the engine runs smoothly and combustion efficiency is high, resulting in low emission of pollutants such as carbon dioxide. LPG itself is colorless and odorless, but a special scent is mixed into vehicle LPG so that leaks can be detected. It is heavier than ordinary air and has a high explosion risk. Because its energy density is lower than that of gasoline or diesel, it produces lower output and poorer fuel economy at the same displacement.

===== 문제 50 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a cause of reducing the braking force of a vehicle?
① Master cylinder failure
② Defective wheel cylinder
③ Deformation of release fork
④ Occurrence of vapor lock
정답: 3
해설: The release fork is a component of the clutch system in a manual-transmission vehicle; it is fitted onto the release-bearing collar and transmits the operating force from the pedal to the release bearing, and it has no direct relation to braking force.

③ When fully or partially releasing the driving assistance device, check whether it works.
④ Even if the driving assistance system is in operation, be ready to intervene at short notice.
정답: 2
해설: When a warning to take over driving is issued, the driver must intervene and drive immediately.

===== 문제 52 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about a driver-assistance system?
① Lane Following Assist (LFA) is a system designed to assist the driver in driving to stay within the lane on the road.
② Adaptive Smart Cruise Control (ASCC) is a system that automatically adjusts the vehicle speed to maintain a safe distance  from vehicles ahead.
③ Active Blind Spot Detection (ABSD) is a system that assists drivers in changing lanes by warning them of vehicles in their  blind spots.
④ Autonomous Emergency Braking (AEB) is a system that prevents the wheels from locking up and reduces the braking  distance.
정답: 4
해설: There are various advanced vehicle functions for safety, such as LFA, ASCC, ABSD, and AEB; some of these can be selected by the driver depending on the purchase options, and the driver can also choose to disable certain functions to some extent as needed while driving. AEB is a function whereby the system automatically applies emergency braking when the driver either does not apply the brakes or applies them weakly in a dangerous situation and a collision cannot be avoided. Option (4) is a description of ABS.

===== 문제 53 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is incorrect regarding provisions on autonomous driving systems?
① The Road Traffic Act stipulates that “driving” does not include the use of an autonomous driving system in accordance with  the original use of motor
② The prohibition of using a mobile phone does not apply to drivers driving a vehicle operated under an autonomous driving  system.
③ The traffic penalty for a driver who fails to respond promptly to the take-over request from the autonomous driving system  is a fee of KRW 40,000.
④ An “autonomous driving system” encompasses a variety of automated apparatus, software, and peripheral devices that allow   a vehicle to move by recogn
정답: 1
해설: Article 2, subparagraph 26 and Article 56-2 of the Road Traffic Act; Table 8 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act; subparagraph 38-3. 'Driving' means using a vehicle or a tram on a road according to its intended method of use (including operating it or using an autonomous driving system). The driver of a vehicle equipped with an autonomous driving system that does not amount to a fully autonomous driving system must, without delay in response to the system's request for direct driving, directly operate the steering device, braking device, and other devices to drive. Where a driver drives using an autonomous driving system, the provisions of Article 49(1)10, 11, and 11-2 do not apply. Article 2(1)2 of the Act on the Promotion and Support of Commercialization of Autonomous Vehicles: an 'autonomous driving system' means automated equipment, software, and all related devices that enable a motor vehicle to be operated by itself, recognizing and judging the surrounding situation, road information, etc., without operation by the driver or a passenger.

===== 문제 54 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a crucial component of an FCEV?
① A fuel cell
② A traction motor
③ An engine
④ A battery
정답: 3
해설: Operating principle of a hydrogen vehicle: hydrogen stored in the hydrogen storage tank is supplied to the fuel-cell system, and electricity is generated in the fuel-cell stack through a chemical reaction between oxygen and hydrogen. The generated electricity drives the motor to move the vehicle, or is stored in the battery depending on the driving conditions. An engine is a component of an internal-combustion-engine vehicle.

① Torque
② Fuel economy
③ Displacement
④ Horsepower
정답: 1
해설: (2) It is the distance that can be driven on 1 liter of fuel. (3) It is the volume swept by the piston in an internal combustion engine. (4) It is the amount of work of moving a weight of 75 kilograms a distance of 1 meter in 1 second.

===== 문제 56 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Act on the Promotion of and Support for Commercialization of Autonomous Vehicles, which of  the following is NOT true about autonomous vehicles?
① The types of autonomous vehicles can be divided into fully autonomous vehicles and semi-autonomous vehicles.
② Fully autonomous vehicles refer to vehicles that can operate solely on autonomous driving systems without the need  for a driver or any intervention f
③ Semi-autonomous vehicles refer to vehicles that cannot operate solely on autonomous driving systems or which require  continuous attention from the dr
④ Autonomous vehicles are limited to passenger vehicles, and this law does not apply to buses or trucks.
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 2(1)1 and (2) of the Act on the Promotion and Support of Commercialization of Autonomous Vehicles: 1. Partially autonomous vehicle: an autonomous vehicle that cannot be operated by the autonomous driving system alone, or that requires the intervention of the driver or a passenger, such as needing the driver's continuous monitoring. 2. Fully autonomous vehicle: an autonomous vehicle that can be operated by the autonomous driving system alone, so that there is no driver or no intervention of the driver or a passenger is required. As in (4), autonomous vehicles are not limited to passenger cars and also apply to buses (vans) and trucks.

===== 문제 57 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which two statements are NOT correct regarding the management of EVs?
① The valid period for the vehicle inspection of non-commercial passenger vehicles is 6 years.
② When driving long distances, check and charge the battery in advance.
③ Refrain from accelerating or suddenly stopping immediately after charging.
④ It is more efficient to use an air heater than a heated seat or a heated handle.
정답: 1, 4
해설: (1) Except for newly manufactured vehicles, the vehicle inspection validity period for a non-commercial passenger car is 2 years (Table 15-2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Motor Vehicle Management Act). (4) In the case of an electric vehicle with no internal combustion engine, a lot of electrical energy is used to run the heater, so it is advisable to use heated seats and a heated steering wheel. (2) Consider the remaining battery level and travel distance so as not to run out of charge while driving. (3) Right after charging, the battery temperature rises; at this time, rapid acceleration or rapid stopping consumes a lot of electrical energy and thus reduces battery efficiency.

===== 문제 58 =====

① Window glass on the side of the back seat

③ Front windshield, window glass on the right and left sides of the driver's seat, and rear windshield
④ All windows
정답: 2
해설: A person must not drive a vehicle whose visible-light transmittance of the front windshield and of the left and right side windows next to the driver's seat is lower than the standard prescribed by Presidential Decree, so as to hinder traffic safety, etc.

===== 문제 68 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a desirable mindset for drivers of autonomous vehicles?
① Since it is an autonomous vehicle, the drivers can drive even under the influence of alcohol.
② The driver must not drive an autonomous vehicle in an exhausted state.
③ Even drivers of autonomous vehicles can take psychoactive medication and drive.
④ The driver may use a mobile phone while driving an autonomous vehicle.
정답: 1
해설: Article 44(1) of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Driving While Intoxicated): no one may drive a motor vehicle, etc., a tram, or a bicycle while intoxicated. Article 45 (Prohibition of Driving While Fatigued, etc.): the driver of a motor vehicle, etc. or a tram must not drive a motor vehicle, etc. or a tram in a state in which normal driving may not be possible, other than while intoxicated, due to fatigue, illness, the effects of drugs (narcotics, cannabis, psychotropic substances, etc.), or other causes. Article 56-2(2) (Matters to Be Observed by Drivers of Autonomous Vehicles, etc.): where a driver drives using an autonomous driving system, the provisions of Article 49(1)10 (prohibition of using a handheld phone), subparagraph 11 (prohibition of watching a video display device), and subparagraph 11-2 (prohibition of operating a video display device) do not apply.

===== 문제 71 =====

④ 50, 61

===== 문제 72 =====

Article 46 (Penalty Provisions): (2) any of the following persons is punished by imprisonment for not more than 1 year or a fine not exceeding 10 million won.

===== 문제 73 =====

===== 문제 74 =====

해설: Article 43 of the Motor Vehicle Management Act (Motor Vehicle Inspection): the owner of a motor vehicle must undergo the new registration inspection, regular inspection, tuning inspection, temporary inspection, and repair inspection conducted by the Minister of Land, Infrastructure and Transport.

===== 문제 75 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following processes the registration of transfer for a vehicle sale?
① Korea Road Traffic Authority
② Si, Gun, or Gu district office
③ Korea Transportation Safety Authority
④ City/Do (Province) police agency
정답: 2
해설: Article 8(1) of the Motor Vehicle Management Act: affairs concerning motor vehicle registration are handled by the Si/Gun/Gu (city/county/district) office.

===== 문제 76 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO are NOT types of vehicle registration?
① Registration of revision
② Registration of rights
③ Registration of patent rights
④ Registration of cancellation
정답: 2, 3
해설: Article 3 of the Motor Vehicle Registration Decree: motor vehicle registrations include new, alteration, transfer, cancellation, seizure, mortgage, correction, and provisional registration, and patent-type registrations include rights registration, establishment registration, etc. (Motor Vehicle Registration Decree).

===== 문제 77 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the appropriate permeability of visible rays through the front windshield of motor  vehicles (other than children’s school buses)?
① 40%
② 50%
③ 60%
④ 70%
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 28 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, the standard for the visible-light transmittance of vehicle windows is 70 percent for the front windshield and 40 percent for the left and right side windows next to the driver's seat.

===== 문제 78 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the proper sequence of applying brakes while driving?
① Identify hazard → make judgment → order action → apply brakes
② Identify hazard → order action → make judgment → apply brakes
③ Make judgment → identify hazard → order action → apply brakes
④ Order action → identify hazard → make judgment → apply brakes
정답: 1
해설: While driving, a hazard is recognized and judged, and after the action command, the brakes are applied.

===== 문제 79 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a required condition for an airbag?
① Tensile strength and heat resistance at a high temperature
② Tensile strength and heat resistance at a low temperature
③ Rupture strength, wear resistance, flexibility
④ Maximizing the impact energy in contact with a driver
정답: 4
해설: When a motor vehicle collides, it should directly contact the driver and absorb the impact energy.

===== 문제 80 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding the precautions to take when getting in and out of a car?
① When getting in or out, check if there is a car coming from behind.
② Get out of the car immediately after completely opening the door.
③ When a person exits a vehicle from the back seat, the driver should look straight forward.
④ When leaving the driver's seat temporarily, you need not turn off the car's engine.
정답: 1
해설: When the driver, etc. get in or out, check whether there are vehicles coming from behind.

===== 문제 81 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following correctly connects the situation and the proper action  that drivers must take?
① School zone - Honk to inform children of the vehicle passing by.
② Railroad crossing - Increase speed to pass quickly when a crossing barrier is about to go down.
③ Intersection without signals - When turning right, drive slowly along the right side of the road and turn right.
④ Night driving - Adjust the headlights to high beam to caution drivers coming from the opposite direction.
정답: 3
해설: When a child is passing on a pedestrian path in front of a school, one must come to a complete stop; when a barrier is about to lower at a railroad crossing, one must not enter. Also, when driving at night, one must adjust the headlights to low beam so as not to interfere with oncoming vehicles' travel.

===== 문제 82 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements about overtaking is correct under the Road Traffic Act?
① Inside tunnels, overtaking is allowed during daytime but banned during nighttime.
② Drivers can overtake the car ahead either on its left or on its right after turning on the headlights and honking.
③ Overtaking is not allowed on bridges or at intersections.
④ A driver may stop, proceed slowly, or overtake a vehicle as directed by a police officer.
정답: 3
해설: Article 21 (Methods of Overtaking, etc.): (1) The driver of any vehicle, to overtake another vehicle, must pass on the left side of the vehicle ahead. Article 22 (Timing and Places Where Overtaking Is Prohibited): (1), (2), (3). (4) 2. A vehicle that has stopped or is slowing down in accordance with a police officer's instruction.

===== 문제 83 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a desirable driver attitude?
① Because the traffic situation is not changing, there is no need to make plans ahead.
② Equip the vehicle with a fire extinguisher to take action immediately in case of fire.
③ Remove lighters and other flammable items from the vehicle.
④ Drive courteously, especially for novice drivers.
정답: 1
해설: When a hazard on the road, such as a broken-down vehicle, is discovered, turn on the hazard lights to warn following vehicles of the situation ahead in advance, slow down to safely clear the hazardous section, and then, if help is judged necessary, take measures to prevent a secondary accident and respond accordingly.

===== 문제 84 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate driving behavior for a driver?
① Drowsy driving entails the risk of a traffic accident, so it is important to stop the car on a roadside to rest when you are  drowsy.
② Novice drivers should continuously drive in the overtaking lane on an expressway.
③ Install and operate a device that interferes with the functions of traffic regulation equipment.
④ Upon identifying a risk to traffic safety, inform others with emergency lights.
정답: 4
해설: Resting on the shoulder, continuously driving in the overtaking lane, and installing devices that cause interference cannot be regarded as proper driving conduct.

===== 문제 85 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a desirable driver attitude?
① Always drive while obeying traffic rules, such as driving under the speed limit.
② Drive on the shoulder in the event of traffic congestion.
③ Compliance with traffic laws and regulations is a mutual commitment among drivers.
④ Put pedestrian safety first before increasing the vehicle speed.
정답: 2
해설: Even when traffic is congested, driving on the shoulder (road edge) is an improper driving attitude.

===== 문제 86 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: In case of an accident, which of the following is NOT considered the responsibility of the driver?
① Criminal liability
② Administrative liability
③ Civil liability
④ Notification liability
정답: 4
해설: Criminal liability such as the imposition of a fine, administrative liability based on penalty points, and civil liability based on damages follow.

===== 문제 87 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a correct response by a driver to a traffic accident while driving on an expressway?
① Take first-aid measures depending on the severity of a passenger's injury.
② Turn on the hazard lights to alert trailing drivers of the danger.
③ Control the traffic at the rear part of the vehicle until a police officer arrives.
④ Move to a safer place to prevent secondary accidents.
정답: 3
해설: Behind an accident vehicle there is a risk of a secondary accident, so it is advisable to move to a safe place.

===== 문제 88 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following actions is the correct behavior of a driver driving a passenger vehicle with an infant?
① An adult in the passenger seat sits with an infant and wears the same seat belt.
② A seat belt is enough for an infant sitting in the back seat; an infant safety harness is not necessary.
③ To appease a crying infant while driving, it is OK to let the infant sit together with the driver in the driver’s seat and   wear the same seatbelt.
④ When riding with an infant, always use an infant safety harness with a seat belt fastened, regardless of the road taken by the        driver.
정답: 4
해설: When carrying an infant in a passenger car, it is safe to install a child restraint device in the rear seat behind the driver's seat and then fasten the seat belt.

===== 문제 89 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO are the responsibilities of a driver?
① Stop the vehicle if there is risk of a traffic accident involving children.
② You must slow down when riding over a puddle so as not to splash others.
③ Apply dark tinting since the brightness of vehicle windows is not regulated.
④ Drive at a reduced speed when pedestrians are crossing a crosswalk.
정답: 1, 2
해설: When a risk of a child traffic accident is discovered on the road, one must come to a complete stop. Also, when a pedestrian is crossing on a crosswalk, one must come to a complete stop, and when there is a pedestrian in a safety zone, one must slow down while keeping a safe distance.

===== 문제 90 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are desirable behaviors when driving on a freeway? (Select TWO)
① If you are drowsy or exhausted from driving, pull the vehicle over on the shoulder to take a rest or a nap.
② You can watch entertaining videos while driving.
③ You should take a rest and ventilate the vehicle regularly to stay awake.
④ You should constantly check for hazards along the route before departure and even while taking a rest.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Set a driving plan in advance; rather than using a mobile phone while driving, identify hazards during rest, and rather than trying to overcome drowsy driving, prevent it through periodic rest or ventilation.

===== 문제 91 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT desirable regarding a driver's necessary preparation for safe driving?
① Control your body condition so that your attention is not diverted.
② Wear clothes that are comfortable and suitable for driving.
③ Prepare emergency signaling devices such as flare.
④ In order to save fuel, preheat the engine by turning it on 10 minutes before departure.
정답: 4
해설: Idling a motor vehicle can cause environmental pollution.

===== 문제 92 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO are correct regarding concentration while driving?
① Continuously chatting with a passenger while driving improves the driver's concentration.
② It is better to remove stickers that can block the driver’s view.
③ Concentration while driving has nothing to do with safe driving.
④ TVs or DMBs should be installed where they can be seen only by passengers in the back seat.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Continuously talking with a passenger while driving distracts one's concentration, and concentration is always needed while driving.

===== 문제 93 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, if an infant is on board a vehicle (except for two-wheeled vehicles), using an  infant safety harness is required. Which of the following is the age criteria for an
① 8 years or below
② Below 8 years
③ Below 6 years
④ 6 years or below
정답: 3
해설: Article 11 of the Road Traffic Act (Protection of Children, etc.): the guardian of an infant (a person under 6 years of age) must not let a child play on a road with heavy traffic.

===== 문제 94 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT true about regulations and proper operation of   PMDs?
① The driver must turn on headlights and daytime running lights when driving at night.
② Among the PMDs, not more than one person can drive an electric kick scooter.
③ A personal mobility device includes self-balancing scooters, electric kick scooters, electric bicycles, electric unicycles,   and mobility scooters.
④ An electric bicycle powered by a motor can carry two persons.
정답: 3
해설: Article 50 of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by Specific Drivers): (9) the driver of a bicycle, etc. must, when passing along a road at night, turn on the headlight and tail lamp or wear a reflective band or other light-emitting device. (10) the driver of a personal mobility device must not carry passengers exceeding the seating capacity prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety when operating the personal mobility device. Article 33-3 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Seating Capacity of a Personal Mobility Device): for an e-scooter and an electric two-wheeled self-balancing device, the seating capacity is 1 person; for a bicycle that can move by motor power alone, the seating capacity is 2 persons.

===== 문제 95 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true regarding wearing a seat belt before driving a vehicle (excluding two-wheeled       vehicles)?
① If the passenger is a child under 13 years old and is not wearing a seat belt, the driver will be slapped with an  administrative fine of KRW 100,000.
② If the passenger is 13 years old or above and is not wearing a seat belt, the driver will be slapped with an  administrative fine of KRW 30,000.
③ Only the driver and front passenger are obligated to wear seat belts when driving on general roads.
④ All passengers are obligated to wear seat belts, but a child under 3 years old can be placed on the lap of the guardian.
정답: 2
해설: Article 50(1) of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by Specific Drivers): the driver of a motor vehicle (excluding motorcycles) must fasten the seat belt when driving the vehicle. Table 6 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act: non-wearing of the seat belt (an administrative fine of 60,000 won if the passenger is under 13, and 30,000 won if 13 or older).

===== 문제 96 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a good driving attitude for accident prevention?
① Using indicators to make sure that the others know the driver’s path of travel
② Frequently making sudden maneuvers and braking
③ Driving to get through traffic as advantageously as possible for oneself
④ Retribution for aggressive driving by other drivers
정답: 1
해설: One must not make abrupt maneuvers or sudden braking; considerate driving toward others and an accurate understanding of the law are needed.

===== 문제 97 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following actions is NOT desirable for a driver?
① Drive according to the flow of traffic within the speed limit.
② If you are a novice driver, use the shoulder on the expressway and do not interfere with the traffic flow.
③ Do not fight without stopping your car on the road first.
④ A student license holder should drive with a companion according to the law.
정답: 2
해설: Even a novice driver must not drive on the shoulder on an expressway.

===== 문제 98 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following best describes "yielding"?
① When driving continuously at slow speed, the driver must yield the lane by moving to the far left side of the road.
② When an emergency vehicle is approaching from behind, the driver must proceed quickly.
③ At an intersection without a traffic light, the driver must yield the right of way to vehicles approaching from the left.
④ Vehicles driving on a road with a yield sign must yield the lane to vehicles on other roads.
정답: 4
해설: Even when an emergency vehicle is following behind, one must yield the right-of-way. Also, one must proceed in accordance with the yielding standards at an intersection without traffic control, and a vehicle on a road where a yield sign is installed must yield the right-of-way to other vehicles.

===== 문제 99 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Act on the Promotion of Transportation Convenience of Mobility Disadvantaged Persons, who  among the following are NOT considered “mobility disadvantaged persons”?
① The elderly
② Pregnant women
③ Persons accompanied by infants
④ Persons with pets
정답: 4
해설: Article 2 of the Act on Promotion of the Transportation Convenience of Mobility-Disadvantaged Persons: a 'mobility-disadvantaged person' means a person who feels inconvenience in movement in daily life, such as persons with disabilities, the elderly, pregnant women, persons accompanied by an infant, and children.

===== 문제 100 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Under the Act on the Promotion of Transportation Convenience of Mobility Disadvantaged Persons, which of  the following is not considered a “pedestrian safety facility” for mobility disadvantaged pers
① Speed reduction facility
② Bicycle lane
③ Transportation information facilities such as public traffic information system
④ Traffic signal for pedestrians' right of way
정답: 2
해설: Article 21(1) of the Act on Promotion of the Transportation Convenience of Mobility-Disadvantaged Persons (Installation of Pedestrian Safety Facilities): a Mayor or the head of a Gun may install the following pedestrian safety facilities in a pedestrian-priority zone so that pedestrians can walk safely and conveniently: 1. speed-reduction facilities; 2. crossing facilities; 3. traffic guidance facilities such as public-transport information notice facilities; 4. traffic signals for the priority passage of pedestrians; 5. bollards to prevent vehicle entry; 6. walking-route guidance devices such as audible signals for mobility-disadvantaged persons; 7. other facilities prescribed by Presidential Decree for the safety and mobility convenience of pedestrians.

===== 문제 101 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following cases requires driving at a slow speed?
① When the traffic signal at the intersection is flashing in red
② Upon passing an intersection where traffic is not controlled and is frequent
③ Upon passing an intersection where traffic is not controlled
④ Upon changing lanes near an intersection
정답: 3
해설: (1) One must come to a complete stop. (3) When passing through an intersection without traffic control, one must slow down and pass.

===== 문제 102 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the best way of proceeding when turning left at a congested intersection?
① If possible, enter the intersection while following the car in front.
② You may enter the intersection if the traffic light is green.
③ If there is space available, you may enter the intersection even if the traffic light is red.
④ Do not enter the intersection even when there is a green arrow.
정답: 4
해설: When the driver of any vehicle is about to enter an intersection with traffic lights, the driver must stop at the intersection depending on the situation of the vehicle ahead in the lane in which they intend to proceed, and must not enter the intersection if there is a risk of obstructing the passage of other vehicles.

===== 문제 103 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of entering a travel lane from an acceleration lane of an  expressway?
① Stop temporarily to check the traffic flow, and then quickly enter the expressway.
② Stop temporarily before entering the expressway and abruptly enter when there are vehicles passing on the road.
③ Enter the expressway even if there is not much space to enter, in consideration of the vehicle behind.
④ Use the acceleration lane, maintaining a certain speed, and enter the expressway when enough space is secured.
정답: 4
해설: When entering an expressway, one must use the acceleration lane and enter while gradually increasing speed. Entering slowly or coming to a complete stop can actually be dangerous because acceleration then becomes difficult. One should confirm that there is sufficient space to enter, accelerate, and then merge.

===== 문제 104 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Vehicle A is moving slowly on the right side of the expressway's main lane. In this case, what is the best method  of entering the main lane?
① Slowly increase the speed and enter, but make sure you do so after Vehicle A passes.
② Accelerate and enter using an empty roadside.
③ Stop at the end of the acceleration lane and enter after Vehicle A passes.
④ Continue driving on the acceleration lane at the same speed as Vehicle A.
정답: 1
해설: The driver of a motor vehicle (excluding emergency vehicles), when about to enter an expressway, must not obstruct the passage of other motor vehicles traveling on that expressway.

===== 문제 105 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate driving behavior when a driver notices a child crossing the road  without a guardian?
① Repeatedly honk so that the child can cross quickly.
② Ensure the safety of the child crossing the road by moving slowly.
③ Temporarily stop and ensure the safety of the child crossing the road.
④ Ensure the safety of the child crossing the road by passing quickly.
정답: 3
해설: Article 49 of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers, etc.): when a child crosses a road without a guardian, the driver must come to a complete stop.

===== 문제 106 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the safest driving method when entering an intersection with no traffic lights and where both the left  and right sides are not visible?
① Drive slowly depending on the surrounding situation and pass through the intersection after checking that it is safe.
② Enter while honking and flashing headlights, and drive slowly while passing through.
③ Make a temporary stop to make sure that it is safe, and then pass through the intersection according to right-of-way  standards.
④ Since a vehicle entering the intersection first has priority, quickly pass through the intersection while checking the  surroundings.
정답: 3
해설: At an intersection without traffic lights, slowing down is the principle, but where traffic at the intersection is heavy or there are obstacles making it impossible to check the left and right, one must come to a complete stop, confirm safety, and then pass.

===== 문제 107 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following would cause the biggest safety hazard when turning left at an intersection?
① A pedestrian crossing a crosswalk on the right side of the intersection
② A motorcycle approaching at high speed from the rear in the right roadway
③ A passenger car turning right from the left side of the intersection
④ A bicycle turning right from the opposite side
정답: 4
해설: When turning left at an intersection, one must watch for vehicles turning right from the road opposite.

===== 문제 108 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Under the Road Traffic Act, which of the following describes the safest attitude for a person driving a personal  mobility device?
① When passing on a sidewalk, drive away from pedestrians.
② When driving after drinking alcohol, special attention is required.
③ If there are a crosswalk and a bicycle crossing, use the bicycle crossing.
④ When driving the device on a crosswalk, avoid pedestrians on the crosswalk.
정답: 3
해설: Article 15-2 of the Road Traffic Act (Installation of Bicycle Crossings): when crossing on a road separately provided with a bicycle crossing while riding a bicycle, etc. (a bicycle or a personal mobility device), one must use the bicycle crossing. Article 13-2 of the Road Traffic Act (Special Provisions on Methods of Passage for Bicycles, etc.): when the driver of a personal mobility device crosses a road using a crosswalk, the driver must dismount and either push or carry it while walking. 109. Which of the following is correct regarding the 'Safety Speed 5030' traffic safety policy? (1) Within 50 km/h on motor-vehicle-only roads, and 30 km/h on residential-area back streets in urban areas. (2) Within 50 km/h on general roads in urban areas, and within 30 km/h on residential-area back streets in urban areas. (3) Within 50 km/h on motor-vehicle-only roads, and within 30 km/h in school zones. (4) Within 50 km/h on general roads in urban areas, and within 30 km/h on bicycle roads.

===== 문제 109 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding the traffic safety policy referred to as Safety Speed 5030?
① Within 50 km per hour on the automobile road and 30 km per hour on the back road in a residential area in a city
② Within 50 km per hour on the main road in a city and 30 km per hour on the back road in a residential area in a city
③ Within 50 km per hour on the automobile road and 30 km per hour in a school zone
④ Within 50 km per hour on the main road in a city and 30 km per hour on the bicycle track
정답: 2
해설: Safety Speed 5030 is a policy that lowers the limit to within 50 km/h on general roads in urban areas where pedestrian traffic is frequent (60 km/h may be applied where traffic flow requires it) and to within 30 km/h on back streets such as residential areas; it was introduced to protect pedestrian safety through speed reduction.

===== 문제 110 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, what are TWO situations or locations where driving at slow speed is required?
① An intersection without any traffic signals
② A road with children crossing without a guardian
③ A road where you see a blind person crossing using a white cane
④ The start of a winding road
정답: 1, 4
해설: At an intersection without traffic lights, one must slow down, and one must come to a complete stop when a child is crossing the road without a guardian and when a visually impaired person is crossing the road with a white cane.

===== 문제 111 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the proper ways of driving around a roundabout? (Select TWO)
① Vehicles already in the roundabout have priority.
② Enter a roundabout as quickly as possible.
③ Turn on the hazard lights when entering a roundabout.
④ The driving direction in a roundabout is counterclockwise.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Article 25-2 (Methods of Passage at a Roundabout): (1) The driver of any vehicle must pass counterclockwise at a roundabout. (2) The driver of any vehicle, when about to enter a roundabout, must slow down or come to a complete stop, and when there is another vehicle already proceeding, must yield the way to that vehicle.

===== 문제 112 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO statements are correct regarding driving on an expressway?
① All passengers must wear seat belts on an expressway.
② A vehicle driving on an expressway must yield to merging vehicles.
③ When driving on an expressway, you are not obliged to yield even if an emergency vehicle approaches.
④ A vehicle breakdown sign (including a safety tripod sign) must be carried in the vehicle.
정답: 1, 4
해설: A vehicle entering an expressway must yield the right-of-way to vehicles already traveling, and if an emergency vehicle enters while traveling, one must yield. (Article 40 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Sign for a Broken-Down Vehicle): (1) Under Article 66 of the Act, when the driver of a motor vehicle becomes unable to operate the vehicle on an expressway or a motor-vehicle-only road (hereinafter 'expressway, etc.') due to a breakdown or other cause, the driver must install the following signs.)

===== 문제 113 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO descriptions are correct?
① The road surface marking for the "yield" instruction is indicated as a white ＂△＂ sign.
② Cars driving in a lane with the yield mark must yield to cars driving in other lanes.
③ On a road, you must give a lane change signal 30 m before changing lanes.
④ In order to maintain smoother traffic flow, you must narrow the distance with the car in front of you by increasing your  speed.
정답: 2, 3
해설: The road marking for yielding is '▽', and one should drive with the mindset that safety is the top priority even if it obstructs the flow of traffic.

===== 문제 114 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO statements are correct regarding yielding at an intersection with no traffic control?
① When turning left, you must yield to vehicles going straight or turning right at the intersection.
② If you are about to enter the intersection, you are not required to yield to any vehicles that are already inside the  intersection and which are turn
③ If you are about to enter the intersection, you must not yield to any vehicles that are about to enter the intersection  from a narrower road.
④ When multiple vehicles assigned the same priority are about to enter the intersection at the same time, the vehicles on  the right have the right of w
정답: 1, 4
해설: At an intersection without traffic control, the driver of a vehicle intending to turn left must yield the way to a vehicle intending to go straight or turn right at that intersection, and when vehicles with the same priority are about to enter the intersection at the same time, one must yield the way to the vehicle on the right.

===== 문제 115 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which TWO statements are NOT correct  regarding personal mobility devices (PMDs)?
① The electric motor must not operate when traveling at speeds of over 25 km/h, and the device must weigh less than 30 kg.
② Anyone aged 13 or older may operate one without a license.
③ A motor-driven bicycle powered solely by a motor is classified as a personal mobility device under the law.
④ Personal mobility devices may freely use both bicycle lanes and sidewalks.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Article 2 (Definitions) of the Road Traffic Act: a personal mobility device means, among motorized bicycles, one that does not have its motor operate when running at 25 km/h or faster and whose vehicle body weighs less than 30 kilograms, as prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety. A bicycle that can move by motor power alone is classified by law as a personal mobility device.

===== 문제 116 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following drivers is most likely to cause an accident?
① A driver who is focused only on driving
② A driver with a habit of sudden starts, sudden braking, and sudden lane changes
③ A driver who keeps a safe distance from bicycles and two-wheeled vehicles
④ A driver who is a patient
정답: 2
해설: Consideration and yielding are important toward inexperienced drivers, and repeatedly making abrupt starts, sudden braking, and abrupt lane changes has a high likelihood of causing a traffic accident.

===== 문제 117 =====
유형: 문장형

① Keep in mind that a police officer's hand signals overrule traffic lights.

④ Watch out for potholes when driving on a road where heavy rainfall is common or during the rainy season.
정답: 3
해설: It is correct to yield the way to an emergency vehicle, but one must yield the way to an emergency vehicle at a place that is not within or near an intersection.

===== 문제 118 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: You are about to enter an uncontrolled intersection to drive straight through. When a vehicle turning left from  the opposite lane has already entered the intersection, what is the correct way of driv
① Yield to the other vehicle already in the intersection.
② Straight traffic takes the right of way over turning traffic. Drive through.
③ Ignore the vehicle in the intersection; check both sides and quickly drive through.
④ If your lane is wider than that of another vehicle in the intersection, you have the right of way.
정답: 1
해설: Article 26(1) of the Road Traffic Act (Yielding at an Intersection Without Traffic Control): the driver of a vehicle about to enter an intersection without traffic control must, when there is another vehicle already in the intersection, yield the way to that vehicle.

===== 문제 119 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT true about the passenger capacity of personal  mobility devices?
① An electric kick scooter can carry one person.
② A self-balancing scooter can carry one person.
③ An electric bicycle powered by a motor can carry one person.
④ The traffic fine for violating the passenger capacity of a personal mobility device is KRW 40,000.
정답: 3
해설: Article 33-3 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Seating Capacity of a Personal Mobility Device): for a bicycle that can move by motor power alone, the seating capacity is 2 persons. A person who violates this is subject to a fine of 40,000 won.

===== 문제 120 =====
