② Giving precedence to cars over people
③ Prioritizing his/her own car over others' vehicles
④ Accidents are a product of bad luck, not unlawful driving
정답: 1
해설: People take priority over vehicles, other vehicles take priority over oneself, and the occurrence of accidents is determined by law-abiding driving rather than by luck.

(4) Article 50-3(6) of the Road Traffic Act.

===== 문제 122 =====

===== 문제 125 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: If you drive when you are tired, you will not be able to sense correctly the speed at which you are driving.  Which of the following is correct?
① On a narrow road, you will feel as if you are going slower than you actually are.
② On roads with wide-open views, you will feel as if you are going faster than you actually are.
③ You could underestimate the speed of a car coming toward you from far away and end up in an accident.
④ There is no possibility of you mistaking a car stopped ahead on an expressway to be moving.
정답: 3
해설: (1) On a narrow road, one feels the speed is faster than the actual speed. (2) On a road with wide-open surroundings, one feels it is slower than actual. (4) On an expressway, one may mistake a vehicle stopped ahead for a moving vehicle and cause a collision.

===== 문제 127 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO statements are correct regarding penalties imposed on drunk drivers?
① Fully inebriated drivers with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.08% or higher will have their driver's license revoked  and receive punishment in acc
② Those who refuse to take the alcohol breathalyzer test or who have caused fatalities or injuries while driving with a  blood alcohol concentration of
③ Those who are caught driving with a blood alcohol concentration of at least 0.03 percent but less than 0.08 percent will  have their driver's license
④ In a case wherein the driver causes an accident for the first time resulting in property damage while driving with a  blood alcohol concentration of a
정답: 1, 2
해설: In the case of simple drunk driving with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.03 percent or higher but less than 0.08 percent, a person receives a 100-day driver's license suspension and criminal punishment; if a drunk driver with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.03 percent or higher causes a traffic accident involving personal injury, the driver's license is revoked.

===== 문제 128 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO statements are correct regarding drunk driving?
① If a person disagrees with the result of a breathalyzer test, he/she may undergo the test again.
② The Road Traffic Act does not contain any provision for punishing acts that interfere with alcohol testing.
③ A person who interferes with an alcohol test after riding a bicycle while drunk may be subject to punishment.
④ A driver of a motor vehicle who refuses to comply with a police officer’s request to take an alcohol test, despite being  reasonably suspected of bein
정답: 3, 4
해설: (1) Article 44(3) of the Road Traffic Act: measurement may be requested by a method such as taking a blood sample. (2) Article 44(5) of the Road Traffic Act: introduced to enhance the accuracy and fairness of driving enforcement and to reduce the social harm caused by drunk driving [partially amended Jan. 3, 2024, enforced Jun. 4, 2025]. (3) Article 156, subparagraph 12-2 of the Road Traffic Act. (4) Article 93(1)3 of the Road Traffic Act (Revocation and Suspension of a Driver's License).

===== 문제 129 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are true about drowsy and/or fatigued driving? (Select TWO)
① If drowsy while driving, you must consume more caffeine and change lanes more often to avoid monotonous driving.
② Your alertness improves on roads with fewer variations and potential hazards, thereby reducing the risk of drowsy driving.
③ Avoid driving if you have taken cold medicines since they may cause drowsiness.
④ Alcohol deactivates certain cerebral functions and increases the chance of drowsiness.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Driving on an expressway, etc. where changes in the traffic environment are monotonous, dulls one's attention more than driving on urban roads or general roads, and drowsy-driving behavior increases regardless of lack of sleep. In addition, driving under the influence dulls the functioning of the cerebrum and increases the likelihood of drowsy driving. In particular, cold medicine is not a prohibited drug under the Road Traffic Act, but it may contain ingredients that induce drowsiness, so one must be careful when driving after taking it, and if one must drive, it is necessary to consult a pharmacist about the ingredients before taking it.

===== 문제 130 =====

① Administrative fine
② Suspension of the driver's license

④ No penalty
정답: 3

Subparagraph 3: a person who, in violation of Article 45, drives a motor vehicle, etc. or a tram in a state in which normal driving may not be possible due to fatigue or illness (however, this excludes the case of operating a personal mobility device).

===== 문제 131 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Under the Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents, what is the correct legal  responsibility of a driver who causes a traffic accident resulting in injury or death while dr
① The driver will be criminally charged despite being covered by liability car insurance and even after having settled  with  the accident victim(s).
② The driver will be exempted from criminal charges if he/she is covered by car insurance.
③ The driver will be exempted from criminal charges if he/she is covered by comprehensive insurance.
④ The driver will be exempted from criminal charges if he/she is covered by comprehensive insurance and he/she has  settled  with the accident victim(s)
정답: 1
해설: Article 3(1) and (2)8 of the Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents: a person who, in a state in which normal driving may not be possible due to taking drugs as prescribed in the Road Traffic Act, drives and causes a traffic accident involving personal injury is punished by imprisonment without labor for not more than 5 years or a fine not exceeding 20 million won. This is an item subject to criminal punishment regardless of whether the person is enrolled in comprehensive insurance or liability insurance and regardless of any settlement.

===== 문제 132 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Driver A, with blood alcohol concentration of over 0.03%, is rear-ended by Driver B while waiting at a red light.  What happens next?
① Since drunk driving is a serious offense, Driver A should be penalized.
② Although Driver B needs to be penalized for the accident, Driver A is also penalized separately for drunk driving.
③ Driver A is the victim in this accident and will not be subject to administrative penalties.
④ Driver B receives no demerit points regardless of the cause or result of the accident.
정답: 2
해설: Even if driver A, the driver of the vehicle ahead, was intoxicated, unless the drunk driving was a direct cause of the accident, A becomes the victim of the traffic accident and is separately subject to criminal punishment and an administrative license disposition only for the simple drunk driving.

===== 문제 133 =====
유형: 문장형

① 0.01% or higher
② 0.02% or higher
③ 0.03% or higher
④ 0.08% or higher
정답: 3
해설: Article 44(4) (Prohibition of Driving While Intoxicated): the standard for a state of intoxication is a driver's blood alcohol concentration of 0.03 percent or higher.

===== 문제 134 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO correct descriptions with regard to fatigued driving and driving under the  influence of drugs?
① Fatigued driving is not likely to lead to falling asleep at the wheel.
② Fatigued driving is dangerous because it leads to poor focus, judgment, and reflexes.
③ Driving under the influence of drugs and causing an accident resulting in injury are subject to punishment.
④ If your driver's license is revoked because of a hit-and-run event while driving under the influence of drugs, it is  possible to get the license back
정답: 2, 3
해설: Driving while fatigued or while under the influence of drugs tends to reduce attention, judgment, and reaction speed and to induce drowsy driving. In particular, where a person drives in a state in which normal driving is difficult due to the effects of drugs such as narcotics and causes a traffic accident resulting in injury, the person is punished under Article 5-11 of the Act on the Aggravated Punishment, etc. of Specific Crimes (Dangerous Driving Resulting in Death or Injury). In the case of (4), 5 years must have elapsed.

===== 문제 135 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT an appropriate method of preventing retaliatory driving?
① Turn on the emergency flashers in case of emergency braking.
② Turn off the head-beam headlights when a car approaches from the opposite direction.
③ Turn on the high-beam headlights when driving at high speed.
④ Wait for three seconds if the vehicle in front does not start moving right away.
정답: 3
해설: Methods to prevent retaliatory driving include using the turn signal when changing lanes, turning on the hazard lights for others, yielding and being considerate, allowing 3 seconds of consideration when starting late, and not provoking others with the horn or high-beam headlights.

===== 문제 136 =====

② Calling 112
③ Reporting to the smartphone app Safety e-Report
④ Reporting the incident to the Cyber Police Agency
정답: 1
해설: When subjected to retaliatory driving, one can report it to 112, the websites of the National Police Agency and the City/Provincial Police Agency, the Safety Report app, etc.

===== 문제 137 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, drivers of (   ) are prohibited from driving their motor vehicles and the like  in a row or side by side without good cause and in a manner that endangers other pers
① self-balancing scooters - imprisonment with labor for not more than 1 year or criminal fine of not more than KRW 5 million
② two-wheeled vehicles - imprisonment with labor for not more than 6 months or criminal fine of not more than KRW 3  million
③ special-purpose vehicles -imprisonment with labor for not more than 2 years or criminal fine of not more than KRW 5 million
④ motorcycles - imprisonment with labor for not more than 6 months or criminal fine of not more than KRW 3 million
정답: 3
해설: Article 46(1) of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Joint Dangerous Conduct): the drivers of motor vehicles, etc. (excluding personal mobility devices) must not, on a road, have two or more persons jointly drive two or more motor vehicles, etc. in a row front-to-back or side-by-side without justifiable cause and thereby harm others or cause a traffic hazard. This may be punished by imprisonment for not more than 2 years or a fine not exceeding 5 million won. An electric two-wheeled self-balancing device is a personal mobility device and is not subject to the above provision. Article 2, subparagraph 21 of the Road Traffic Act: 'motor vehicle, etc.' means a motor vehicle and a motorized bicycle. Article 2, subparagraph 18 of the Road Traffic Act: a 'motor vehicle' means a vehicle driven using an engine without using railways or overhead lines (a towed motor vehicle is also regarded as part of a motor vehicle), being any of the following: (a) the following motor vehicles under Article 3 of the 'Motor Vehicle Management Act' (however, excluding motorized bicycles): 1) passenger cars, 2) buses (vans), 3) trucks, 4) special vehicles, 5) motorcycles; (b) construction machinery under the proviso to Article 26(1) of the 'Construction Machinery Management Act'.

===== 문제 138 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: You may be subject to a penalty according to the Criminal Act if you overtake a vehicle over the centerline or  perform any retaliatory driving such as sudden braking. In this case, what is the correc
① You will be sentenced to imprisonment for up to 7 years or criminal fine of not more than KRW 10 million.

③ You will be sentenced to imprisonment for a limited term of more than 1 year.
④ You will be sentenced to imprisonment for a limited term of more than 18 months.

해설: Under Article 284 of the 'Criminal Act' (Special Intimidation), a person who commits the crime of intimidation under the Criminal Act using a motor vehicle, a dangerous object, is punished by imprisonment for not more than 7 years or a fine not exceeding 10 million won.

===== 문제 139 =====
유형: 문장형

① You will be sentenced to imprisonment for a limited term in the range of 1 year - 10 years.
② You will be sentenced to imprisonment for a limited term in the range of 1 year - 20 years.
③ You will be sentenced to imprisonment for a limited term in the range of 2 years - 10 years.
④ You will be sentenced to imprisonment for a limited term in the range of 2 years - 20 years.

===== 문제 140 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not subject to legal action for reckless driving under the Road Traffic Act?
① Violating the maximum speed limit
② Violating a prohibition of crossing the road, making a U-turn, or moving backward
③ Cutting in a lane
④ Honking continuously
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act: (1) speed violation under Article 17(3); (2) violation of the prohibition of crossing, U-turn, or reversing under Article 18(1); (4) generating noise without justifiable cause under Article 49(1)8. Option (3) concerns Article 23, and cutting in is not a subject of the reckless-driving violation.

===== 문제 141 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A driver of a motor vehicle (excluding personal mobility devices) threatening another driver by committing the  acts below in a repeated manner will be punished for reckless driving. Which of the foll
① Violation of traffic signals or instructions
② Violation of prohibitions on crossing, making a U-turn, and driving backward
③ Creating noise without reasonable cause
④ Violating a designated lane of an expressway
정답: 4
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Reckless Driving): violation of a signal or instruction, crossing the center line, speed violation, violation of the prohibition of crossing/U-turn/reversing, failure to keep a safe distance, violation of the prohibition of lane change, violation of the prohibition of sudden braking, violation of overtaking methods or of the prohibition of obstructing overtaking, generating noise without justifiable cause, violation of overtaking methods on an expressway, and crossing/U-turn/reversing on an expressway, etc.

===== 문제 142 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the correct punishment for reckless driving?
① Notice for payment of traffic penalty of KRW 60,000
② Administrative fine of KRW 30,000
③ Imprisonment for up to 6 months or criminal fine of not more than KRW 2 million
④ Imprisonment for up to 1 year or criminal fine of not more than KRW 5 million
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 46-3 and Article 151-2 of the Road Traffic Act, reckless driving is punished by imprisonment for not more than 1 year or a fine not exceeding 5 million won.

===== 문제 143 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the most incorrect action by the driver when a vehicle's cargo (including the driver's vehicle) falls into  the lane while driving on an expressway?
① Stop the vehicle in a safe place while checking for vehicles behind.
② Report the case promptly to the relevant authorities such as expressway management agency.
③ Stop the vehicle in a safe place and check the cargo loading status.
④ Conduct retaliatory driving against the driver who dropped the cargo.
정답: 4
해설: Article 39(4) of the Road Traffic Act (Methods of and Restrictions on Riding or Loading): the driver of any vehicle must take necessary measures, such as covering or tying down, so that the cargo loaded does not fall off while driving, ensuring it is securely fastened.

===== 문제 144 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following cannot be construed as reckless driving for a  motorcycle (excluding personal mobility devices)?
① The driver repeatedly ignored traffic signals three times.
② The driver committed successive violations of exceeding the speed limit and disobeying instructions.
③ The driver committed successive violations of ignoring traffic signals and crossing the centerline.
④ The driver committed successive violations of crossing the centerline as well as the duty to protect pedestrians.
정답: 4
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act: one must not consecutively perform two or more of the acts of violation of a signal or instruction, crossing the center line, speed violation, violation of the prohibition of crossing/U-turn/reversing, failure to keep a safe distance, violation of the prohibition of changing lanes, violation of the prohibition of sudden braking, violation of overtaking methods or of the prohibition of obstructing overtaking, generating noise without justifiable cause, violation of overtaking methods on an expressway, and crossing/U-turn/reversing on an expressway, etc., or continuously or repeatedly perform a single act, thereby threatening or harming others or causing a traffic hazard. Violation of the duty to protect pedestrians is not included in the acts of reckless driving.

===== 문제 145 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding reckless driving and retaliatory driving?
① A motorcycle driver who generates noise without just cause to threaten many unspecified drivers can be penalized for  retaliatory driving.
② The driver of a passenger car who drives over the centerline and exceeds the speed limit repeatedly to threaten many  unspecified drivers can be penal
③ The driver of a large truck who intentionally overtakes a car and brakes suddenly can be penalized for reckless driving.
④ A bus driver who repeatedly violates the legal way of overtaking a car, thereby increasing the risk of traffic accident, can  be penalized for retalia
정답: 2
해설: Reckless driving is a driving act that endangers and obstructs others and gives displeasure and danger to unspecified persons, and is governed by the 'Road Traffic Act'; retaliatory driving is an act of intentionally and deliberately threatening a specific person, and is governed by the 'Criminal Act'.

===== 문제 146 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Causing harm to the life and safety of others by repeatedly driving over the centerline or causing risk in the  traffic flow are (    ) according to the Road Traffic Act. Which of the following fits i
① joint risky behavior
② reckless driving
③ violent driving
④ retaliatory driving
정답: 2
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Reckless Driving): the drivers of motor vehicles, etc. (excluding personal mobility devices) must not consecutively perform two or more of the following acts, or continuously or repeatedly perform a single act, thereby threatening or harming others or causing a traffic hazard.

===== 문제 147 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A driver of a motor vehicle (excluding personal mobility devices) threatening another driver by committing the  acts below in a repeated manner on a general road will be punished for reckless driving.
① Violating a designated lane on a general road
② Driving over the centerline and violating the prohibition on sudden braking
③ Not complying with the safe distance, violating lane-changing prohibitions
④ Improperly overtaking a car on a general road
정답: 1
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Reckless Driving): violation of a signal or instruction, crossing the center line, speed violation, violation of the prohibition of crossing/U-turn/reversing, failure to keep a safe distance, violation of the prohibition of lane change, violation of the prohibition of sudden braking, violation of overtaking methods or of the prohibition of obstructing overtaking, generating noise without justifiable cause, violation of overtaking methods on an expressway, and crossing/U-turn/reversing on an expressway, etc.

===== 문제 148 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A driver of a motor vehicle (excluding personal mobility devices) threatening another person by repeatedly  committing a series of two or more of the acts below will be punished for reckless driving.
① Not keeping the eyes forward; operating a video display device while driving
② Exceeding the speed limit while overtaking a car on his/her right side
③ Not securing sufficient safe distance and repeating sudden braking
④ Exceeding the speed limit to obstruct a trailing car that is attempting to pass by
정답: 1
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Reckless Driving): violation of a signal or instruction, crossing the center line, speed violation, violation of the prohibition of crossing/U-turn/reversing, failure to keep a safe distance, violation of the prohibition of lane change, violation of the prohibition of sudden braking, violation of overtaking methods or of the prohibition of obstructing overtaking, generating noise without justifiable cause, violation of overtaking methods on an expressway, and crossing/U-turn/reversing on an expressway, etc.

===== 문제 149 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A driver of a motor vehicle (excluding personal mobility devices) threatening another driver by repeatedly  committing the acts below will be punished for reckless driving. Which of the following is N
① Violation of traffic signals and instructions, driving over the centerline
② Not securing sufficient safe distance, violating the prohibition on sudden braking
③ Violating the prohibition on obstructing attempts to pass, improperly overtaking a car
④ Violating the prohibition on passage, using a mobile phone while driving
정답: 4
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Reckless Driving): violation of a signal or instruction, crossing the center line, speed violation, violation of the prohibition of crossing/U-turn/reversing, failure to keep a safe distance, violation of the prohibition of lane change, violation of the prohibition of sudden braking, violation of overtaking methods or of the prohibition of obstructing overtaking, generating noise without justifiable cause, violation of overtaking methods on an expressway, and crossing/U-turn/reversing on an expressway, etc.

===== 문제 150 =====

① Stopping or parking on the roadside of an expressway
② Driving while under the influence of alcohol
③ Driving in an exclusive lane on a general road
④ Driving over the centerline
정답: 4
해설: Article 46-3 of the Road Traffic Act (Prohibition of Reckless Driving): violation of a signal or instruction, crossing the center line, speed violation, violation of the prohibition of crossing/U-turn/reversing, failure to keep a safe distance, violation of the prohibition of lane change, violation of the prohibition of sudden braking, violation of overtaking methods or of the prohibition of obstructing overtaking, generating noise without justifiable cause, violation of overtaking methods on an expressway, and crossing/U-turn/reversing on an expressway, etc.

===== 문제 152 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A driver was arrested for causing special injury under the Criminal Act using vehicles, etc. (retaliatory driving).  What is the administrative action for this person's driver's license?
① Cancellation of driver's license
② Suspension of driver's license for 100 days
③ Suspension of driver's license for 60 days
④ No actions to be taken
정답: 1
해설: Table 28 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act: the license is revoked when a person is detained for committing special bodily injury, special intimidation, or special property damage under the Criminal Act using a motor vehicle, etc. When the person is criminally booked, 100 penalty points are imposed.

===== 문제 153 =====
유형: 문장형

① Dangerous collective conduct
② Intersection tailgating
③ Cutting in
④ Disorderly conduct
정답: 1
해설: Article 46(1) (Prohibition of Joint Dangerous Conduct): the drivers of motor vehicles, etc. must not, on a road, have two or more persons jointly drive two or more motor vehicles, etc. in a row front-to-back or side-by-side without justifiable cause and thereby harm others or cause a traffic hazard.

===== 문제 154 =====

④ A driver who turns off his/her headlights and parks on the shoulder of the freeway at night, posing a danger to others

===== 문제 155 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a desirable driving habit?
① Turn off the engine while refueling.
② Unless there is an urgent situation, do not apply the brake suddenly, instigating other vehicles to do the same.
③ To predict potential hazards and practice defensive driving, observe the speed limit and keep a safe distance.
④ To ensure driving stability, tire pressure should be 10% higher than the recommended standard regardless of the season.
정답: 4
해설: The appropriate tire pressure is 80% of the maximum pressure, and by season it is helpful for safety to inflate about 10% less in summer and about 10% more in winter.

===== 문제 157 =====

정답: 1
해설: 'Sidewalk' means the part of a road marked by a curb line, traffic sign, or similar artificial structure so as to allow passage of pedestrians (including persons who move using devices and equipment prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety, such as a baby carriage, a wheelchair for walking assistance, or a walker for the elderly and infirm, and outdoor mobile robots under subparagraph 21-3; the same applies hereinafter). (Article 2, subparagraph 10 of the Road Traffic Act.) The equipment and devices prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety are those with a width of 1 meter or less: baby carriages, wheelchairs for walking assistance, walkers for the elderly and infirm, children's play equipment, non-powered handcarts, motorcycles, etc. that the operator pushes or carries by dismounting, equipment used for road repair and maintenance, etc., and outdoor mobile robots under subparagraph 21-3 (Article 2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act).

===== 문제 159 =====
유형: 문장형

===== 문제 160 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about a driver's duty to protect pedestrians?
① A driver who sees pedestrians crossing a crosswalk must come to a full stop before he/she reaches the crosswalk.
② The driver must slow down while keeping a safe distance when passing by pedestrians on a narrow road with no markings.
③ If a crosswalk in a school zone has no traffic lights, the driver has no duty to stop if there are no pedestrians crossing.
④ The driver should not obstruct pedestrians crossing an intersection with no traffic lights.
정답: 3
해설: Article 27(1), (3), (4), and (7) of the Road Traffic Act. (1) The driver of any vehicle or tram must, when a pedestrian (including the driver of a bicycle, etc. who, under Article 13-2(6), dismounts and pushes or carries the bicycle, etc.) is crossing or about to cross a crosswalk, come to a complete stop in front of that crosswalk (or, where a stop line is installed, at that stop line) so as not to obstruct or endanger the pedestrian's crossing. (3) The driver of any vehicle must not obstruct the passage of pedestrians crossing an intersection without traffic control or a road near it. (4) The driver of any vehicle must, when there is a pedestrian in a safety zone installed on the road, and when passing beside a pedestrian on a narrow road without lanes, slow down while keeping a safe distance. (7) The driver of any vehicle or tram must, in front of a crosswalk without a traffic light among the crosswalks installed within a school zone under Article 12(1) (or, where a stop line is installed, at that stop line), come to a complete stop regardless of whether a pedestrian is crossing. <Newly established Jan. 11, 2022>

===== 문제 161 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about pedestrian-first roads?
① On a pedestrian-first road, pedestrians can only walk along the right edge of the road.
② Drivers are obligated to exercise various forms of pedestrian protection such as slowing down and coming to a  complete stop.
③ A driver who fails to fulfill his/her duty to protect pedestrians is subject to a traffic penalty of KRW 40,000 and a penalty  of 10 points based on t
④ If deemed necessary for pedestrian protection, the chief of a police station can limit the vehicle speed to 20 km/h or  less.
정답: 1
해설: On a pedestrian-priority road, pedestrians may pass along the entire part of the road. In this case, pedestrians must not intentionally obstruct the progress of vehicles (Article 8(3)1 of the Road Traffic Act). The driver of a vehicle permitted to pass on a pedestrian-only road must operate the vehicle at pedestrian walking speed or come to a complete stop so as not to endanger pedestrians or obstruct their passage (Article 28(3) of the Road Traffic Act). Where the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency or the chief of a police station deems it necessary to protect pedestrians on a pedestrian-priority road, they may limit the passing speed of vehicles to within 20 km/h (Article 28-2 of the Road Traffic Act).

===== 문제 162 =====

① Brake suddenly because the pedestrian is crossing the road.
② Go past the pedestrian by predicting his/her movement.
③ Slow down your car, turn on the emergency flashers to notify the car behind you, and stop safely.
④ Slightly increase the speed to pass while warning the pedestrian by honking.
정답: 3
해설: Upon discovering a pedestrian crossing illegally (jaywalking), reduce speed, prepare to stop, warn following vehicles with the hazard lights, and come to a stop safely.

===== 문제 164 =====

===== 문제 165 =====
유형: 문장형

정답: 4
해설: An intoxicated pedestrian crossing illegally is also a subject of protection. Even when the green pedestrian signal is flashing, one must not neglect pedestrian protection, and when turning right at an intersection with traffic lights, one must not obstruct pedestrians who are following the signal.

===== 문제 166 =====

④ A funeral procession holding flags or banners
정답: 1
해설: A person leading a large animal; a person carrying a ladder, timber, or other object likely to obstruct pedestrian passage; a person doing work such as cleaning or repair on the road; a procession of a military unit or a comparable organization; a procession carrying a flag, banner, etc.; and a funeral procession must pass along the right side of the roadway. (Article 9 of the Road Traffic Act and Article 7 of its Enforcement Decree.)

===== 문제 167 =====

③ When there are no pedestrians crossing, proceed quickly.
④ Honk while driving through to alarm the pedestrians.
정답: 1
해설: At a crosswalk without a traffic light, one must carefully watch the path ahead and nearby and drive at reduced speed for any pedestrian who may be there.

===== 문제 168 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: If a vehicle is stopped on the railroad crossing due to vehicle failure while passing through, which of the  following is NOT proper behavior by the driver?
① Immediately evacuate all passengers.
② Use emergency signal devices.
③ Notify a railway official.
④ Quickly check the cause of the vehicle’s failure.
정답: 4
해설: Article 24 of the Road Traffic Act (Passing Railroad Crossings): where the driver of any vehicle or tram, while passing a crossing, becomes unable to operate the vehicle or tram within the crossing due to a breakdown or other cause, the driver must immediately evacuate the passengers and use an emergency signal or other means to notify a railroad employee or a police officer of the fact.

===== 문제 169 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A vehicle is about to go across a sidewalk to enter a building. Which of the following is the correct sequence?

② Stop → honk → enter quickly
③ Slow down → check both sides → enter slowly
④ Stop → check both sides → enter slowly
정답: 4
해설: Article 13(2) of the Road Traffic Act (Passage of Vehicles): the driver of a vehicle must, immediately before crossing a sidewalk, come to a complete stop, check the left and right portions, etc., and then cross so as not to obstruct the passage of pedestrians.

===== 문제 170 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is an INCORRECT description of safe pedestrian  crossing?
① Do not walk right in front of or behind cars to cross the road.
② You must cross the road by using a crosswalk or such facilities even if you have a disability.
③ Do not cross the road at a section marked with a safety sign or one that specifically instructs pedestrians not to cross.
④ When there is no crosswalk, cross the road at its shortest section.
정답: 2
해설: Article 10(2) of the Road Traffic Act (Crossing the Road): a person with a physical disability who cannot use a road-crossing facility such as an underpass or a pedestrian overpass may cross the road without using such crossing facility by a method that does not obstruct other traffic.

===== 문제 171 =====

④ Right edge of the road

===== 문제 172 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding safe pedestrian crossing?
① Pedestrians must walk quickly around the front or rear of vehicles to cross the street.
② Disabled pedestrians must cross the road using road-crossing facilities where such facilities are present.
③ Where a sign prohibiting road crossing is present, pedestrians must cross the road quickly.
④ On a road with no crosswalk, pedestrians must cross using the shortest line of travel possible.
정답: 4
해설: On a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, pedestrians must always pass on the sidewalk. A person with a physical disability may cross without using a road-crossing facility. However, where crossing is prohibited by a traffic sign, etc., they may not cross. (Article 10 of the Road Traffic Act.)

===== 문제 176 =====

In this case, the processions, etc. must pass along the right side of the roadway. Article 13(3): the driver of a vehicle must pass along the right portion of the center of the road (on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, the roadway) (where a center line is installed, the center line; the same applies hereinafter).

===== 문제 180 =====

① Sidewalk
② Roadside area
③ Crosswalk

===== 문제 181 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT appropriate behavior on the part of a driver toward pedestrians?
① Watch out for children in areas with the "school zone" sign.
② Drive slowly to protect pedestrians who are about to cross a crosswalk.
③ Pedestrians crossing the road illegally must also be protected.
④ Stop if there is a child crossing the road without a guardian.
정답: 2
해설: Article 13(1) and (2) of the Road Traffic Act: immediately before crossing a sidewalk, one must come to a complete stop, check the left and right portions, etc., and then cross so as not to obstruct the passage of pedestrians.

===== 문제 182 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an INCORRECT method of crossing the road for a pedestrian?
① Pedestrians must take the shortest route when crossing a road without a crosswalk.
② Pedestrians must not cross the road around the front or rear of a vehicle.
③ Pedestrians can climb over a lane partition placed to prevent illegal crossing on the road.
④ Pedestrians can walk on the road if the sidewalk is closed for construction.
정답: 3
해설: Article 8 of the Road Traffic Act (Passage of Pedestrians): (1) on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, pedestrians must always pass on the sidewalk. However, this does not apply when crossing the roadway, when passage on the sidewalk is prohibited due to road construction, etc., or in other unavoidable cases. Article 10 of the Road Traffic Act (Crossing the Road): (2) on a road where a crosswalk, underpass, pedestrian overpass, or other road-crossing facility under paragraph (1) is installed, pedestrians must cross there. However, a person with a physical disability who cannot use a road-crossing facility such as an underpass or a pedestrian overpass may cross the road without using such crossing facility by a method that does not obstruct other traffic. (3) on a road without a crosswalk under paragraph (1), pedestrians must cross by the shortest distance. (4) pedestrians must not cross directly in front of or behind a vehicle or a tram. However, this does not apply when crossing on a crosswalk, or when crossing the road in accordance with a traffic light or the signal or instruction of a police officer, etc.

===== 문제 183 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT classified as a person corresponding to one who is visually impaired?
① Infant or child
② An individual who is unable to walk without a prosthetic limb
③ An individual who has trouble maintaining bodily balance
④ A deaf person
정답: 1
해설: Under Article 11(2) of the Road Traffic Act and Article 8 of its Enforcement Decree, a person equivalent to a person who cannot see means a person falling under any of the following: 1. a person who cannot hear; 2. a person with a disability in the body's balance function; 3. a person who cannot walk without using a prosthetic leg, etc.

===== 문제 184 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: The driver of a passenger car who commits a violation of a traffic signal between ( ) and ( ) in a school zone  receives double the demerit points. Which of the following belongs in the pair of bracke
① 6 AM, 6 PM
② 7 AM, 7 PM
③ 8 AM, 8 PM
④ 9 AM, 9 PM
정답: 3
해설: Article 12 (Designation, Removal, and Management of School Zones); Table 28 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Standards for Revocation and Suspension Dispositions of a Driver's License), item 4: notwithstanding the above table, a driver who commits any of the following violations between 8 a.m. and 8 p.m. within a school zone or a zone for the protection of the elderly or persons with disabilities is assigned the penalty points prescribed in each item: (a) a violation falling under subparagraph 1 or subparagraph 3: 120 points; (b) a violation falling under subparagraph 3-2, 9, 14, 15, or 25 (excluding Article 27(7) of the Act): twice the penalty points assigned to that violation.

===== 문제 185 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the administrative fine imposed on the driver of a 4.5-ton truck parked illegally in a silver zone between  10 AM and 11 AM?
① KRW 40,000
② KRW 50,000
③ KRW 90,000
④ KRW 100,000
정답: 3
해설: Table 7 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, subparagraph 3: for a vehicle that has stopped or parked in violation of Articles 32 to 34 of the Act, the employer, etc. is subject to an administrative fine of 90,000 won for a bus (van), etc. where the violation is within 2 hours.

===== 문제 186 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an INCORRECT method of traveling for a pedestrian?
① Walk on the left side of a sidewalk.
② Pedestrians are allowed to use any part of the pedestrian-first road.
③ Pedestrians must walk on the sidewalk on roads where the sidewalk and roadways are separated.
④ For roads without a clear distinction of the sidewalks but have a centerline, pedestrians must use the roadside area to  walk.
정답: 1
해설: Article 8 of the Road Traffic Act (Passage of Pedestrians): as a principle, pedestrians keep to the right on the sidewalk.

===== 문제 187 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT permitted to travel on a roadway?
① Cleaning or maintenance personnel working on roads
② A person driving a large animal such as horse or cattle
③ A person who is pushing a stroller
④ A funeral procession
정답: 3
해설: <Article 7 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act (Persons or Processions That May Pass Along the Roadway)>: the 'persons or processions prescribed by Presidential Decree' in the former part of Article 9(1) of the Act means persons or processions falling under any of the following.

===== 문제 188 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What are the grounds for the penalty faced by a driver who stops over the stop line when the traffic light has  turned red?
① Violation of intersection thoroughfare
② Failure to stop
③ Violation of traffic signal
④ Violation of slow driving
정답: 3
해설: If a vehicle stops beyond the stop line when the traffic light is red, it is punished for a signal violation.

===== 문제 189 =====
유형: 문장형

① Keep going if stopping or parking is not allowed in the area.
② Stop momentarily.
③ Stop and help the person go back the way he/she came.
④ Protect the person by honking.
정답: 2
해설: <Article 49 of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers, etc.)>: (1) the driver of any vehicle must observe the following matters. 2. Come to a complete stop in any of the following cases: (b) when a person who cannot see is crossing the road with measures such as carrying a white cane or being accompanied by a guide dog for the disabled.

===== 문제 190 =====
유형: 문장형

① slow down
② stop
③ slow down or stop
④ reduce speed
정답: 2
해설: Article 27 of the Road Traffic Act (Protection of Pedestrians): (7) the driver of any vehicle must, in front of a crosswalk without a traffic light among the crosswalks installed within a school zone, come to a complete stop regardless of whether a pedestrian is crossing.

===== 문제 191 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding the protection of pedestrians crossing roads?
① You have no responsibility to protect pedestrians on sections of a road other than an intersection.
② You have no responsibility to protect pedestrians crossing the road illegally.
③ You have to protect pedestrians even when they are crossing the road illegally.
④ You have no responsibility to protect pedestrians crossing a one-way road illegally.
정답: 3
해설: <Article 27 of the Road Traffic Act (Protection of Pedestrians)>: (5) the driver of any vehicle or tram must, when a pedestrian is crossing a road without a crosswalk under Article 10(3), come to a complete stop while keeping a safe distance so that the pedestrian can cross safely.

===== 문제 192 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Who is NOT allowed to drive on the road according to the Road Traffic Act?
① A person using a wheelchair to aid his/her movement
② A person marching on the streets for an important social event
③ A person cleaning or doing maintenance work on a road
④ A person carrying an object that can hinder pedestrian thoroughfare, such as a ladder
정답: 1
해설: <Article 7 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act (Persons or Processions That May Pass Along the Roadway)>: the 'persons or processions prescribed by Presidential Decree' in the former part of Article 9(1) of the Act means persons or processions falling under any of the following: 1. a person leading a large animal such as a horse or an ox; 2. a person carrying a ladder, timber, or other object likely to obstruct pedestrian passage; 3. a person doing work such as cleaning or repair on the road; 4. a procession of a military unit or a comparable organization; 5. a procession carrying a flag or a banner, etc.; 6. a funeral procession.

===== 문제 193 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the best method of crossing the road for a pedestrian when the green light at the  crosswalk is flashing?
① A pedestrian who is not yet in the crosswalk must wait for the next green light to start crossing.
② Stand still in the middle of the crosswalk.
③ Cross the road without waiting for the next signal.
④ The pedestrian can start crossing the road as long as the light does not turn red.
정답: 1
해설: <Table 2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, Types of Signals Displayed by Traffic Lights and Their Meanings>: flashing green light: pedestrians must not begin crossing, and a pedestrian who is crossing must promptly complete the crossing or stop crossing and return to the sidewalk.

===== 문제 194 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is correct regarding pedestrians using the sidewalk?
① A person who rides a two-wheeled vehicle up to 125cc on the sidewalk can be considered a pedestrian.
② A person riding a bicycle can be considered a pedestrian.
③ A person who uses a wheelchair can be considered a pedestrian.
④ A person who rides a 49cc motorcycle can be considered a pedestrian.
정답: 3
해설: Article 2 (Definitions), subparagraph 10 of the Road Traffic Act: 'sidewalk' means the part of a road marked by a curb line, traffic sign, or similar artificial structure so as to allow passage of pedestrians (including a baby carriage and a wheelchair for walking assistance prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety; the same applies hereinafter).

===== 문제 195 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which TWO correctly describe the rules for a pedestrian-only road?
① The driver of a motor vehicle or a horse allowed on a pedestrian-only road must travel at the same speed as the  pedestrians.
② In principle, the driver of a motor vehicle or a horse may use a pedestrian-only road.
③ The driver of a motor vehicle or a horse may not be allowed to enter a pedestrian-only road if and when deemed  necessary by the chief of police.
④ If there is a possibility that pedestrians could be exposed to danger, the driver of a motor vehicle or a horse on a  pedestrian-only road must stop a
정답: 1, 4
해설: Article 28 of the Road Traffic Act (Installation of Pedestrian-Only Roads): (1) the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency or the chief of a police station may install pedestrian-only roads on a road where it is particularly necessary to protect the passage of pedestrians. (2) the driver of a vehicle must not pass on a pedestrian-only road under paragraph (1). However, the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency or the chief of a police station may permit the passage of vehicles on a pedestrian-only road where they deem it particularly necessary. (3) the driver of a vehicle permitted to pass on a pedestrian-only road under the proviso to paragraph (2) must operate the vehicle at pedestrian walking speed or come to a complete stop so as not to endanger pedestrians or obstruct their passage.

===== 문제 196 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When a cargo falls from a vehicle and injures a pedestrian, necessitating a two-week period of hospitalization,  which two penalties among the following will be imposed on the driver?
① Criminal offense charges are imposed on the driver regardless of the victim’s wishes to file charges.
② No charges are filed if the driver comes to an agreement with the victim.
③ No charges are filed if the driver has a full-coverage insurance policy.
④ The driver is charged with a criminal offense regardless of the presence of a full-coverage insurance policy.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Article 3(2)12 of the Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents (Special Cases Concerning Punishment): this applies to the case of driving in violation of Article 39(4) of the 'Road Traffic Act' without taking the necessary measures to prevent the motor vehicle's cargo from falling off, so the person is subject to criminal punishment even if enrolled in comprehensive insurance.

===== 문제 197 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the most appropriate method of crossing a road  without a crosswalk for a pedestrian?
① Cross the road right behind a passing vehicle.
② Cross the road quickly when there are no cars passing by.
③ Cross anywhere as there is no crosswalk.
④ Cross the road using the shortest distance.
정답: 4
해설: <Article 10(3) of the Road Traffic Act>: on a road without a crosswalk under paragraph (1), pedestrians must cross by the shortest distance.

===== 문제 198 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT a correct explanation of how to traverse a  crosswalk?
① A person may cross the crosswalk while pushing a personal mobility device.
② A person may cross the crosswalk while on a motorized chair.
③ A person may cross the crosswalk while riding a bicycle.
④ A person may cross the crosswalk while pushing a stroller.
정답: 3
해설: Article 2, subparagraph 12 of the Road Traffic Act: a crosswalk means the part of a road marked by a traffic sign so that pedestrians can cross the road. Article 27(1) of the Road Traffic Act: the driver of any vehicle must, when a pedestrian (including the driver of a bicycle who, under Article 13-2(6), dismounts and pushes the bicycle) is crossing a crosswalk, come to a complete stop in front of that crosswalk (or, where a stop line is installed, at that stop line) so as not to obstruct or endanger the pedestrian's crossing.

===== 문제 199 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following methods of passage used by motor vehicles or horses  is NOT correct?
① When the roadway and sidewalk are separated, be sure to use the roadway.
② Drive slowly just before crossing a crosswalk, check left and right, and cross in a manner that does not impede the  passage of pedestrians.
③ Be sure to use the right-side roadway.
④ When it is a one-way road, you can drive in the center or left portion of the road.
정답: 2
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 13(2)> In the case of the proviso to paragraph (1), the driver of a vehicle or horse must come to a complete stop immediately before crossing the sidewalk, check the left and right sides, and then cross without obstructing the passage of pedestrians.

===== 문제 200 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following guidelines for the protection of pedestrians is NOT  correct?
① When a pedestrian is using the crosswalk, the driver must make a temporary stop before the crosswalk.
② The driver shall not impede the passage of pedestrians who are crossing the road according to the signals or directions  given by a police officer.
③ A driver shall not impede the passage of pedestrians who are crossing the road at an intersection.
④ When a pedestrian is crossing a road without a crosswalk, the driver must drive slowly while maintaining a safe distance.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians) (1) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when a pedestrian (including a cyclist who, pursuant to Article 13-2(6), dismounts and pushes or carries a bicycle, etc.) is crossing or about to cross a crosswalk, come to a complete stop before that crosswalk (or at the stop line where one is installed) so as not to obstruct or endanger the crossing of the pedestrian. (2) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar who intends to turn left or right at an intersection where traffic is being controlled must not obstruct the passage of pedestrians crossing the road in accordance with the signals or instructions of a traffic light or a police officer, etc. (3) Every driver of a vehicle must not obstruct the passage of pedestrians crossing an intersection where traffic is not being controlled or a road in its vicinity. (5) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when a pedestrian is crossing a road on which no crosswalk is installed pursuant to Article 10(3), keep a safe distance and come to a complete stop so that the pedestrian can cross safely.

===== 문제 201 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of passing through a crosswalk for a vehicle if traffic signals for  both vehicles and pedestrians are out of order?
① If a pedestrian is crossing, pass through at a slow speed.
② If there are no pedestrians on the crosswalk, do not slow down but be sure to pass through quickly.
③ The driver need not drive slowly, as the crosswalk has lost its function due to the breakdown of the signal lights.
④ If a pedestrian is crossing, make a temporary stop just in front of the crosswalk.
정답: 4
해설: <Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 27(1)> Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when a pedestrian (including a cyclist who, pursuant to Article 13-2(6), dismounts and pushes or carries a bicycle, etc.) is crossing or about to cross a crosswalk, come to a complete stop before that crosswalk (or at the stop line where one is installed) so as not to obstruct or endanger the crossing of the pedestrian.

===== 문제 202 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the most appropriate method of using a road with  no clear distinction of a sidewalk but has a centerline for a pedestrian?
① Walk along the center of the roadway.
② Walk along the right side of the roadway.
③ Walk along the roadside area.
④ Use all areas of the road to walk on.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 8 (Passage of Pedestrians) (1) On a road where the sidewalk and roadway are not separated but that has a center line (including, in the case of a one-way road, a road divided by lane lines), a pedestrian must walk on the edge of the road or in the road-edge zone. (2) A pedestrian may walk on all parts of the road in any of the following places. In this case, the pedestrian must not intentionally obstruct the progress of a vehicle or horse: 1. a road where the sidewalk and roadway are not separated and that has no center line (in the case of a one-way road, limited to a road not divided by lane lines; hereinafter the same shall apply); 2. a pedestrian-priority road.

===== 문제 203 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the correct method of passing for a motor vehicle  or a horse that is permitted to pass through a pedestrian-only road?
① If there are any pedestrians, proceed slowly.
② Proceed while honking.
③ Drive at the walking speed of pedestrians or make a temporary stop.
④ If there are no pedestrians, proceed quickly.
정답: 3
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 28(3)> The driver of a vehicle or horse permitted to pass on a pedestrian-only road must drive the vehicle at the walking speed of pedestrians or come to a complete stop so as not to endanger pedestrians or obstruct their passage.

===== 문제 204 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is a section of road where boundaries are indicated by  a line or curbstones, safety signs, or any similar artificial structures that allow pe
① A sidewalk
② A roadside area
③ A crosswalk
④ A bicycle crossing
정답: 1
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 10> A sidewalk means the part of a road demarcated by curb lines, traffic signs, or similar artificial structures so that pedestrians (including baby carriages and walking-aid wheelchairs prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety; hereinafter the same shall apply) can pass.

===== 문제 205 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT the correct method  of crossing when pedestrian signals are flashing?
① Pedestrians should not start crossing.
② Pedestrians in the middle of the crossing should quickly finish crossing.
③ Pedestrians should stop crossing and return to the sidewalk.
④ Pedestrians should stop crossing and wait in place for the next signal.
정답: 4
해설: <Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 6(2), Table 2> Among the types and meanings of signals indicated by traffic lights, the pedestrian signal light.

===== 문제 206 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is a situation wherein the driver of a motor vehicle  must drive slowly?
① When seeing a person crossing a crosswalk with a bicycle
② When driving past a pedestrian along a back road
③ When seeing a pedestrian crossing a crosswalk
④ When seeing a pedestrian crossing a road with no crosswalk
정답: 2
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 27(4)> Every driver of a vehicle must, when there are pedestrians on a safety zone installed on the road, or when passing beside a pedestrian on a narrow road with no lanes, keep a safe distance and slow down.

===== 문제 207 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, raised crosswalks are installed on roads where it is necessary to limit the  speed to (    ) km/h or less. Which of the following is correct?
① 10
② 20
③ 30
④ 50
정답: 3
해설: A raised crosswalk is installed at points on a road where the speed limit needs to be restricted to 30 km/h or less and where it is necessary to raise the crosswalk above the road surface to draw the driver's attention. (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, 5, 533)

===== 문제 208 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT a circumstance that requires stopping  temporarily  while driving?
① When a child crosses the road without a guardian
② When the vehicle traffic light is flashing a red light
③ When a child is sitting or standing on the road
④ When the vehicle traffic light is flashing yellow
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 49 (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers) paragraph 1 subparagraph 2; Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 2. When the vehicle traffic light shows a flashing yellow light, a vehicle may proceed while paying attention to other traffic or traffic signs.

===== 문제 209 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the correct way of driving when the pedestrian  signal at a diagonal crosswalk is green?
① When intending to go straight, you must stop just before the stop line.
② If there are no pedestrians, you can increase your speed and turn right.
③ If there are no pedestrians, you can increase your speed and turn left.
④ You can proceed in the direction where pedestrians are not crossing.
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, 532-2, the diagonal crosswalk marking. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 6(2), Table 2, among the types and meanings of signals indicated by traffic lights, the red light.

===== 문제 210 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the correct term for the action of a driver to bring  his/her motor vehicle to a complete stop temporarily?
① Driving slowly
② Stopping
③ Parking
④ Making a temporary stop
정답: 4
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 30> Coming to a complete stop means the driver of a vehicle temporarily and completely stopping the wheels of the vehicle.

===== 문제 211 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is not permitted to be used by an electric wheelchair  for medical purposes?
① Bicycle-only roads
② A roadside area
③ A sidewalk
④ The edge of a road
정답: 1
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 8(1)> On a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, a pedestrian must always walk on the sidewalk. However, this does not apply when crossing the roadway, when passage on the sidewalk is prohibited due to road construction, etc., or in other unavoidable cases.

===== 문제 212 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate way of turning left at an intersection without any traffic control?
① On general roadways, turn on the direction indicator immediately before the intersection and turn left.
② Slow down and follow the centerline of the road, and turn left by going around the central island in the outer lane of  the intersection.
③ Turning left on the outer lane of the intersection is not allowed even when permitted by the commissioner of the  City/Do (Province) police agency.
④ You must slow down. You may or may not stop at your discretion as you enter.
정답: 4
해설: When intending to turn left on an ordinary road, the driver must turn on the turn signal at a point at least 30 meters before the point where they intend to turn left, slow down along the center line of the road, and turn left inside the center of the intersection; at a place designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency, the driver may turn left outside the center of the intersection. And when turning left, there is always a duty to slow down, but coming to a complete stop may or may not be required depending on the situation.

===== 문제 213 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, the width of a lane installed shall be ( ) m or wider. In such cases where an  unavoidable need is recognized, such as for the installation of a left turn-only lane,
① 5, 300
② 4, 285
③ 3, 275
④ 2, 265
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 15 (Installation of Lanes) paragraph 2. The width of a lane must be at least 3 meters, but where deemed unavoidable, such as for the installation of a left-turn-only lane, it may be at least 275 centimeters.

===== 문제 214 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: In which of the following cases is a vehicle allowed to overtake another vehicle on a road whose right side is  narrower than 6 m?
① When the left side of the road cannot be checked
② When the traffic in the opposite direction may be impeded
③ When the car ahead proceeds at a low speed and a safe distance can be secured from other cars
④ When overtaking is prohibited or restricted as indicated by safety signs, etc.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13 (Passage of Vehicles and Horses) paragraph 4 subparagraph 3.

===== 문제 215 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following lanes indicates the direction of traffic with a signal  apparatus on the road where the traffic volume of both directions varies greatly depen
① The reversible lane
② Bus-only lane
③ The acceleration lane
④ The overtaking lane
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 14 (Installation of Lanes, etc.) paragraph 1. The Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency may install, on a road where the volume of two-way traffic clearly differs by time of day, a variable lane that indicates the direction of travel of the lane by means of traffic lights so that the number of lanes can be expanded toward the side with heavier traffic.

===== 문제 216 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, the driver of any type of vehicle trying to make a (   ) must be in the  right-most lane and slow down to make a (   ) at an intersection. In this case, the driver t
① right turn – right turn – right turn
② left turn – left turn – left turn
③ right turn – left turn – right turn
④ left turn – right turn – left turn
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 25 (Method of Passing Through Intersections) (1) Every driver of a vehicle who intends to turn right at an intersection must turn right while slowing down along the right edge of the road in advance. In this case, the driver of the vehicle turning right must pay attention to pedestrians or cyclists, etc. who stop or proceed in accordance with the signal. <Amended June 9, 2020>

===== 문제 217 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is correct regarding a change of lane?
① Because it can be dangerous on a bridge, a change of lane is often restricted by solid white lines.
② Broken white lines mark traffic lanes where a change of lane is limited.
③ Do not change lanes in an area where lane change is prohibited, even if there is roadwork or other such obstructions  that prevent you from traveling
④ It is not against the law to change lanes in an area where lane change is prohibited, provided that it is done safely.
정답: 1
해설: When unable to proceed due to damage to the road, etc., the driver must change lanes and drive; changing lanes even safely in a place where lane changes are prohibited still constitutes a violation of the law. The lane-change-prohibition line is marked with a solid line.

===== 문제 218 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following falls under the violation of “tailgating,” an act of obstructing passage by other cars by  not allowing them to pass even when the traffic light has changed after entering an in
① A violation of means for passing through intersections
② A violation of a temporary stop
③ A violation of means for changing lanes
④ A violation of measures for preventing congestion
정답: 1
해설: Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar who intends to enter an intersection where traffic is being controlled by traffic lights must not enter that intersection if, depending on the situation of the vehicle or streetcar ahead in the intended path, they would come to a stop within the intersection (or, where a stop line is installed, the part beyond that stop line) and there is a risk of obstructing the passage of other vehicles or streetcars (Road Traffic Act Article 25(5)). Violating this is called blocking the box (tail-gating into the intersection).

===== 문제 219 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding an acceleration lane on an expressway?
① An acceleration lane allows vehicles on an expressway to change lanes so that they can take an exit.
② An acceleration lane allows the vehicles entering an expressway to increase their speed.
③ An acceleration lane allows increasing speed for vehicles trying to overtake other vehicles on an expressway.
④ An acceleration lane is designed to lessen the effects of the lowered speed of large vehicles when driving uphill.
정답: 2
해설: A speed-change lane means an additional lane installed so that a vehicle traveling at high speed can decelerate safely when entering another road, or a vehicle traveling at low speed can accelerate safely when merging into a group of vehicles traveling at high speed, without obstructing the travel of other high-speed vehicles on the main line. Generally, the former is called a deceleration lane and the latter an acceleration lane [Commentary on the Rules on the Structure and Facility Standards of Roads (2020), Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport, p.40].

===== 문제 220 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate action to take when a driver realizes that he/she incorrectly  entered an expressway after he/she has already entered it?
① Pull over, turn on the emergency flashers, and call the expressway patrol for help.
② Take the next exit as he/she has already entered the expressway.
③ Turn on the emergency flashers and slowly reverse the vehicle back to the entry point to exit the expressway.
④ If there are two or more lanes, make a U-turn to exit the expressway.
정답: 2
해설: If you have entered a highway by mistake, drive to the next exit and then exit.

===== 문제 221 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is an INCORRECT match between the type of lane and  the color painted on the lane?
① Yellow color for separating bidirectional traffic in a safety zone
② Blue color for bus-only lanes
③ Pink, light green, or green colors for guide lines on the lane
④ White color for the outer line of the speed limit mark installed within a school zone
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6 (Types of Traffic Signs, Manufacturing Methods, and Installation/Management Standards), General Standards subparagraph 2. The border line of the speed limit marking installed within a school zone is red.

===== 문제 222 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is the correct type of  vehicle that may be driven in the left-side lane on roads other than expressways?
① Passenger cars and compact, small, and mid-sized buses
② Large passenger vehicles
③ Trucks
④ Special-purpose cars and two-wheeled vehicles
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 9 (Standards for Vehicles Passing by Lane). On roads other than highways, the left lane is the lane on which passenger cars and light/small/medium-sized passenger buses may pass.

===== 문제 223 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following road markings allow a driver to make a U-turn? (Given that there is a U-turn sign)
① Yellow solid line on the center of the road
② White solid line on the center of the road
③ White broken line on the center of the road
④ Blue solid line on the center of the road
정답: 3
해설: In a section where a road marking in the form of a white dashed line is installed in the center of the road, a U-turn is permitted.

===== 문제 224 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Below are the classifications of traffic by type of lane according to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement  Decree. Which of the following is INCORRECT?
① With regard to the ordering of lanes, the lane next to the median lane of a road is regarded as the first lane.
② If a person drives at a slow speed, possibly impeding the normal traffic of other cars, he/she must switch to the lane  right of the one he/she was or
③ On a road with one-way traffic, the right side of the road is regarded as the first lane.
④ In principle, all vehicles on an expressway with two lanes in one direction must drive in the second lane.
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 16 (Classification of Passage by Lane) - On a one-way road, the leftmost lane is counted as the first lane.

===== 문제 225 =====

① 50/100
② 40/100
③ 30/100
④ 20/100
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 19 (Speed of Motor Vehicles, etc. and Streetcars) (2) In adverse weather such as rain, fog, or snow, notwithstanding paragraph (1), the driver must reduce speed in accordance with the following standards.

===== 문제 226 =====

① Passenger cars with 3 or more persons
② Trucks with 3 or more persons
③ Buses with 3 or more persons
④ Two-wheeled vehicles with 2 or more persons
정답: 1, 3
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Table 1. Types of exclusive lanes and vehicles that may pass in exclusive lanes (related to Article 9(1)). A passenger car or passenger bus carrying three or more persons (where a high-occupancy exclusive lane and a bus-exclusive lane are installed at the same time, vehicles that may pass in the bus-exclusive lane are excluded).

===== 문제 227 =====
유형: 문장형

① Safety area
② Lane change restriction line sign
③ Roadside
④ Roadside area
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 (Definitions) subparagraph 11 (road-edge zone). A road-edge zone means the part at the edge of a road, on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are not separated, demarcated by traffic signs, etc. to secure the safety of pedestrians.

===== 문제 228 =====

③ Large buses must use only the first lane to turn left.
④ Large buses must use only the second lane to turn left.
정답: 4
해설: At an intersection where two or more left-turn lanes are installed, a vehicle intending to turn left must turn left within the installed left-turn lanes in accordance with the lane classification for roads other than highways in Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 9.

② A vehicle making a stop on a road with no distinction between the roadway and the sidewalk should stop at a distance  of at least 50 cm from the right

(Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 19(1) subparagraph 2)

===== 문제 230 =====

① 60 km/h
② 70 km/h
③ 80 km/h
④ 90 km/h
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 19(1). On a highway with two or more lanes each way, the maximum speed of a truck with a loading capacity exceeding 1.5 tons is 80 kilometers per hour.

===== 문제 233 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding the driving distance between vehicles?
① Free running distance is the distance traveled by a vehicle  from the point when the brake is pressed after detecting  danger until the point at which
② Stopping distance is the distance necessary to avoid a rear-end collision with the car in front of you if it makes an  abrupt stop.
③ Safe distance is the distance traveled by a vehicle from the point the brake is applied to the point at which the vehicle  comes to a complete stop.
④ Braking distance is the distance between the moment the driver detects danger and the moment the car comes to a  full stop.
정답: 1

===== 문제 234 =====

질문: In which of the following areas is overtaking a vehicle possible?
① Intersection
② Centerline (yellow broken line)
③ Inside the tunnel (white solid line)
④ On the bridge (white solid line)
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 22 (Time and Places Where Overtaking Is Prohibited), Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] 501 intersection. Intersections, sections with yellow solid lines, inside tunnels, on bridges, and places designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency are places where overtaking is prohibited.

===== 문제 235 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is the most appropriate method of slowing down at  an intersection?
① Proceeding at a low speed such that the vehicle can be stopped immediately
② Proceeding while maintaining a speed of 30 km/h
③ Proceeding at such a low speed that no accident occurs
④ Proceeding at a speed that enables the driver to avoid any sudden stops by the vehicle in front
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 (Definitions) subparagraph 28. Slowing down (徐行) means the driver proceeding at a slow speed at which the vehicle can be stopped immediately.

===== 문제 236 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Below are various explanations of the punishment criteria for driving a vehicle (except for personal mobility  devices) in violation of the maximum speed on the road. Which of the following is correct
① Anyone who drives three or more times at a speed of over 100 km/h above the speed limit is subject to a criminal fine  of up to KRW 5 million or deten
② Anyone who drives three or more times at a speed of over 100 km/h above the speed limit is subject to imprisonment  for up to one year or a criminal f
③ Anyone who drives twice at a speed of over 100 km/h above the speed limit is subject to a criminal fine of up to KRW  3 million.
④ Anyone who drives at a speed of over 80 km/h above the speed limit is subject to a criminal fine of up to KRW 500,000  or detention.
정답: 2
해설: (1)(2) Road Traffic Act Article 151-2 subparagraph 2: a person who drives a motor vehicle, etc. three or more times at a speed exceeding the maximum speed by 100 kilometers per hour shall be punished by imprisonment for up to 1 year or a fine of up to 5 million won; (3) Road Traffic Act Article 153(2) subparagraph 2: a person who drives a motor vehicle, etc. at a speed exceeding the maximum speed by 100 kilometers per hour shall be punished by a fine of up to 1 million won or detention; (4) Road Traffic Act Article 154 subparagraph 9: a person who drives a motor vehicle, etc. at a speed exceeding the maximum speed by 80 kilometers per hour shall be punished by a fine of up to 300,000 won or detention.

===== 문제 237 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of turning right at an intersection with no traffic lights?
① Turn right as fast as possible.
② Wait until the vehicles entering the intersection ahead of you pass by, and then turn right.
③ When a vehicle coming from the opposite direction is turning left, turn right at the same time with caution.
④ There is no need to be mindful of other vehicles when turning right from a wide road onto a narrow one.
정답: 2
해설: When turning right at an intersection, you must turn right while slowing down and yield the right-of-way to a left-turning vehicle that entered first. And when turning right from a wide road onto a narrow road, you must also pay attention to other vehicles.

===== 문제 238 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A driver intends to turn right at an intersection with traffic signals but no auxiliary signals for vehicles.  According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is n
① If the traffic signal has turned red, a temporary stop must be made in front of the stop line regardless of the signal  light for pedestrians at the c
② If the traffic signal has turned green, a right turn may be made without making a temporary stop in front of the stop  line.
③ When the vehicle signal is a left-turn signal, you must come to a complete stop just before the stop line regardless of  the pedestrian signal at the
④ Regardless of the traffic signal, a right turn may be made without making a temporary stop when other vehicles are not  impeded.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 25, Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Table 2, Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 2. (1) When the vehicle signal is a red light, regardless of the pedestrian signal at the crosswalk, come to a complete stop immediately before the stop line and then turn right without obstructing the traffic of other vehicles proceeding in accordance with the signal. (2) When the vehicle signal is a green light, there is no duty to come to a complete stop at the crosswalk. (3) When the vehicle signal is a green arrow light (left turn), regardless of the pedestrian signal at the crosswalk, come to a complete stop immediately before the stop line and then turn right without obstructing the traffic of other vehicles proceeding in accordance with the signal. * If you do not come to a complete stop, it is punished as a signal violation; if you come to a complete stop but obstruct the passage of pedestrians, it is punished as a violation of the duty to protect pedestrians.

===== 문제 239 =====
유형: 문장형

① When turning right, quickly pass through the intersection while carefully checking for pedestrians and bicycles that stop  or proceed according to the
② When turning left, you must pass through the outside of the intersection center.
③ When turning right, reduce speed in advance while driving along the right edge of the road.
④ When turning left at an intersection without traffic signals while a pedestrian is crossing the road, quickly pass in front  of the pedestrian.
정답: 3
해설: Every driver of a vehicle who intends to turn right at an intersection must turn right while slowing down along the right edge of the road in advance. In this case, the driver of the vehicle turning right must pay attention to pedestrians or cyclists who stop or proceed in accordance with the signal.

===== 문제 240 =====

③ The distance traveled by a vehicle from the point at which the driver detects danger and steps on the brake pedal to  the point at which the vehicle m
④ The distance from the point where a driver senses danger and presses the brake pedal to the point where the brake  function actually starts to work
