===== 문제 241 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the correct way of passing through an intersection?
① When the red traffic light is flashing, drive slowly.
② When the yellow traffic light is flashing, drive through as quickly as you can.
③ Do not overtake other vehicles at an intersection.
④ When approaching the intersection, always use high-beam headlights.
정답: 3
해설: At an intersection, in the case of flashing yellow, proceed with caution; in the case of flashing red, come to a complete stop. Turning on the high beams when approaching an intersection poses a threat to the safe driving of the other party.

===== 문제 242 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: The following describes a Hi-pass lane. Which is correct?
① A Hi-pass lane is always found in lane 1; drivers intending to take it must change lanes accordingly and pass through the toll road safely.
② Since the Hi-pass lane for trucks is exclusive to trucks and is colored blue, there is no need to slow down to pass through the toll road.
③ Drivers must reduce speed in advance since the speed limit of a multi-lane Hi-pass toll road is 30 km/h.
④ Drivers must observe the speed limit of a multi-lane Hi-pass toll road. If the Hi-pass device malfunctions upon entry, drivers can pay the toll upon exit.
정답: 4
해설: The hi-pass guide line for trucks is orange, the ordinary hi-pass lane is blue, and the multi-lane hi-pass section is 50-80 kilometers per hour, varying by section.

===== 문제 243 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the maximum speed for a passenger car traveling on an expressway under the Road Traffic Act?
① 60 km/h
② 70 km/h
③ 80 km/h
④ 90 km/h
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 19 (Speed of Motor Vehicles, etc. and Streetcars) (1) subparagraph 2. On a motor-vehicle-only road, the maximum speed is 90 kilometers per hour and the minimum speed is 30 kilometers per hour.

===== 문제 244 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following speed limits can be placed on a general road in  residential, commercial, and industrial areas?
① Within 20 km/h
② Within 30 km/h
③ Within 40 km/h
④ Within 50 km/h
정답: 4
해설: As part of the 5030 policy for the safety of road traffic participants through speed reduction, the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act took effect on April 17, 2021, setting the passage speed of motor vehicles, etc. and streetcars on ordinary roads in residential, commercial, and industrial areas at within 50 kilometers per hour, except on routes or sections designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it particularly necessary, where it is within 60 kilometers per hour.

===== 문제 245 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the policy that limits the maximum speed in the city center to 50 km/h while lowered  to 30 km/h on back roads (including residential areas) in order to reduce traffic accide
① New Deal Policy
② Safety Speed 5030
③ Campaign for Unity in Order to Reduce Traffic Accidents
④ Intelligent Transportation System (ITS)
정답: 2
해설: The New Deal refers to the various policies pursued to overcome the Great Depression (Doosan Encyclopedia). The Traffic Accident Reduction One-Heart Rally is a traffic safety awareness event hosted by the Korea Road Traffic Authority and sponsored by the Ministry of the Interior and Safety and the National Police Agency. The Intelligent Transport System refers to a transport system that combines technologies such as electronics, information, communication, and control with the transport system (Doosan Encyclopedia). The traffic safety policy in this question is Safety Speed 5030. Following the amendment of Article 19 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act on April 16, 2019, the passage speed of motor vehicles, etc. and streetcars on ordinary roads in residential, commercial, and industrial areas under Article 36(1) subparagraph 1 items (a) through (c) of the National Land Planning and Utilization Act was set at within 50 kilometers per hour, except on routes or sections designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it particularly necessary, where it is within 60 kilometers per hour. Effective date (April 17, 2021).

===== 문제 247 =====

질문: You are driving in a city that operates an exclusive median bus lane system. Which of the following are safe  ways of driving? (Select TWO)
① You should continuously honk to prevent other vehicles from cutting in front of you.
② You should drive with caution as pedestrians on the right may try to jaywalk.
③ You should slow down as pedestrians may cross the road from the bus station on the left.
④ You should drive and pass through a median bus lane as quickly as possible before the traffic light turns red.
정답: 2, 3
해설: When driving on a city road in a central bus-exclusive lane (BRT) section, because bus stops are located in the center and the crosswalks are short, there are many jaywalking accidents by pedestrians, so you must slow down with caution.

===== 문제 248 =====

③ Change lanes without obstructing traffic.
④ You can change lanes only on a road marked with a white broken line.

===== 문제 249 =====

① Use the turn indicator at least 30 m before where you want to turn right.
② Move into the right-hand lane carefully if there is a solid white line on the road.
③ Turn right after checking your left side to make sure that there are no other vehicles headed in the same direction as  you.
④ Turn right quickly while paying attention to the traffic in the area.
정답: 1, 3
해설: If a white solid line is drawn as you approach an intersection, you must not change your path in that section, and you must turn while slowing down with attention to other traffic. And when turning right at an intersection without traffic lights, you must yield the right-of-way to vehicles that have the right of way.

===== 문제 250 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct methods for a passenger car driver to overtake another vehicle?
① Make sure that there is enough space on the left before overtaking.
② Overtaking must be done within the speed limit of the road being traveled.
③ If it is safe to do so, overtaking another vehicle on its right hand side is acceptable.
④ After passing a vehicle on its left, move back into the lane when the vehicle that you just passed is no longer visible in  the rear-view mirror.
정답: 1, 2
해설: Every driver of a vehicle who intends to overtake another vehicle must pass on the left side of the vehicle ahead. Every driver intending to overtake must also pay sufficient attention to oncoming traffic and to traffic ahead of the vehicle in front, and must overtake at a safe speed and by a safe method, such as using the turn signal, lights, or horn, in accordance with the speed and lane of the vehicle ahead and other road conditions.

===== 문제 251 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What are TWO safe driving methods when driving on the road?
① Park the vehicle in a safety zone if possible.
② When the traffic lights turn yellow, stop inside the intersection to observe the traffic signal.
③ Maintain sufficient distance to prevent a collision with the vehicle ahead if it brakes suddenly.
④ When overtaking another vehicle, pass along its left side.
정답: 3, 4
해설: When overtaking while securing a distance that allows a collision to be avoided in preparation for the vehicle ahead braking suddenly, pass on the left side of the vehicle ahead.

===== 문제 252 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not a motor vehicle that is required to install or place a fire extinguisher?
① A passenger car with 5 or more seats
② A bus
③ A truck
④ A two-wheeled vehicle
정답: 4
해설: Act on Installation and Management of Fire-Fighting Systems Article 11 (Fire Extinguishers Installed or Kept in Motor Vehicles) paragraph 1. A person who intends to manufacture, assemble, import, or sell a motor vehicle under Article 3(1) of the Motor Vehicle Management Act that falls under any of the following, or the owner of such motor vehicle, must install or keep a vehicle fire extinguisher: 1. a passenger car with 5 or more seats; 2. a passenger bus.

===== 문제 253 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following measures must be taken when an emergency vehicle  driving with an emergency siren on approaches?
① When an emergency vehicle approaches an intersection, drivers of other vehicles must stop at the left edge of the  intersection.
② On the road other than the intersection, other vehicles should yield so that emergency vehicles can pass first.
③ Drive faster than the emergency vehicle.
④ Stop temporarily and wait until the emergency vehicle passes.
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 29 (Priority Passage of Emergency Vehicles) (4) When an emergency vehicle approaches at or near an intersection, the driver of a vehicle, horse, or streetcar must avoid the intersection and come to a complete stop. (5) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when an emergency vehicle approaches at a place other than those under paragraph (4), yield the right-of-way so that the emergency vehicle can pass first.

===== 문제 254 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest measure to take when a fire truck approaches with flashing red lights and  siren while you are trying to turn right at an intersection?
① Temporarily stop, avoiding the intersection.
② Stop right where you are.
③ Slow down, and turn right.
④ Pass through the intersection quickly and continue driving.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 29 (Priority Passage of Emergency Vehicles) (4) When an emergency vehicle approaches at or near an intersection, the driver of a vehicle, horse, or streetcar must avoid the intersection and come to a complete stop.

===== 문제 255 =====

③ Restriction of cutting in
④ Ensuring pedestrian safety
정답: 4

However, matters from subparagraph 4 to subparagraph 12 do not apply only to emergency vehicles among those under Article 2 subparagraph 22 items (a) through (c) and police vehicles prescribed by Presidential Decree. <Amended January 12, 2021> 1. Speed limits of motor vehicles, etc. under Article 17. However, where the speed of an emergency vehicle has been limited under Article 17, that Article applies. 2. Prohibition of overtaking under Article 22. 3. Prohibition of cutting in under Article 23. 4. Signal violation under Article 5. 5. Encroachment on the sidewalk under Article 13(1). 6. Encroachment on the center line under Article 13(3). 7. Prohibition of crossing, etc. under Article 18. 8. Securing safe distance, etc. under Article 19. 9. Method of overtaking, etc. under Article 21(1). 10. Prohibition of stopping and parking under Article 32. 11. Prohibition of parking under Article 33. 12. Measures for breakdown, etc. under Article 66.

===== 문제 256 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Emergency vehicles should be properly outfitted, and they should convey that they are undertaking an  emergency operation by turning on their sirens or flashing lights. Which of the following vehicles
① Vehicles cracking down on illegal parking
② Fire trucks
③ Ambulances
④ Police cars cracking down on violations of speed limit
정답: 4
해설: An emergency vehicle must be equipped with the structure of an emergency vehicle prescribed in the standards necessary for the safe operation of motor vehicles under the Motor Vehicle Management Act, and when it intends to receive priority passage, the special provisions for emergency vehicles, and other special provisions prescribed by law, it must sound a siren or turn on the warning light. However, this does not apply to an emergency vehicle that is cracking down on a motor vehicle, etc. violating speed regulations, or a motor vehicle used for official duties in performing escort duties for domestic or foreign VIPs.

===== 문제 257 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: In order for fire trucks and ambulances to be granted a special privilege to overtake other vehicles or drive at a  speed exceeding the limit in a no-passing zone, which of the following is the measur
① Drive while honking.
② Equip the cars in a manner required for safe driving according to the Motor Vehicle Management Act and turn on the  sirens or flashing lights.
③ Drive with the headlights on.
④ Those vehicles can be granted such privileges without taking additional measures.
정답: 2
해설: For an emergency vehicle to receive the special provisions, it must be equipped with the structure necessary for the safe operation of a motor vehicle under the Motor Vehicle Management Act and must sound a siren or turn on the warning light.

===== 문제 258 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is necessary for a general vehicle to be recognized as an emergency vehicle when  transporting a patient in critical condition?
① The car should be authorized by the chief of the district police station.
② The driver should turn on the headlights or emergency lights.
③ The car does not need to get special permission because it is transporting a patient in critical condition.
④ The car must be escorted by another vehicle.
정답: 2
해설: In an urgent situation where an ambulance cannot be called and a patient whose life is in danger must be transported by an ordinary motor vehicle, when driving to a hospital, etc. while receiving the yield of surrounding vehicle drivers, in order to receive the special provisions as an emergency vehicle, the driver must turn on the headlights or hazard lights or otherwise indicate by an appropriate method that the vehicle is being operated for an urgent purpose.

===== 문제 259 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which TWO are considered emergency vehicles?
① A fire truck going to a repair shop for repairs
② A vehicle transporting patients who are injured or in a critical condition, or transporting blood for transfusion
③ An ambulance transporting a patient discharged from a medical institution
④ A vehicle authorized by the commissioner of the City/Do (Province) police agency to transport urgent postal mail, etc.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 22. An emergency vehicle means the following motor vehicles that are being used for their original urgent purposes: (a) fire engines; (b) ambulances; (c) blood supply vehicles; (d) other motor vehicles prescribed by Presidential Decree. Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 5, a motor vehicle used for official duties in performing escort duties for domestic or foreign VIPs; subparagraph 10, a motor vehicle used for transporting urgent postal items.

===== 문제 260 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following are TWO things that an ambulance driver is allowed to  do while operating an emergency vehicle in an emergency situation?
① Continue to operate the vehicle after a traffic accident without administering first aid to the injured.
② Continue to operate the vehicle even if it obstructs pedestrians from crossing.
③ Drive along the center or along the left side of the road.
④ Cut in front of other vehicles on a congested road.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Article 29 (Priority Passage of Emergency Vehicles) paragraph 1. Notwithstanding Article 13(3), an emergency vehicle may pass on the center or left part of the road in urgent and unavoidable cases. Paragraph 2. Notwithstanding cases where it must stop under this Act or an order under this Act, an emergency vehicle may refrain from stopping in urgent and unavoidable cases. Paragraph 3. In the cases of paragraph 1 or 2, the driver of an emergency vehicle must pass while paying special attention to traffic safety.

===== 문제 261 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, what is the reason a fire truck driver who is operating a vehicle for its  original purpose cannot be granted exceptions as an emergency vehicle?
① Not wearing a seat belt
② Drunk driving
③ Crossing of the centerline
④ Violation of traffic light
정답: 2
해설: Article 30 (Special Provisions for Emergency Vehicles) The following matters do not apply to emergency vehicles. However, matters from subparagraph 4 to subparagraph 12 do not apply only to emergency vehicles among those under Article 2 subparagraph 22 items (a) through (c) and police vehicles prescribed by Presidential Decree. <Amended January 12, 2021> 1. Speed limits of motor vehicles, etc. under Article 17. However, where the speed of an emergency vehicle has been limited under Article 17, that Article applies. 2. Prohibition of overtaking under Article 22. 3. Prohibition of cutting in under Article 23. 4. Signal violation under Article 5. 5. Encroachment on the sidewalk under Article 13(1). 6. Encroachment on the center line under Article 13(3). 7. Prohibition of crossing, etc. under Article 18. 8. Securing safe distance, etc. under Article 19. 9. Method of overtaking, etc. under Article 21(1). 10. Prohibition of stopping and parking under Article 32. 11. Prohibition of parking under Article 33. 12. Measures for breakdown, etc. under Article 66.

===== 문제 262 =====
유형: 문장형

① 1
② 2
③ 3
④ 5
정답: 3
해설: Regular traffic safety education: education conducted regularly every 3 years for persons who drive emergency vehicles. In this case, the education must be received between January 1 and December 31 of the year in which the third year, counted from the date on which the person last received emergency-vehicle traffic safety education, falls. (Road Traffic Act Article 73(4), Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 38-2(2) subparagraph 2)

Speed limits of motor vehicles, etc. under Article 17. However, where the speed of an emergency vehicle has been limited under Article 17, that Article applies. 2. Prohibition of overtaking under Article 22. 3. Prohibition of cutting in under Article 23. 4. Signal violation under Article 5. 5. Encroachment on the sidewalk under Article 13(1). 6. Encroachment on the center line under Article 13(3). 7. Prohibition of crossing, etc. under Article 18. 8. Securing safe distance, etc. under Article 19. 9. Method of overtaking, etc. under Article 21(1). 10. Prohibition of stopping and parking under Article 32. 11. Prohibition of parking under Article 33. 12. Measures for breakdown, etc. under Article 66.

===== 문제 264 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Stopping and parking are prohibited within (  ) meters of fire-extinguishing water supply system, emergency  firefighting equipment, and firefighting systems under the Road Traffic Act. Which of the f
① 5
② 6
③ 8
④ 10
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 32 (Prohibition of Stopping and Parking) Every driver of a vehicle must not stop or park the vehicle in any of the following places. However, this does not apply when following this Act, an order under this Act, or the instructions of a police officer, or when coming to a complete stop to prevent danger. 6. Within 5 meters from any of the following places: (a) a place where a fire-fighting water facility or emergency fire-extinguishing device under Article 10 of the Framework Act on Fire Services is installed; (b) a place where a fire-fighting system prescribed by Presidential Decree under Article 2(1) subparagraph 1 of the Act on Installation and Management of Fire-Fighting Systems is installed.

② A gasworks vehicle used for emergency gas leak repair

However, motor vehicles under subparagraphs 6 through 11 are limited to cases designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency upon application by the person or institution, etc. using them.

===== 문제 266 =====

However, motor vehicles under subparagraphs 6 through 11 are limited to cases designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency upon application by the person or institution, etc. using them.

===== 문제 267 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT a situation wherein an emergency vehicle can  flash its warning lights for non-emergency matters?
① When a fire truck is on patrol for fire prevention, rescue, and emergency activities
② When a fire truck is being driven to the workshop for urgent repair
③ When a vehicle commissioned for civil defense activities participates in a drill relevant to its usual emergency  responsibilities
④ When a police vehicle is on patrol for crime prevention and control
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 10-2 (Cases Where Warning Lights, etc. May Be Used Other Than for Urgent Purposes) The driver of a motor vehicle under each item of Article 2 subparagraph 22 of the Act may, pursuant to the proviso to Article 29(6) of the Act, turn on the warning light or operate the siren installed on that motor vehicle under the Motor Vehicle Management Act, even when not operating the motor vehicle for its original urgent purpose, in any of the following cases.

===== 문제 268 =====

질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following actions by an emergency vehicle on an emergency  rescue mission is considered an offense?
① Driving at 100 km/h on a general road with two lanes in one direction
② Overtaking a vehicle inside a tunnel where lanes are marked with a solid white line
③ Cutting in front of a vehicle at an intersection to turn right
④ Failing to report or take the necessary actions in the event of a traffic accident resulting in personal injury because the  vehicle is on an emergenc
정답: 4
해설: The speed limit of motor vehicles, etc., the prohibition of overtaking, and the prohibition of cutting in do not apply to emergency vehicles. (Road Traffic Act Article 30 (Special Provisions for Emergency Vehicles), Road Traffic Act Article 54 (Measures at the Time of an Accident) paragraph 5)

===== 문제 269 =====

===== 문제 270 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct methods of yielding to an emergency vehicle on the road?
① All vehicles must pull over to the left side of the road.
② If the emergency vehicle is climbing up a narrow slope and you are facing it, the emergency vehicle must pull over to  the right to make way for you.
③ Near an intersection, pull over at some distance from the intersection.
④ When an emergency vehicle approaches from a place other than an intersection or its vicinity, the roadway shall be  yielded so that the emergency vehi
정답: 3, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 29 (Priority Passage of Emergency Vehicles) (4) When an emergency vehicle approaches at or near an intersection, the driver of a vehicle, horse, or streetcar must avoid the intersection and come to a complete stop. (5) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when an emergency vehicle approaches at a place other than those under paragraph (4), yield the right-of-way so that the emergency vehicle can pass first.

===== 문제 271 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO are considered emergency vehicles?
① A vehicle being guided by a police car for emergency purposes
② An investigation vehicle used for non-investigatory purposes
③ A tow truck returning after completing a rescue activity
④ A vehicle transporting patients who are injured or in critical condition, or transporting blood for transfusion
정답: 1, 4
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 2 (Types of Emergency Vehicles) (1) A motor vehicle prescribed by Presidential Decree in Article 2 subparagraph 22 item (d) of the Road Traffic Act (hereinafter the Act) means a motor vehicle that is used for an urgent purpose and falls under any of the following. However, motor vehicles under subparagraphs 6 through 11 are limited to cases designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency upon application by the person or institution, etc. using them. 1. Among police motor vehicles, a motor vehicle used for criminal investigation, traffic enforcement, or other urgent police duties. 2. Among motor vehicles for the national armed forces and the United Nations forces stationed in Korea, a motor vehicle used to maintain internal order within the military or to guide the orderly movement of units. 3. Among motor vehicles of investigative agencies, a motor vehicle used for criminal investigation. 4. Among motor vehicles of the following facilities or institutions, a motor vehicle used for the arrest of escapees or the escort/guarding of inmates or persons subject to probation: (a) a prison, juvenile prison, or detention center; (b) a juvenile reformatory or juvenile classification review center; (c) a probation office. 5. A motor vehicle used for official duties in performing escort duties for domestic or foreign VIPs. 6. A motor vehicle used for emergency work to prevent danger by an institution engaged in the electricity business, gas business, or other public utility business. 7. A motor vehicle used for dispatch for emergency prevention or restoration by an institution performing civil defense duties. 8. Among motor vehicles used for road management, a motor vehicle used for emergency work to prevent danger on the road or to crack down on motor vehicles whose operation is restricted. 9. A motor vehicle used for emergency work such as the repair of telegraph or telephone lines. 10. A motor vehicle used for transporting urgent postal items. 11. A motor vehicle used for radio monitoring duties. (2) In addition to the motor vehicles under each subparagraph of paragraph (1), a motor vehicle falling under any of the following is deemed an emergency vehicle: 1. a motor vehicle being guided by a police emergency vehicle under paragraph (1) subparagraph 1; 2. a motor vehicle of the national armed forces or the United Nations forces stationed in Korea being guided by an emergency vehicle for the armed forces or the United Nations forces stationed in Korea under paragraph (1) subparagraph 2; 3. a motor vehicle transporting a patient or injured person whose life is in danger, or blood for transfusion.

===== 문제 272 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about the duty of a school bus driver/operator?
① A school bus driver must turn on the hazard lights only when children and infants are getting on or off the bus.
② A school bus driver must make sure that all children and infants on board are wearing seat belts before moving the bus.
③ In case parents or guardians are on board together with the children, a school bus operator may post a sign indicating the  accompaniment of parents/g
④ In case parents or guardians are on board, there is no need to write safety operation records.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 53 paragraphs 1, 2, 6, and 7.

===== 문제 273 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: In which location should the driver come to a full stop, regardless of the existence of pedestrians?
① A road with no centerline among the roads where the roadway and sidewalk are not distinguishable
② Front of a crosswalk in a school zone with no traffic light
③ Pedestrian-first road
④ A place other than a road
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27(6): Every driver of a vehicle must, when passing beside a pedestrian on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are not separated and there is no center line, on a pedestrian-priority road, or at a place off the road, keep a safe distance and slow down, and when the passage of the pedestrian is obstructed, slow down or come to a complete stop so that the pedestrian can pass safely. <Amended January 11, 2022> Road Traffic Act Article 27(7): A driver must, at a crosswalk without a traffic light among the crosswalks installed within a school zone (or at the stop line where one is installed), come to a complete stop regardless of whether a pedestrian is crossing.

===== 문제 274 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A school bus for children is driving in the first lane of a road with two lanes in one direction with markings to  show that it has children or infants on board. Which of the following is the safest c
① Do not overtake the bus even if the second lane is empty.
② Overtake the bus using the second lane.
③ Honk to send a signal to make way.
④ Check the situation of the traffic in the opposite direction, and overtake the bus by crossing the centerline.
정답: 1
해설: The safest driving method is not to overtake even if the second lane is empty. Every driver of a vehicle must not overtake a school bus that is passing on the road displaying the sign that it is carrying children or infants. (Road Traffic Act Article 51)

===== 문제 275 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding school zones?
① Areas in front of kindergartens or middle schools are designated as school zones.
② Mayors and other officials can limit or prohibit vehicles from entering a school zone.
③ For the purposes of a school zone, children are defined as those under 12 years old.
④ A school zone can limit vehicles to a speed of 30 km/h.
정답: 4
해설: In a school zone, the passage speed of vehicles may be limited to within 30 kilometers per hour.

===== 문제 276 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Under the Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents, what is the appropriate  punishment for a driver’s negligence resulting in injury to a child while driving at 40 km/h wit
① Only if demanded by the victim will the offender be subjected to criminal punishment.
② Regardless of the victim's request, criminal punishment will be imposed.
③ When insured by comprehensive insurance, the driver will not face criminal punishment.
④ An agreement with the victim can prevent criminal punishment.
정답: 2
해설: If a driver injures a child while driving in a school zone, the driver is subject to criminal punishment regardless of the victim's intent to punish.

===== 문제 277 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the allowed seating capacity for a vehicle to be registered as a school bus for children?  (1 child is considered 1 passenger)
① 11-seater or larger
② 16-seater or larger
③ 17-seater or larger
④ 9-seater or larger
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 34 (Motor Vehicles That May Be Used as School Buses) A motor vehicle that may be used as a school bus is a motor vehicle with a seating capacity of 9 or more (one child is counted as one seat).

===== 문제 278 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the correct number of demerit points for the driver of a passenger car who has violated the speed limit  by exceeding the maximum speed by 25 km/h at around 08:30 am in a school zone?
① 10 points
② 15 points
③ 30 points
④ 60 points
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28] For a driver who commits a speed violation in a school zone between 8 a.m. and 8 p.m., penalty points equivalent to twice the penalty points are imposed.

===== 문제 279 =====

① 10 points
② 15 points
③ 30 points
④ 40 points
정답: 3
해설: If the driver of a passenger car overtakes a school bus that is passing on the road displaying the sign that it is carrying children or infants, 30 penalty points are imposed.

===== 문제 280 =====

① 1 hour or more
② 3 hours or more
③ 5 hours or more
④ 6 hours or more
정답: 2
해설: Safety education for school buses is conducted for 3 hours or more by methods such as lectures and audiovisual education on the behavioral characteristics of children for traffic safety, laws related to the operation of school buses, analysis of major accident cases involving school buses, and other matters necessary for the protection of children while driving and during boarding/alighting. (Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 31-2)

===== 문제 281 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, who are considered children and infants?
① Those 13 years old or below are considered children.
② Those who are below 6 years old are considered infants.
③ Those who are below 15 years old are considered children.
④ Those who are below 7 years old are considered infants.
정답: 2
해설: A child means a person under 13 years of age (Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 23), and an infant means a person under 6 years of age (Road Traffic Act Article 11).

===== 문제 282 =====

① KRW 50,000
② KRW 70,000
③ KRW 120,000
④ KRW 150,000
정답: 3
해설: For the driver of a passenger car who commits a signal violation in a school zone between 8 a.m. and 8 p.m., a penalty of 120,000 won is imposed.

===== 문제 283 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest method of driving on a road where a child is playing alone without a  guardian?
① The child should not be there in the first place. Just drive on.
② Honk to scare the child away and drive past.
③ Stop temporarily.
④ Keep an eye on the child and speed away.
정답: 3
해설: If a child is playing on the road without a guardian, come to a complete stop and protect the child.

===== 문제 284 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is a correct action taken by a driver when a child is  crossing in a crosswalk?
① Upon noticing a pedestrian crossing the crosswalk, check for safety and pass slowly.
② In the case of a crosswalk on a general road with no traffic light, the driver can pass the stop line as long as he/she  does not enter the crosswalk
③ In the case of a crosswalk on a general road with no traffic light, the driver can drive slowly behind crossing children.
④ Pedestrian crossing signs are placed on both ends of a crosswalk, and all drivers must come to a full stop before  reaching the crosswalk.
정답: 4
해설: The crosswalk sign indicates that pedestrians should pass on the crosswalk; you must come to a complete stop before the crosswalk, and it is installed on both sides of the road at the necessary points of the place where the crosswalk is installed. When a child is passing through a crosswalk without traffic lights, come to a complete stop before the crosswalk and wait for the child to pass.

===== 문제 285 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: If a school bus for children makes a stop at a road with one lane in one direction and switches on the flashers  to indicate that infants are getting on and off the bus, which of the following is the
① Stop temporarily, check that it is safe, and drive on slowly.
② Drive past at a reduced speed to ensure safety.
③ Continue to drive on.
④ Drive on while honking.
정답: 1
해설: When a school bus has stopped on a two-lane (one each way) road and the flashing light indicating that infants are boarding or alighting is operating, the driver of a vehicle proceeding in the opposite direction must come to a complete stop, confirm safety, and then slow down.

===== 문제 286 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate behavior when a driver hits a child?
① A driver of a two-wheeled vehicle asked if the child was injured or not, but the child did not say anything. So the driver  decided that the child was
② The driver of a passenger car stopped the vehicle immediately, visually checked the child, and concluded that the child  was not injured and so kept d
③ The driver of a truck saw the child who was knocked over but got up immediately. Upon concluding that the child was  not injured, the driver kept driv
④ A cyclist saw the child who was knocked over but quickly got up and ran. The cyclist reported the case to the police  station and waited at the scene.
정답: 4
해설: Do not just trust what the child says; you must report to a police agency.

===== 문제 287 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A vehicle just hit a child who suddenly emerged from an alleyway. The child appeared unharmed and claimed  that he was fine. Which of the following is the correct action taken by the driver?
① The driver can leave since this is a crime that is not prosecuted against objection.
② The driver can leave since the child was not hurt, so this is not considered an accident.
③ The driver must call the parents of the child and take other necessary measures before leaving the scene.
④ Since the fault lies with the child, the driver can just take the child's contact information and leave.
정답: 3
해설: A driver who has injured a child in a traffic accident must take all necessary measures such as contacting the parents.

===== 문제 289 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO correct statements about school zones?
① A school zone refers to a section of a road located within 100m of the main gate of an elementary school.
② A traffic penalty can be imposed for cars that are stopped or parked illegally in a school zone between 8 AM and 8 PM.
③ The length of the green signal of traffic lights installed in a school zone is based on the fastest walking speed of an  average child.
④ The demerit points applied to drivers who fail to consider pedestrian safety are doubled in school zones between 8 AM  and 8 PM.
정답: 2, 4
해설: A school zone means a certain section of the road within 300 meters of the main entrance of an elementary school, and the pedestrian time of the traffic lights installed in the school zone is based on the average walking speed of children.

===== 문제 290 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are true about the special protection for school buses? (Select TWO)
① The method of overtaking a school bus for children is the same as the method prescribed for overtaking other kinds of  vehicles.
② When children are getting on or off the bus, a car coming from the opposite direction on a road without a centerline  should check that it is safe and
③ When children are getting on or off the bus, a car coming from the opposite direction on a road with one lane in one  direction should stop momentaril
④ When children are getting on or off the bus, vehicles in the same lane and adjacent lanes should stop momentarily,  check that it is safe, and drive s
정답: 3, 4
해설: When a school bus has stopped on the road and is operating a device such as a flashing light indicating that children or infants are boarding or alighting, vehicles passing in the lane where the school bus has stopped and in the lane immediately next to it must come to a complete stop, confirm safety, and then slow down. And on a road with no center line installed and on a two-lane (one each way) road, the driver of a vehicle proceeding in the opposite direction must also come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down.

===== 문제 291 =====

④ A rider of a bicycle who intends to cross the road at a crosswalk may do so while riding the bicycle.

===== 문제 292 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which ministries are responsible for jointly establishing the procedure and criteria for designating priority zones  under the Road Traffic Act?
① A school zone is jointly designated by the Ministry of Interior and Safety, Ministry of Health and Welfare, and Ministry  of Land, Infrastructure, and
② A silver zone is jointly designated by the Ministry of Interior and Safety, Ministry of Land, Infrastructure, and Transport,  and Ministry of Environm
③ A priority zone for persons with disabilities is jointly designated by the Ministry of Interior and Safety, Ministry of Health  and Welfare, and Minis
④ A priority zone for people with mobility impairments is jointly designated by the Ministry of Interior and Safety, Ministry  of Environment, and Minis
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 12 (Designation, Cancellation, and Management of School Zones) paragraph 2. Matters necessary for the procedures and standards for designating and canceling school zones under paragraph 1 shall be prescribed by joint ordinance of the Ministry of Education, the Ministry of the Interior and Safety, and the Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport. Matters necessary for the procedures and standards for designating senior protection zones or disabled protection zones shall be prescribed by joint ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety, the Ministry of Health and Welfare, and the Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport.

===== 문제 293 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of driving considering the protection of school buses for children?
① If you are facing and approaching a school bus for children on a road with one lane in one direction, you must stop  before reaching the bus, check th
② If a school bus for children has switched on flashers indicating that children are getting off, you must take another lane  and drive past the bus qui
③ If there is no centerline and you are facing and approaching a school bus for children, you must keep driving at the  same speed.
④ You can overtake a school bus for children, even if it has signs indicating that children are on board.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 51 paragraph 2 (Special Protection of School Buses) (1) When a school bus has stopped on the road and is operating a device such as a flashing light indicating that children or infants are boarding or alighting, the driver of a vehicle passing in the lane where the school bus has stopped and in the lane immediately next to it must come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (2) In the case of paragraph (1), on a road with no center line installed and on a two-lane (one each way) road, the driver of a vehicle proceeding in the opposite direction must also come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (3) Every driver of a vehicle must not overtake a school bus that is passing on the road displaying the sign that it is carrying children or infants.

===== 문제 294 =====

② A school bus for children must be a vehicle with maximum passenger capacity of nine or more (1 child is considered 1  passenger).
③ If applying to renew the registration of a school bus for children because the old one needs to be replaced, one must  submit the old registration tog
④ The registration certificate of a school bus for children must be attached in a place where it will be easily visible on the  top left side of the win
정답: 2, 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 52 (Reporting of School Buses, etc.) (1) A person who intends to operate a school bus must report in advance to the head of the competent police station and receive a report certificate. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 35 (Procedures for Reporting School Buses, etc.) (3) The school bus report certificate must be attached to a conspicuous place on the upper right of the front windshield of the motor vehicle. (4) When reapplying because the school bus report certificate has become worn out and unusable, the applicant must attach the worn-out and unusable report certificate to the application for reissuance of the school bus report certificate and submit it.

===== 문제 295 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of facilitating the safe boarding and disembarking of a child for a  driver operating a school bus for children?
① Flashers must be used to ensure safety while the child is boarding the vehicle.
② If traffic is congested, turn off the flashers momentarily and allow the child to board.
③ Drop off children around the school bus and leave immediately.
④ Fastening a seatbelt is not required in a school zone.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 53 (Duties of School Bus Drivers and Operators, etc.) (1) A person who drives a school bus must operate a device such as a flashing light only when children or infants are boarding or alighting, and must display the sign under Article 51(3) only when operating the vehicle carrying children or infants. (2) A person who drives a school bus must, when children or infants board the school bus, have all boarded children or infants fasten their seat belts before departing, and when they alight, confirm that they have arrived at a safe place away from the motor vehicle, such as a sidewalk or road-edge zone, before departing.

===== 문제 296 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT true about school zones?
① The traffic penalty for a parking violation is the same as that for the silver zone.
② A "slow" sign can be installed in school zones.
③ Parking and stopping are prohibited within a school zone.
④ One will face a criminal penalty for causing an accident that leaves a child injured, regardless of any settlements.
정답: 1
해설: (1) Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 10] related to Article 93(2); (2) Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] 520; (3) Road Traffic Act Article 32 subparagraph 8, stopping and parking are prohibited in a school zone; (4) Under the proviso to Article 3(2) of the Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents, if an accident occurs in a school zone, criminal punishment is imposed regardless of whether a settlement is reached.

===== 문제 297 =====

④ Since children and infants walk slowly, keep a safe distance and drive while avoiding them.
정답: 1
해설: For the safety of the crossing pedestrian, come to a complete stop and drive on once the crossing is finished.

===== 문제 299 =====

Subparagraph 4: Foreign schools and alternative schools may also be designated as school zones. Paragraph 5 subparagraph 2: In a school zone, traffic signs concerning speed limits and crosswalks may be installed on a priority basis for the safety of children.

===== 문제 300 =====
유형: 문장형

(2) In the case of paragraph (1), on a road with no center line installed and on a two-lane (one each way) road, the driver of a vehicle proceeding in the opposite direction must also come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (3) Every driver of a vehicle must not overtake a school bus that is passing on the road displaying the sign that it is carrying children or infants.

===== 문제 304 =====

③ The traffic penalty and demerit points are three times higher than those on general roads.
④ Parking and stopping are prohibited.

===== 문제 305 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following children is NOT walking in a safe manner?
① A child walking on the edge of a roadway that is clearly separated from the sidewalk
② A child walking on the edge of a one-way road while facing the oncoming traffic
③ A child walking on the edge of a roadway that does not have a clear separation from the sidewalk while facing the  oncoming traffic
④ A child walking on the right-hand side of a sidewalk
정답: 1
해설: A pedestrian must always walk on the sidewalk on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated. On a road where the sidewalk and roadway are not separated, a pedestrian must walk on the edge of the road or in the road-edge zone. As a rule, a pedestrian walks on the right side of the sidewalk (Road Traffic Act Article 8).

===== 문제 306 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT correct regarding the protection of children?
① If a child is crossing a road without a crosswalk, drivers must drive slowly.
② If a child is standing in a safety zone, drivers must drive slowly while maintaining a safe distance.
③ If a child is walking in a narrow alley, drivers must drive slowly while maintaining a safe distance.
④ If a child is crossing a crosswalk, drivers shall make a temporary stop in front of the crosswalk.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians) (1) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when a pedestrian (including a cyclist who, pursuant to Article 13-2(6), dismounts and pushes or carries a bicycle, etc.) is crossing or about to cross a crosswalk, come to a complete stop before that crosswalk (or at the stop line where one is installed) so as not to obstruct or endanger the crossing of the pedestrian. (2) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar who intends to turn left or right at an intersection where traffic is being controlled must not obstruct the passage of pedestrians crossing the road in accordance with the signals or instructions of a traffic light or a police officer, etc. (3) Every driver of a vehicle must not obstruct the passage of pedestrians crossing an intersection where traffic is not being controlled or a road in its vicinity. (4) Every driver of a vehicle must, when there are pedestrians on a safety zone installed on the road, or when passing beside a pedestrian on a narrow road with no lanes, keep a safe distance and slow down. (5) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, when a pedestrian is crossing a road on which no crosswalk is installed pursuant to Article 10(3), keep a safe distance and come to a complete stop so that the pedestrian can cross safely. (6) Every driver of a vehicle must, when passing beside a pedestrian in any of the following places, keep a safe distance and slow down, and when the passage of the pedestrian is obstructed, slow down or come to a complete stop so that the pedestrian can pass safely: 1. a road where the sidewalk and roadway are not separated and there is no center line; 2. a pedestrian-priority road; 3. a place off the road. (7) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must, at a crosswalk without a traffic light among the crosswalks installed within a school zone under Article 12(1) (or at the stop line where one is installed), come to a complete stop regardless of whether a pedestrian is crossing.

===== 문제 307 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following correctly describes the driver's duty regarding the  special protection of school buses?
① If a school bus for children with a red flasher being operated passes next to your lane, it is necessary to stop  temporarily.
② You cannot overtake any school bus for children passing on the road.
③ If you violate this duty, you will incur 15 demerit points on your driver's license.
④ This duty does not apply when a school bus for children stops on a road with one lane in one direction with its red  flasher activited.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 19, Road Traffic Act Article 51, Motor Vehicle Management Act, Rules on the Performance and Standards of Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Parts. (1) When a school bus has stopped on the road and is operating a device such as a flashing light indicating that children or infants are boarding or alighting, the driver of a vehicle passing in the lane where the school bus has stopped and in the lane immediately next to it must come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (2) In the case of paragraph (1), on a road with no center line installed and on a two-lane (one each way) road, the driver of a vehicle proceeding in the opposite direction must also come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (3) Every driver of a vehicle must not overtake a school bus that is passing on the road displaying the sign that it is carrying children or infants. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 28. In the case of a violation of the duty of special protection of school buses, 30 penalty points are imposed on the driver's license.

===== 문제 308 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following constitutes a situation wherein a child’s guardian is  slapped with an administrative fine?
① A guardian who lets a child ride a bicycle on the roadway
② A guardian who lets a child ride an electric scooter in a playground
③ A guardian who lets a child ride an electric scooter on the roadway
④ A guardian who lets a child ride a bicycle in a playground
정답: 3
해설: A child's guardian must not let the child operate a personal mobility device on the road (Road Traffic Act Article 11(4)), and violating this results in an administrative fine of up to 200,000 won (Road Traffic Act Article 160(2) subparagraph 9).

===== 문제 309 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What are the criminal penalties under the Act on the Aggravated Punishment, etc. of Specific Crimes for causing injury to a child in a child protection zone while driving?
① Imprisonment with labor for at least 1 year but not more than 15 years or a criminal fine of at least KRW 5 million but not more than KRW 30 million
② Imprisonment with labor (or for an indefinite term) for at least 5 years
③ Imprisonment with labor for not more than 2 years or a criminal fine of not more than KRW 5 million
④ Imprisonment with labor for not more than 5 years or a criminal fine of not more than KRW 20 million
정답: 1
해설: Act on the Aggravated Punishment, etc. of Specific Crimes Article 5-13, Road Traffic Act Article 12(3). In a school zone, the measures under Article 12(1) must be observed, and pursuant to Article 5-13 of the Act on the Aggravated Punishment, etc. of Specific Crimes, in the case of causing bodily injury, the punishment is imprisonment for not less than 1 year and not more than 15 years, or a fine of not less than 5 million won and not more than 30 million won.

===== 문제 310 =====
유형: 문장형

② After completing the trip, the operator must confirm that all children and infants have disembarked from the school bus.

④ A record of confirmation of wearing seat belts and accompanying guardians must be submitted to the supervisory  authority of the facility operating th
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 53 paragraphs 3 and 6. A person operating a school bus must, when boarding children or infants on the school bus, have an adult guardian designated by the operator ride along, and the accompanying guardian must, when children or infants board or alight, get off the motor vehicle to confirm that the children or infants board or alight safely, and during operation must take measures necessary for child protection, such as having the children or infants seated with their seat belts fastened. Road Traffic Act Article 53 paragraph 6: A person operating a school bus may, when operating with a guardian aboard under paragraph 3, attach a sign indicating that a guardian is aboard (hereinafter guardian-aboard sign) prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety, and no one may attach a guardian-aboard sign when operating without a guardian aboard. Paragraph 7: A person operating a school bus must prepare and keep a record of seat belt use and guardian-aboard confirmation (hereinafter safe operation record) and submit the safe operation record every quarter to the head of the competent authority supervising the facility operating the school bus. (2) is a duty of the school bus driver.

===== 문제 311 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the punishment imposed on the operator when an “accompanying guardian sign” is  attached even though there is no adult guardian on a school bus for children?
① A criminal fine of up to KRW 200,000 or detention
② A criminal fine of up to KRW 300,000 or detention
③ A criminal fine of up to KRW 400,000 or detention
④ A criminal fine of up to KRW 500,000 or detention
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 53, Article 154. A person who attaches a guardian-aboard sign to a school bus operating without a guardian aboard shall be punished by a fine of up to 300,000 won or detention.

===== 문제 312 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about the definition of the senior driver sign under the Road Traffic Act?
① A senior driver sign indicates that the vehicle is driven by a licensed driver aged 65 or older.
② The background of the sign should be blue and the text should be yellow.
③ The front side should be made of rubber magnet for removal, and the back side should be made of reflective paper.
④ Attach it to the front of the vehicle in a visible location that does not interfere with safe driving.
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 10-2 (Production and Distribution of Elderly Driver Signs) (1) The Commissioner General of the National Police Agency may, pursuant to Article 7-2(1) of the Act, produce and distribute a sign indicating that the vehicle is being driven by a person aged 65 or older who has obtained a driver's license (hereinafter elderly driver sign). Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 8-2 <Production Method> (a) The background is sky blue and the letters are white. (b) The front is made of reflective paper, and the back is made of rubber magnet so that it can be attached and detached. (c) The font is the Ministry of Culture, Sports and Tourism title Dotum typeface. (d) Necessary phrases, etc. may be inserted within the scope of not changing the sign specifications and letter size. <Attachment Location> Attach it to a location on the rear of the vehicle that does not interfere with safe driving and that ensures visibility.

===== 문제 313 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT an authority that a commissioner of the City/Do (Province) police agency or a  police chief can exercise in a silver zone?
① Prohibiting or restricting the passage of vehicles and horses
② Designating and operating a back road as a one-way street
③ Setting a speed limit of 30 km/h for horses and vehicles
④ Designating an on-road parking lot for the elderly on a road next to the gate of a house
정답: 4
해설: Rules on the Designation and Management of Child, Senior, and Disabled Protection Zones Article 9 (Necessary Measures in Protection Zones) paragraph 1. The City/Provincial Police Agency or the head of a police station may take the following measures in a protection zone by section and by time of day: 1. prohibiting or restricting the passage of vehicles or horses; 2. prohibiting the stopping or parking of vehicles or horses; 3. limiting the operating speed to within 30 kilometers per hour; 4. designating and operating side roads as one-way roads.

===== 문제 314 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following may be prohibited from entering or driving in a silver  zone?
① Personal mobility devices (PMD), bicycles
② Truck-mounted drilling rigs and kick scooters for children
③ Motorcycles and rollators with width of 1 m or less
④ Road stabilizers and walking frames with width of 1 m or less
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 12-2 (Designation, Cancellation, and Management of Senior and Disabled Protection Zones) (1) The mayor, etc. may, when deemed necessary to protect the elderly or the disabled from the danger of traffic accidents, designate a certain section of the roads around the facilities or places under subparagraphs 1 through 3 and subparagraph 3-2 as a senior protection zone, and a certain section of the roads around the facilities under subparagraph 4 as a disabled protection zone, respectively, and take necessary measures such as restricting or prohibiting the passage of vehicles, horses, and streetcars. A bicycle also falls under vehicles and horses.

===== 문제 315 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is true about a violation that took place in a silver  zone at 10 AM?
① If the driver of a dump truck violates the traffic signal, the traffic penalty is KRW 130,000.
② If the driver of a passenger vehicle obstructs a senior pedestrian, the traffic penalty is KRW 70,000.
③ If a bicycle rider obstructs a senior pedestrian crossing a crosswalk, the traffic penalty is KRW 50,000.
④ If the driver of a cultivator fails to fulfill his/her duty of guaranteeing pedestrian safety, the traffic penalty is KRW 30,000.
정답: 1
해설: Penalties are imposed for signal violation (dump truck 130,000 won), obstruction of pedestrian crossing at a crosswalk (passenger car 120,000 won, bicycle 60,000 won), and obstruction of pedestrian passage or failure to protect pedestrians (passenger car 80,000 won, bicycle 40,000 won).

===== 문제 316 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following ministries is NOT authorized to enact enforcement ordinances regarding the procedures  and criteria for the designation and abolition of senior or disabled protection zones?
① Ministry of Culture, Sports, and Tourism
② Ministry of Interior and Safety
③ Ministry of Health and Welfare
④ Ministry of Land, Infrastructure, and Transport
정답: 1
해설: Matters necessary for the procedures and standards for designating and canceling senior protection zones or disabled protection zones shall be prescribed by joint ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety, the Ministry of Health and Welfare, and the Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport.

===== 문제 318 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about the general characteristics of senior citizens who are considered people  with mobility impairments?
① Their reflexes are slow but they are quick in making decisions from experience.
② They have poor eyesight but high hearing sensitivity.
③ They may not be quick in responding to unexpected situations, but their cognitive ability is heightened in such cases.
④ They lose mobility as their health conditions deteriorate.
정답: 4
해설: The general characteristics of the elderly are that reflexes become dull and eyesight and hearing weaken; as the physical condition ages, the ability to respond to sudden situations and cognitive ability also gradually decline.

===== 문제 319 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is true about the measures that a mayor can take for a  silver zone?
① The mayor can restrict or prohibit access by motor vehicles and horses and trams.
② The mayor can prohibit access by buses, but cannot restrict access by trams.
③ The mayor can prohibit access by two-wheeled vehicles, but cannot restrict access by bicycles.
④ The mayor cannot prohibit access by heavy equipment, but can restrict such access.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 12-2 (Designation, Cancellation, and Management of Senior and Disabled Protection Zones) paragraph 1. The mayor, etc. may designate protection zones and take necessary measures such as restricting or prohibiting the passage of vehicles, horses, and streetcars. [Effective date: October 19, 2023]

===== 문제 320 =====

① The driver is not responsible at all as there were no pedestrian signal lights.
② The driver is subject to civil liability.
③ Only the elderly person is subject to criminal prosecution for crossing illegally.
④ The driver is subject to criminal liability.
정답: 2, 4
해설: For a traffic accident at a crosswalk, the driver bears civil and criminal liability.

===== 문제 321 =====

① Prohibition or restriction of vehicle passage
② Prohibition of stopping/parking
③ Installation of a road parking lot
④ Prohibition or restriction of pedestrian passage
정답: 1, 2
해설: Rules on the Designation and Management of Child, Senior, and Disabled Protection Zones Article 9 (Necessary Measures in Protection Zones) 1. prohibiting or restricting the passage of vehicles or horses (車馬); 2. prohibiting the stopping or parking of vehicles or horses; 3. limiting the operating speed to within 30 kilometers per hour; 4. designating and operating side roads (roads in urban areas that are not arterial roads and are used for general traffic) as one-way roads.

===== 문제 325 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about driving across a crosswalk with a traffic light in a silver zone and  penalty for violation?
① When the traffic light turns green, the driver of a motor vehicle must look around first and speed up slowly.
② If the driver of a passenger vehicle violates the traffic signal between 8 AM and 8 PM, the traffic penalty is KRW  120,000.
③ A bicycle rider must also wait for all senior pedestrians to finish crossing the street.
④ If a motorcycle rider obstructs pedestrians who are crossing at a crosswalk between 8 a.m. and 8 p.m., a traffic penalty  of KRW 90,000 will be impose
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 10] In a senior protection zone, a penalty of 120,000 won is imposed on a passenger car driver who commits a signal violation, and a penalty of 80,000 won is imposed on a two-wheeled vehicle driver who obstructs the crossing of a pedestrian at a crosswalk.

===== 문제 326 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements is INCORRECT regarding a silver zone?
① A traffic penalty is imposed for speeding between 8 AM and 8 PM.
② Green traffic lights for pedestrians are longer that those on general roadways.
③ No road parking lots can be installed.
④ Larger traffic lights are installed so that they can be seen more easily by the elderly.
정답: 4
해설: Considering the physical condition of the elderly, the length of the pedestrian signal is set based on the average walking speed of the disabled and is longer than the pedestrian signal on ordinary roads (see Rules on the Designation and Management of Child, Senior, and Disabled Protection Zones Article 6(2); 2023 Traffic Signal Installation and Operation Handbook, Part 2, Chapter 2, Pedestrian Signal Operation). Traffic lights may not be installed larger than the regulations.

===== 문제 328 =====

===== 문제 333 =====

② A pedestrian who crosses a road without using a crosswalk, as there are no cars passing by.
③ A pedestrian who crosses a road behind a stopped truck.
④ A pedestrian who walks on the left side of a sidewalk.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Articles 8 and 10. (1)(2) On a road where no crosswalk is installed, a pedestrian must cross by the shortest distance. (3) A pedestrian must not cross in front of or behind any vehicle. (4) As a rule, a pedestrian walks on the right side of the sidewalk.

A mobility-disadvantaged person means a person who feels inconvenience in movement in daily life, such as a disabled person, an elderly person, a pregnant woman, a person accompanying an infant, or a child.

===== 문제 337 =====

① Always drive slowly for safety reasons when traveling long distances, regardless of the type of road.

③ Keep an eye on the road situation and drive by complying with the speed limit.
④ Drive mainly at night when there is less traffic.
정답: 3
해설: Elderly drivers should refrain from long-distance driving, long-hour driving, and late-night driving.

===== 문제 338 =====

질문: Which of the following is the least related to safe driving by a senior citizen?
① Taking sufficient rest prior to driving
② Periodic checks of health condition
③ Verification of the route to the destination prior to driving
④ Encouraging nighttime driving rather than daytime driving.
정답: 4
해설: It is desirable for elderly drivers to take sufficient rest before driving and to check their health condition periodically. It is good to check the route to the destination before driving and, if possible, not to drive late at night.

===== 문제 339 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT the correct explanation regarding criminal punishment if a driver of a passenger  car causes an injury that will take 3 weeks to heal to an elderly person in the silver z
① If a driver has comprehensive insurance, he/she is not subject to criminal punishment.
② Criminal punishment is applied when there is a road sign indicating a silver zone.
③ If a victim does not want punishment, a driver is not subject to criminal punishment.
④ If both parties reach an agreement, criminal punishment is not imposed.
정답: 2
해설: Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents Article 3 (Special Cases on Punishment) paragraph 2: prosecution (公訴) may not be instituted against the explicit will of the victim. Article 4 (Special Cases When Insured with Comprehensive Insurance, etc.) paragraph 1: when insured with insurance or mutual aid, prosecution may not be instituted against the driver of a vehicle who has committed the crime prescribed in the main text of Article 3(2). However, for the items prescribed in the proviso to Article 3(2) of the Act on Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents, criminal punishment may be imposed even against the explicit will of the victim or even when insured with comprehensive insurance. However, since a senior protection zone falls under the special cases on punishment, criminal punishment is not imposed when there is comprehensive insurance coverage and an intent not to punish.

① A vehicle with a tag permitting parking in parking spaces reserved for persons with disabilities may park there even if a  disabled person is not ridi
② If a person issued with a tag permitting parking in parking spaces reserved for persons with disabilities uses it in an  unlawful manner such as trans

===== 문제 346 =====
유형: 문장형

===== 문제 347 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following correctly describes a Dutch Reach (method of opening the door) when exiting a  vehicle?
① When exiting the vehicle, grab the handle with the hand farthest from the window, check if there is oncoming traffic  from the rear, and open the door
② When exiting the vehicle, grab the handle with the hand closest to the window, check if there is oncoming traffic from  the front, and open the door.
③ The Dutch Reach prevents the driver from moving the vehicle with a door open.
④ The Dutch Reach is a traffic safety campaign started in the UK.
정답: 1
해설: The Dutch Reach is a traffic safety campaign started in the Netherlands in the 1960s to prevent accidents with bicycles approaching from the rear side of a passenger car. It is a method in which the driver or passenger, when opening the car door to get out, grasps the handle with the hand farther from the window. Opening the door this way naturally rotates the body more than 45 degrees, allowing the person to visually check the rear, thereby preventing accidents with bicycles, motorcycles, etc. approaching from the rear side of the passenger car. An 'open-door move-off' accident refers to an accident that occurs when the driver, etc., pulls away (starts moving) while the car door is open.

===== 문제 348 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: How long can an EV or a plug-in hybrid vehicle park at a charging station after commencing charging?
① 1 hour
② 2 hours
③ 3 hours
④ 4 hours
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Act on Promotion of Development and Distribution of Environment-Friendly Motor Vehicles Article 18-8 (Standards for Acts Obstructing the Charging of Environment-Friendly Motor Vehicles, etc.) In a charging area of a fast-charging facility, the act of an electric vehicle or externally rechargeable hybrid vehicle continuing to park even after 1 hour, the time announced by the Minister of Trade, Industry and Energy within the range of 2 hours, has passed, constitutes an act obstructing the charging of environment-friendly motor vehicles.

===== 문제 349 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements about parking and stopping on a slope under the Road Traffic Act is incorrect?
① When parking, operate the parking brake and use wheel chocks or blocks to prevent the vehicle from slipping.
② When stopping, if the driver remains seated and directly operates the brake, no additional anti-slip measures are required.
③ When parking, operate the parking brake and turn the steering wheel toward the road.
④ The parking brake alone is insufficient when stopping and leaving the driver’s seat temporarily; rubber or plastic must be  used to prevent the vehicl
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 11 paragraph 3. When the driver of a motor vehicle intends to stop or park on a slope (including stopping or parking on a slope off the road) pursuant to Article 34-3 of the Act, the driver must, after operating the parking brake of the motor vehicle, take any of the following measures. However, this is excluded when the driver does not leave the driver's seat and is directly operating the brake.

===== 문제 350 =====
