===== 문제 351 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO statements correctly describe how a driver should behave?
① The passenger is responsible for his/her safety when exiting the vehicle.
② Even when parking temporarily to buy something, the driver must turn off the engine.
③ Before exiting a vehicle, the driver must turn off the engine and check that it is safe.
④ When parking in an area other than a parking lot, the parking brake must be left released so that the vehicle can be  moved if necessary.
정답: 2, 3
해설: It is prescribed that when the driver leaves the driver's seat, the driver must turn off the engine, thoroughly apply the brake, etc. to safely maintain the stationary state of the vehicle, and take necessary measures to prevent others from operating the vehicle at will.

===== 문제 352 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO safest methods of parking on a steeply sloped road?
① Engage the parking brake and put the vehicle into reverse gear, regardless of the direction of the slope.
② Engage the parking brake and turn the steering wheel toward the roadside (meaning toward the side closest to the vehicle).
③ Engage the parking brake and place a wheel stopper or other device that can prevent the vehicle from slipping in the  downhill direction.
④ For manual transmission vehicles, engage the parking brake and leave the gear in neutral.
정답: 2, 3
해설: Under Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 11 paragraph 3, when parking, after operating the brake, install something that can prevent the motor vehicle from sliding, such as a chock, on the wheels in the downhill direction of the slope, and turn the steering device toward the edge of the road (the side closer to the vehicle). It is not that reverse gear should be engaged unconditionally; a method is to engage the gear so that the vehicle does not roll down according to the slope, and putting the gear in neutral should be avoided.

===== 문제 353 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO correct statements about stopping/parking?
① When stopping on a road with a separate sidewalk, park at the far right edge of the roadway.
② There is no need to follow the stopping/parking instructions shown on safety signs.
③ In the case of manual vehicles, you may leave the vehicle in 1st or reverse gear without using the parking brake when  parking on level ground.
④ On sloped roads, engage the parking brake and place a wheel stopper.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Pursuant to Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Article 11 paragraph 3 subparagraph 1, when the driver of a motor vehicle intends to stop or park on a slope (including stopping or parking on a slope off the road) pursuant to Article 34-3 of the Act, the driver must, after operating the parking brake of the motor vehicle, take any of the following measures. However, this is excluded when the driver does not leave the driver's seat and is directly operating the brake.

===== 문제 354 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO constitute parking?
① A vehicle remaining stationary after breaking down
② A temporary stop after recognizing a safety hazard
③ A stopped vehicle that is left unattended by the driver so that it cannot be driven immediately, even if it is for less than  five minutes
④ Stopping briefly near a subway station to drop off a friend
정답: 1, 3
해설: Stopping to wait for a signal, or coming to a complete stop to prevent danger, does not constitute parking even if it exceeds 5 minutes. However, a state in which the driver has left the vehicle and cannot drive it immediately, even if it does not exceed 5 minutes, constitutes parking. (Road Traffic Act Article 2)

===== 문제 355 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO constitute stopping?
① A taxi stopping at a taxi stand for the driver to go to the restroom
② A vehicle that is left unattended by its driver who is away to pick up a load to transport
③ The vehicle stops while waiting for a traffic signal.
④ The driver stops the car to ask a passerby for directions.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Stopping means the driver stopping the vehicle for not more than 5 minutes, and refers to a stationary state other than parking. (Road Traffic Act Article 2 subparagraph 25)

===== 문제 356 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following are TWO locations that allow a motor vehicle to make  a stop or park?
① Within 50 m from the main entrance in a school zone
② Within 10 m from a crosswalk
③ Within a distance of 5 m from where emergency fire-extinguishing equipment is installed
④ Within 10 m from a safety zone in all four directions
정답: 3, 4
해설: Places where stopping and parking are prohibited. Every driver of a vehicle must not stop or park the vehicle in any of the following places. However, this does not apply when following this Act, an order under this Act, or the instructions of a police officer, or when coming to a complete stop to prevent danger. 1. an intersection, crosswalk, railroad crossing, or the sidewalk of a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated (excluding on-street parking lots installed across the roadway and sidewalk under the Parking Lot Act); 2. within 5 meters from the edge of an intersection or a corner of the road; 3. within 10 meters in all directions from a safety zone on a road where one is installed; 4. within 10 meters from a pillar, sign, or line indicating a bus passenger vehicle stop (停留地). However, this does not apply when the driver of a bus passenger vehicle stops or parks to pick up or drop off passengers at a stop along the operating route during the operating hours of that bus passenger vehicle; 5. within 10 meters from the edge of a railroad crossing or a crosswalk; 6. within 5 meters from any of the following places: (a) a place where a fire-fighting water facility or emergency fire-extinguishing device under Article 10 of the Framework Act on Fire Services is installed; (b) a place where a fire-fighting system prescribed by Presidential Decree under Article 2(1) subparagraph 1 of the Act on Fire Prevention and Installation, Maintenance, and Safety Control of Fire-Fighting Systems is installed; 7. a place designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it necessary to prevent danger on the road and to secure the safety and smooth flow of traffic; 8. a school zone designated by the mayor, etc. under Article 12(1). Article 34-2 (Special Cases on Places Where Stopping or Parking Is Prohibited) (1) In any of the following cases, notwithstanding Article 32 subparagraphs 1, 4, 5, 7, and 8 or Article 33 subparagraph 3, stopping or parking is allowed: 1. when stopping or parking a bicycle at an electric bicycle charging station or bicycle parking device among the bicycle-use facilities under Article 2 subparagraph 2 of the Act on Promotion of Bicycle Use; 2. when the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency has permitted the stopping or parking of bicycles, etc. with traffic signs at the request of the mayor, etc. (2) At a place where the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency has permitted stopping or parking by designating the zone, time, method, and type of vehicle with traffic signs, stopping or parking is allowed notwithstanding Article 32 subparagraphs 7 and 8 or Article 33 subparagraph 3.

===== 문제 357 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following are TWO locations that allow a motor vehicle to park?
① 5 m from the corner of a road
② At a distance of 7 m or more from where a fire-extinguishing water facility is installed
③ At a distance of 7 m or more from where emergency fire-extinguishing equipment is installed
④ 5 m from a safety zone
정답: 2, 3
해설: Places where parking is prohibited: (1) within 10 meters from a crosswalk; (2) within 5 meters from a place where a fire-fighting water facility is installed; (3) within 5 meters from a place where an emergency fire-extinguishing device is installed; (4) within 10 meters in all directions from a safety zone (Road Traffic Act Article 32). Article 34-2 (Special Cases on Places Where Stopping or Parking Is Prohibited) (1) In any of the following cases, notwithstanding Article 32 subparagraphs 1, 4, 5, 7, and 8 or Article 33 subparagraph 3, stopping or parking is allowed: 1. when stopping or parking a bicycle at an electric bicycle charging station or bicycle parking device among the bicycle-use facilities under Article 2 subparagraph 2 of the Act on Promotion of Bicycle Use; 2. when the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency has permitted the stopping or parking of bicycles, etc. with traffic signs at the request of the mayor, etc. (2) At a place where the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency has permitted stopping or parking by designating the zone, time, method, and type of vehicle with traffic signs, stopping or parking is allowed notwithstanding Article 32 subparagraphs 7 and 8 or Article 33 subparagraph 3.

===== 문제 358 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: At an intersection with no traffic control, what is the safest method of turning left?
① Drive slowly and turn left with care even if there are other vehicles that have entered the intersection ahead of you.
② Yield to cars entering from wider roads.
③ Yield to cars passing through straight across, but take the right of way over cars trying to turn right.
④ Go slowly along the centerline of the road beforehand and turn left using the outside center of the intersection.
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 26. You must yield the right-of-way to the vehicle that entered first, a left-turning vehicle must yield the right of way to vehicles going straight and turning right, and must turn left using the inside of the center of the intersection.

===== 문제 359 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following INCORRECTLY describes a safe method of using a roundabout?
① Slow down before entering a roundabout.
② Stop temporarily at the yield line and enter slowly.
③ A vehicle entering the roundabout has the right of way, so the vehicle making a turn in the roundabout must yield.
④ Turn counterclockwise.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 25-2 (Method of Passing Through Roundabouts) (1) At a roundabout, you must pass counterclockwise. (2) When intending to enter a roundabout, you must slow down or come to a complete stop, and when there are other vehicles already proceeding, you must yield the right-of-way to those vehicles.

===== 문제 360 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A vehicle that has entered an intersection without traffic lights ahead of you is turning left. What should you do?
① Proceed with caution if you are going to pass straight through.
② If you are going to turn right, do so at low speed.
③ If you are going to go straight through or turn right, yield to vehicles turning left.
④ If you are driving on a narrow road, pass through the intersection at low speed.
정답: 3
해설: At an intersection where traffic is not being controlled, even a left-turning vehicle has the right of way if it entered the intersection first, so even a vehicle going straight or turning right must yield the right of way to the left-turning vehicle. (Road Traffic Act Article 26)

===== 문제 361 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the most appropriate method of turning left at an intersection under the Road Traffic Act?
① Slow down along the centerline and turn left through the inner side of the intersection’s center.
② Left turns are allowed at a red light at a signalized intersection if there is an “unprotected left turn” sign.
③ At a signalized intersection, a left-turning vehicle has priority over a vehicle traveling straight ahead.
④ On a one-way road with three lanes, turning left from the right lane is allowed if the traffic is light.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Articles 25 and 26. Every driver of a vehicle who intends to turn left at an intersection must turn left while slowing down along the center line of the road in advance, using the inside of the center of the intersection. Where there is an unprotected left turn traffic sign, one must turn left on the straight (green) light, and when entering at the same time, the vehicle going straight takes priority over the left-turning vehicle.

===== 문제 362 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the proper method of passing through an intersection without traffic control?
① Drive slowly when it is impossible to check the sides.
② A vehicle turning left has the right of way over a vehicle traveling straight.
③ A vehicle turning right has the right of way over a vehicle traveling straight.
④ You should drive slowly when the intersection is wider than the road you are on.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 26. When unable to check left and right, you must come to a complete stop, and when the width of the intersecting road is wider than the width of the road on which the vehicle is traveling, you must slow down.

===== 문제 363 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: In which of the following locations is the installation of a roundabout recommended for ensuring smooth traffic  flow and safety on the road?
① Locations where traffic volumes are not very high, but there are many intersection accidents
② Locations where there are one or more access roads at the intersection with three or more lanes in one direction
③ Locations where the traffic volume at the roundabout exceeds its capacity
④ Locations where the presence of a roundabout in a segment necessary for signal coordination would reduce the  effectiveness of coordination
정답: 1
해설: Roundabout Design Guideline (August 2022, Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport) 1) Cases where installing a roundabout is recommended: (1) when the traffic volume level is too high to operate as a non-signalized intersection but too low to operate as a signalized intersection, so the efficiency of signal operation is low; (2) when the traffic volume level is not high but many traffic accidents occur at the intersection; (3) when it is difficult for drivers to recognize the right of way; (4) a Y-shaped intersection, T-shaped intersection, or intersection with an unusual shape; (5) an intersection within a traffic-calming project section. 2) Cases where installing a roundabout is not recommended: (1) when the traffic volume level of the roundabout exceeds its processing capacity; (2) when the roundabout design standards cannot be satisfied; (3) when a variable lane is operated during peak hours; (4) when it is an intersection within a section where signal coordination is implemented; (5) when one or more approach roads at the intersection have three or more lanes each way.

===== 문제 364 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements correctly describes a roundabout?
① Roundabouts have more conflicting points than signalized intersections.
② When entering, you must wait at the yield line until there is enough space within the roundabout.
③ Traffic lights are installed at a roundabout to guide vehicles and facilitate traffic flow through the intersection.
④ A vehicle that has already entered a roundabout must yield to an entering vehicle.
정답: 2
해설: Compared to a signalized intersection, a roundabout has fewer conflict points, a vehicle intending to enter must yield to vehicles already circulating, and if there is no available space within the roundabout, you must not enter.

④ Regulation lines

해설: A side-collision accident occurring while proceeding at an intersection is highly likely due to a lack of understanding of the lane guidance markings. (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6 I. General Standards 2. Road Markings, II. Individual Standards 5. Road Markings)

===== 문제 366 =====

===== 문제 367 =====

정답: 1, 3

(Road Traffic Act Article 25)

===== 문제 368 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a correct guideline with regard to passing through a roundabout?
① A car inside the roundabout has the right of way.
② A car about to enter the roundabout has the right of way.
③ A car exiting the roundabout has the right of way.
④ There are no set rules on who has the right of way.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 25-2 (Method of Passing Through Roundabouts) When intending to enter a roundabout, you must slow down or come to a complete stop, and when there are other vehicles already proceeding, you must yield the right-of-way to those vehicles.

===== 문제 369 =====

① Stopping
② Parking
③ Driving slowly and stopping temporarily
④ Overtaking
정답: 3
해설: <Road Traffic Act Article 32 (Prohibition of Stopping and Parking)> Every driver of a vehicle must not stop or park the vehicle in any of the following places. However, this does not apply when following this Act, an order under this Act, or the instructions of a police officer, or when coming to a complete stop to prevent danger.

===== 문제 370 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following vehicles are granted the right of way at a roundabout?
① Vehicles moving on the section of the road within the roundabout
② Vehicles trying to turn left before entering the roundabout
③ Vehicles trying to turn right before entering the roundabout
④ Vehicles trying to turn left or right before entering the roundabout
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 25-2 (Method of Passing Through Roundabouts) (1) Every driver of a vehicle must pass counterclockwise at a roundabout. (2) Every driver of a vehicle must, when intending to enter a roundabout, slow down or come to a complete stop, and when there are other vehicles already proceeding, yield the right-of-way to those vehicles. (3) When there is a vehicle signaling with a hand, turn signal, or lights to pass through the roundabout in accordance with paragraphs 1 and 2, the driver of the vehicle behind it must not obstruct the progress of the signaling vehicle ahead.

===== 문제 372 =====

① Before entering a roundabout, come to a complete stop and enter when there are no approaching vehicles from the left.
② At a roundabout, the traffic circulates clockwise.
③ There is no need to use turn signals when entering or exiting a roundabout.
④ Even after entering a roundabout, you should continue to be cautious of other vehicles and proceed with care.
정답: 1, 4

===== 문제 373 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a place where you should stop temporarily while driving?
① Near a curve on a road
② Descent on a steep slope
③ Near the top of a slope
④ At an intersection with no traffic control and heavy traffic
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 31 (Places Where You Must Slow Down or Come to a Complete Stop). Places where you must slow down include a curved section of the road, a steep downhill slope, and near the crest of a slope. An intersection where traffic is not being controlled and left and right cannot be checked or where traffic is heavy, and a place designated with traffic signs by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it necessary to prevent danger on the road and to secure the safety and smooth flow of traffic, are places where you must come to a complete stop.

===== 문제 374 =====
유형: 문장형

① An intersection without a traffic control officer where traffic to the left and right cannot be checked
② An intersection where the lights have turned green
③ A busy bridge/tunnel
④ In the vicinity of a curve or the highest point on a slope
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 31 paragraph 2. At an intersection where traffic is not being controlled and left and right cannot be checked or where traffic is heavy, you must come to a complete stop.

===== 문제 375 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a place where you should stop temporarily pursuant to the Road Traffic Act?
① Inside a tunnel and on a bridge
② At a busy intersection with no traffic lights
③ On a descending section of a steep hill
④ A curved section of a road
정답: 2
해설: Overtaking-prohibited zones and places where you must come to a complete stop are prescribed differently. Inside tunnels, on bridges, steep downhill slopes, and curved sections of the road are overtaking-prohibited zones. An intersection where traffic is not being controlled and left and right cannot be checked or where traffic is heavy, and a place designated with traffic signs by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it necessary to prevent danger on the road and to secure the safety and smooth flow of traffic, are places where you must come to a complete stop. (Road Traffic Act Article 31 paragraph 2)

===== 문제 376 =====

When the maximum speed is set by a variable speed limit sign, you must follow it, and when the maximum speed set by a variable speed limit sign differs from the maximum speed set by another traffic sign, you must follow the variable speed limit sign.

===== 문제 377 =====
유형: 문장형

① any vehicle - slow down
② any motor vehicle or heavy equipment - slow down

④ any vehicle or tram - stop

===== 문제 378 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest method of driving at an expressway interchange?
① As interchanges tend to be congested, apply sudden brakes to discourage vehicles behind you from following closely  and prevent potential accidents ac
② As drivers tend to become less aware of speed at an interchange, stop momentarily before continuing.
③ If the desired interchange has already been passed, use the next interchange.
④ Accelerate quickly to enter an interchange.
정답: 3
해설: Sudden braking increases the risk of an accident with the vehicle behind. If you decelerate suddenly near an interchange and come out onto an ordinary road, your sense of speed becomes dull, so you must slow down in advance and drive at the regulation speed of the ordinary road. If you pass the interchange you should exit at, you must use the next interchange to exit.

===== 문제 379 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most correct action to take when the driver of the vehicle ahead stretches his/her  arm horizontally out of the left side of the vehicle?
① Slow down as the front vehicle is expected to turn right.
② Switch to an upper lane as the front vehicle is expected to cross.
③ Overtake the front vehicle as it is expected to make a U-turn.
④ Slow down as the front vehicle is expected to change lanes.
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act Table 2. When turning left, crossing, making a U-turn, or changing lanes to the left while proceeding in the same direction, upon reaching a point at least 30 meters (100 meters on a highway) before the point where you intend to perform the act (at the edge of the intersection when turning left), extend the left arm horizontally out of the left side of the vehicle body, or extend the right arm out of the right side of the vehicle body and bend the elbow to raise it vertically, or operate the left turn signal or lights.

===== 문제 380 =====

① Extend your left arm out to the left and bend your forearm vertically upward.

③ Extend your right arm out to the right and hold it parallel to the road, and wave the extended arm forwards and  backwards.
④ Stick your left arm out of the vehicle and bend your forearm downward at an angle of 45°.
정답: 1
해설: When the driver intends to turn right, the driver extends the left arm out of the left side of the vehicle body and bends the elbow to raise it vertically.

===== 문제 381 =====

① Stop just before the stop line.
② Decelerate suddenly to slow down.
③ Proceed according to the signal from the traffic light.
④ Enter the intersection slowly.
정답: 1
해설: When the signal or instruction indicated by a traffic safety facility differs from the signal or instruction of a police officer, etc. for traffic control, you must follow the signal or instruction of the police officer, etc.

===== 문제 382 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about overtaking on a road with a combination of dotted yellow and solid yellow  centerlines?
① Overtaking is not allowed from either the yellow broken line or the yellow solid line.
② Overtaking is allowed from the side of the yellow broken line.
③ Overtaking is allowed from the sides of both yellow broken line and yellow solid line if it is clear that it is safe.
④ Overtaking is allowed from the side of the yellow solid line.
정답: 2
해설: On the side with the yellow dashed line, you may overtake by crossing the center line, but on the side with the yellow solid line, you may not overtake by crossing the center line.

===== 문제 383 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of driving through a railway crossing?
① Drive through if there are no trains coming.
② Come to a stop, check that it is safe, and drive through.
③ Drive through at a speed above the speed limit.
④ Drive at an increased speed if the crossing gate is about to close.
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 24 (Passage of Railroad Crossings) (1) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar who intends to pass through a railroad crossing (hereinafter crossing) must come to a complete stop before the crossing, confirm that it is safe, and then pass. However, when following the signal indicated by a traffic light, etc., the driver may pass without stopping. (2) Every driver of a vehicle or streetcar must not enter a crossing while the crossing barrier is down or about to come down, or while the crossing alarm is sounding.

===== 문제 384 =====
유형: 문장형

③ Turn on the left turn signal at least 20m (80m on expressways) away from where you want to turn left.
④ Turn on the left turn signal when you reach the location where you want to turn left.
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 2] Timing and Method of Signals. When intending to change course, operate the turn signal upon reaching a point at least 30 meters before that point.

===== 문제 387 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO constitute a violation of a traffic signal?
① Stopping over the stop line at a red light
② Entering an intersection when the lights have already turned yellow

④ Stopping temporarily in front of the stop line when the lights are blinking red, before driving through carefully
정답: 1, 2

===== 문제 388 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the correct methods of turning right at an intersection on a road with three lanes in  one direction? (Select TWO)
① When turning right, you should turn on the turning signal at the intersection just before proceeding to indicate your  intended direction of travel.
② Even if the cross light at the right-side crosswalk is green, you can proceed if there are no pedestrians.
③ If the right-turn signal is red, you cannot proceed even if there are no pedestrians.
④ It is safer to turn right from the second lane on a road with three lanes in one direction.
정답: 2, 3
해설: When turning right at an intersection, even if the pedestrian signal light of the crosswalk on the right-side road is green, you may pass within the range that does not obstruct the passage of pedestrians. However, if a vehicle auxiliary signal light is installed on the side of the pedestrian signal light, passing when the auxiliary signal light is red may constitute a signal violation, so you may not pass; and if a pedestrian is on the crosswalk, you may not pass because it obstructs the pedestrian's passage even if the proceeding vehicle and the pedestrian are far apart.

===== 문제 389 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Motor Vehicle Management Act, which of the following are true about the criteria for a bus  and the precautions when turning left behind a bus? (Select TWO)
① A bus refers to a vehicle with seating capacity of 36 or more persons. When driving behind a bus, you must drive slowly,  keeping a safe distance from
② A mini-bus refers to a vehicle with seating capacity of 16 ~ 35 persons. When you are driving behind a mini-bus and the  mini-bus driver turns on a tu
③ A van refers to a vehicle with seating capacity of 15 or fewer persons. Since vans are more prone to overturning due to their  high center of gravity
④ A light van refers to a vehicle with engine displacement below 1,200 cc. Although light vans have the same center of gravity  as passenger vehicles, y
정답: 1, 3
해설: A signal must be given upon reaching a point at least 30 meters (100 meters on a highway) before the point where you intend to perform the act (at the edge of the intersection when turning left), and you must reduce speed in advance and enter the first lane or the left-turn lane while slowing down. Enforcement Rule of the Motor Vehicle Management Act Article 2 (Classification of Motor Vehicle Types, Table 1 Types of Motor Vehicles).

===== 문제 390 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO correctly describe how to change lanes?
① Change lanes while maintaining speed if the car behind you in the lane you are about to enter is far back enough.
② Slow down when changing lanes if the car behind you in the lane you are about to enter is far back enough.
③ Let the car behind you in the lane you are about to enter pass if it is approaching.
④ Change lanes quickly if the car behind you in the lane you are about to enter is approaching.
정답: 1, 3
해설: When there is distance from the following vehicle, change lanes while maintaining speed, and when it is approaching, reduce speed and let the vehicle behind pass first.

===== 문제 391 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO correct statements about the lines that separate lanes?
① Where a solid line and a broken line run parallel, you cannot change from the lane near the solid-line to the lane near  the broken-line.
② Where a solid line and a broken line run parallel, you can change from the lane near the solid line to the lane near the  broken line.
③ Where a solid line and a broken line run parallel, you cannot change from the lane near the broken line to the lane  near the solid line.
④ Where a solid line and a broken line run parallel, you can change from the lane near the broken line to the lane near  the solid line.
정답: 1, 4
해설: (1) Among the lines dividing lanes, a solid line is a line where lane changes cannot be made. (2) A dashed line is a line where lane changes can be made. (3) Where a solid line and a dashed line run parallel, a lane change from the solid line side toward the dashed line direction is not possible. (4) Where a solid line and a dashed line run parallel, a lane change from the dashed line side toward the solid line direction is possible.

===== 문제 392 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, what does a flashing red light indicate?
① Vehicles and horses must drive slowly while looking out for traffic.
② Vehicles and horses may proceed while looking out for traffic.
③ Vehicles and horses may reverse while looking out for safety signs.
④ Vehicles and horses must temporarily stop right before the stop line and then proceed while looking out for traffic.
정답: 4
해설: When the red light is flashing, a vehicle or horse must, where there is a stop line or crosswalk, come to a complete stop immediately before it or immediately before the intersection, and then may proceed while paying attention to other traffic.

===== 문제 393 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following instructions about an intersection with an unprotected left-turn sign is correct?
① You can turn left regardless of the signal as long as you look out for traffic.
② You can turn left at a red light while looking out for traffic.
③ You can turn left at a green light while looking out for traffic.
④ You can turn left at a yellow light while looking out for traffic.
정답: 3
해설: At a place with an unprotected left turn sign, you may turn left while paying attention to other traffic when the green light is on. If you collide with an oncoming straight-proceeding vehicle while turning left on the green light, the left-turning vehicle becomes the at-fault party in an ordinary accident caused by minor negligence.

===== 문제 394 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is a correct statement regarding the speed of motor  vehicles?
① The minimum speed limit on expressways is 50 km/h.
② Automobile roads have a maximum speed limit, but no minimum speed limit.
③ There are maximum and minimum speed limits on normal roads.
④ The maximum speed limit on expressways with more than two lanes in one direction is the same for all types of  vehicles.
정답: 1
해설: The minimum speed on an expressway is uniformly prescribed as 50 kilometers per hour on all expressways, and on a motor-vehicle-only road, both a maximum speed and a minimum speed are limited. On ordinary roads, there is no minimum speed limit, and the maximum speed on an expressway with two or more lanes each way is prescribed differently depending on the vehicle type.

===== 문제 395 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding overtaking is correct?
① You can overtake a vehicle that is in the process of overtaking another vehicle.
② You must overtake on the right when in a tunnel.
③ Overtaking on a road with one lane in one direction is permitted only in an area with solid yellow lines.
④ Overtaking is prohibited in intersections.
정답: 4
해설: A yellow solid line prohibits overtaking; inside tunnels and on bridges are overtaking-prohibited places, and it is prescribed that overtaking cannot be done when the vehicle ahead is overtaking another vehicle.

===== 문제 396 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the centerline on a road is correct?
① Overtaking is allowed if the center is marked with a yellow solid line.
② The centerline of a reversible lane is the leftmost yellow broken line in the direction of traffic indicated by traffic signals.
③ A bus stopped on a provincial road with one lane in one direction can be overtaken by crossing the yellow solid  centerline.
④ A centerline can be installed on roads at least 4.75 m wide.
정답: 2
해설: Under Article 2, Item 5 and Article 13 of the Road Traffic Act, and Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6], serial number 501, whether the center line is a double line or a single line it may not be crossed, and when a bus has stopped to pick up or drop off passengers, overtaking is not allowed. Also, a center line is installed where the roadway width is 6 meters or more.

===== 문제 397 =====

② Special vehicles
③ Vehicles fitted with construction equipment
④ Small and mid-sized passenger vehicles
정답: 4
해설: On a three-lane (one-way) expressway, the 2nd lane is a left lane, so the vehicles allowed to travel in it are passenger cars and light, small, and medium-sized passenger vans. (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 9])

===== 문제 398 =====

① 80
② 90
③ 100
④ 110
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 9], standards for vehicles traveling by lane: on a two-lane (one-way) expressway, any vehicle intending to overtake. However, where, due to road conditions such as increased traffic volume, a vehicle unavoidably has to travel at less than 80 km/h, it may travel in that lane even when not overtaking.

===== 문제 402 =====

④ A 5-ton truck uses Lane 2 to overtake.

===== 문제 406 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the standard for driving on an expressway with three lanes in one direction? (Assuming smooth traffic  flow and no exclusive bus lanes)
① Passenger cars are to use Lane 1 so they must use only Lane 1.
② A small passenger vehicle whose designated lane is Lane 2 should use Lane 1 when overtaking.
③ Large vans must use the first lane.
④ A truck with maximum load weight of 1.5 tons or less must drive in Lane 1.
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 9]: on a three-lane (one-way) expressway, the driving lane for passenger cars and light, small, and medium-sized passenger vans is the 2nd lane on the left, and when overtaking from the 2nd lane, they must use the 1st lane to overtake.

===== 문제 407 =====

② The second lane
③ The third lane
④ Any of the above
정답: 1
해설: You may overtake using the 1st lane.

===== 문제 408 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT correct regarding overtaking according to the Road Traffic Act?
① To overtake another vehicle, you must pass on the left lane of the vehicle in front.
② If the centerline is a yellow broken line, it is possible to overtake when there is no vehicle in the opposite direction.
③ In case of a reversible lane, overtaking is not allowed on the leftmost yellow broken line in the direction indicated by a  traffic light.
④ On an expressway with four lanes in one direction, vehicles driving on the right lane can move up to the first lane.
정답: 4
해설: (1) Road Traffic Act Article 21 (Method of Overtaking, etc.) Paragraph 1: the driver of any vehicle intending to overtake another vehicle must pass to the left of the vehicle ahead. (2) Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6], center line marking (road marking No. 501). (3) Road Traffic Act Article 2 (Definitions) Item 5: where a reversible lane is installed, the yellow dotted line farthest to the left in the direction of travel indicated by the signal is the center line. (4) Under Road Traffic Act Article 16 Paragraph 1 and Article 39 Paragraph 1, and Enforcement Rule [Table 9], when overtaking, a vehicle may travel in the left lane immediately next to it according to the standards for vehicles traveling by lane; therefore, on an expressway of three or more lanes (one-way), a vehicle traveling in a right lane cannot enter all the way to the 1st lane and may overtake using the lane immediately next to it.

===== 문제 409 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Below are explanations of the standard for driving according to the lane under the Road Traffic Act. Which of  the following is NOT true? (Expressways with smooth traffic flow and no exclusive bus lan
① When proceeding at a low speed, the driver may use the lane to the right of the current lane in which he/she has been  driving.
② The first lane of an expressway with two lanes in one direction may be used by all motor vehicles to overtake.
③ On a one-way traffic road, the rightmost lane is considered the first lane.
④ The right lane of an expressway with three lanes in one direction is to be used by a truck to drive.
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 16: lane numbering starts with the 1st lane from the lane on the center-line side of the road. However, on a one-way road, the 1st lane is counted from the left of the road.

===== 문제 410 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is true about the standard for driving on an  expressway with three lanes in one direction? (Assuming smooth traffic flow and no exclusive bus
① The first lane is the lane for overtaking a passenger car driving on the second lane.
② The first lane is the driving lane for a van.
③ The roadside is the driving lane for emergency vehicles and tow vehicles.
④ When operating an exclusive bus lane, the first lane is the driving lane for trucks.
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 9]: on an expressway of three or more lanes (one-way), the 1st lane may be used by passenger cars intending to overtake and by light, small, and medium-sized passenger vans intending to overtake (however, where, due to road conditions such as increased traffic volume, a vehicle unavoidably has to travel at less than 80 km/h, it may travel there even when not overtaking); the left lanes may be used by passenger cars and light, small, and medium-sized passenger vans; and the right lanes may be used by large passenger vans, freight vehicles, special vehicles, and construction machinery under Article 2, Item 18(b) of the Act.

===== 문제 411 =====

① An exclusive lane for buses
② An exclusive lane for high-occupancy vehicles
③ An exclusive lane for motor vehicles
④ An exclusive lane for bicycles
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 1] (Types of Exclusive Lanes and Vehicles Allowed to Travel in Exclusive Lanes): exclusive lanes are classified into three types: bus exclusive lanes, multi-passenger (high-occupancy) exclusive lanes, and bicycle exclusive lanes.

===== 문제 412 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following describes aquaplaning most appropriately?
① You must change tires with wider widths to reduce the risks associated with aquaplaning.
② Aquaplaning is more common in snow than rain. You must reduce speed.
③ Tires with worn-down treads will experience less aquaplaning due to reduced traction.
④ Underinflation can cause the tires to aquaplane more, especially at higher speeds.
정답: 4
해설: Wide tires, low tire pressure, and worn tread make hydroplaning more likely, while new tires reduce the occurrence of hydroplaning.

===== 문제 413 =====
유형: 문장형

① Steer into the skid.
② In the case of a car with manual transmission, shift to high gear.
③ Operate the steering wheel in the opposite direction of the skid.
④ Stop the car using the parking brake.
정답: 1
해설: When the vehicle skids on an icy road, it is safer to steer the wheel in the direction the vehicle is skidding.

===== 문제 414 =====

③ On a foggy road, switch on the fog light only.
④ When visibility is secured to some extent, drive slowly while monitoring the guard rail, centerline, and traffic lane to stay  aware of the vehicle's
정답: 4
해설: When driving on a foggy road with some visibility secured, use landmarks such as guardrails, the center line, and lane lines that allow you to determine the vehicle's position, and slow down.

===== 문제 415 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest method of stopping on a snowy/icy road?
① Apply the brake hard.
② Stop quickly using the foot brake and the parking brake at the same time.
③ Use the engine brake only to slow down until the car comes to a complete stop.
④ After slowing down with the engine brake, step lightly on the brake pedal several times.
정답: 4
해설: Because snowy or icy roads are slippery, when stopping it is safer to decelerate first with engine braking and then apply the foot brake in several short presses.

===== 문제 417 =====

① It occurs mainly in tunnels, and the road surface becomes an icy road as the fog particles freeze.
② Black ice often occurs on bridges, at tunnel entrances, and on shady roads.
③ Black ice occurs when snow contaminated with vehicle fumes accumulates on the road surface.
④ When passing through an icy road, reduce speed as much as possible by not operating the steering wheel and braking  suddenly.
정답: 2

===== 문제 418 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most inappropriate way to respond when your car has been flooded during  localized heavy rainfall?
① Try to escape by opening the door on the opposite side of the rushing currents.
② If the car door won't open, use a sharp object (such as headrest, seatbelt lock, etc.) to strike the edge of the window to  break it and attempt to es
③ If you cannot break the window, do not panic; call 911 and quickly open the door to escape when the water levels inside  and outside of the vehicle be
④ Once you have escaped, take shelter in the lowest ground or hide under the vehicle.
정답: 4
해설: Ministry of the Interior and Safety, National Disaster Safety Portal, Public Action Guidelines: Before the tires are two-thirds submerged, move the vehicle to a safe place; if it is submerged, use the metal rod of the driver's seat headrest to break the window and escape. If you cannot break the window, wait until the difference in water level between the inside and outside of the vehicle is 30 cm or less, then escape quickly the moment the door opens. When rain of 100 mm per hour falls, road signs beyond 100 meters cannot be identified, so move the vehicle to a safe place and wait until the rain weakens. If water begins to pool inside an underpass, never enter it; if you have entered, leave the vehicle and evacuate quickly. If water overtops a bridge, never enter; detour or wait in a safe place. If the vehicle is trapped, open the door on the side away from the current or break a window and escape. * A car window has a certain elasticity, so striking the center requires great force; strike a corner instead. (Korea Transportation Safety Authority official blog card news) * If the vehicle is submerged, escape quickly and evacuate to ground higher than the water, or climb onto the vehicle roof and wait for rescue. (National Fire Agency official blog card news)

===== 문제 419 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate action to take when the brakes are not working on a downhill  road?
① Turn the ignition off at once.
② Jump out of the car after shifting to low gear.
③ Reduce speed by zigzagging.
④ Run the car into a guard rail or a wall after reducing speed by shifting to low gear.
정답: 4
해설: When the brakes fail and the vehicle cannot stop, there is a high likelihood of a major accident from a rear-end collision or a collision with an oncoming vehicle. When the brakes fail, do not panic; shift to a low gear to decelerate, then bring the vehicle to a stop by scraping it against a guardrail, wall, or the like, which is the way to prevent a secondary accident.

===== 문제 420 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: If you witness a fire caused by a traffic accident while driving inside a tunnel, which of the following is the most  appropriate method of responding?
① It is safe to stop your vehicle even if traffic is still flowing.
② If traffic is at a standstill, stop the vehicle and wait inside until the fire is extinguished.
③ If traffic is at a standstill, stop the vehicle and leave the scene with your car keys.
④ If there is a lot of smoke after disembarking, lower your body as much as possible and move in the opposite direction  of the smoke following the emer
정답: 4
해설: Correct response when witnessing a fire caused by a vehicle accident, etc., while traveling through a tunnel: (1) If traffic can flow, quickly exit the tunnel. (2) Even if a fire has broken out, if visibility is secured and traffic can flow, drive the vehicle out as it is. (3) If visibility is not secured and vehicles are congested or cannot move, stop the vehicle in an emergency parking bay or on the shoulder. Turn off the engine, leave the key in place, and get out of the vehicle. Report to 119, etc., using a mobile phone or the emergency phone inside the tunnel, and check for injured persons if any. If there is heavy smoke, lower your body as much as possible and move in the direction away from the smoke, following the guidance indicator lights inside the tunnel.

===== 문제 421 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct explanation when driving on a sharp curve?
① Drive through the curve at the same speed as that before entering the curve.
② After entering a curve, it is better to increase the change gear ratio to reduce the centrifugal force.
③ A rear-wheel drive vehicle may experience understeer on a curved road.
④ In order to reduce oversteer on a curved road, the steering wheel should be turned slightly in the opposite direction of  the steering direction.
정답: 4
해설: (1) Continuing to drive at the same speed as before entering the curve risks running off the road. (2) After entering the curve, you must shift to a lower gear ratio to reduce centrifugal force. (3) On a curve, a front-wheel-drive vehicle may experience understeer. (4) To reduce oversteer on a curve, you must use countersteering, turning the wheel in the direction opposite to the steering direction to balance the vehicle.

===== 문제 422 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What do you call a problem that occurs when the brakes do not work due to bubbles that form in the brake  fluid overheated by excessive braking?
① A standing wave
② A vapor lock
③ A road holding
④ An understeering
정답: 2
해설: The phenomenon in which excessive use of the foot brake generates frictional heat that creates bubbles in the brake fluid so that braking fails is called vapor lock.

===== 문제 423 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a proper way of driving in fog?
① Honk when driving around a curve or on a hill.
② If the visibility in fog is less than 70 m, reduce speed to less than half of the speed limit.
③ Try to maximize visibility by turning on fog lamps and high-beam headlights.
④ Even in case of vehicle failure or minor accident, move the vehicle to the side of the road.
정답: 3
해설: Turning on the high beams causes the tiny water particles in fog to refract and scatter the light, which can obstruct the vision of the other driver, so it is better to keep the fog lights and low beams on.

===== 문제 424 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not a correct explanation of black ice in winter?
① It is a phenomenon wherein a thin ice film forms like a coating on the road surface.
② It is a phenomenon wherein pollutants in the air are mixed with snow and moisture on the asphalt surface, absorbed,  and then frozen into black ice.
③ It occurs mainly in places with low temperatures during the winter season, such as on bridges, tunnel entrances, shady  roads, and in the shade of mou
④ It refers to the phenomenon wherein the snow melts and seeps into the sunny road, and the black color of the road is  reflected by sunlight and sparkl
정답: 4
해설: One type of road-surface icing, expressed as black ice or clear ice, refers to a phenomenon in which a thin film of ice forms on the road surface as if coated. It includes the phenomenon in which snow and moisture seep into cracks in the asphalt, mix with soot and dust in the air, and freeze black. It mainly occurs in cold winter on shaded, low-temperature roads such as bridges, tunnel entrances and exits, shaded roads, and shaded mountain corners. Because it is not easily identified with the naked eye, the risk of accidents is very high.

===== 문제 425 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about icy roads during winter months?
① Icy roads are more common on concrete roads than on asphalt.
② Ice melts slower on asphalt roads than on concrete.
③ The friction coefficient of an asphalt surface increases to 1.6 when dry.
④ Under the same conditions, the road friction coefficients of frozen concrete and asphalt surfaces are equal.
정답: 3
해설: (1) Roads paved with concrete freeze more than those paved with asphalt. (2) Asphalt-paved roads take longer to thaw than concrete ones. (3), (4) Under identical icing conditions, the road-surface friction coefficient of both concrete- and asphalt-paved roads drops to 0.3, less than half that of a dry road surface.

===== 문제 426 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the least appropriate measure for a driver to take when an earthquake occurs?
① Increase your vehicle's speed and quickly pass through the area.
② If it is impossible to move using a vehicle, park the vehicle to the side and take shelter.
③ Be prepared to move the parked vehicle later by keeping the key in the ignition, and take shelter.
④ Turn on the radio to listen to the disaster broadcast.
정답: 1
해설: When an earthquake occurs, first turn on the radio and focus on disaster broadcasts; to keep the center of the road clear so that ambulances and police cars can use it first, pull the vehicle you were driving over to the right edge of the road and park it. In case the parked vehicle needs to be moved, leave the key in the ignition, take only minimal belongings, park the vehicle at the edge, and then evacuate. <Ministry of the Interior and Safety earthquake evacuation guidelines>

===== 문제 427 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO descriptions of the correct method of driving in strong winds or storms?
① Firmly hold the steering wheel with both hands and stay in your current lane.
② Ignore the gusting winds and speed up.
③ Park near a sign, the traffic lights, or a roadside tree.
④ Be particularly careful in areas vulnerable to strong winds, such as mountainous areas, bridges, and tunnel exits.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Strong winds and gusts easily occur in mountainous areas, high places, on bridges, and at tunnel entrances and exits, so be careful when passing through such areas. In these situations, grip the steering wheel firmly with both hands to keep your lane and reduce speed for safety. Also, since strong winds or gusts can knock down signs, traffic lights, and roadside trees, do not park nearby.

===== 문제 428 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO most appropriate explanations regarding driving on a gravel road?
① Hold the steering wheel as loosely as possible to minimize impact on the arm.
② Increase speed while driving to ensure that the wheels are in contact with the road surface as much as possible.
③ Slow down to ensure that gravel is not projected onto pedestrians or other vehicles or horses.
④ Slightly lower tire pressure is better for driving than the appropriate level of air pressure.
정답: 3, 4
해설: A gravel road has an uneven surface, and the gravel can damage tires or cause large steering movements. Keep steering movements as small as possible, reduce speed, use a low gear to maintain a steady speed; a slightly lower-than-appropriate tire pressure gives better traction and minimizes shock, which is advantageous for driving.

===== 문제 429 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Two vehicles are driving in the rain, and the rear vehicle sees the front vehicle stopping and applies the brakes.  Which of the following are NOT correct? (Select TWO)
① Hard braking can cause the vehicle to nose dive due to increased friction between the road surface and tires.
② Free running distance is increased due to reduced friction of the road surface.
③ Aquaplaning and partial braking may occur simultaneously, causing the vehicle to slide out of the lane.
④ The higher the tire wear rate, the shorter the braking distance.
정답: 2, 4
해설: (1) During sudden braking, friction between the tires and the road surface causes the vehicle to nose-dive forward. (2) On a wet road, the friction between the tires and the road surface decreases, so the braking distance lengthens. (3) Hydroplaning and uneven braking can occur, causing the steering direction to shift and the vehicle to leave its lane. (4) The more the vehicle's tires wear, the longer the braking distance.

===== 문제 430 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A vehicle is approaching near the top of an uphill. Which of the following are TWO are safe ways of driving?
① Increase engine RPM (revolutions per minute) to reduce fuel consumption.
② At the top of the uphill, be sure to stop temporarily before starting.
③ Maintain a safe distance from the front vehicle.
④ Drive in a lower gear than a higher gear.
정답: 3, 4
해설: (1) High RPM increases fuel consumption. (2) Slow down at the crest of an uphill; a complete stop is not necessarily required. (3) It is good to widen the distance from the vehicle ahead and maintain a safe distance. (4) It is better to drive in a low gear with greater engine power than in a high gear with less engine power.

===== 문제 431 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When you drive downhill, which of the following are the TWO safest methods to drive?
① Only use the foot brake because shifting gears is not necessary.
② If an emergency situation occurs, use the parking brake immediately.
③ Use the same gear you used when driving up the slope.
④ Use both the foot brake and engine brake properly.
정답: 3, 4
해설: On a downhill road, the weight of the vehicle body and the force of inertia can put excessive pressure on the foot brake, so you must use a low gear (engine braking) to reduce the pressure on the foot brake while driving.

===== 문제 432 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements is the least appropriate regarding safe driving under icy road conditions in  winter?
① When driving on roads in winter, you should check the weather information and traffic conditions in advance before  driving.
② Extra caution should be exercised when driving through tunnels and bridge sections that are vulnerable to icing.
③ If de-icing agents have been applied before and after snowfall near tunnels and bridges, it is possible to drive at the  usual speed limit safely.
④ Some city or provincial police agencies have regulations requiring the use of winter equipment when driving on snowy  or icy roads.
정답: 3
해설: (1) Safe driving is necessary through weather information and road-icing information about traffic conditions. (3) After de-icing agent is spread, the melted snow may refreeze together with snowfall, brine, etc., so you must drive at reduced speed.

===== 문제 433 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements about potholes is correct?
① Potholes generally form during torrential rain.
② Driving faster will prevent tire damage from potholes.
③ Sudden braking by larger vehicles is likely to occur near crosswalks when the temperature of the road surface increases.
④ Potholes are readily visible when the road is dry and you can just drive over them.
정답: 1
해설: A pothole is a phenomenon in which rainwater weakens the ground and creates cracks, and frequent vehicle passage causes the asphalt surface to break off, forming a hole in the road.

===== 문제 434 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following approaches to driving in heavy rainfall is the least safe?
① Drive with the headlights and taillights on.
② Turn on the heater in internal air circulation mode.
③ Check the tires for wear to prevent aquaplaning.
④ Maintain double the usual safe distance on wet roads.
정답: 2
해설: When the heater or air conditioner is set to recirculate interior air, fogging on the interior windows can worsen, so operate it in the outside-air intake mode.

===== 문제 435 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT an appropriate action by a driver while driving on a road during a typhoon  accompanied by strong winds and heavy rain?
① Maintain at least double the normal distance from the car in front, as the braking distance is longer.
② Upon passing the flooded area, it is better to pass without stopping.
③ Avoid areas with a high risk of flooding such as riversides and streams when parking.
④ It is safe to park a vehicle by a wall or under a large sign.
정답: 4
해설: Because the braking distance lengthens, maintain more than twice the usual distance from the vehicle ahead to prevent a rear-end collision. When passing through a flooded area, if you stop midway, rainwater may enter the muffler and stall the engine, so it is desirable to pass through without stopping if possible. When parking, avoid places with a high flood risk such as riverbanks and areas near streams, and park on higher ground if possible. Parking next to a wall at risk of collapse or under a large sign can also be dangerous, so avoid it. Vehicles parked in the underground space of a building where flooding is expected should be moved to a safe place.

===== 문제 436 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an INCORRECT method of driving on snowy roads?
① Just clearing the windshield of piled up snow is enough for safe driving.
② The foot brake and engine brake must be used together.
③ For safety, drive at 30km/h or below with snow chains on.
④ Maintain a longer safe distance than usual.
정답: 1
해설: It is safe to clear the snow accumulated on every part of the vehicle before driving. In particular, snow on the front hood can obstruct your view, so removing the snow from the front and then driving is even more important.

===== 문제 437 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT safe driving in rain?
① You must reduce speed to less than half of the speed limit depending on the driving condition.
② You must drive with reasonable consideration so as not to splash water on pedestrians.
③ It is not good to drive in idle creep (a state where the tires move either forward or in reverse even when the accelerator and  brake pedals are not a
④ It is safer to drive with the headlights and taillights on even during the day.
정답: 3
해설: (1) Depending on the situation, slow down by 20 percent below the speed limit on wet roads, and by 50 percent or more where visibility is within 100 meters due to heavy rain, etc. (2) To avoid splashing water on pedestrians, keep an interval of at least 1 meter while driving. (3) To prevent skidding caused by dust and impurities on the road surface mixing with rainwater in the early stage of rain, it is good to drive in a creep state where the wheels roll without pressing the accelerator or brake pedal. (4) Even when driving in the daytime, it is good to drive with the tail lights and headlights on.

===== 문제 438 =====
유형: 문장형

① Turn on the sidelights, taillights, and headlights to indicate your position to other drivers.
② Turn the high-beam headlights on to indicate your position to oncoming traffic.
③ Maintain a safe distance and reduce speed.
④ If moisture is formed on the windshield, use the wipers to secure visibility.
정답: 2
해설: Because high beams scatter due to the water particles in fog, do not turn them on; use low beams or fog lights instead.

===== 문제 439 =====

④ 100 km/h

===== 문제 445 =====

===== 문제 453 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: From a safety perspective, which of the following is the most inappropriate way of driving when passing  through a tunnel?
① Before entering the tunnel, remove your sunglasses to allow your eyes to adapt to the light, and turn on your  headlights as you would at nighttime.
② If the lanes inside the tunnel are marked with solid white lines, do not change lanes while in the tunnel.
③ Keep a safe following distance from the car ahead, and be ready for sudden stops.
④ Before entering the tunnel, check the road information at the entrance to the tunnel.
정답: 1
해설: To prevent accidents caused by dark adaptation (the phenomenon in which, when entering a dark place from a bright one, things not visible at first begin to be seen after time passes) and light adaptation (the phenomenon in which, when coming out into a bright place from a dark one, one gradually adapts to the bright light), when traveling through a tunnel slow down by 10-20 percent from the usual speed and be sure to turn on the headlights, clearance lamps, tail lights, and other lights. Also, to prevent icing and secondary accidents, secure a greater safe distance than on ordinary roads and be prepared for sudden braking.

===== 문제 454 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding braking in an emergency situation that occurs while driving is  correct?
① If aquaplaning occurs, the braking force of the brakes gets stronger.
② Utilizing an impact-absorbing barrier in an emergency situation is one way of preventing a fatal accident.
③ If the road surface is wet, the vehicle always slides straight forward upon pressing the brake suddenly.
④ ABS-equipped vehicles have longer braking distances than non-ABS vehicles.
정답: 2
해설: (1) Braking force decreases. (3) Uneven braking can cause the vehicle to skid sideways. (4) ABS reduces centrifugal force on wet roads and reduces the braking distance to some extent at a given speed.

===== 문제 455 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct safety measures to take during an earthquake?
① Drive as quickly as possible out of the area affected by the earthquake.
② Maintain a safe distance from other vehicles and pull over to the right-hand side of the road.
③ Turn off the engine if you need to get out of the car to take cover.
④ Ignore the earthquake and drive on.
정답: 2, 3
해설: Driving a car during an earthquake is very dangerous. While being sufficiently careful, avoid intersections and stop at the right side of the road, listen carefully to radio information, and if there is a police officer nearby, act according to their instructions. If you leave the vehicle to evacuate, turn off the engine and leave the key in place before evacuating.

===== 문제 458 =====

===== 문제 459 =====

② When the driver abruptly turns the steering wheel on a curved road
③ When driving on a downhill road
④ When the road surface is slippery
정답: 2, 4

Also, if the tire tread is worn, the tire pressure is too high, or the road surface is slippery, the friction between the road and the tires decreases, and the vehicle may leave the road or cross the center line.

===== 문제 460 =====
유형: 문장형

② Move to the roadside, turn off the engine, and evacuate the vehicle.

④ If you fail to extinguish the fire, call 119 and wait right next to the vehicle.
정답: 2, 3
해설: How to respond when a fire breaks out in a vehicle while driving on an expressway: 1. Move the vehicle to the shoulder. 2. Turn off the engine and get out of the vehicle quickly. 3. If early firefighting is possible, use the fire extinguisher kept in the vehicle to put out the fire. 4. If early firefighting fails, the vehicle may explode, so evacuate far away. 5. Report the vehicle fire to 119, etc.

===== 문제 461 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: At which point do lanes start decreasing in the zone requiring lane changes for safe passage due to road  construction?
① Starting point of a warning zone
② Starting point of a transition zone
③ Starting point of a work zone
④ Starting point of a termination zone
정답: 2
해설: A road construction site consists of the warning, transition (merge), work, and termination sections. Among these, the transition section is where the number of lanes decreases and a lane change is required. For safe passage, changing lanes in advance and slowing down are essential.

===== 문제 462 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the MOST correct description regarding driving at night?
① It is possible to secure a wide view at night if you have the front window tinted.
② On a clear day, the braking distance is longer during daytime than at night.
③ It is better to secure a front view when driving at night with the fog light on rather than headlights.
④ If you look at the light from the opposite vehicle directly, it may be hard to secure a front view due to the glare.
정답: 4
해설: To prevent the evaporation phenomenon (a glare-induced effect in which an object such as a pedestrian becomes invisible in the oncoming headlight glare), do not look directly at the light of the oncoming vehicle.

===== 문제 463 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a correct statement regarding the glare effect (cases wherein you are unable to see  ahead due to the glare from the vehicle’s headlights) during nighttime?
① The glare effect will disappear if you turn off your headlights immediately when it occurs.
② The glare effect frequently occurs when two vehicles facing each other both have their high-beam headlights on.
③ The glare effect tends to occur when driving through busy downtown streets at nighttime.
④ The glare effect refers to the condition of momentary invisibility caused by bright lights upon entering a tunnel at  nighttime.
정답: 2
해설: The evaporation phenomenon (in which an object such as a pedestrian becomes invisible in the glare between two vehicles) occurs when both oncoming vehicles have their high beams on.

===== 문제 464 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding a driver’s "Hypovigilance in driving" at nighttime is correct?
① Cognitive ability is better than usual.
② Eye movements become more active.
③ It often occurs when driving on congested roads in a city.
④ The eyes become used to a monotonous view and experience a type of sensory paralysis.
정답: 4
해설: Night driving and reduced alertness: at night the field of vision is limited to the range illuminated by the headlights, so compared with daytime only the road surface and the tail lights of the vehicle ahead are visible, making the field of vision very monotonous. As a result, the driver unconsciously grows accustomed to the monotonous view and falls into a kind of sensory-numbness state. When this happens, eye movement inevitably becomes less active and the reaction to stimuli becomes duller. As this phenomenon intensifies, the responses of muscles and brain waves also decline, gradually reaching a drowsy state. Driving in such a state of reduced alertness is called so-called 'reduced-alertness driving.'

===== 문제 465 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a measure to be taken by a driver when the sun begins to set and it becomes  dark?
① Turn on the sidelights and taillights.
② Drive slower than you would during the day.
③ The eyes need some time to adjust to the darkness after the sun sets.
④ Driving is easier compared to daytime driving because of greater visibility.
정답: 4
해설: Because securing a field of vision is harder than in the daytime, caution is needed.

① It should have separate polarity and grounding electrode as it has a structure that cannot be replaced with other wiring  equipment.
② The grounding electrode should have a structure that is connected last when starting and separated first upon shutoff.
③ It should have a mechanical device for locking or an attachment/detachment to prevent unintended load shedding.
④ It should have an interlock function that suspends the power supply of charging cable when the connector of an EV is  separated from the connection po
정답: 2

===== 문제 467 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the best method of preventing vehicle fires?
① Alter the wiring arrangement to install an amplifier in the vehicle.
② Avoid generating static electricity while filling up in wintertime.
③ LPG vehicles should carry a disposable butane gas tank in preparation for an emergency.
④ A lighter can be stored inside the vehicle in summer.
정답: 2
해설: You must not tamper with the wiring at will, and keeping a disposable butane gas canister inside the vehicle is dangerous. A disposable lighter has no explosion-prevention device, so leaving it inside the car in summer is dangerous.

===== 문제 468 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the best method of preventing the risk of collision caused by the sudden braking of a  vehicle ahead?
① Step on the brake pedal to slow down.
② Turn the steering wheel sharply to the left and drive over the centerline to avoid hitting the vehicle ahead.
③ Close your eyes to minimize the impact.
④ Turn on both the wipers and the high-beam headlights.
정답: 1
해설: To prevent a rear-end collision with the vehicle ahead, secure a sufficient safe distance and be ready to brake at any time in preparation for danger. In case a collision becomes unavoidable, press the brake pedal firmly to decelerate and minimize the damage. Turning the steering wheel sharply to the left and crossing the center line can cause an accident with a vehicle traveling in the opposite direction. Also, closing your eyes or operating the wipers or high beams has nothing to do with reducing the damage of a rear-end collision.

===== 문제 469 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are TWO phenomena caused by the tires of a vehicle driving at high speed?
① Vapor lock
② Standing wave
③ Fading
④ Aquaplaning
정답: 2, 4
해설: If a high-speed vehicle has insufficient tire pressure, standing wave occurs; and if a high-speed vehicle passes over a pool of water with worn tires, hydroplaning occurs. Fade is a phenomenon in which, when the brake pedal is used excessively, the friction coefficient of the pads and linings drops, worsening braking force and causing slipping. Vapor lock and fade are phenomena that appear due to problems in the braking system.

===== 문제 470 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding wearing a seat belt is correct?
① Fix the seat belt above the waist to protect against the shock from a traffic accident.
② Firefighters who are dispatched to suppress fire need not wear a seat belt.
③ It is safest for children to take the front seat and wear a seat belt.
④ If a driver does not ensure that children under the age of 13 wear a seat belt, an administrative fine of KRW 30,000 is  imposed.
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6]: where the passenger is under 13 years old, an administrative fine of 60,000 won. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 31 (Grounds for Not Wearing a Seat Belt) Item 4: when an emergency vehicle is being operated for its original purpose.

===== 문제 471 =====

① Distance of the seat from the pedals
② Angle of the backrest
③ Height of the headrest
④ Seat height
정답: 3
해설: Drivers often do not adjust the headrest when adjusting their seat. Therefore, the headrest, which can hold the head in place upon impact in a traffic accident, also needs to be adjusted to fit your own head.

===== 문제 472 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding safe driving in a tunnel is correct?
① Turn on the left-turn signal and use the left lane for overtaking.

③ When entering the tunnel, be careful of the light adaptation.
④ At the exit of a tunnel, dark adaptation occurs.
정답: 2
해설: Intersections, bridges, tunnels, and the like are places where overtaking is prohibited; dark adaptation occurs when entering a tunnel; and where the road marking is a white dotted line, a lane change is possible.

===== 문제 473 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about using turn signals?
① It is not a violation to change lanes without signaling if it does not obstruct the other vehicles.
② You must keep the turn signal on for a while after changing lanes.
③ The traffic penalty for failure to signal lane change for passenger vehicles and buses is a fine of KRW 30,000.
④ When changing lanes on an expressway, turn on the turn signal from 30 m before and keep it on until you have completed  changing lanes.
정답: 3
해설: (1) Changing course without signaling is subject to a traffic-law violation for failure to signal, even if it did not obstruct other traffic. (2) After the lane change is complete, you must stop signaling immediately. (3) Failure to signal when changing lanes subjects passenger cars, passenger vans, etc., to a fine of 30,000 won. (4) On an expressway, signal from a point 100 meters before the lane change until the change is complete.

===== 문제 474 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: In which of the following situations is a driver allowed to overtake a vehicle?
① When the vehicle ahead is overtaking another vehicle
② When the vehicle ahead stops or it is driving slowly to prevent a dangerous situation
③ When the vehicle ahead is driving next to another vehicle in the left lane
④ When the vehicle ahead is driving slowly to secure a safe distance from another vehicle
정답: 4
해설: The driver of any vehicle may not overtake the vehicle ahead where another vehicle is running alongside the vehicle ahead on its left, or where the vehicle ahead is overtaking or intends to overtake another vehicle. (Road Traffic Act Articles 21 and 22)

===== 문제 475 =====
유형: 문장형

① There is no speed limit applied to a vehicle overtaking another one.
② A vehicle overtaking another vehicle can exceed the legal speed limit of the road by 50%.
③ Vehicles are allowed to overtake other vehicles regardless of the speed limit, depending on the driver's driving skill.
④ Overtaking other vehicles is allowed only when performed within the speed limit of the road.
정답: 4
해설: A vehicle intending to overtake another vehicle may overtake only within the maximum speed of that road.

===== 문제 476 =====

③ Motor vehicles can idle on the toll road to pay their tolls.
④ Only emergency vehicles such as police cars and fire trucks can idle or park at the emergency parking area inside a tunnel.
정답: 4

===== 문제 477 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When you sense danger while driving and step on the brake pedal, which TWO will lead to the longest stopping  distance? (i.e., the distance from the point where you press the brake pedal to the point
① Relatively light vehicle weight
② Relatively higher speed
③ Right after installing new tires
④ Fatigue or drunk driving
정답: 2, 4
해설: The so-called stopping distance of a vehicle, from when the driver perceives danger and presses the brake pedal until the vehicle actually stops, lengthens when driving while fatigued or drunk, the heavier the vehicle's weight or the faster the speed, and the more severe the tire wear.

===== 문제 478 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct statements regarding the number of passengers in a vehicle?
① On expressways, it is not allowed to carry more people than the defined number of passengers for the vehicle.
② The number of passengers specified on the vehicle registration certificate is the number of passengers excluding the  driver.
③ When permission is obtained from the head of the police station having jurisdiction over the place of departure, the  vehicle may operate in excess of
④ If the number of passengers is exceeded, permission must be obtained from the head of the police station having  jurisdiction over the destination.
정답: 1, 3
해설: The driver of any vehicle must not carry passengers or load beyond the operational safety standards regarding the number of passengers, load weight, and load capacity, and drive. However, this does not apply where permission has been obtained from the chief of the police station having jurisdiction over the point of departure. (Road Traffic Act Article 39)

===== 문제 479 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When a vehicle driving in front brakes suddenly due to a traffic accident ahead, which of the following are the  TWO safest measures to avoid colliding with the vehicle?
① Maintain a longer distance from the vehicle ahead than the stopping distance.
② Turn on the emergency flashers and follow an emergency vehicle.
③ Reduce speed and maintain sufficient distance from the vehicle ahead to prevent a collision.
④ When there is danger, use both the foot brake and parking brake to shorten the braking distance.
정답: 1, 3
해설: Maintain more than the stopping distance from the vehicle ahead, decelerate sufficiently so as not to rear-end it, and secure a safe distance. (Road Traffic Act Article 19)

===== 문제 480 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are true about wearing seat belts? (Select TWO)
① The penalty for a driver failing to wear a seat belt is an administrative fine of KRW 30,000.
② Whiplash injuries are more common with two-point seat belts.
③ The penalty for a child under the age of 13 not wearing a seat belt is a traffic penalty of KRW 60,000.
④ There are two, three, and four-point seat belts.
정답: 2, 4
해설: (1) If the driver does not wear a seat belt, a fine of 30,000 won is imposed. (2) Whiplash injury to the neck generally occurs more with a two-point belt. (3) If a child under 13 does not wear a seat belt, an administrative fine of 60,000 won is imposed. (4) Seat belts are classified as two-point, three-point, and four-point according to how they are worn.

===== 문제 481 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct statements regarding wearing a seat belt?
① You need not wear a seat belt when driving to a place close by.
② Passengers in a car should wear a seat belt for safety.
③ It is safe to make a child sit in the passenger seat so that the parents can help the child wear a seat belt.
④ Except when there is an emergency, the driver of an emergency vehicle should also wear a seat belt.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Vehicle occupants must wear seat belts for safety, and except when responding to an urgent duty, the driver of an emergency vehicle must also wear a seat belt without fail. (Road Traffic Act Article 50)

===== 문제 482 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A person seems to be suffering from a fractured spine after an accident. Which of the following is the most  appropriate measure you can take?
① Check if the person is conscious and perform cardiopulmonary resuscitation immediately.
② Move the person to a safe place and call 119.
③ Apply the Heimlich maneuver to prevent upper airway obstruction.
④ Unless urgent, do not move the person. If moving is inevitable, use a spine splint and assist the person in moving to a safe  place.
정답: 4
해설: Where there is a patient in a traffic accident with a suspected spinal fracture, do not transport them unless it is an emergency. This is because, if appropriate treatment is not given before transport, there is a great risk of worsening irreversible neurological damage. Therefore, if transport becomes unavoidable to prevent risks such as a secondary accident, first immobilize the spine using a splint and then evacuate them to a safe place as a priority.

===== 문제 483 =====
유형: 문장형
