① Check his/her pulse.
② Try to start a conversation or gently pat his/her shoulders.
③ Find the location of any wound that is bleeding heavily.
④ Look into the mouth to check if anything is blocking the airway.
정답: 2
해설: To check the state of consciousness, methods include speaking to the injured person, lightly tapping their shoulder, or pinching their arm.

===== 문제 484 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which TWO vehicles are allowed to continue driving while asking a passenger to take actions during a traffic  accident?
① A passenger car that is carrying an injured person to a hospital
② A fire truck returning to the fire station after extinguishing a fire
③ A tow vehicle going to the scene of a traffic accident
④ A postal vehicle transporting mail.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 54 (Measures at the Time of an Accident) Paragraph 5: the driver of an emergency vehicle, a vehicle carrying an injured person, a mail vehicle, a tram, or the like may, in an emergency, have a passenger or the like take accident measures or report to the police, and may continue driving.

===== 문제 485 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following are the TWO correct statements regarding a vehicle  that is allowed to continue driving despite a traffic accident?
① The ambulance driver who was transporting an urgent patient had the passenger take the necessary measures and kept  driving.
② The driver who was traveling to attend an urgent meeting had the passenger take the necessary actions and kept  driving.
③ The driver of the vehicle transporting urgent mail made the passenger take the necessary measures and kept driving.
④ The ambulance driver who was transporting urgent medicines had the passengers take the necessary actions and kept  driving.
정답: 1, 3
해설: The driver of an emergency vehicle, a vehicle transporting an injured person, a mail-carrying vehicle, or the like may, in an emergency, have a passenger take the necessary measures or make the report, and may continue driving. (Road Traffic Act Article 54 Paragraph 5)

===== 문제 487 =====

④ Korea Transportation Safety Authority
정답: 2
해설: The emergency towing service of the Korea Expressway Corporation (call center 1588-2504) is a system that, free of charge, tows a vehicle stopped on the expressway due to an accident or breakdown and at risk of a secondary accident to a safe area such as the nearest rest area, business office, or drowsiness rest stop.

===== 문제 488 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a proper way to respond after a road kill?
① Do not touch the animal carcass to prevent exposure to potential diseases.
② Call any wildlife rescue center or a local government call center (area code + 120).
③ Load the animal carcass on your vehicle to prevent a secondary accident.
④ There is no need to file a report if necessary measures have been taken to prevent a secondary accident and facilitate   traffic flow.
정답: 3
해설: According to the Korea Disease Control and Prevention Agency, an animal carcass carries a risk of infection, so you must not touch it directly; when an accident occurs, report it to the local government or the road management office, and the call center at area code +120 (living-information counseling service) to get help. Since an animal that was in an accident is legally treated as an object, you do not have to report it if you have prevented danger and ensured smooth traffic flow to avoid a secondary accident.

===== 문제 489 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about anger control techniques for preventing road rage and car crashes?
① An anger control technique that is a mindfulness-based practice designed to help you defuse stress is called the STOP  technique.
② An anger control technique during which you try to push out the negative thoughts and focus on happy thoughts for  1–2 minutes is called the time-out
③ An anger control technique during which you question your passive recognition of the belief system and distorted  thoughts that trigger anger is calle
④ An anger control technique during which you repeatedly contract and relax your arms, legs, abdomen, chest, shoulders,  and other body parts is called
정답: 4
해설: Traffic Safety Guidelines, 2021 revised 2nd edition: behavioral techniques for controlling anger include the timeout technique, the stop-button technique, and the relaxation-training technique. (1) The anger-control method of briefly stepping out of a situation where emotions boil over, taking time, and finding peace of mind is called the timeout technique. (2) The anger-control method of stopping the negative thoughts that provoke anger and recalling and focusing on a happy scene you have thought of in advance for 1-2 minutes is called the stop-button technique. (3) The experience-recall questioning technique is not an anger-control method. (4) The method of repeatedly tensing each part of the body such as the arms, legs, lower abdomen, chest, and shoulders as much as possible and then relaxing to reach a comfortable state is called the relaxation-training technique.

===== 문제 490 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest method of driving when the underground road ahead is submerged in  water due to heavy rainfall?
① Drive through slowly. You can make it as long as the wheels are not completely submerged in water.
② Get through the water as fast as you can.
③ Take another road to bypass the water.
④ Drive through. If the engine stops, start it back immediately and drive through.
정답: 3
해설: If, due to heavy rain, an underpass is flooded so that water reaches up to the vehicle's bumper or more than half the height of the wheels, the vehicle cannot pass. Also, when passing through such an area at high speed, the vehicle pushes the water and raises the water level in front, which may allow water to enter the engine. If the engine stalls in a flooded area, restarting it will ruin the engine.

===== 문제 491 =====

① Check the risk.
② Check the response of a patient.
③ Secure the airway and check the breathing.
④ Check the destination and identification of a patient.
정답: 4
해설: When an emergency occurs, check for danger and observe the patient's response, request help from those nearby, secure the airway as needed so the patient can breathe, and call for rescue.

===== 문제 495 =====

질문: Which of the following constitutes grounds for revocation of a driver's license?
① When 6 months have lapsed after the expiration date of the regular aptitude test
② When a motorist is booked without detention for assaulting a law enforcement officer (policeman or local government  official)
③ When a motorist drives his/her car without affixing a license plate after registration
④ When 2 years have lapsed without renewal of a Class II driver’s license for an ordinary motor vehicle
정답: 2
해설: (1) One year. (2) Where a person is criminally booked (placed under investigation), it is a ground for revocation. (3) The driver's license is revoked where a person drives a vehicle that is not registered under the Motor Vehicle Management Act or has not obtained temporary operation permission; but driving a registered vehicle without attaching the registration plate is not a ground for license revocation. (Road Traffic Act Article 93)

===== 문제 496 =====

③ Original traffic penalty + 30% late penalty
④ Original traffic penalty + 40% late penalty
정답: 2
해설: A person who has not paid the fine within the payment period must pay, within 20 days from the day after the payment period expires, the notified fine plus 20/100. (Road Traffic Act Article 164 Paragraph 2)

===== 문제 497 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct standard for the revocation of a driver's license based on cumulative  demerit points?
① 100+ points within 1 year
② 191+ points within 2 years
③ 271+ points within 3 years
④ 301+ points within 5 years
정답: 3
해설: The license is revoked at 121 or more points in 1 year, 201 or more points in 2 years, or 271 or more points in 3 years. (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28])

===== 문제 498 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the standards for demerit points based on the effects of a traffic  accident is correct?
① As for the personal injury of the driver who will receive an administrative punishment, demerit points will be imposed  based on the rules regarding t
② In an accident between a car and a pedestrian, demerit points will not be imposed if the accident occurred due to  mistakes committed by both parties.
③ If the accident was unavoidable or had been caused clearly by a mistake of the injured, demerit points will be halved.
④ In an accident between cars, demerit points are imposed only on the driver who committed a serious violation that  caused the accident.
정답: 4
해설: In case (1), penalty points are not assessed for the harm to the driver who is to receive the administrative disposition themselves. In case (2), it is reduced by one half. In case (3), no penalty points are imposed. (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28])

===== 문제 499 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following statements regarding the administrative fine for  parking violations is correct?
① The owner of a passenger car involved in a parking violation should pay an administrative fine of KRW 30,000.
② The owner of a van involved in a parking violation should pay an administrative fine of KRW 70,000.
③ No administrative fine is imposed even if you do not pay the fine within the designated period.
④ For parking violations that continue for more than two hours at the same location, additional administrative fines are  imposed.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 33, Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6]: for a parking-prohibition violation, an administrative fine of 40,000 won for a passenger car and 50,000 won for a passenger van is imposed; for parking violations of 2 hours or more, an additional 10,000 won is added, and if unpaid, a surcharge and a heavy surcharge are imposed.

===== 문제 500 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a scenario where the exemption according to the Act on Special Cases Concerning the  Settlement of Traffic Accidents is applied because the driver who caused the accident has
① When the driver has violated his/her duty to drive safely, resulting in car damage or minor accident
② When the driver has caused an accident that resulted in loss of human life
③ When the driver has fled the scene after causing an accident without attending to the injured
④ When the driver has violated traffic signals, causing an accident involving minor injuries
정답: 1
해설: Act on Special Cases concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents Article 4.

===== 문제 501 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Seven people riding together in four vehicles on the road and causing harm to others were criminally charged  according to the Road Traffic Act. Which of the following is NOT true about their punishme
① Imprisonment with labor for not more than 2 years or criminal fine of not more than KRW 5 million
② Immediate license suspension upon apprehension
③ License revocation if detained
④ A penalty of 40 points if prosecuted
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 46 (Prohibition of Joint Dangerous Acts) Paragraph 1: the drivers of vehicles, etc., where two or more of them jointly drive two or more vehicles, etc., in a row front-to-back or side-to-side on the road without just cause, must not harm others or cause a traffic hazard. Road Traffic Act Article 150 (Penal Provisions) Item 1: imprisonment for up to 2 years or a fine of up to 5 million won. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28]: license revocation upon detention, and 40 penalty points upon criminal charges.

===== 문제 502 =====

① Revocation of the driver’s license
② 100-day suspension of the driver’s license
③ 60-day suspension of the driver’s license
④ 40-day suspension of the driver’s license

===== 문제 503 =====

① No punishment
② Notification of a traffic penalty of KRW 30,000
③ Punished through an administrative fine of KRW 40,000
④ Punished through a criminal fine of not more than KRW 100,000 or by detention
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 8], No. 64-2: a fine of 30,000 won for riding a bicycle while intoxicated.

===== 문제 504 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, what is the punishment for a bicycle rider who is deemed to be intoxicated  and who refuses a police officer's lawful request for a field sobriety test?
① No punishment
② Punished through an administrative fine of KRW 70,000
③ Notification of a traffic penalty of KRW 100,000
④ Punished through a criminal fine of not more than KRW 100,000 or by detention
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 8]: where a bicycle rider with substantial grounds to be deemed intoxicated refuses a police officer's breath test, the penalty fine is 100,000 won.

===== 문제 505 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO situations subject to criminal prosecution in accordance with the Act on  Special Cases Concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents?
① A driver without comprehensive insurance reaches a settlement with the victim after causing an accident resulting in  property damage.
② A taxi driver who is a member of the Taxi Mutual Aid Association causes a traffic accident resulting in injury or death  after driving over the center
③ A driver with comprehensive insurance causes a traffic accident resulting in injury or death after committing a violation  of a traffic signal.
④ A truck covered by cooperative insurance causes injury due to careless driving.
정답: 2, 3
해설: A person who causes a traffic accident with personal injury by crossing the center line or violating a signal is punished even if they have subscribed to comprehensive insurance or a mutual-aid association. (Act on Special Cases concerning the Settlement of Traffic Accidents Articles 3 and 4)

===== 문제 506 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: You fail to pay a traffic penalty for which you have been notified in writing even by its second deadline. Which  TWO are applicable?
① You must request a summary judgment immediately.
② Your license will be suspended for 40 days if no summary judgment is reached.
③ An administrative fine will be charged.
④ No summary judgment needs to be requested if you pay the traffic penalty along with an additional 30% as delay  penalty.
정답: 1, 2
해설: Where a person who received a fine payment notice fails to meet the second payment period, an immediate ruling (summary judgment) must be requested without delay. If the immediate ruling is not obtained, the driver's license is suspended for 40 days. If the person pays the fine amount plus 50/100, no immediate ruling is requested. (Road Traffic Act Article 165)

===== 문제 507 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What will happen if the driver of a passenger car causes a traffic accident that damages only parked or stopped  vehicles and, even upon becoming aware of the situation, fails to provide personal info
① No punishment
② An administrative fine of KRW 100,000 is imposed.
③ A traffic penalty of KRW 120,000 is notified.
④ A criminal fine of up to KRW 300,000 or detention is imposed.
정답: 3
해설: A person who causes a traffic accident damaging only a parked or stopped vehicle and does not provide the victim with their personal information faces, under Road Traffic Act Article 156 (Penal Provisions), a punishment of a fine of up to 200,000 won, detention, or a minor fine. In practice, under Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 8], a passenger car, etc., is subject to a notified penalty fine of 120,000 won.

===== 문제 508 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, what is the correct penalty for a person who drives a passenger car with a  blood alcohol concentration (BAC) of 0.03 percent or higher but less than 0.08 percent (f
① Imprisonment for up to 1 year or criminal fine of up to KRW 5 million
② Imprisonment for up to 2 years or criminal fine of up to KRW 10 million
③ Imprisonment for up to 3 years or criminal fine of up to KRW 15 million
④ Imprisonment in the range of 2 years - 5 years or criminal fine of KRW 10 million or more (up to KRW 20 million)
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 148-2 (Penal Provisions) Paragraph 3: a person who, in violation of Article 44 Paragraph 1, drives a vehicle, etc., or a tram while intoxicated is punished as follows. 1. A person with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.2 percent or more: imprisonment for 2 to 5 years or a fine of 10 million to 20 million won. 2. A person with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.08 percent or more but less than 0.2 percent: imprisonment for 1 to 2 years or a fine of 5 million to 10 million won. 3. A person with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.03 percent or more but less than 0.08 percent: imprisonment for up to 1 year or a fine of up to 5 million won.

===== 문제 510 =====

① 0.03
② 0.05
③ 0.08
④ 0.10

해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 29]: where the holder of a learner's driver's license drives with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.03 percent or higher, the learner's license is revoked.

===== 문제 511 =====
유형: 문장형

① 40 demerit points
② 100 demerit points
③ Revocation of the driver’s license
④ Request for a summary trial
정답: 3
해설: The driver's license is revoked where a person assaults an enforcing police officer, etc., or a Si/Gun/Gu public official and is criminally booked (placed under investigation). (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28], 2. Individual Standards for Revocation Dispositions, No. 16)

===== 문제 512 =====
유형: 문장형

① 10 points
② 20 points
③ 30 points
④ 40 points
정답: 4
해설: A driver who apprehends, or reports and thereby causes the apprehension of, the driver of a vehicle that caused a traffic accident with personal injury and fled (limited to cases where the reporter is not the victim of the traffic accident) is granted 40 bonus points per apprehension or report; and if that reporting driver comes to receive a suspension or revocation disposition, these points are deducted from the accumulated points regardless of the period. In this case, the points deducted are in units of 40. (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28], 1. General Standards)

===== 문제 515 =====

===== 문제 516 =====

===== 문제 517 =====

===== 문제 518 =====

===== 문제 519 =====

③ 3 years

해설: Paragraph 2: the Korea Road Traffic Authority may determine whether the aptitude test standards under each item of Paragraph 1 are met using the following documents. 1. Any of the following documents issued within 2 years from the date of the driver's license test application: (a) a physical examination report issued by a clinic, hospital, or general hospital; (b) a health checkup result notice under Article 52 of the National Health Insurance Act; (c) a medical certificate issued by a doctor; (d) a military service physical examination (including the physical examination for active-duty volunteers) result notice.

===== 문제 523 =====
유형: 문장형

② Within 90 days of becoming aware of the revocation
③ Within 60 days of the revocation
④ Within 60 days of becoming aware of the revocation
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 94: a person who objects to a driver's license revocation or suspension disposition, or a learner's license revocation disposition, may file an objection with the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency within 60 days from the day the disposition was received.

===== 문제 524 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When a person holding a student license practices driving on a road, which of the following is the disposition  given to the driver if he/she drives by him/herself without being accompanied by a perso
① Disposition of notification
② Imposition of an administrative fine
③ Suspension of the student license
④ Revocation of the student license
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 55 (Matters to Be Observed by a Holder of a Learner's Driver's License), [Table 29]: the learner's license is revoked where a person violates the matters to be observed for a learner's license (they must ride together with, and receive the guidance of, a person who has held a driver's license enabling them to drive the vehicle to be practiced for 2 years or more from the date of obtaining it).

===== 문제 525 =====

질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is the correct punishment  if a driver drives a personal mobility device (PMD) without receiving a driver's license

② A criminal fine of up to KRW 300,000 or detention
③ A criminal fine of up to KRW 500,000 or detention
④ Up to 6 months' imprisonment or criminal fine of up to KRW 2 million
정답: 1
해설: Where a person drives a personal mobility device without a license, the punishment standard under Road Traffic Act Article 156 (Penal Provisions) is a fine of up to 200,000 won, detention, or a minor fine. In practice, under Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 8], a notified penalty fine of 100,000 won is imposed.

===== 문제 526 =====

===== 문제 527 =====

===== 문제 528 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding a reduction of demerit points through an accident-free and  violation-free driving pledge (Good Driving Mileage System) is correct?
① Special points of 40 points are issued.
② Two years without any traffic accidents or violations are required to earn special points.
③ Except in certain cases, if a driver receives a suspension order, special benefit points are deducted from his or her  accumulated points.
④ A driver must visit a driver licensing office and submit a written pledge.
정답: 3
해설: 10 bonus points are granted only if there have been no traffic accidents or legal violations for one year. The pledge can be submitted not only by visiting a police office but also online (www.efine.go.kr). (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28], Paragraph 1 General Standards, (b) Comprehensive Management of Penalty Points.) However, cases where a person causes a death by a traffic accident, drunk driving, reckless driving, a crime using a vehicle, etc., or stealing or robbing a vehicle are excluded.

===== 문제 530 =====

③ A driver who completed proactive driving training
④ A driver whose license is suspended for reckless driving
정답: 4

===== 문제 531 =====

② KRW 50,000
③ KRW 60,000
④ KRW 70,000
정답: 2

===== 문제 532 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a valid reason for revocation of a driver's license?
① Failing to take the regular aptitude test for one year
② Being arrested for retaliatory driving
③ Driving twice at a speed exceeding the speed limit by 100 km/h.
④ Using vehicles to kidnap, induce, or detain another person
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 93 (Revocation/Suspension of a Driver's License): where a person drives a vehicle, etc., three or more times at a speed exceeding the maximum speed by more than 100 km/h.

===== 문제 533 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the penalty for a driver who, upon being stopped for drink driving, and who is known to have refused a  breathalyzer test 3 years previously, refuses to comply once again?
① Imprisonment with labor for at least 1 year but not more than 6 years, or criminal fine of at least KRW 5 million but not  more than KRW 30 million
② Imprisonment with labor for at least 2 years but not more than 6 years, or criminal fine of at least KRW 5 million but  not more than KRW 20 million
③ Imprisonment with labor for at least 3 years but not more than 5 years, or criminal fine of KRW at least 10 million but  not more than KRW 30 million
④ Imprisonment with labor for at least 1 year but not more than 5 years, or criminal fine of at least KRW 5 million but not  more than KRW 20 million
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 148-2 (Penal Provisions) Paragraph 1: a person who, having been sentenced to a fine or heavier punishment for a violation of Article 44 Paragraph 1 or 2 (limited to a person who drove a vehicle, etc., or a tram; however, the case of driving a personal mobility device is excluded) and whose sentence became final, again violates the same Article Paragraph 1 or 2 within 10 years from the day the sentence became final (including a person whose sentence has lapsed) is punished as follows: 1. A person who violated Article 44 Paragraph 2: imprisonment for 1 to 6 years or a fine of 5 million to 30 million won. 2. Among those who violated Article 44 Paragraph 1, a person with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.2 percent or more: imprisonment for 2 to 6 years or a fine of 10 million to 30 million won. 3. Among those who violated Article 44 Paragraph 1, a person with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.03 percent or more but less than 0.2 percent: imprisonment for 1 to 5 years or a fine of 5 million to 20 million won.

===== 문제 534 =====

해설: Road Traffic Act Article 148-2 (Penal Provisions) Paragraph 3: a person who, in violation of Article 44 Paragraph 1, drives a vehicle, etc., or a tram while intoxicated is punished as follows.

===== 문제 535 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following are the demerit points  given to an automobile driver who flees after causing a traffic accident involving property
① 15 points
② 20 points
③ 30 points
④ 40 points
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28]: under the penalty-point standards for failure to take measures, etc., 15 penalty points are imposed for fleeing after causing a traffic accident that resulted in property damage.

===== 문제 536 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is the traffic penalty  imposed if measures are not taken to prevent freight from falling off a 4.5-ton truck?

② KRW 40,000
③ KRW 30,000
④ KRW 20,000
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 39 Paragraph 4, Enforcement Decree [Table 8]: the act of a freight vehicle with a loading capacity exceeding 4 tons violating the prevention of load falling is subject to a penalty fine of 50,000 won.

===== 문제 537 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is true about using  designated lanes?
① A passenger vehicle carrying 2 or more persons can use the high-occupancy vehicle lane.
② A 9-seater passenger vehicle carrying 6 or more persons can use the exclusive bus lane on an expressway.
③ A 12-seater bus carrying 5 or more persons can use the exclusive bus lane on an expressway.
④ A 16-seater personal vehicle can use all exclusive bus lanes found on roads other than expressways.
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 1]: (1) At least 3 persons must be aboard. (3) There must be at least 6 occupants. (4) Passage is possible only if it is a commercial passenger van, or it meets conditions such as being designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency for school-commuting or work-commuting use. Large passenger vans of 36 seats or more, commercial passenger vans of fewer than 36 seats, and children's school buses that have received a report certificate may travel in a bus exclusive lane on roads other than expressways.

===== 문제 550 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following are the TWO correct  statements regarding the meaning of traffic signals?
① Flashing yellow light: Vehicles and horses can proceed while being careful of other forms of traffic and safety signs.
② Red light: Pedestrians can cross at the crosswalk with caution.
③ Green arrow-shaped light: Vehicles and horses can proceed in the direction that the arrow points.
④ Yellow light: If vehicles and horses have already entered an intersection, they should stop within the intersection.
정답: 1, 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 2]: - Pedestrian signal, red light: pedestrians must not cross the crosswalk. - Vehicle signal, yellow light: vehicles or horses must stop before the stop line if there is one, or before the crosswalk or intersection; but if any part of the vehicle or horse has already entered the intersection, it must proceed out of the intersection quickly.

===== 문제 551 =====

① Drilling equipment (truck-mounted)
② A road stabilizer
③ A bicycle
④ A stroller
정답: 1, 2
해설: Under Road Traffic Act Article 2 and the proviso of Construction Machinery Management Act Article 26 Paragraph 1, among construction machinery, dump trucks, asphalt spreaders, road stabilizers, concrete mixer trucks, concrete pumps, and boring machines (truck-mounted) are included in the definition of motor vehicles.

===== 문제 552 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following are TWO INCORRECT statements regarding the  punishment of a driver (excluding personal mobility device)?
① A person who drives under the influence of alcohol and whose blood alcohol concentration is 0.2% or more – At least  1 year to 2 years of imprisonment
② A person who committed risky behavior jointly - Up to 2 years of imprisonment or criminal fine of up to KRW 5 million
③ A person who engaged in reckless driving - Up to 1 year of imprisonment or criminal fine of up to KRW 5 million
④ Driving a motorcycle without a license – A criminal fine of up to KRW 500,000 or detention
정답: 1, 4
해설: (1) Drunk driving with a blood alcohol concentration of 0.2% or more (first violation) - imprisonment for 2 to 5 years or a fine of 10 million to 20 million won. (2) Joint dangerous acts - imprisonment for up to 2 years or a fine of up to 5 million won. (3) Reckless driving - imprisonment for up to 1 year or a fine of up to 5 million won. (4) Driving a motorized bicycle without a license - a fine of up to 300,000 won or detention.

===== 문제 553 =====
유형: 문장형

② A person who has driven a motor vehicle and who commits an act of obstructing the alcohol test may be punished by  imprisonment for up to 1 year or cr

정답: 2, 4

Article 148-2 (Penal Provisions) Paragraph 2: any of the following persons is punished by imprisonment for 1 to 5 years or a fine of 5 million to 20 million won. 2. A person with substantial grounds to be deemed intoxicated who, in violation of Article 44 Paragraph 5, drove a vehicle, etc., or a tram and then committed an act of obstructing an alcohol test. Article 156 (Penal Provisions): any of the following persons is punished by a fine of up to 200,000 won, detention, or a minor fine. 12-2. A person with substantial grounds to be deemed intoxicated who, in violation of Article 44 Paragraph 5, rode a bicycle, etc., and then committed an act of obstructing an alcohol test.

===== 문제 557 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following cannot be regarded as a mobility aid chair vehicle (i.e.  a vehicle with a width of no more than 1 meter)?

② An electric wheelchair
③ A mobility scooter
④ An electric bicycle
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 2 (Devices and Apparatus Excluded from Vehicles or Horses (車馬)) Paragraph 1: the term 'baby strollers, walking-aid wheelchairs, walkers for the elderly and infirm, and other devices and apparatus prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety' in Article 2 Item 10 and Item 17(a)5) of the 'Road Traffic Act' (hereinafter 'the Act') means the following devices and apparatus that are 1 meter or less in width. 1. Baby strollers. 2. Walking-aid wheelchairs (manual wheelchairs, electric wheelchairs, and medical scooters that meet the standard specifications for medical devices set by the Minister of Food and Drug Safety under Article 19 of the Medical Devices Act). 3. Walkers for the elderly and infirm. 4. Play equipment under Article 11 Paragraph 3 of the Act (limited to those used by children). 5. Non-powered handcarts. 6. A motorcycle, motorized bicycle, or bicycle that the driver has dismounted from and is pushing or carrying. 7. Devices and apparatus used for road repair and maintenance, road construction, and other work (limited to those that do not carry people or transport cargo).

===== 문제 558 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following statements regarding  novice drivers is correct?
① Novice drivers are defined only for Class 1 regular licenses.
② Refers to someone who has received only a motorized bicycle license for which less than 2 years have passed since the  date of issuance.
③ If a driver's license is revoked before 2 years have passed after the date of issuing the first driver's license and then  reissued, the record of dri
④ A person with a driver’s license issued no more than two years previosuly is considered a novice driver.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 Item 27: a 'novice driver' means a person for whom 2 years have not passed since the day they first obtained a driver's license (where the person received a license revocation disposition before 2 years passed from the day they first obtained a driver's license, it means the day they again obtained a driver's license thereafter). In this case, where a person who held only a motorized-bicycle license obtains a driver's license other than the motorized-bicycle license, it is deemed that they obtained a driver's license for the first time.

===== 문제 559 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is correct regarding a motorized bicycle under the Road Traffic Act?
① The term "motorcycle" refers to all two-wheeled vehicles.
② Under the Automobile Management Act, a motorcycle is defined as a two-wheeled vehicle with displacement of up to  250 cc.
③ A car embedding a motor with displacement of 150 cc or more is considered to be a motorcycle.
④ In the case of electricity-powered vehicles, a vehicle using a motor with maximum rated output of 11 kW or less  (excluding electric bicycles) is cons
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 Item 19: a 'motorized bicycle' means a vehicle falling under any of the following. (a) Among two-wheeled vehicles under Article 3 of the Motor Vehicle Management Act, a two-wheeled vehicle with an engine displacement of 125 cc or less (or, if electrically powered, a maximum rated output of 11 kW or less). (b) In addition, a vehicle fitted with an engine of 125 cc or less in displacement (or, if electrically powered, a maximum rated output of 11 kW or less) (excluding electric bicycles).

===== 문제 569 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct definitions based on those stipulated by the Road Traffic Act?
① A "lane" refers to the part of a road where all vehicles can travel and which is demarcated by a line of curbstones, a  safety sign, or a similar arti
② A "vehicle line" refers to a boundary marked by a safety sign in order to indicate separate roadways.
③ A "roadway" refers to the part of a road marked by a line to have vehicles and horses travel in a single file.
④ A "sidewalk" refers to the part of a road demarcated by a line of curbstones for thoroughfare by pedestrians.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2.

===== 문제 570 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT true about riding a  bicycle?
① If a road is divided into a roadway and a sidewalk, bicycle riders must travel on the roadway.
② To turn right at an intersection, bicycle riders must use the rightmost lane and slow down before making the turn.
③ To turn left at an intersection, bicycle riders must use the centerline or leftmost lane and slow down before making the turn.
④ Bicycle riders must use the bicycle lane if there is any.
정답: 3
해설: Ministry of Government Legislation reply to a civil petitioner's inquiry (the National Police Agency's answer to the inquiry on how a bicycle rider turns left at an intersection, related to Road Traffic Act Article 25 Paragraph 3): this refers to a hook-turn, meaning that a bicycle rider cannot turn left directly on a left-turn signal but must, following the straight-ahead signal in the direction of travel, first move over to the right edge of the road in advance and turn left in two stages by going straight and then straight.

===== 문제 571 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about the definition of terms under the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement  Decree?
① The term “automobile road” refers to a road designed exclusively for motor vehicles.
② The term “bicycle track” refers to a road where safety signs, fences, or similar artificial structures mark the boundaries to  allow only bicycles to
③ The term “motor vehicles, etc.” refers to motor vehicles and horses.
④ The term “bicycles, etc.” refers to bicycles and electric bicycles.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 Item 2, Item 8, Item 21, Item 21-2: a 'bicycle road' means a road installed so that bicycles and personal mobility devices can travel. 'Vehicles, etc.' means motor vehicles and motorized bicycles. 'Bicycles, etc.' means bicycles and personal mobility devices.

===== 문제 572 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT true about drivers  of personal mobility devices (PMDs)?
① A driver's license is not required to operate a PMD.
② It is not allowed to have more riders than the permitted capacity on a PMD.
③ Drivers of PMDs must wear protective gear to operate the device.
④ Drivers of PMDs must use the bicycle lane if there is any.
정답: 1
해설: Since a personal mobility device is part of the motorized-bicycle category, a person intending to operate one must obtain a motorized-bicycle license or higher. (Road Traffic Act Article 2 Item 19-2, Article 80)

===== 문제 573 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following persons may NOT use a sidewalk while riding a bicycle?
① A person registered as physically handicapped according to the Act on the Welfare of Persons with Disabilities
② A child
③ A person with a plaster cast due to a bodily injury
④ A person of distinguished service to the state with Disability Rating 1 through 7 according to the Act on the Honorable  Treatment of and Support for
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13-2 (Special Cases for the Method of Bicycle Travel) Paragraph 4, Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 14-4 (Physically Disabled Persons Who May Ride a Bicycle on the Sidewalk).

===== 문제 574 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: There is a pedestrian walking his/her bicycle to cross the street ahead of you. Which of the following is the  safest way to approach?
① Swiftly drive past the pedestrian from whichever side is more open.
② Use the headlights and horn to let the pedestrian know you are approaching.
③ Fully stop the vehicle at a certain distance from the pedestrian.
④ Make the pedestrian stop as you have the right of way.
정답: 3
해설: When there is a person ahead crossing the road while pushing a bicycle, the safest driving method is to keep a safe distance and come to a complete stop so that they can cross safely.

===== 문제 575 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the amount of penalty imposed on a person who rides a bicycle on a narrow road  within a school zone where a lane is not installed and who does not slow down while passing by
① KRW 100,000
② KRW 80,000
③ KRW 40,000
④ KRW 20,000
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 Paragraph 4: the driver of any vehicle must slow down while keeping a safe distance where there are pedestrians in a safety zone installed on the road, and when passing beside a pedestrian on a narrow road without lanes. Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 10]: penalty fine 40,000 won.

===== 문제 576 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not a high-risk personal mobility device/sports equipment stipulated by the Ordinance  of the Ministry of Interior and Safety to require children to wear protective gear when
① Kick scooters
② Self-balancing scooters (segway)
③ Roller skates
④ Skateboards
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 13: the high-risk play equipment prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety means kickboards, roller skates, inline skates, skateboards, and similar play equipment; and for personal mobility devices such as electric two-wheeled self-balancing vehicles, Road Traffic Act Article 11 Paragraph 4 requires a child's guardian to prohibit the child from operating them.

===== 문제 577 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following are the TWO correct  statements regarding cycling?
① Unless allowed by a safety sign, two or more cyclists must not ride side by side on a road.
② When crossing a crosswalk, a cyclist must dismount and push his/her bicycle across.
③ A cyclist must not use a sidewalk even if the road is not accessible due to damage, construction, or other interferences.
④ Where there is no bicycle track, a cyclist must ride close to the center of the road.
정답: 1, 2
해설: Where a bicycle road is separately provided, you must travel on that bicycle road; where no bicycle road is installed, you must travel close to the right edge of the road; and when traveling in a shoulder edge zone (excluding sections where bicycle travel is prohibited by traffic signs), you must slow down or come to a complete stop when you would obstruct the passage of pedestrians.

===== 문제 578 =====
유형: 문장형

① 10
② 13
③ 15
④ 18
정답: 2
해설: Act on the Promotion of Bicycle Use Article 22-2 (Restrictions on Operating Electric Bicycles): the guardian of a child under 13 must not let the child operate an electric bicycle.

===== 문제 579 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT the correct way to  ride a bicycle?
① Turn left on the leftmost lane.
② Pass through the sidewalk as there was construction to repair damage on the roadway.
③ When using a crosswalk, get off and drag across.
④ Slow down to prevent pedestrian accidents.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13-2 (Special Cases for the Method of Travel of Bicycles, etc.): a bicycle must also keep to the right, and where no bicycle road is installed, must travel close to the right edge of the road. Road Traffic Act Article 25 (Method of Passing Through Intersections) Paragraph 3: notwithstanding Paragraph 2, when the rider of a bicycle, etc., intends to turn left at an intersection, they must move over to the right edge of the road in advance and turn left slowly using the edge portion of the intersection.

===== 문제 580 =====
유형: 문장형

① If the bicycle impedes the passage of pedestrians, slow down or stop temporarily.

③ Dragging a bicycle is not allowed in sections where the passage of bicycles is prohibited.
④ Up to two bicycles can pass side by side in the roadside area.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13-2 (Special Cases for the Method of Travel of Bicycles, etc.).

===== 문제 581 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT an advisable practice for cyclists (excluding electric bicycles)?
① A child riding a bicycle on a sidewalk
② Cycling on a sidewalk with a safety sign indicating that cyclists are allowed
③ Cycling on a sidewalk because the road is damaged
④ Cycling along the middle of the road as there are no cars around
정답: 4
해설: In cases (1), (2), and (3), you may travel on the sidewalk. (Road Traffic Act Article 13-2 Paragraph 4)

===== 문제 582 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT true about drivers  of personal mobility devices?
① When crossing a crosswalk, get off the device and walk by either carrying or pushing the device.
② If there is no bicycle lane, ride the device on the far right side of the road.
③ A self-balancing scooter is designed to carry more than 1 person.
④ When driving at night, turn on the headlights and tail lights or wear other light-emitting devices.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 50 Paragraph 10: the rider of a personal mobility device must not operate the personal mobility device carrying passengers in excess of the seating capacity prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 33-3, seating capacity of a personal mobility device: 1. For an electric two-wheeled self-balancing vehicle: 1 person.

===== 문제 583 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is the correct method for  a cyclist to turn left at an intersection?
① Ride slowly along the right edge of the road before turning left around the edge of the intersection.
② Ride slowly along the left edge of the road before turning left around the edge of the intersection.
③ Ride slowly down the middle of the first lane of the road before turning left through the middle of the intersection.
④ Ride slowly down the far-right lane of the road before turning left through the center of the intersection.
정답: 1
해설: When a bicycle rider intends to turn left at an intersection, they must move over to the right edge of the road in advance and turn left slowly using the edge portion of the intersection. (Road Traffic Act Article 25, Method of Passing Through Intersections)

===== 문제 584 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, what is the penalty imposed on the driver of a  passenger car if he/she is caught driving on an exclusive bicycle track?
① Imprisonment for up to 1 year
② A criminal fine of not more than KRW 3 million
③ Notification of traffic penalty of KRW 40,000
④ No penalty
정답: 3
해설: Under the types of exclusive lanes (Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 1]: Types of Exclusive Lanes and Vehicles Allowed to Travel in Exclusive Lanes) and the installation of exclusive lanes (Road Traffic Act Article 15 Paragraphs 2 and 3), a penalty fine of 40,000 won is imposed.

===== 문제 585 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT true about the  criteria for electric bicycles that can travel on bicycle tracks?
① The gross weight of the bicycle, including the weight of attached devices, is less than 30 kg.
② The electric motor must not operate if the speed exceeds 25 km/h.
③ The bicycle must not move solely by the electric motor.
④ Electric bicycle with maximum rated output of more than 11 kW.
정답: 4
해설: Act on the Promotion of Bicycle Use Article 2 (Definitions) Item 1: a vehicle with two or more wheels. Item 1-2: an 'electric bicycle' means a bicycle equipped with an electric motor to supplement human power and meeting all of the following requirements. (a) It moves by the simultaneous power of the pedals (including hand pedals) and the electric motor, and does not move by the electric motor alone. (b) The electric motor does not operate when moving at 25 km/h or more. (c) The total weight of the bicycle, including attached devices, is less than 30 kg.

===== 문제 586 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following is NOT true about riding a  bicycle at night?
① Frequently use the bicycle bell or horn.
② Turn on the headlights and tail lights.
③ Wear a reflective vest.
④ Wear a light-emitting device.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 50 (Matters to Be Observed by Specific Drivers) Paragraph 9: when traveling on the road at night, the rider of a bicycle, etc., must turn on the headlight and tail light or wear a light-emitting device such as a reflective band.

===== 문제 587 =====
유형: 문장형

② Crossing the centerline
③ Obstructing pedestrians on a crosswalk
④ Exceeding the speed limit
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act [Table 8]: a penalty fine of 30,000 won for a signal violation, 30,000 won for crossing the center line, and 30,000 won for obstructing a pedestrian's crossing at a crosswalk; and as for the speeding provision, Road Traffic Act Article 17 (Speed of Motor Vehicles, etc., and Trams) Paragraph 1: the road travel speed of vehicles, etc. (excluding personal mobility devices; the same applies in this Article hereinafter) and trams is prescribed by Ordinance of the Ministry of the Interior and Safety.

===== 문제 588 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following describe the INCORRECT  use of the bicycle lane? (Select TWO)
① A senior person can ride a bicycle on the sidewalk.
② Motorcycles are not allowed on exclusive bicycle tracks.
③ Personal mobility devices are not allowed on exclusive bicycle tracks.
④ According to the Road Traffic Act, exclusive bicycle tracks are not considered roads.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 2 (Definitions): both bicycles and personal mobility devices (bicycles, etc.) may travel on a bicycle road, and a bicycle-only road is also included in the definition of road.

===== 문제 589 =====
유형: 문장형

① Exclusive bicycle track
② Bicycle priority lane
③ Bicycle and motorcycle lane
④ Bicycle priority road
정답: 2, 3
해설: Roads on which a bicycle may travel include bicycle-only roads, bicycle-only lanes, bicycle-priority roads, and shared bicycle-and-pedestrian roads.

===== 문제 590 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following driving methods results in the highest fuel efficiency?
① Driving at the highest speed
② Driving at the lowest speed
③ Driving at an economical speed
④ Driving at a safe speed
정답: 3
해설: Driving at an economical speed is the driving method that most improves fuel efficiency.

===== 문제 591 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the best approach to eco-friendly, economical driving?
① Accelerate as fast as possible.
② Turn off the engine as you enter a downhill road.
③ Lower the air pressure of tires.
④ Avoid rapid deceleration, if possible.
정답: 4
해설: Sudden acceleration and deceleration are causes of lower fuel efficiency; lowering tire pressure excessively reduces the tire diameter and lowers fuel efficiency; and turning off the engine on a downhill disables the brake booster so that braking fails, so these are incorrect driving methods.

===== 문제 592 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following correctly describes how an air-conditioner in a vehicle should be used and maintained?
① Turn on the air-conditioner to full and then turn it down to a lower level.
② Replace the coolant every six months.
③ The ideal temperature setting for an air-conditioner is 16°C.
④ Operate the air-conditioner in outer air inflow mode for greater cooling efficiency.
정답: 1
해설: Air-conditioner use is related to fuel efficiency, and the air-conditioner refrigerant is an environmental pollutant that destroys the ozone layer, so it is desirable to reduce its use as much as possible or use it efficiently.

===== 문제 593 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate method of improving a vehicle’s fuel efficiency?
① Always fill up the fuel tank completely.
② When replacing engine oil, replace the oil filter and the air filter as well.
③ When making a stop, step on the brake pedal once with strong force.
④ Frequently use the accelerator and brake pedals.
정답: 2
해설: Engine oil functions to improve engine performance and extend engine life through lubrication and cooling, sealing, and cleaning inside the engine. If you keep driving with insufficient engine oil, the moving parts inside the engine may seize, causing engine failure; and if the replacement cycle is exceeded, the viscosity of the engine oil increases and adversely affects fuel efficiency, so before long-distance driving, inspect and replace it considering the mileage and period of use, and it is good to replace the oil filter and air filter together.

===== 문제 594 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a brake/gear that helps bring down a car's speed when the driver takes his/her foot  off the accelerator or downshifts?
① Neutral gear
② Foot brake
③ Parking brake
④ Engine brake
정답: 4
해설: This is an explanation of the use of engine braking.

===== 문제 595 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most advisable method of driving in order to improve a vehicle’s fuel efficiency?
① Drive with various tools and parts loaded so that you are prepared for a possible breakdown.
② Maintain a steady speed while driving.
③ Perform quick starts, quick acceleration, and quick braking frequently.
④ Drive with your tire pressure reduced by 30 percent for improved fuel efficiency.
정답: 2
해설: Driving at a constant speed helps improve fuel efficiency.

===== 문제 596 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT an eco-friendly, economical way of driving through the improvement of driving  habits?
① Keep the vehicle fuel at the full level.
② Start the car smoothly.
③ Keep driving at a constant speed.
④ Comply with the economical speed.
정답: 1
해설: Economical driving that can be achieved through improving driving habits includes minimizing idling, starting off smoothly, maintaining a constant speed, observing economical speeds, using coasting (inertia driving), and refraining from using the air conditioner. (Economical driving as presented by the Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport and the Korea Transportation Safety Authority.)

===== 문제 597 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an eco-friendly, economical method of driving?
① Reduce air pressure in the tires.
② Start the air-conditioner at a low level
③ Do not replace the oil filter and air filter when replacing the engine oil.
④ Keep the fuel tank filled to about half its capacity.
정답: 4
해설: Keep the tire pressure at the proper level, run the air conditioner starting at a high setting and then maintaining it at a low setting, and thoroughly manage consumables such as the air cleaner. Also, to reduce the vehicle's weight, remove unnecessary loads to empty the trunk and fill the fuel tank only about halfway.

===== 문제 598 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about an FCEV?
① When the vehicle catches fire, quickly evacuate and move far from the fire for safety.
② If the hydrogen leak detector turns on, immediately park in a safe location and turn off the engine.
③ The driver of an FCEV is not required to take safety education.
④ The driver of an FCEV can manipulate equipment at the hydrogen charging station.
정답: 4
해설: Only drivers who intend to operate a large hydrogen-powered passenger vehicle (seating capacity of 36 or more) must complete safety education (special training). The hydrogen vehicle charging station equipment must not be operated arbitrarily by the driver.

===== 문제 599 =====

질문: Which of the following is the least desirable driving habit in terms of economical driving?
① Do not step on the accelerator pedal when driving downhill.

③ Start slowly and do not stop suddenly.
④ Check the tire pressure regularly.
정답: 2
해설: Using counterfeit fuel can cause vehicle breakdowns and environmental pollution.

===== 문제 600 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Act on the Promotion of Development and Distribution of Environment-friendly Motor Vehicles,  which of the following vehicles is NOT allowed to park in an EV parking space?
① EV
② Solar vehicle
③ Hybrid vehicle
④ FCEV
정답: 2
해설: Under Article 11-2 (7) of the Act on Promotion of Development and Distribution of Environment-Friendly Motor Vehicles, a vehicle that is not an electric vehicle, hybrid vehicle, or hydrogen fuel cell vehicle must not be parked in a parking space reserved exclusively for environment-friendly vehicles.

===== 문제 601 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is true about an FCEV?
① Hydrogen is highly flammable, and all drivers of FCEVs must complete special education as stipulated in the High-pressure  Gas Safety Control Act.
② FCEVs burn hydrogen, causing air pollution.
③ FCEVs are not equipped with safety equipment that is used to prevent explosions in case of an emergency such as fire.
④ The driver of an FCEV must regularly inspect the vehicle to ensure safety and drive safely.
정답: 4
해설: (1) Under Table 31 of the Enforcement Rule of the High-Pressure Gas Safety Control Act, a driver who intends to newly operate a large hydrogen-powered passenger vehicle (seating capacity of 36 or more) must complete special training, but other drivers are excluded from the education requirement. (2) A hydrogen vehicle moves by driving a motor with electricity generated by the chemical reaction between hydrogen stored in a tank and oxygen; it does not burn hydrogen. (3) A hydrogen vehicle is equipped with a safety valve to prevent the tank from exploding when the temperature rises due to fire or other causes; in an emergency the safety valve opens and hydrogen is released to the outside, preventing an explosion. (4) Under Article 7 of the Traffic Safety Act, a person who drives a vehicle must inspect whether the vehicle has any problem in safe operation as prescribed by statute and drive safely.

===== 문제 602 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate method of reducing fine dust from vehicle exhaust gases?
① When starting, step hard on the accelerator pedal.
② Start smoothly instead of accelerating quickly.
③ When driving, accelerate and stop on a frequent, repeated basis.
④ When stopping or parking, keep the engine running.
정답: 2
해설: Eco-friendly driving requires refraining from sudden starts, sudden braking, and sudden acceleration, and while driving one should maintain a constant speed; it is undesirable to repeatedly accelerate and stop. Also, when stopping or parking, one should not keep the engine idling.

===== 문제 603 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a crucial component of an FCEV?
① A fuel cell stack
② A hydrogen tank
③ An internal combustion engine generator
④ A traction motor
정답: 3
해설: A hydrogen vehicle moves by driving a motor with electricity generated in the fuel cell system (stack) through the chemical reaction between hydrogen stored in a tank and oxygen.

===== 문제 604 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO correct descriptions of eco-friendly driving practices?
① It is related to the Kyoto Protocol, which stipulates targets for reducing greenhouse gas emissions.
② Substances that cause air pollution include hydrocarbons, carbon monoxide, carbon dioxide, and nitrogen oxides.
③ To increase the temperature inside the vehicle, start the engine and let it idle for a long time.
④ Inspect the vehicle frequently and replace the engine oil every 2,000km driving distance.
정답: 1, 2
해설: One should not idle the engine, should inspect the vehicle frequently, and it is economical to change the engine oil according to its degree of contamination (about every 10,000 km). The Kyoto Protocol is an international agreement setting greenhouse gas reduction targets for developed countries and officially took effect on February 16, 2005.

===== 문제 605 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following vehicle types discharges the highest amount of hazardous exhaust gases?
① EV
② Hydrogen fuel cell vehicle
③ LPG vehicle
④ Old diesel vehicle
정답: 4
해설: An old diesel vehicle that uses diesel as fuel is the vehicle that emits the most harmful exhaust gases.

===== 문제 606 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a measure that can help reduce fuel consumption for economical, eco-friendly  driving?
① Take your foot off the accelerator pedal before entering an intersection and use the engine brake.
② Do not reduce speed on level roads but keep pressing down on the accelerator pedal.
③ Use the engine brake as necessary while driving downhill.
④ Take advantage of inertia by accelerating before driving onto an uphill road.
정답: 2
해설: Make it a habit to practice coasting (inertia driving, meaning not pressing the accelerator pedal while maintaining a constant speed), actively using the fuel cut function.

===== 문제 607 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following gaseous substances is primarily controlled by a vehicle’s Exhaust Gas Recirculation (EGR)  system?
① Nitrogen oxides (NOx)
② Hydrocarbon (HC)
③ Carbon monoxide (CO)
④ Carbon dioxide (CO2)
정답: 1
해설: The Exhaust Gas Recirculation (EGR) device recirculates a portion of the inert exhaust gas into the intake system, where it mixes with the air-fuel mixture drawn into the engine, lowering the maximum combustion temperature and thereby mainly suppressing NOx (nitrogen oxides), which are harmful pollutants.

===== 문제 608 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about an FCEV inspection?
① Hydrogen is a flammable gas; therefore, FCEVs require regular inspection.
② FCEV inspections must be conducted in a well-ventilated area.
③ During an inspection, it is crucial to check for hydrogen leak in the gas pipeline and charging inlet.
④ The driver of an FCEV is not responsible for conducting safety inspections of the vehicle.
정답: 4
해설: According to Article 7 of the Traffic Safety Act (Obligations of Vehicle Drivers, etc.), a person who drives a vehicle must inspect whether the vehicle has any problem in safe operation as prescribed by statute and must drive safely so as not to cause danger or harm to pedestrians and cyclists.

===== 문제 609 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about using a hydrogen charging station?
① You are not allowed to smoke near a hydrogen charging station.
② You are allowed to move your vehicle while charging.
③ You must turn off the engine while charging.
④ You must not operate the charger when the station operator is not present.
정답: 2
해설: (1) Smoking is prohibited around a hydrogen vehicle charging station. (2) Confirm that fuel charging is complete before moving. (3) Always turn off the engine before charging hydrogen fuel. (4) The hydrogen vehicle charging station equipment may be operated only by station staff.

===== 문제 610 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT appropriate when charging an FCEV?
① Turning off the engine before charging
② Smoking a cigarette near the hydrogen charging station
③ Not operating charging equipment on your own
④ Starting the engine after charging your car
정답: 2
해설: Inside a hydrogen vehicle charging station, smoking is prohibited except in designated areas. Under Table 5 of the Enforcement Rule of the High-Pressure Gas Safety Control Act and KGS Code FP216/FP217 2.1.2 (distance from open flames), a detour distance of at least 8 m must be maintained from the outer surface of the gas equipment to the place where open flames are handled.

===== 문제 611 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest method of driving a loaded dump truck when going down a hill?
① Save fuel by driving in neutral gear.
② Because stepping on the brake pedal several times does not produce the desired braking effect, step on it all at once.
③ Maintain adequate distance from the vehicle driving ahead to guard against sudden stopping.
④ Honk loudly and increase speed.
정답: 3
해설: A loaded dump truck risks having its brake system fail due to the weight of the load, so keep the gear in a low position to use engine braking, press the brake pedal frequently in intervals to minimize strain on the brake system, and maintain a sufficient safe distance.

===== 문제 612 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct method of preventing traffic accidents caused by poor cargo loading?
① Put a cover over the cargo you are carrying.
② There is no need to check fixed attachments such as spare tires.
③ Keep the cargo tied down loosely so you can get it on and off quickly.
④ There is no need to tie down the cargo if you are not going too far.
정답: 1
해설: When moving with cargo, always firmly secure the cargo and cover it so it does not shift, even over a short distance, and before departing, inspect and confirm that attachments such as the spare tire have no abnormalities and correct any before departing.

===== 문제 613 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the LEAST appropriate method of loading freight onto a truck?
① Freight is to be loaded starting at the center of the freight container to the left and right.
② The center of gravity of a truck is in the front, so freight should be loaded starting from the back.
③ Relatively heavier freight is to be loaded on the bottom of the freight container.
④ After loading all of the freight, fix the freight so that it does not fall out of the truck.
정답: 2
해설: According to the cargo handling guidelines in the freight transport worker qualification exam textbook, when loading cargo onto the cargo bed, load it in balance so that the weight is not biased toward the front or the rear.

===== 문제 614 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true regarding the braking characteristics of large and special motor vehicles?
① The characteristics vary depending on changes in load.
② The braking distance changes if the inflation pressure and tread of the tires are not even.
③ The characteristics vary depending on the weight of the vehicle.
④ The actual braking distance shortens with increase in the weight of items loaded inside the vehicle.
정답: 4
해설: The greater the vehicle's weight (load), the longer the actual braking distance becomes, so caution is required in maintaining a safe distance and in operating the brake pedal.

===== 문제 615 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding a low-floor bus is INCORRECT?
① A low-floor bus can be boarded easily by the elderly or the disabled.
② The floor of a low-floor bus is lower than the floor on normal buses.
③ A ramp is installed at the entrance and exit instead of stairs.
④ The height of a low-floor bus is half that of normal buses.
정답: 4
해설: This is a bus with a low floor and no steps at the entrance. It was introduced to guarantee the mobility rights of transportation-vulnerable people who have difficulty going up and down the steps of conventional buses, especially persons with disabilities.

===== 문제 616 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the punishment regulation applicable to a driver who drives a special recovery vehicle  (limited to vehicles compliant with the Trucking Transport Business Act) that is not e
① Administrative fine of KRW 100,000
② Traffic penalty of KRW 100,000
③ Administrative fine of KRW 70,000
④ Traffic penalty of KRW 70,000
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 50 (5) of the Road Traffic Act and item 16 of Table 8 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, driving a bus or the like (special vehicle) not equipped with an tachograph carries a traffic fine of 70,000 won; under Article 55 (1) of the Traffic Safety Act and Table 9 of the Enforcement Decree of the Traffic Safety Act, failing to install an tachograph carries an administrative fine of 500,000 to 1,500,000 won.

===== 문제 617 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT the correct explanation on preventing loads on a truck from falling?
① For cargo that is easy to roll, use fixed neck or cargo support.
② When loading construction equipment, etc., use wires and ropes.
③ When there is space in the front, rear, and on the left and right sides of the loading box, use a detent, etc.
④ Traffic penalty of KRW 50,000 and demerit of 10 points are imposed for violation of fall prevention of the load.
정답: 4
해설: Under Table 8 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, violation of load fall prevention: a traffic fine of 50,000 won for a freight vehicle over 4 tons and 40,000 won for a freight vehicle of 4 tons or less; under Table 28 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, violation of load fall prevention carries 15 penalty points.

===== 문제 618 =====

③ A taxi driver's license
④ A Class I driver's license for special motor vehicles
정답: 2
해설: A driver of a commercial (for-hire) freight vehicle (light freight (yongdal), individual, or general) must obtain a freight transport worker qualification before driving.

===== 문제 619 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the characteristics of a large truck is correct?
① The turning radius and stability of a large truck can be dramatically affected by different cargo types.
② The stability of a large truck is greatly reduced due to the long wheelbase.
③ The steering wheel recovery of a large truck is better than that of a passenger car.
④ Although a large truck is lightweight, it is larger in size than a passenger car.
정답: 1
해설: Compared with a passenger car, a large freight vehicle has poorer steering wheel return, and due to its heavy body and long wheelbase it is relatively more stable, but its turning radius and stability can vary greatly depending on the type of cargo.

===== 문제 620 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the appropriate conduct for the driver of a freight business vehicle that must be  equipped with a tachometer according to the Road Traffic Act?
① Drive without a tachometer.
② Drive with a tachometer that is not in working condition due to a breakdown.
③ Drive with a tachometer that is used for purposes other than the intended use.
④ Drive with a tachometer that has been regularly maintained to prevent malfunctions.
정답: 4
해설: Under each subparagraph of Article 50 (5) of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by Certain Drivers), a driver must not drive a vehicle in which no tachograph is installed or in which an tachograph that cannot be used due to malfunction or the like is installed, and must not drive a vehicle while using the tachograph for a purpose other than its intended purpose.

===== 문제 621 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the minimum hearing level required for the acquisition of a Class I driver’s license for  large motor vehicles (excluding persons using hearing aids)?
① 25 dB
② 35 dB
③ 45 dB
④ 55 dB
정답: 4
해설: Under Article 45 (1) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, a person who intends to obtain a Type 1 large or special license must, as an aptitude standard for driving, be able to hear a sound of 55 decibels (40 decibels for a person using a hearing aid).

===== 문제 622 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the characteristics of a large truck is correct?
① The center of gravity of a large truck varies depending on the load position and height.
② The center of gravity of a large truck does not move up or down.
③ The height of the center of gravity has no effect on the vibration generated by a large truck.
④ Compared to passenger cars, large trucks produce very little vibration.
정답: 1
해설: A large freight vehicle has vibration characteristics, and its vibration angle is much greater than that of a passenger car; the height of the center of gravity affects the vibration characteristics, and the center of gravity also acts in the up-and-down direction.

===== 문제 623 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the driving characteristics of a large truck is correct?
① The stability of a large truck is reduced by the heavy weight and long wheelbase.
② The straight-driving stability of a large truck can be momentarily reduced at high speeds due to vehicle body vibration.
③ The steering wheel recovery of a large truck is better than that of a compact passenger car.
④ The higher driving position in a large truck enables improved field of vision in poor weather conditions.
정답: 2
해설: Even though a large freight vehicle has a high driver's seat, that does not mean visibility improves in abnormal weather; compared with a small passenger car it has poorer steering wheel return, and due to its heavy weight and long wheelbase its stability is higher than that of a passenger car.

===== 문제 624 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding blind spots and load change when braking a large truck is correct?
① The blind spots are not much different in the case of a vehicle with a hood and a vehicle without it.
② The braking force applied to the front and rear wheels is not influenced by change of load.
③ The blind spot on the left side of the driver is much larger than the one on the right in the case of a large truck.
④ The braking power of a large truck will vary depending on the cargo load.
정답: 4
해설: The blind spots of a large freight vehicle differ greatly depending on whether it has a bonnet, changes in load affect the braking force of the front and rear wheels, and the right-side blind spot is much wider than the left-side blind spot from the driver's seat.

===== 문제 625 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its Enforcement Decree, which of the following vehicles violates the safety  standards of a truck's loading capacity?
① Length calculated by adding 2/10 of the vehicle length
② Width within the range of rearview for rear visibility
③ 3.9 m above the ground
④ 105% of payload depending on construction and performance
정답: 1
해설: Article 22 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act (Safety Standards for Operation) provides that the "safety standards for operation prescribed by Presidential Decree" in the main text of Article 39 (1) of the Act mean the following: 3. The loading weight of a freight vehicle shall be within 110 percent of the loading weight according to its structure and performance. 4. The loading capacity of a vehicle (limited to freight vehicles, motorcycles, and small three-wheeled vehicles) shall not exceed the standards according to the following classifications: (a) Length: the vehicle's length plus one-tenth of that length. However, for a motorcycle, it means the length of its riding device or loading device plus 30 centimeters. (b) Width: the width of the range within which the rear can be checked using the vehicle's rear-view mirror (if the cargo is loaded lower than the height of the rear-view mirror, the cargo; if loaded higher than the rear-view mirror, the range within which the rear can be checked). (c) Height: for a freight vehicle, 4 meters from the ground (4 meters 20 centimeters for road routes designated and announced as not impairing the preservation of the road structure and the safety of traffic); for a small three-wheeled vehicle, 2 meters 50 centimeters from the ground; and for a motorcycle, 2 meters from the ground.

===== 문제 626 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the correct description of a Class I driver’s license for special motor vehicles?
① A person holding a driver’s license for small towing vehicles may drive a special towing vehicle with 3.5-ton loading  weight.
② A driver’s license for small towing vehicles may be used to drive a truck with 4-ton loading weight.
③ A driver’s license for recovery vehicles authorizes one to drive a 12-seater passenger vehicle.
④ A person holding a driver’s license for large towing vehicles may drive a truck with 10-ton loading weight.
정답: 2
해설: Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act. (1) A small tow truck license allows driving a towing-type special vehicle with a gross weight of 3.5 tons. (2) A small tow truck license allows driving a freight vehicle with a loading weight of 4 tons. (3) A wrecker license allows driving a bus with a seating capacity of 10 or fewer. (4) A large tow truck license allows driving a freight vehicle with a loading weight of 4 tons.

===== 문제 627 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about the driving characteristics of large vehicles?
① Despite having a great level of stability due to their heavy weight and long wheelbase, the steering recovery is slow for  large vehicles compared to
② Because the driver’s seat in a large vehicle is located higher than that of a small vehicle, the driver drives by looking  only at the immediate front
③ Cornering characteristics change significantly depending on the type and load placement of the cargo.
④ The stability of a large vehicle varies significantly depending on the type and load placement of the cargo.
정답: 2
해설: Because the body of a large vehicle sways during high-speed driving, straight-line stability can momentarily deteriorate; moreover, because the driver's seat is high, in bad weather such as at night, in heavy rain, or in fog, the driver ends up looking only just ahead of the vehicle, which further worsens straight-line stability. To improve straight-line stability, keep your point of focus as far ahead as possible and operate the steering wheel with care.

(Article 49 of the Road Traffic Act (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers, etc.), Table 8 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act (Offenses and Penalty Fine Amounts (Drivers)), Table 28 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act (Criteria for Revocation and Suspension of Driver's License).)

===== 문제 630 =====

===== 문제 632 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following is NOT true about getting on or loading in a vehicle?
① The driver must not load more persons than the number prescribed by the Presidential Decree in the vehicle that he/she is  driving.
② The driver must ensure the safety of passengers, such as making sure that the doors are properly closed so that no one falls  off the vehicle while dr
③ The driver must tightly fasten or cover all cargo on his/her vehicle to prevent them from falling off.
④ For safety reasons, the driver must place an infant or an animal on his/her lap while driving.
정답: 4
해설: Article 39 of the Road Traffic Act (Method of and Restrictions on Riding or Loading). No driver of any vehicle may drive while holding an infant or animal and operating the driving controls, or while loading objects around the driver's seat, in a manner likely to impair safety.

===== 문제 633 =====

===== 문제 634 =====

===== 문제 635 =====

===== 문제 636 =====
