===== 문제 637 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the maximum permitted speed when a passenger vehicle with total weight of 5,000 kg is towing a  vehicle with total weight of 1,500 kg on a road that is not an expressway?
① 50 km/h
② 40 km/h
③ 30 km/h
④ 20 km/h
정답: 3
해설: Under Article 20 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, when a vehicle with a gross weight of less than 2,000 kilograms is towed by a vehicle whose gross weight is three or more times that weight, the speed is 30 kilometers per hour.

===== 문제 638 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a correct description of towing a vehicle?
① When towing a vehicle weighing over three tons, the driver must possess a driver’s license to operate a tow truck and a  class 1 large towing vehicle
② When a motor vehicle with gross weight of less than 2,000 kilograms is being towed by another vehicle whose gross  weight is at least thrice that of t
③ When towing a vehicle on a normal road using a vehicle that is not a tow truck, the driver can drive at a speed equal  to the speed limit.
④ When towing a vehicle using a vehicle that is not a tow truck, the driver must possess a driver's license for that vehicle.
정답: 3
해설: Article 19 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act - On an expressway with two or more lanes in one direction, the maximum speed is 100 kilometers per hour [for freight vehicles (limited to those exceeding a loading weight of 1.5 tons; hereinafter the same in this subparagraph), special vehicles, dangerous-goods transport vehicles (vehicles carrying dangerous goods, etc. under Note 6 of Table 9; hereinafter the same in this subparagraph), and construction machinery, the maximum speed is 80 kilometers per hour], and the minimum speed is 50 kilometers per hour. Article 20 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act - When a vehicle that is not a towing vehicle tows another vehicle and travels on a road (excluding expressways), the speed, notwithstanding Article 19, shall be as prescribed in the following subparagraphs: 1. When a vehicle with a gross weight of less than 2,000 kilograms is towed by a vehicle whose gross weight is three or more times that weight, within 30 kilometers per hour. 2. In cases other than subparagraph 1, and when a motorcycle tows, within 25 kilometers per hour. Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the same Act - A trailer may be towed by a person holding a Type 1 large license, a Type 1 ordinary license, or a Type 2 ordinary license, using a vehicle that person can drive with that license (excluding motorcycles under Article 3 of the Motor Vehicle Management Act). In this case, to tow a trailer with a gross weight exceeding 750 kilograms but not exceeding 3 tons, one must hold both a license to drive the towing vehicle and either a small tow truck license or a large tow truck license; and to tow a trailer exceeding 3 tons, one must hold both a license to drive the towing vehicle and a large tow truck license.

===== 문제 639 =====

① A Class I ordinary student license

④ A Class I driver’s license for small motor vehicles
정답: 3

===== 문제 640 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act, which of the following applies to the driver of a small towing vehicle?
① The driver of a small towing vehicle need not fasten his/her seat belt as he/she is on emergency duty.
② The driver of a small towing vehicle is allowed to use a mobile phone for his/her routine work while driving.
③ The driver of a small towing vehicle is required to obtain a Class I driver’s license for special licenses (small towing  vehicles) and carry it at al
④ The driver of a small towing vehicle may exceed the speed limit when in transit to an accident site.

===== 문제 641 =====
유형: 문장형

① The first lane
② The second lane
③ The third lane
④ Any of the above

===== 문제 642 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following terms refers to the phenomenon wherein a towed vehicle pushes the tow truck that is in  front in a linear motion during rapid deceleration or sudden braking, and the joints are
① Swing-out
② Jack knife
③ Hydroplaning
④ Vapor lock
정답: 2
해설: A 'jack knife' is a phenomenon that can occur in road conditions such as a wet surface when the tractor's braking force is greater than the trailer's braking force, in which the trailer's inertia pushes the tractor forward and the coupling between the tractor and trailer folds like the letter 'L' (the Korean character ㄱ). A 'swing-out' refers to the trailer swinging left and right like a pendulum when, for some abnormal reason, only the trailer's wheels are braked. 'Hydroplaning' refers to a state in which, when driving at high speed on a wet road surface, the tires do not contact the surface, causing a loss of or inability to steer. 'Vapor lock' refers to a phenomenon in which bubbles form in the brake fluid so that the brakes do not work properly.

===== 문제 643 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a correct description of towing a vehicle according to the Road Traffic Act?
① A driver may tow a two-wheeled vehicle using a large towing vehicle with a Class 1 driver’s license for large motor  vehicles.
② A driver may drive a special vehicle for towing that weighs 3.5 tons or less with a small tow truck license.
③ A driver may tow a broken down passenger car using a 10-ton truck with a Class 1 driver’s license for ordinary motor  vehicles.
④ A driver may tow a 1,500 kg vehicle using a 5,000 kg vehicle driving at 30 km/h.
정답: 1
해설: Table 18 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act - A Type 1 large license cannot be used to drive a large tow truck, small tow truck, or wrecker. - Large tow truck: a towing-type special vehicle, and vehicles that can be driven with a Type 2 ordinary license. - Small tow truck: a towing-type special vehicle with a gross weight of 3.5 tons or less, and vehicles that can be driven with a Type 2 ordinary license. - A trailer may be towed by a person holding a Type 1 large license, a Type 1 ordinary license, or a Type 2 ordinary license, using a vehicle that person can drive with that license (excluding motorcycles under Article 3 of the Motor Vehicle Management Act). In this case, to tow a trailer with a gross weight exceeding 750 kilograms but not exceeding 3 tons, one must hold both a license to drive the towing vehicle and either a small tow truck license or a large tow truck license; and to tow a trailer exceeding 3 tons, one must hold both a license to drive the towing vehicle and a large tow truck license.

===== 문제 644 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding a trailer is correct?
① When turning left, a trailer must maintain a turning angle similar to the one used for passenger cars.
② Shift to neutral when driving downhill to prevent sliding.
③ The difference of radius between the inner wheels of a trailer is larger than that of a passenger car.
④ The wheelbase of a trailer is shorter than that of a passenger car.
정답: 3
해설: If a trailer maintains a turning angle similar to that of a passenger car when turning left, its rear wheels will strike vehicles waiting to turn left, so it must turn more widely than a passenger car; and putting the gear in neutral on a downhill can cause a major accident.

===== 문제 645 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the safest way to drive a trailer truck loaded with cargo, traveling at a speed of 50  km/h on a road with one lane in one direction that curves to the right?
① Slowing down can cause the vehicle to overturn. It is safer to drive at an increased speed into the curve.
② Drive into the other lane to reduce the turning radius.
③ Drive into the curve at the current speed so as not to hold up the traffic behind.
④ Slow down and safely enter the curve to prevent the vehicle from rolling over due to centrifugal force.
정답: 4
해설: On a curved road, there is a risk of the vehicle overturning due to centrifugal force, so reduce speed and enter safely.

===== 문제 646 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a type of special vehicle defined by the Motor Vehicle Management Act?
① The towing type
② The rescue type
③ The general type
④ The special-purpose type
정답: 3
해설: Under the Motor Vehicle Management Act, special vehicles are classified by type into towing type, wrecker type, and special-purpose type.

===== 문제 647 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following types is NOT classified as a trailer?
① A pull trailer
② A low-bed trailer
③ A semi-trailer
④ A tractor-trailer
정답: 4
해설: Trailers include full trailers, low-bed trailers, semi-trailers, center-axle trailers, and modular trailers.

===== 문제 648 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the definition of vehicle weight according to the Regulations on the Performance and Standards of  Vehicles and Automotive Parts?
① The weight of an empty vehicle
② The weight of a loaded vehicle
③ The axle weight of an empty vehicle
④ The axle weight of a loaded vehicle
정답: 1
해설: Vehicle weight means the weight of the vehicle in an unloaded state. Gross vehicle weight means the weight of the vehicle in a loaded state.

===== 문제 649 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When driving a vehicle towed using a camping trailer on the road, which of the following is the phenomenon  wherein the towed vehicle shakes like a fish tail due to physical factors such as cross-wind
① Jack knife phenomenon
② Swaying
③ Hydroplaning
④ Wheel alignment
정답: 2
해설: Most serious accidents when operating a caravan are due to the sway phenomenon, in which the caravan sways like a fish's tail; this is also called the fishtail phenomenon.

===== 문제 650 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following must be displayed on the back or right side of a special vehicle for towing?
① Total vehicle weight and maximum permitted cargo weight
② Weight calculated by adding the vehicle's weight and maximum seating capacity
③ Weight calculated by adding the total vehicle weight and maximum seating capacity
④ Total vehicle weight, maximum permitted cargo weight, maximum permitted cargo volume, and cargo manifest
정답: 2
해설: Article 19 of the Rules on the Performance and Standards of Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Parts. On the rear of a freight vehicle, the prescribed gross vehicle weight and maximum loading capacity (for a tank lorry, the gross vehicle weight, maximum loading capacity, maximum loading volume, and name of the loaded goods) shall be displayed; on the rear or right side of a towing-type special vehicle, the vehicle weight plus the weight of the seating capacity shall be displayed; and on the rear of other vehicles, the prescribed maximum loading capacity shall be displayed. However, if the gross vehicle weight is less than 15 tons, the gross vehicle weight need not be displayed.

===== 문제 651 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the part that connects the tractor and the trailer of a tow truck?
① A coupler
② A kingpin
③ An outrigger
④ A boom
정답: 1
해설: Coupler (connector): a device that connects the tractor (towing vehicle) and the trailer (towed vehicle). The rear of the tractor has a semicircular upper portion that narrows toward the center, with a groove of about 20 centimeters; the right side of the coupler consists of the coupler operating handle and the spring lock.

===== 문제 652 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: What is the length limit prescribed for connected vehicles according to the Regulations on the Performance and  Standards of Vehicles and Automotive Parts?
① 13.5 m
② 16.7 m
③ 18.9 m
④ 19.3 m
정답: 2
해설: Article 19 (2) of the Rules on the Performance and Standards of Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Parts. In accordance with Article 4 (1) of the Rules on the Performance and Standards of Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Parts, the length of a motor vehicle must not exceed 13 meters, and for a combination (articulated) vehicle it must not exceed 16.7 meters.

===== 문제 653 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a towed motor vehicle designed for the transport of extremely large cargo as a single  or a combined double unit, and equipped with a device for weight balancing?
① A semi-trailer
② A low-bed trailer
③ A module trailer
④ A center-axle trailer
정답: 3
해설: (1) Semi-trailer: a towed vehicle structured so that part of it rests on the upper part of the towing vehicle and a substantial portion of the weight of the vehicle and its load is borne by the towing vehicle. (2) Low-bed trailer: a towed vehicle suitable for transporting heavy goods and having the structure of a semi-trailer, with most of its upper-surface ground clearance being 1,100 millimeters or less and manufactured lower than the height of the top of the towing vehicle's coupler. (4) Center-axle trailer: a towed vehicle with one or more axles, structured so that its center of gravity when evenly loaded is ahead of the vehicle's axle center, the coupling with the towing vehicle does not articulate vertically, and it distributes to the towing vehicle a load smaller than 10 percent of the gross vehicle weight or 1,000 kilograms.

===== 문제 654 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a towing vehicle designed in such a way that a towed vehicle is partially loaded so  that most of the vehicle weight and its cargo weight are shifted to the towing vehicle?
① A pull trailer
② A semi-trailer
③ A low-bed trailer
④ A center-axle trailer
정답: 2
해설: (1) Full trailer: a towed vehicle structured so that most of the weight of the vehicle and its load is supported by that vehicle's own axles. (2) Semi-trailer: a towed vehicle structured so that part of it rests on the upper part of the towing vehicle and a substantial portion of the weight of the vehicle and its load is borne by the towing vehicle. (3) Low-bed trailer: a towed vehicle suitable for transporting heavy goods and having the structure of a semi-trailer, with most of its upper-surface ground clearance being 1,100 millimeters or less and manufactured lower than the height of the top of the towing vehicle's coupler. (4) Center-axle trailer: a towed vehicle with one or more axles, structured so that its center of gravity when evenly loaded is ahead of the vehicle's axle center, the coupling with the towing vehicle does not articulate vertically, and it distributes to the towing vehicle a load smaller than 10 percent of the gross vehicle weight or 1,000 kilograms.

===== 문제 655 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the characteristics of a trailer is correct?
① The heavy weight of a trailer makes its braking distance shorter than that of passenger cars.
② A trailer has a high risk of rolling over during sudden lane changes.
③ The high seating position of a trailer makes vehicles ahead appear to be closer than they actually are.
④ The large size of a trailer has no impact on the difference of radius between the inner wheels.
정답: 2
해설: (1) The heavier the body, the longer the braking distance. (2) Because the body is long, the risk of overturning is high, and a jackknife phenomenon can occur during a sudden lane change. (Jackknife phenomenon: even when the front of the trailer makes a sudden lane change, the container connected at the rear does not follow into the changed lane but shoots straight ahead in the original direction.) (3) Because a trailer has a high driver's seat, the vehicle ahead appears farther away than the actual gap, so there is a tendency not to secure a safe distance (the driver mistakenly believes a safe distance has been secured). (4) Because the body is long and large, a large difference between the inner turning paths of the front and rear wheels occurs.

===== 문제 656 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the turning and vibration characteristics of a large truck is correct?
① Centrifugal force does not have much influence on the vibration level.
② A vehicle's height of gravitational center does not have much influence on the vibration level.
③ The turning radius and stability of a large truck can change dramatically depending on the cargo type and position.
④ Accidents involving collisions are more likely due to the vibration level of a large truck being lower than that of  passenger cars.
정답: 3
해설: The turning radius and stability of a large freight vehicle can vary greatly depending on the type and loading position of the cargo, and the vehicle's centrifugal force and the height of its center of gravity greatly affect the vibration angle, with the vibration angle being larger than that of a passenger car.

===== 문제 657 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following correctly describes a rule that should be followed by the driver of a trailer?
① Routine inspection of the vehicle must take place only at the end of a shift.
② Transport of cargo must not be refused unless there is a valid reason.
③ The vehicle should be kept clean only when carrying high-priced cargo.
④ Be sure to check the placement of covers and packaging used to hold cargo in place when the destination is reached.
정답: 2
해설: Before operating, the driver must firmly secure the covering and packaging to prevent the loaded cargo from coming loose, always keep the vehicle clean, and carry out daily inspection and checking of the vehicle steadily not only before but also after operation.

===== 문제 658 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which lane is to be used by a recovery vehicle on an expressway with three lanes in one direction?
① The first lane
② The left lane
③ The right lane
④ Any lane
정답: 3
해설: See Table 9 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act.

===== 문제 659 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate method for a recovery vehicle to tow an all-wheel drive vehicle?
① Tow the vehicle from behind using another vehicle.
② Tow by lifting the all-wheel drive vehicle entirely off the ground.
③ The drive system and the towing method are unrelated.
④ The parking brakes of all vehicles being towed must be in braking state.
정답: 2
해설: When towing a full-time four-wheel-drive vehicle with a wrecker, the entire vehicle must be lifted, placed on the cargo bed, and transported.

===== 문제 660 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following describes a special recovery vehicle as defined by the Motor Vehicle Management Act?
① A vehicle that is structurally designed to tow other vehicles
② A vehicle that is structurally designed to tow and salvage other vehicles
③ A vehicle that is structurally designed to salvage and tow a vehicle rendered immobile by a malfunction or an accident
④ A vehicle that is designed to perform special tasks other than the three above
정답: 3
해설: Table 1 of the Enforcement Rule of the Motor Vehicle Management Act. Among special vehicles, the detailed criteria for the wrecker type mean a vehicle structured so as to be able to rescue and tow a vehicle that is difficult to operate due to a breakdown, accident, or the like.

===== 문제 661 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: According to the Regulations on the Performance and Standards of Vehicles and Automotive Parts, what is the  maximum length of a motor vehicle? (Not referring to connected cars)
① 13 m
② 14 m
③ 15 m
④ 16 m
정답: 1
해설: Article 4 of the Rules on the Performance and Standards of Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Parts (Length, Width, and Height). (1) The length, width, and height of a motor vehicle shall not exceed the following standards. 1. Length: 13 meters (16.7 meters for a combination vehicle). 2. Width: 2.5 meters (for indirect-view devices, ventilation devices, or outward-opening windows, the width of these devices shall be 25 centimeters for passenger vehicles and 30 centimeters for other vehicles. However, where the width of the towed vehicle is wider than that of the towing vehicle, only the indirect-view device of the towing vehicle may not exceed 10 centimeters beyond the outermost part of the towed vehicle). 3. Height: 4 meters.

===== 문제 662 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate action for the driver of a recovery vehicle upon arrival at the  scene of an accident?
① Collect the driver's license belonging to the driver of the vehicle that was involved in the accident.
② Immediately upon arrival, tow the vehicle involved in the accident to a maintenance shop.
③ Negotiate the cost of repairs with the driver of the vehicle that was involved in the accident.
④ Check the driver of the vehicle involved in the accident for any injuries, and take safety measures to prevent further  accidents.
정답: 4
해설: A wrecker (tow truck) driver has no authority to perform accident-processing administrative duties, so the accident scene must be preserved. However, the driver may take safety measures appropriate to the surrounding situation to rescue the injured and to guard against a secondary accident.

===== 문제 663 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most desirable action for a recovery vehicle driver?
① Reach the location of a vehicle breakdown as soon as possible and tow the vehicle immediately.
② When towing a vehicle, obey the laws and safely proceed with towing.
③ Drive safely only when the towing cost by distance is expensive.
④ Ignore signals while on the way to the location of a breakdown.
정답: 2
해설: A wrecker driver should respond quickly but drive lawfully, and must not ignore the vehicle owner's wishes or damage the accident scene.

===== 문제 664 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most important consideration when a recovery vehicle is towing a broken-down  vehicle from the shoulder to the travel lane?
① On an expressway, pay attention to vehicles maintaining constant speed ahead.
② Ensure the safety of people in the towed vehicle.
③ Watch out for fast-moving vehicles in the main lane.
④ There is no need to pay much attention to tow trucks as they are highly visible.
정답: 3
해설: When a wrecker tows a broken-down vehicle from the shoulder and enters the driving lane, it must enter while taking care not to obstruct the passage of following vehicles traveling in the driving lane. Regarding riding in the towed vehicle, there is no legal restriction as long as the tow truck's safety and the occupant's safety are secured, but tow truck drivers do not allow anyone to ride in the towed vehicle.

===== 문제 665 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an inappropriate measure on the part of the driver of a recovery vehicle at the scene  of an accident with injured persons?
① Check the injured for consciousness.
② Check the injured for breathing.
③ Check the injured for bleeding.
④ Load the crashed vehicle onto the towing vehicle and negotiate the towing cost.
정답: 4
해설: At an accident scene, one must not induce the parties involved in the accident to forgo proper accident processing or pressure them into settling the accident.

===== 문제 666 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is a correct description of equipment attached to a recovery vehicle?
① The arm structure hung from the main body of the crane is called a hook.
② The device used to connect the recovery vehicle to a structure when towing is called the PTO switch.
③ The structure attached to the front and rear sides in order to secure stability during tasks is called an outrigger.
④ The device installed on the crane and on which a weighted item is hooked with a wire rope is called a boom.
정답: 3
해설: (1) Crane boom: the arm part of the crane attached to the crane body. (2) A-frame tow bar (towing triangle): a device that connects the structure to the wrecker when towing it; PTO switch: the power required for the crane and rescue winch comes from the vehicle's PTO (power take-off). (3) Outrigger: a structure attached to the front and rear sides to secure stability during operation. (4) Hook: mounted on the crane, hook-shaped, and hung on the wire rope to hang heavy objects.

===== 문제 667 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate method for the driver of a recovery vehicle to tow a broken  down vehicle with an FF layout (Front engine Front wheel drive)?
① Tow by lifting the front part of the vehicle.
② Tow by lifting the rear part of the vehicle.
③ It does not matter which part is lifted.
④ Tow by lifting the entire vehicle.
정답: 1
해설: In a front-engine front-wheel-drive (FF) vehicle, the engine is at the front and it is front-wheel drive, so to prevent damage it is towed by lifting the front part of the body.

===== 문제 668 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: The following are measures that the driver of a recovery vehicle may take at the scene of an accident. Which  one is the most advisable?
① Advise the parties involved in the accident to pursue a civil settlement.
② Immediately tow the crashed vehicle without consulting the parties involved in the accident.
③ Just prepare for towing without taking any safety measures, as visibility is good in daytime.
④ Help the driver of the vehicle that crashed to calm down.
정답: 4
해설: One must not get involved in accident processing, such as pressuring the parties to a traffic accident into a settlement, and must take safety measures even during the daytime.

===== 문제 669 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate measure for the driver of a recovery vehicle to take if he/she  finds an injured person at the scene of an accident?
① Try to start a conversation or gently tap the injured person’s shoulders to check the state of consciousness.
② If the injured person is not conscious, attempt CPR.
③ For a person with a fracture, immediately support the fractured area with a splint and wait until an ambulance arrives.
④ For severe bleeding, position the bleeding area below the heart.
정답: 1
해설: As an ordinary person without medical expertise, checking the level of consciousness is the top priority; it is advisable to follow the instructions of paramedics (medical personnel) obtained by calling 119 to treat the injured and to wait for the ambulance crew. Rash actions based simply on visual judgment are highly likely to lead to greater injury or after-effects.

===== 문제 670 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is not a reason why the driver of a recovery vehicle arriving at the scene of an accident  should perform emergency first-aid measures on an injured person(s)?
① To facilitate the speedy transport of the victim
② To protect the life of the injured.
③ To protect the victim's assets
④ To increase the chances of the victim's survival
정답: 3
해설: First-aid measures for the injured must keep in mind saving life, increasing the survival rate, and transport for professional treatment, and should be limited to the minimum first aid necessary so that the injured can receive treatment from professional medical personnel as much as possible. An action taken simply to protect property cannot be regarded as a reason for first aid.

===== 문제 671 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following terms refers to the phenomenon wherein the rear of a vehicle shakes from side to side  during driving or sudden braking?
① Fishtailing
② Hydroplaning
③ Standing wave
④ Vapor lock
정답: 1
해설: 'Fishtailing' is a phenomenon in which the rear of the vehicle body sways left and right like a fish's tail fin during driving or sudden braking. 'Hydroplaning' refers to a state in which, when driving at high speed on a wet road surface, the tires do not contact the surface, causing a loss of or inability to steer. 'Standing wave' is a phenomenon in which, when the vehicle drives at high speed, heat builds up in the contact area of the tire and deformation appears. 'Vapor lock' refers to a phenomenon in which bubbles form in the brake fluid so that the brakes do not work properly.

===== 문제 672 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following actions is appropriate for the driver of a recovery vehicle?
① Carry a fire extinguisher within the vehicle for early suppression of fire.
② Drive past the centerline to rush to the scene of an accident.
③ In a minor accident, persuade the drivers to settle the issue.
④ Take both the driver and the passenger, and then tow the vehicle involved in the accident.
정답: 1
해설: Because a wrecker driver may be the first to arrive at an accident scene, it is advisable to equip the wrecker with fire-extinguishing equipment that can suppress a vehicle fire in its early stage. Violating traffic laws to arrive quickly or pressuring for a settlement at the scene are behaviors to avoid, and allowing occupants to ride in the accident vehicle is extremely dangerous and must never be done.

===== 문제 673 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: A recovery vehicle was caught driving at 145 km/h on an expressway with a 100 km/h speed limit. Which of the  following corresponds to the correct demerit points and traffic penalty?
① 70 demerit points and traffic penalty of KRW 140,000
② 60 demerit points and traffic penalty of KRW 130,000
③ 30 demerit points and traffic penalty of KRW 100,000
④ 15 demerit points and traffic penalty of KRW 70,000
정답: 2
해설: Because a wrecker falls under item 3 (b) of Article 19 (1) of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, its maximum speed on an expressway with two lanes in one direction where the limit is 100 kilometers per hour becomes 80 kilometers per hour; and because it is classified as a special vehicle under Table 1 of the Enforcement Rule of the Motor Vehicle Management Act, and special vehicles are included among buses and the like under Table 8 of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act and Table 28 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, answer (2) is correct.

===== 문제 674 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following may be painted or marked on a recovery vehicle?
① Vehicle painting and signs similar to those used on a traffic control vehicle
② Vehicle painting and signs similar to those used on a crime investigation vehicle
③ Vehicle painting and signs similar to those used on an emergency vehicle
④ Indication of the driver's phone number to be called in case of an emergency
정답: 4
해설: Article 42 of the Road Traffic Act (Restrictions on Similar Markings and Prohibition of Operation), Article 27. Article 27 of the Enforcement Decree of the same Act (Scope of Similar Markings, Painting, etc.): under Article 42 (2) of the Act, the painting or markings restricted on motor vehicles, etc. (excluding personal mobility devices) are as follows. 1. Coloring or markings that could be mistaken for an emergency vehicle. 2. Pictures, symbols, or letters that express profanity or depict obscene acts and thereby cause revulsion to others.

===== 문제 675 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most appropriate method for the driver of a recovery vehicle to tow a broken  down vehicle with an RR layout (Rear engine Rear wheel drive)?
① Tow by lifting the front part of the vehicle.
② Tow by lifting the rear part of the vehicle.
③ It does not matter which part is lifted.
④ Tow by lifting the entire vehicle.
정답: 2
해설: In a rear-engine rear-wheel-drive (RR) vehicle, the engine is at the rear and it is rear-wheel drive, so to prevent damage it is towed by lifting the rear part of the body.

===== 문제 676 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is the most desirable way to drive a recovery vehicle when responding to an accident  scene?
① Speedy arrival is the foremost priority, so it is acceptable to drive on the opposite side.
② It is acceptable to drive over the speed limit because a recovery vehicle is classified as an emergency vehicle.
③ On a congested expressway, drive along the roadside while honking loudly.
④ Drive safely to prevent more accidents, while striving to get to the scene of the accident as quickly as possible.
정답: 4
해설: When approaching a traffic accident scene, competing to secure the towing job creates psychological pressure that increases the likelihood of causing a traffic accident, so one must drive safely.

===== 문제 677 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following does NOT correspond to the classification of special motor vehicle types under the  Motor Vehicle Management Act?
① Vehicle used for towing
② Vehicle used for special operation
③ Vehicle used for recovery
④ Vehicle used for emergency gas recovery
정답: 4
해설: Table 1 of the Enforcement Rule of the Motor Vehicle Management Act (Types of Motor Vehicles (related to Article 2)). Special vehicles are classified by type into towing type, wrecker type, and special-purpose type.

===== 문제 678 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding the driving skills test for a Class I driver’s license for small towing  vehicles is correct?
① The participant is required to score at least 90 out of 100 to pass.
② The participant is required to pass the bent course, curved course, change of direction, and driving tests.
③ The pass criteria for the small towing vehicle test course is within 5 minutes for each course.
④ The participant will lose 5 points every time he or she fails to touch the confirmation line on each test course.
정답: 1
해설: Article 66 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, and Table 24 of the Enforcement Rule of the same Act (Skill Test Scoring Criteria and Passing Criteria). The skill test for the small tow truck license requires passing the articulation course, the curve course, and the direction-change course; for each course, 10 points are deducted for exceeding 3 minutes, for touching a detection line, or for failing to touch the confirmation line of the direction-change course. The passing standard is 90 points or higher.

===== 문제 679 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: When a recovery vehicle is towing a vehicle that has broken down, which of the following lights should the  vehicle being towed turn on?
① Headlights and emergency flashers
② Headlights and taillights
③ Taillights, sidelights, and license plate lights
④ Left turn indicator
정답: 3
해설: Article 37 of the Road Traffic Act, and Article 19 of the Enforcement Decree of the same Act. A vehicle being towed must turn on its tail lamps, clearance lamps, and license plate lamp.

===== 문제 680 =====
유형: 문장형
질문: Which of the following is an appropriate action for the driver of a recovery vehicle?
① In some instances, it is permissible for the driver of a recovery vehicle to use a police radio scanner.
② The driver of a recovery vehicle must obey the Road Traffic Act.
③ A recovery vehicle may violate the speed limit when it is on the way to the scene of an accident.
④ In the event of an accident, a recovery vehicle may proceed without regard for traffic signals.
정답: 2
해설: Article 2 subparagraph 22 of the Road Traffic Act limits emergency vehicles to fire trucks, ambulances, blood-supply vehicles, police vehicles, and the like, and wreckers are not included therein. Therefore a wrecker cannot enjoy the exceptions granted to emergency vehicles (violating signals, exceeding the speed limit, etc.), must strictly observe the Road Traffic Act, and it is illegal for a wrecker driver to eavesdrop on police radio.

===== 문제 681 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are incorrect ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0000.jpg
① When intending to go straight, proceed at the green  traffic light.
② When intending to turn left, proceed at the green traffic  light while paying attention to oncoming traffic.
③ When intending to turn left, proceed at the green  left-turn arrow.
④ When intending to turn right from the first lane, stop  temporarily before the stop line, and then proceed  slowly.
⑤ When intending to turn right, proceed slowly along the  far-right lane of the road before making the turn.
정답: 3, 4
해설: The traffic light in the photo is a horizontal three-color light consisting only of red, yellow, and green lamps, and does not include a left-turn arrow lamp. If such a horizontal three-color light and an unprotected left-turn sign are installed, one may turn left while paying attention to oncoming traffic. When a driver of any vehicle intends to turn right at an intersection, the driver must turn right while slowing down along the right edge of the road in advance. In this case, the driver turning right must pay attention to pedestrians or cyclists, etc., who stop or proceed according to the signal (Article 25 (1) of the Road Traffic Act).

===== 문제 685 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following pairings of information from  the given situation and appropriate driving methods  are incorrect? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0004.jpg
① Crosswalk – Pay attention to both sides, be cautious of  pedestrians.
② Pedestrians on the roadway – Slow down and drive with  caution for safety.
③ Horizontal dual-color traffic lights – Continue driving on  the lane marked with a red X.
④ Reversible lane – Lane direction may change depending  on the situation.
⑤ Broken yellow centerline – This line cannot be crossed  even when trying to overtake a vehicle.
정답: 3, 5
해설: On a road where the volume of traffic in the two directions clearly differs by time of day, the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency may install a reversible lane that indicates the direction of travel of the lane by a traffic signal so that the number of lanes can be expanded toward the side with heavier traffic (Article 14 (1) of the Road Traffic Act). Horizontal two-color lights are installed at the entrance, middle, and exit of a road section designated as a reversible lane (Table 3 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act). When a green arrow lamp (pointing downward) is shown, vehicles may proceed in the designated lane, and when a red X-mark lamp is shown, they cannot proceed in that lane (Table 2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act). Among center lines, a yellow solid line indicates that vehicles may not cross it, while a yellow broken line indicates that a vehicle may temporarily cross into the opposite lane while paying attention to oncoming traffic but must return to its lane of travel (Table 6 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, serial number 501).

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0005.jpg

When it is raining and the road surface is wet, or when less than 20 millimeters of snow has accumulated, one must drive at a speed reduced by 20 percent of the maximum speed (Article 19 (2) 1 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act).

===== 문제 700 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: In the given situation, which of the following are  the two safest ways of proceeding?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0017.jpg
① Slow down gradually and stop immediately before the  stop line as the signal is yellow.
② Check the rear of lanes 1 and 2, and if there are no  vehicles, switch to lane 1 to make a U-turn.
③ Let the white vehicle and the bus in lanes 4 and 5 pass  you before changing lanes to turn right.
④ Do not change lanes as you are driving on a section of  road marked by a solid white line.
⑤ Accelerate to follow the vehicle ahead as the signal  ahead is yellow.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Every driver must comply with the instructions of traffic signs and traffic signals (Article 5 of the Road Traffic Act). When the light is yellow, where there is a stop line or crosswalk, a vehicle must stop just before it or just before the intersection, and if any part of the vehicle has already entered the intersection, it must proceed quickly out of the intersection (Table 2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act). Also, because lane changes are prohibited in a white solid line section, attempting to change lanes to U-turn or turn right is a violation of the law and a dangerous driving behavior. Option (5), passing through the intersection at increased speed while ignoring the yellow signal, is not only a violation of the law but also raises the chance of a collision within the intersection, which can cause a major traffic accident.

===== 문제 701 =====
유형: 사진형

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0018.jpg
① Stop just before the stop line to travel straight.
② To make a U-turn, make the turn safely from the second  lane through the intersection if there are no vehicles  ahead.
③ Stop briefly before the crosswalk to drop off a passenger.
④ To turn right, stop before the stop line and slowly turn  right.
⑤ If you are using a crosswalk, you must dismount from  the bicycle and walk with it.
정답: 2, 3
해설: If an intersection has a traffic signal showing only the three colors green, yellow, and red, and there is no separate marking permitting an unprotected left turn or a U-turn, a left turn or U-turn by a vehicle is in principle not permitted. Therefore, if at the above intersection a vehicle did not stop at the stop line at a red signal but proceeded to turn left or U-turn, this should, absent special circumstances, be regarded as a case of driving in violation of the traffic signal under Article 5 of the Road Traffic Act (Supreme Court, 1996. 5.).

③ Two-wheeled vehicle drivers - Two-wheeled vehicles are  allowed to drive on bus-only lanes to avoid congestion.
④ Passenger vehicle drivers - Drive with caution to avoid  collision in congested roads.
⑤ Bus drivers - Follow the general traffic light when  driving in a lane other than the bus-only lane, instead of  following the bus-only traffic lights
정답: 2, 3
해설: The riding capacity of an electric kick scooter is one person (Article 33-3 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act). A vehicle that is not permitted to use an exclusive lane must not travel in the exclusive lane (Article 15 (3) of the Road Traffic Act).

===== 문제 705 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safest ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0022.jpg
① Drive while keeping in mind that pedestrians may  appear from behind parked vehicles.
② Keep your distance from pedestrians on the right and  stop temporarily or pass them slowly.
③ Speed up and move quickly when the roads are slippery  as the snow has been accumulated.
④ Accelerate 20 percent above the maximum speed on  snowy roads.
⑤ Keep honking while driving to avoid sudden changes in  pedestrian behavior.
정답: 1, 2
해설: When a driver of any vehicle passes by a pedestrian on a road that is not divided into a sidewalk and roadway and has no center line, the driver must slow down while keeping a safe distance, and when it would obstruct the pedestrian's passage, must slow down or come to a complete stop so that the pedestrian can pass safely (Article 27 (6) 1 of the Road Traffic Act). A driver must travel on the right side of the center of the road (on a road divided into a sidewalk and roadway, this means the roadway; where a center line is installed, this means that center line; hereinafter the same) (Article 13 (3) of the Road Traffic Act). In preparation for abnormal weather, the speed-reduction rules apply. The cases requiring driving at a speed reduced by 20 percent of the maximum speed are when it is raining and the road surface is wet, or when less than 20 millimeters of snow has accumulated; and the cases requiring driving at a speed reduced by 50 percent of the maximum speed are when visibility is within 100 meters due to heavy rain, heavy snow, fog, etc., when the road surface is frozen, or when 20 millimeters or more of snow has accumulated (Article 19 (2) of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act).

===== 문제 716 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: In the given situation, which of the following are  the two safest ways of driving?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0033.jpg
① Since it is darker than during the day at sunset, turn on  the high beams and sound the horn continuously while  driving.
② Let the vehicles coming from the opposite direction pass  so as to secure a clear field of vision, and then overtake  quickly.
③ Keep a safe distance from the truck ahead as sudden or  unexpected situations may require the truck to make an  emergency stop.
④ Do not overtake as overtaking is prohibited due to the  solid centerline.
⑤ Sound the horn to incite the vehicle ahead to go faster.
정답: 3, 4
해설: When a driver of any vehicle follows behind a vehicle ahead traveling in the same direction, the driver must secure the distance necessary to avoid a collision with the vehicle ahead should it suddenly stop (Article 19 (1) of the Road Traffic Act). In particular, when driving behind a large freight vehicle, forward visibility is poor and sudden situations can arise, so keeping a sufficient safe distance is necessary. Also, overtaking is prohibited in a section where the center line is a solid line (Table 6 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, center line marking No. 501). On the other hand, using high beams and continuously sounding the horn can obstruct other drivers' vision or generate unnecessary noise, creating danger.

===== 문제 717 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safest ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0034.jpg
① The yield sign on the right is the one that entering  vehicles must follow.
② The driver must overtake to the left side of the car  ahead when intending to do so within a roundabout.
③ All vehicles may use the exit to 1 o'clock without  restriction.
④ Pass immediately as vehicles traveling through the  roundabout have the right of way over vehicles entering  the roundabout.
⑤ The driver must turn on the turn signal when exiting a  roundabout.
정답: 1, 5
해설: Overtaking at an intersection is prohibited (item 1 of Article 22 (3) of the Road Traffic Act). When a driver intends to enter a roundabout, if there is another vehicle already proceeding, the driver must yield the way to that vehicle (Article 25-2 (2) of the Road Traffic Act). A vehicle already circulating has no obligation to yield. Stopping and parking are prohibited at an intersection (Article 32 subparagraph 1 of the Road Traffic Act). When turning right or changing lanes to the right, one must turn on the right turn signal (Table 2 of the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act), and when intending to exit a roundabout, one must turn on the turn signal (Article 38 (1) of the Road Traffic Act). When traveling on a bridge, one must comply with the contents of the operation-restriction sign.

===== 문제 722 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safest ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0039.jpg
① To overtake, wait for the passenger vehicle traveling in  the left lane to pass, and then move into the left lane to  overtake safely.
② You cannot park on a road over a bridge.
③ You can change lanes from the second to the first.
④ You cannot overtake on a road over a bridge.
⑤ If the vehicle ahead is driving slowly, follow closely  behind it.
정답: 2, 4
해설: The place in the photo is a bridge road section, which falls under a no-overtaking area (Article 22 (3) of the Road Traffic Act). Therefore, one must not attempt to overtake even after the vehicle in the left lane has passed. Also, because the left side is marked with a white solid line, lane changes are prohibited, so changing from the second lane to the first lane is likewise not possible (Article 14 (5) of the Road Traffic Act). On the other hand, parking is prohibited inside a tunnel and on a bridge (Article 33 of the Road Traffic Act). Therefore, the safest driving methods are not parking on the bridge (2) and not overtaking (4).

===== 문제 725 =====

===== 문제 726 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the correct ways to  change lanes in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0043.jpg
① A passenger car driving in the second lane can switch to  the first lane.
② A passenger car driving in the first lane cannot switch to  the second lane.
③ You can switch from the entry lane directly to the first  lane.
④ A passenger car traveling in the second lane cannot  switch to the entry lane.
⑤ Lane changes are allowed in all lanes.
정답: 2, 4
해설: A driver must not change the vehicle's course where a lane change is specifically prohibited by an installed traffic sign. However, this does not apply where there is an obstacle due to damage to the road or road construction, etc. (Article 14 (5) of the Road Traffic Act). Lane changes are possible in a white broken line section but must not be made in a white solid line section. Also, when a broken line and a solid line form a double line, a lane change is possible only from the side with the broken line.

===== 문제 729 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safest ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0046.jpg
① Turn on the left-turn signal, maintain a safe distance,  and exit to the right when appropriate.
② Watch out for the traffic ahead and behind you as you  exit the off-ramp.
③ If you miss the off-ramp, reverse the vehicle and try to  get back to the intended route.

⑤ Increase the distance between you and the car ahead to  avoid skidding.

One must not change lanes suddenly, and should secure a sufficient safe distance and turn on the turn signal for the direction of intended exit in advance before proceeding.

⑤ Do not try to overtake, as there are many vehicles in the  left lane.
정답: 4, 5
해설: In winter, driving on a snowy road makes the surface slippery, and since one must soon enter a tunnel, particular caution is required. To overtake another vehicle, a driver must pass on the left side of the vehicle ahead (Article 21 (1) of the Road Traffic Act). When changing the vehicle's course, one must not change course if there is a risk of obstructing the normal passage of another vehicle coming from the direction of the intended change (Article 19 (3) of the Road Traffic Act). Also, turn on the left turn signal in advance, secure a safe distance, enter the left lane, and then attempt to overtake. Intersections, inside tunnels, and bridges are no-overtaking areas (Article 22 (3) of the Road Traffic Act). Therefore, the safest driving methods are not overtaking inside the tunnel (4) and avoiding a forced overtake because there are many vehicles on the left (5).

===== 문제 751 =====

질문: If the accelerator pedal gets stuck in the driver's  seat mat and continues to accelerate, which of the  following are the ways to stop safely or slow down?  (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q751.png

② Continuously press the hazard light button.
③ Keep honking firmly while driving.
④ Continuously operate the electronic parking brake (EPB).
⑤ Steer the steering wheel strongly to the left and right.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Even if the accelerator pedal is caught by an object (a mat, foreign material, etc.) and continuously depressed, operating the brake pedal activates the Brake Override System (BOS), which prioritizes the braking signal to enable deceleration and stopping. Because the Electronic Parking Brake (EPB) includes an emergency braking function, continuous operation enables deceleration or control. - Even in an unintended acceleration situation, stopping or deceleration is possible by the same method. * What is an unintended acceleration situation? - When the accelerator pedal does not return due to sticking of the accelerator pedal caused by a mechanical defect, the accelerator pedal catching on the floor mat, or an external object (water bottle, shoe, wet wipes, etc.) being caught.

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q753.png
① An act of obstructing a parking space reserved for  persons with disabilities by stacking objects
② An act of stacking or parking objects in front, behind, or  on either side of a parking space reserved for persons  with disabilities
③ An act of parking a car without a permit in a parking  space reserved for persons with disabilities
④ An act of obstructing a parking space reserved for  persons with disabilities by erasing or damaging its lines  and signs

The same applies even where a vehicle bearing the disabled-only parking zone parking marker does not have a person with a walking disability aboard. - No one may commit an act obstructing parking in a disabled-only parking zone, such as stacking objects or blocking its passage. Article 9 of the Enforcement Decree of the Act on Guarantee of Convenience Promotion for the Disabled, the Aged, Pregnant Women, etc. (Acts Obstructing Parking in Disabled-Only Parking Zones): the parking-obstruction acts under Article 17 (5) of the Act are the following: 1. Stacking objects, etc. within a disabled-only parking zone to obstruct parking. 2. Stacking objects, etc. or parking in front of, behind, or on both sides of a disabled-only parking zone. 3. Stacking objects, etc. or parking on the access road to a disabled-only parking zone. 4. Erasing or damaging the lines and disabled-only markings of a disabled-only parking zone to obstruct parking. 5. Otherwise obstructing parking in a disabled-only parking zone.

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0067.jpg
① Since there are no vehicles in the direction of travel, you  can stop on the right side of the road.
② Even if you are driving in a school zone, you need not  observe the speed limit if no children are present.
③ Observe the maximum speed limit indicated by the  safety sign as you drive.
④ Drive with caution and watch your surroundings, as  children may suddenly jump into the road.
⑤ You should speed up and drive as fast as possible to  exit school zones.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Because it is within a school zone, observe the maximum speed of within 30 km/h, and when a child is spotted, look carefully ahead while paying attention to the child's movements. Accidents in a school zone are caused by failure to drive safely, violation of the duty to protect pedestrians, illegal stopping/parking, signal violations, and other failures to obey the law. And when a pedestrian is crossing, one must come to a complete stop, then, once the pedestrian's crossing is finished, confirm safety and pass.

===== 문제 755 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safest ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0068.jpg
① Drive fast while continuously honking.
② Slow down sufficiently in advance to drive safely.
③ Drive slowly even if you do not see any elderly people  walking.
④ For safety, it is advisable to tailgate the vehicle in front  whenever possible.
⑤ Speed up and quickly exit the silver zone as there is a  crosswalk ahead.
정답: 2, 3
해설: This section is a senior protection zone, so slow down to within 30 km/h and drive safely. Also, even if no pedestrian is visible, one must slow down when approaching a crosswalk and prepare for pedestrians appearing. Therefore, the safest driving methods are slowing down in advance and paying attention to safety (2) and driving slowly even if no elderly person is visible (3). Conversely, using the horn unnecessarily and driving fast (1), tailgating the vehicle ahead closely (4), and increasing speed to leave the zone (5) all belong to unsafe driving methods.

(2) The head of a fire headquarters, the head of a fire station, or a fire captain may, when deemed urgent to rescue a person or prevent the spread of fire, make the disposition under paragraph (1) with respect to fire-fighting objects and land other than those under paragraph (1). ===== 문제 773 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the most incorrect ways  to drive in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0083.jpg
① Bicycle riders may ride their bicycle when crossing a  crosswalk.
② A bicycle rider who is a child may ride on the sidewalk.
③ A bicycle rider must wear a helmet.
④ Bicycle riders must turn on their front and rear lights or  wear other light-emitting devices when riding on the  road at night.
⑤ School buses can park or stop on the left side of the  road for the convenience of children getting on and off  the bus.
정답: 1, 5
해설: Where a bicycle road (including an exclusive lane installed for bicycles only under Article 15 (1); hereinafter the same in this Article) is separately provided, a cyclist, etc. must travel on that bicycle road (Article 13-2 (1) of the Road Traffic Act). Where no bicycle road is installed, a cyclist, etc. must travel hugging the right edge of the road (Article 13-2 (2) of the Road Traffic Act). Notwithstanding paragraph (1) and Article 13 (1), a cyclist, etc. who is a child, an elderly person, or a person with a physical disability may travel on the sidewalk. In this case, the cyclist, etc. must slow down toward the roadway side from the center of the sidewalk or at a place designated by a traffic sign, and must come to a complete stop when it would obstruct pedestrians' passage (Article 13-2 (4) 1 of the Road Traffic Act). When traveling on a bicycle road or a road under the Road Act, a cyclist, etc. must wear the personal protective gear prescribed by Ministry of the Interior and Safety ordinance and must ensure that any passenger also wears it (Article 50 (4) of the Road Traffic Act). When traveling on a road at night, a cyclist, etc. must turn on the headlight and tail lamp or wear a reflective band or other light-emitting device (Article 50 (9) of the Road Traffic Act). When stopping on a road, a driver of any vehicle must stop at the right edge of the roadway (item 1 of Article 11 (1) of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act).

③ Even if the cultivator driver signals you to go ahead,  maintain a safe distance and follow safely.
④ If the cultivator driver yields by moving to the second  lane, proceed to the left of the cultivator without  crossing the centerline.
⑤ There is no need to anticipate pedestrians appearing in  front of parked vehicles.
정답: 3, 4
해설: When there is slow farm machinery such as a power tiller ahead, its speed is slow compared with a car, so one must drive while carefully watching the state of the power tiller. Anticipating in advance and driving or attempting to overtake is dangerous. Even if the power tiller operator gestures with his hand to request overtaking, one must not overtake by crossing the center line. Overtaking through the space on the right is not a proper method of overtaking.

Therefore, obeying the signals at an intersection is essential for safe driving.

② Turn on the left-turn signal and enter the truck apron.
③ Turn on the right-turn signal and drive in the 12 o’clock  direction.
④ If the vehicle misses the chance to get out from the  roundabout, it should circle around one more time to  exit in the right direction.
⑤ The vehicle is trying to head in the 12 o'clock direction,  so turn signals are not required.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 25-2 (Method of Passing Through Roundabouts) (1) The driver of every vehicle must travel counterclockwise at a roundabout. (2) The driver of every vehicle intending to enter a roundabout must slow down or come to a complete stop, and when there is another vehicle already proceeding, must yield the way to that vehicle. (3) When there is a vehicle signaling with a hand, turn signal, or lights to pass through a roundabout under Paragraphs 1 and 2, the driver of the vehicle behind it must not obstruct the progress of the preceding vehicle that gave the signal. Road Traffic Act Article 38 (Signals by Vehicles) (1) The driver of every vehicle must, when making a left turn, right turn, crossing, U-turn, slowing down, stopping, or reversing, or when changing lanes while proceeding in the same direction, and when entering or exiting a roundabout, signal with a hand, turn signal, or lights until the action is completed. If a passenger car or truck attempts to force its way in without yielding, refrain from exiting at the 12 o'clock direction, and go around counterclockwise 360 degrees again and exit at 12 o'clock.

===== 문제 799 =====

① Since it is difficult to have clear visibility on the right,  come to a stop and then turn right.
② Since there are no pedestrians on the crosswalk,  proceed through swiftly.
③ Stop before the stop line to avoid obstructing the  movement of oncoming vehicles.
④ Since you entered the intersection first, proceed to turn  right.
⑤ Honk to prevent pedestrians from entering the  crosswalk.
정답: 1, 3
해설: Hazard prediction. The crosswalk installed on the road is a crosswalk without a traffic light. At this point the driver can see a driver intending to make a left turn from the opposite direction and a child running toward the crosswalk on the right. Also, when intending to turn right, one must not obstruct the traffic of other vehicles under the Road Traffic Act, and one must anticipate a pedestrian who may be in the blind spot where the crosswalk on the right is hidden by vehicles that violated the parking and stopping rules on the right, then stop, look out for hazards, and only then make the right turn.

===== 문제 801 =====
유형: 사진형

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q801.png
① Accelerate to prevent the white vehicle from entering.
② Drive slowly and pay attention as the white car may be  driving straight ahead.
③ Traffic is allowed to pass on the left side during road  maintenance, so stay on the left.

⑤ The red car in the opposite direction may enter the  left-turn lane, so stop and observe the situation if  necessary.

해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13. When it is impossible to travel on the right side due to road maintenance and repair work, one may travel on the left side under the Road Traffic Act. In this case, marked caution is needed regarding vehicles proceeding from the opposite direction. If an oncoming passenger car intends to turn left, there is a high possibility of a collision, so one must stop if necessary. Also, in the situation given, the white passenger car is in motion and may do one of the following: stop, turn right, go straight (crossing the center line), or turn left (crossing the center line). In particular, since some drivers cross the center line on a back road to go straight or turn left, it is dangerous to assume that all drivers of vehicles entering from the front will stop or to prejudge that they will only turn right.

===== 문제 802 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: When driving on an urban side street, which of the  following are the two safest ways of driving?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q802.png
① Watch out for bicycles and dogs being walked, as they  may suddenly enter the roadway.
② Honk to let the cyclist know that you want to pass.
③ Children may suddenly come into the center of the road,  so slow down.
④ Increase speed to avoid the bicycle and pass by quickly.
⑤ Flash your headlights as a warning signal to the car  coming from the opposite direction.
정답: 1, 3
해설: Back roads in urban areas have many sudden hazards because various traffic participants such as pedestrians, bicycles, and pets are mixed together. Drivers must drive carefully in preparation for such hazards. In particular, since a child may suddenly dart out into the road, one must reduce speed and slow down (③). On the other hand, unnecessarily using the horn or headlights to threaten pedestrians or other drivers (②, ⑤) and passing through quickly by increasing speed (④) can all violate the law and are unsafe. Therefore, the correct answers are ① and ③.

===== 문제 803 =====

In an underground parking lot, hazards different from those on the road may exist, so one must drive carefully. Meanwhile, one must always observe road markings (③). Looking only at a particular parking area (④) and continuously sounding the horn unnecessarily (⑤) are both unsafe driving methods. Therefore, the correct answers are ① and ②.

===== 문제 808 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the risks you must pay  attention to in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q808.png
① An electric kick scooter rider may suddenly move to the  left to avoid pedestrians ahead of him/her.
② Pedestrians on the right may cross to the left.
③ Driving slowly may cause collision with trailing cars.
④ You may collide with a white car coming from the  opposite direction.
⑤ An electric scooter may collide with a pedestrian at a  bus stop.
정답: 1, 2
해설: The most dangerous situations in the given scenario are as follows. First, the e-scooter (electric kickboard) rider traveling ahead in the same direction, veered to the right, may move to the left to avoid a collision with a pedestrian ahead. If one fails to predict this behavior of the e-scooter and accelerates into the space ahead, a traffic accident can easily occur. Second, the pedestrian traveling on the right tends to cross easily where there is no crossing facility. Therefore, when traveling on a back road, forcibly accelerating or predicting that the traffic participants ahead or on the right will always maintain the same direction of motion is disadvantageous for preventing traffic accidents.

===== 문제 809 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safe ways to drive in  the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0106.jpg
① Turn on the left-turn signal and headlights, and then  pass safely.
② Pay attention to the signals from the cultivator operator  and pass safely.
③ Proceed ahead once the signals from the cultivator  operator have ended.
④ Ignore the hand signals of the cultivator operator and  follow behind.
⑤ Maintain sufficient safe distance from the cultivator.
정답: 4, 5
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13 Paragraph 3. The driver of a vehicle must travel on the right portion of the road (on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, the roadway) with respect to its center (where a center line is installed, the center line; the same applies hereinafter). Paragraph 4. Notwithstanding Paragraph 3, the driver of a vehicle may travel on the center or left portion of the road in any of the following cases: 1. When the road is one-way. 2. When it is impossible to travel on the right portion of the road due to road damage, road construction, or other obstacles. 3. When overtaking another vehicle on a road where the width of the right portion is less than 6 meters. However, this does not apply in any of the following cases: (a) When the left portion of the road cannot be checked; (b) When there is a risk of obstructing traffic from the opposite direction; (c) When overtaking is prohibited or restricted by a traffic sign, etc. 4. When the width of the right portion of the road is insufficient for the passage of the vehicle. 5. When traveling in accordance with the designation of the section and method of passage designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it necessary to prevent traffic danger at a curve on a steep hill. A farm-machinery operator is not a person who can give hand signals. When traveling on the road, drivers of trucks, special vehicles, or farm machinery moving at slow speed sometimes gesture to mean "just go ahead and pass," but this gesture is not a hand signal.

===== 문제 810 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safe ways to drive in  the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0107.jpg
① The entering vehicle must yield to the vehicles already  in the roundabout.
② Slow down or stop the vehicle before entering the  roundabout.
③ Since the entering vehicle has the right of way, enter  quickly and proceed to your intended destination.
④ Enter the roundabout in front of vehicles already inside.
⑤ You can ignore the vehicles around you.
정답: 1, 2
해설: At a roundabout, circulating vehicles have priority over entering ones, so a yielding driving attitude is always needed, and when circulating it is important to maintain a safe distance from surrounding vehicles and to slow down. That is, at a roundabout, a vehicle intending to enter must yield the way to a vehicle already circulating. Also, when entering, one must slow down or, if necessary, come to a complete stop, and must not obstruct the passage of circulating vehicles (②). Conversely, the claim that entering vehicles have priority (③, ④) and driving without looking out for surrounding vehicles (⑤) are all unsafe driving methods. Therefore, the correct answers are ① and ②.

If the motorcycle rider traveling in the right lane (second lane) judges that passage ahead is obstructed, there is a high possibility of changing lanes to the left. In such a case, there is a need to be especially careful of the motorcycle on the right.

===== 문제 812 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safest ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0108.jpg
① The black car driver uses the P space to pass quickly.
② The black car driver stops behind the school bus.
③ The white car driver proceeds slowly while paying  attention to the school bus.
④ The white car driver stops before reaching the school  bus, and then proceeds slowly.
⑤ The white car driver honks continuously to indicate that  he/she is going straight.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Article 51 (Special Protection of School Buses) (1) When a school bus has stopped on the road and is operating a device such as flashing lights indicating that children or infants are getting on or off, the driver of a vehicle traveling in the lane where the school bus has stopped and in the lane immediately next to it must come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (2) In the case of Paragraph 1, on a road without a center line and on a road with one lane each way, the driver of a vehicle proceeding from the opposite direction must also come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. (3) The driver of every vehicle must not overtake a school bus that is traveling on the road displaying the indication that it is carrying children or infants.

===== 문제 815 =====

① Enter the intersection quickly according to the green  signal.
② Follow the vehicle in front closely.
③ Since the left-turn lane is congested, use the third lane  to turn left.
④ Do not enter the intersection, as you may obstruct  traffic by tailgating.
⑤ Slow down in advance to prevent a rear-end collision, as  there may be vehicles approaching from behind and  trying to enter the intersection.
정답: 4, 5

In addition, since there may be other drivers who try to force their way onto the road, defensive driving such as reducing speed in advance is also needed.

===== 문제 819 =====

===== 문제 820 =====

===== 문제 821 =====

① Switch to the first lane in advance to prepare for the  upcoming left turn as instructed by the navigation.
② As lanes 1 and 2 go into an underpass, let the vehicle to  your rear pass, and then safely change to lane 3.
③ Use the SATNAV repeatedly to confirm the route.
④ Quickly change lanes without signaling since your  vehicle is ahead of the vehicle to your rear.
⑤ Maintain a safe distance in case the vehicle ahead of  you brakes suddenly.
정답: 2, 5
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 28]. Operating the navigation system while driving disperses concentration and increases the risk of an accident, so one must refrain from this. Also, while driving, one should not rely only on the navigation system but should develop the habit of also checking road signs, which helps with safe driving.

===== 문제 825 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which TWO carry the highest risk of causing an  accident?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q825.png
① A bicycle traveling on the right-hand side of the  sidewalk
② A car waiting to turn left to enter the building
③ A car driving from the left-hand side of the road toward  the right
④ A car parked in an empty lot on the opposite side
⑤ A car reflected on a convex mirror
정답: 1, 2
해설: Near an entrance/exit, since pedestrians and bicycles may suddenly enter, the driver must slow down and be careful. Also, a car waiting to turn left to enter a building has a high risk of colliding with entering/exiting vehicles, pedestrians, bicycles, etc., so the possibility of an accident is high. On the other hand, a vehicle proceeding normally along its lane (③), a stopped vehicle (④), and a vehicle reflected in a mirror (⑤) have a relatively low accident risk. Therefore, those with the highest possibility of an accident are ① and ②. Before entering the road from a place such as a parking lot, one must always look left and right to check the movement of pedestrians and other vehicles.

===== 문제 826 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO most  appropriate ways to proceed in the given situation?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0115.jpg
① Yield the right of way to the oncoming delivery vehicle.
② Proceed as the passenger car driver has the priority.
③ Flash the high-beam headlights repeatedly to prevent  the other driver from proceeding.
④ Watch out for people getting out of parked vehicles.
⑤ Stop and wait until the oncoming driver gives way.
정답: 1, 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 20 (Duty to Yield the Way) (1) The driver of every vehicle (excluding emergency vehicles) must, when intending to travel at a slower speed than the vehicle following behind, pull over to the right edge of the road and yield the way. However, this does not apply to roads where separate passage lanes are established. (2) When vehicles other than emergency vehicles proceed facing each other on a narrow road, the vehicle indicated in the following classifications must pull over to the right edge of the road and yield the way: 1. When vehicles proceed facing each other on a narrow uphill road, the ascending vehicle. 2. When, on a narrow road other than a sloped narrow road, a vehicle carrying people or loaded with goods and a vehicle with no passengers and no load proceed facing each other, the vehicle with no passengers and no load.

===== 문제 829 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the desirable ways to  drive in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0117.jpg
① Switch to the first lane and proceed quickly.
② Turn on the hazard lights to alert other drivers to the  presence of your vehicle.
③ Since the road is wet, reduce your speed and drive  slowly.
④ Match your speed to the speed of the vehicle in front of  you and drive while keeping an eye on it.
⑤ Switch to the road shoulder and pass the vehicles in  front.
정답: 2, 3
해설: Hazard prediction. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 19 Paragraph 2. In rough weather due to rain, fog, snow, etc., one must drive at reduced speed according to the following standards notwithstanding Paragraph 1. However, where the Commissioner of the National Police Agency or the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency has set a maximum speed with a variable speed-limit sign under Table 6 I. Item 1(t), one must follow it, and when the maximum speed set by the variable speed-limit sign differs from the maximum speed set by other traffic signs, one must follow the variable speed-limit sign. 1. Cases where one must drive at a speed reduced by 20/100 of the maximum speed: (a) When it is raining and the road surface is wet; (b) When snow is less than 20 millimeters deep. 2. Cases where one must drive at a speed reduced by 50/100 of the maximum speed: (a) When visibility is within 100 meters due to heavy rain, heavy snow, fog, etc.; (b) When the road surface is frozen; (c) When snow is 20 millimeters or more deep. When traveling on a foggy road, one must reduce speed under the Road Traffic Act, and since the road surface is wet and damp, the distance needed for braking is longer than on a clear day. Therefore, it is important for the driver to ensure that there is no situation of sudden braking. At this time, some drivers tend to feel anxious when they cannot see the car ahead, and behavior appears in which they travel without securing a safe distance, even from a fast-moving car ahead, while keeping it in view. The method of safe driving on a foggy road is to comply with the deceleration standards even when the car ahead is not visible due to short visibility, and, depending on the case, to have the attitude of decelerating even more than the legally set deceleration standard. And one can also use the method of operating the lights so that other drivers can identify one's presence.

===== 문제 830 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO most  appropriate ways to proceed in the given situation?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q830.png

② Be prepared in case a truck skids and spins on the  snowy road.
③ Drive 10% slower than the maximum speed limit.
④ Turn on the hazard lights and drive slowly.
⑤ Slow down by driving in a zigzag pattern.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Deceleration rules are applied according to the weather situation. The cases where one must drive at a speed reduced by 20/100 of the maximum speed are when it is raining and the road surface is wet, and when snow is less than 20 millimeters deep; the cases where one must drive at a speed reduced by 50/100 of the maximum speed are when visibility is within 100 meters due to heavy rain, heavy snow, fog, etc., when the road surface is frozen, and when snow is 20 millimeters or more deep (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Article 19 Paragraph 2).

===== 문제 832 =====
유형: 사진형

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0119.jpg

It stipulates that when a driver overtakes another vehicle on an expressway, one must use the turn signal, lights, or horn to travel safely in the designated lane. According to the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 9] Standards for Vehicles by Lane, a passenger car intending to overtake may travel in the first lane and the lane to the left of the designated lane. Also, even when overtaking, one must comply with the prescribed speed.

===== 문제 835 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Among the vehicles driving on the expressway,  which TWO are violating the designated lane rules?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q835.png
① A (a passenger car overtaking another vehicle)
② B (a large 36-seater bus)
③ C (a 1-ton cargo truck)
④ D (a large 26-seater bus)
⑤ E (a passenger car driving normally)
정답: 2, 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 9] Standards for Vehicles by Lane. On an expressway with two lanes each way, the first lane is for all vehicles intending to overtake, and the second lane is available for all vehicles. On roads with three or more lanes each way, the first lane is available for passenger cars and light/small/medium buses intending to overtake, the left lane is available for passenger cars and light/small/medium buses, and the right lane is available for large buses, trucks, special vehicles, and construction machinery under Article 2, Subparagraph 18(b) of the Act. The "left lane," in the case of an expressway, means the lanes on the side closer to the first lane when the lanes excluding the first lane are divided in half (however, when the number of lanes excluding the first lane is odd, the middle lane is excluded), and the "right lane" means the remaining lanes excluding the first lane and the left lane.
