유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the TWO safest ways to  proceed in the given situation?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q846.png
① Honk continuously to alert others of danger while  passing.
② Use headlights to signal “danger” to the school bus  driver while passing.
③ Be cautious as children may suddenly run out from  between illegally parked vehicles on the right.
④ Come to a complete stop before reaching the school  bus on the left, and then pass by it slowly.
⑤ Quickly pass the stopped school bus that is picking up  or dropping off children on the right in order to get out  of the alley.
정답: 3, 4
해설: When a school bus has stopped on the road and is operating a device such as flashing lights indicating that children or infants are getting on or off, the driver of a vehicle traveling in the lane where the school bus has stopped and in the lane immediately next to it must come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down. Also, on a road without a center line and on a road with one lane each way, the driver of a vehicle proceeding from the opposite direction must also come to a complete stop before reaching the school bus, confirm safety, and then slow down (Road Traffic Act Article 51).

When facing a crosswalk after turning right at an intersection, if there is a pedestrian, one must come to a complete stop and pass after the pedestrian's crossing is finished (Road Traffic Act Article 27).

===== 문제 849 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the correct ways to drive  in the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0123.jpg
① Ignore the emergency vehicle violating traffic laws and  continue driving.
② Use the high-beam headlights several times to prevent  the emergency vehicle from committing violations.
③ Apply the brakes multiple times to signal to the trailing  driver.
④ Stop at a distance to allow the emergency vehicle to  drive against the direction of traffic.
⑤ Stop in front of the emergency vehicle to prevent it  from proceeding.
정답: 3, 4
해설: Article 29 (Priority Passage of Emergency Vehicles) (1) Notwithstanding Article 13 Paragraph 3, an emergency vehicle may travel on the center or left portion of the road in urgent and unavoidable cases. (2) Notwithstanding cases where it must stop under this Act or an order under this Act, an emergency vehicle may refrain from stopping in urgent and unavoidable cases. (3) The driver of an emergency vehicle must, in the cases of Paragraph 1 or 2, travel with special attention to traffic safety. (4) When an emergency vehicle approaches at or near an intersection, the drivers of vehicles and streetcars must avoid the intersection and come to a complete stop. (5) When an emergency vehicle approaches at a place other than the place under Paragraph 4, the drivers of all vehicles and streetcars must yield the way so that the emergency vehicle can pass with priority. (6) The drivers of the vehicles under each item of Article 2, Subparagraph 22 must not turn on the warning lights or operate the siren installed under the Motor Vehicle Management Act when they are not operating that vehicle for its original emergency purpose. However, this does not apply when conducting patrols, drills, etc., for crime and fire prevention, etc., as prescribed by Presidential Decree. The situation identified in the figure in the problem is a time when the vehicle is operating for its original emergency purpose under the Road Traffic Act. Therefore, since it is a time of the emergency vehicle's priority passage, the drivers of all vehicles and streetcars must yield the way so that the emergency vehicle can pass with priority. And at this time, behavior warning of stopping, such as operating the brake pedal in several short, repeated presses to provide information to the drivers of following vehicles, may be needed.

===== 문제 851 =====

Also, if vehicle passage is possible, move the vehicle so that space can be provided for fire trucks, etc., to enter, and then evacuate.

===== 문제 854 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safe ways to drive in  the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0126.jpg
① Drive in the left lane to avoid a collision with the bicycle.
② Slow down sufficiently before reaching the bicycle.
③ Accelerate to maintain communication with the trailing  vehicle.
④ Slow down in preparation for pedestrians entering the  roadway and watch out for pedestrians.
⑤ Drive through the edge of the road to protect  pedestrians.
정답: 2, 4
해설: Hazard prediction. The situation identified in the figure in the problem is one in which a pedestrian and a bicycle are traveling in the roadside zone. In such a situation, generally the bicycle's speed is faster than the pedestrian's, and the driving behavior appears in which the bicycle rider overtakes the pedestrian. In the process of the bicycle overtaking on the left or right, a collision occurs and traffic accidents such as suddenly entering the roadway or falling over are frequent. Therefore, when there are pedestrians and bicycles in the roadside zone, it is advisable for the driver to reduce speed in advance, predict the entry of pedestrians and bicycles onto the roadway, and prepare to stop. Also, at this time, cases of crossing the center line to travel on the left in order to avoid the pedestrian and bicycle appear frequently, but this cannot be said to be advisable behavior.

===== 문제 855 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are safe ways to drive in the  given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0127.jpg
① To facilitate smooth traffic flow, drive behind the vehicle  in front without making any changes.
② Accelerate faster than the bicycle in order to pass  through.
③ Come to a stop at the stop line just before the  crosswalk.
④ Since the pedestrian has already crossed the crosswalk,  proceed quickly.
⑤ Slow down to prepare for the sudden departure of the  stopped vehicle.
정답: 3, 5
해설: Hazard prediction. In the situation of the figure in the problem, the driver of the car stopped on the left may be in a hurry or may suddenly enter the original lane without checking the right side. Such a situation occurs frequently on the road. Therefore, one must be especially careful of a car stopped at the edge. And behind the car stopped on the left, a bicycle rider is in a situation of about to enter the crosswalk; even though it is not a pedestrian, the driver must protect that person. Therefore, without considering the bicycle's entry speed and one's own vehicle's traveling speed, one must stop at the stop line just before the crosswalk.

On the other hand, driving behavior such as continuously sounding the horn to pass quickly, or accelerating on the grounds that the distance from the bicycle is sufficient, is wrong driving behavior that can bring about a dangerous situation.

===== 문제 864 =====
유형: 사진형
질문: Which of the following are the safe ways to drive in  the given situation? (Select TWO)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/img_0133.jpg
① Prepare to stop as the pedestrian in the front may  rapidly turn left.
② To go straight, slowly enter the intersection as it is  currently empty.
③ Prior to entering the intersection, stop to check for  vehicles coming from both sides.
④ Honk repeatedly to alert the bicycle riders and  pedestrians nearby.
⑤ Drive along the right side of the road to induce the  pedestrians to move to the edge of the road.
정답: 1, 3
해설: Hazard prediction. Road Traffic Act Article 31 (Places to Slow Down or Come to a Complete Stop) Paragraph 2. The driver of every vehicle or streetcar must come to a complete stop at any of the following places: 1. An intersection where traffic is not being controlled and the left and right cannot be checked, or where traffic is heavy. Article 25 (Method of Passing Through Intersections) (1) The driver of every vehicle intending to turn right at an intersection must slow down along the right edge of the road in advance and turn right. In this case, the driver turning right must be careful of pedestrians or bicycles, etc., stopping or proceeding in accordance with the signal. (2) The driver of every vehicle intending to turn left at an intersection must slow down along the center line of the road in advance and turn left using the inner side of the center of the intersection. However, at a place designated by the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency who deems it especially necessary depending on the intersection's situation, one may pass the outer side of the center of the intersection. (3) Notwithstanding Paragraph 2, the rider of a bicycle, etc., intending to turn left at an intersection must slow down while keeping to the right edge of the road in advance and turn left using the edge portion of the intersection. (4) When there is a vehicle signaling with a hand, turn signal, or lights to turn right or left under Paragraphs 1 through 3, the driver of the vehicle behind it must not obstruct the progress of the preceding vehicle that gave the signal. (5) The driver of every vehicle or streetcar must not enter an intersection where traffic is being controlled by a traffic light if, depending on the situation of the vehicle or streetcar ahead in the intended course, there is a risk of stopping within the intersection (where a stop line is installed, the portion beyond that stop line) and obstructing the passage of another vehicle or streetcar. (6) The driver of every vehicle must, when about to enter an intersection where traffic is not being controlled and a traffic sign indicating a complete stop or yielding is installed, come to a complete stop or yield so as not to obstruct the progress of other vehicles. Road Traffic Act Article 49 (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers, etc.) Paragraph 1 Subparagraph 8. A driver must not, without justifiable reason, generate noise that harms others by doing any of the following acts: (a) Starting a vehicle abruptly or increasing speed sharply; (b) Increasing the engine's revolutions without transmitting the engine's power to the vehicle's wheels; (c) Sounding the horn repeatedly or continuously.

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q871.png
① Sign A shall be placed on the right-hand side of the road, 50 –200 meters before a location prone to crosswinds.
② Sign B shall be placed on the right-hand side of the road, 50 –200 m before the entrance to a tunnel.
③ Sign C shall be placed on the right-hand side of the road, 50 –200 m before a dangerous area.
④ Sign D shall be placed on the right-hand side of the road, 50 –200 m before the entrance to a public parking lot. A B C D
정답: 4
해설: This traffic sign is a caution sign, and its installation rules and locations are prescribed in the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6]. Sign A is installed on the right side of the road 50 to 200 meters before a point where there is a risk of crosswind (caution sign No. 137). Sign B is installed on the right side of the road 50 to 200 meters before a tunnel entrance (caution sign No. 138). Sign C is installed on the right side of the road 50 to 200 meters before a hazard area (caution sign No. 140). Sign D is installed on the right side of the road 50 to 200 meters before a chronic congestion section (caution sign No. 141).

===== 문제 872 =====

이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q872.png
① Uphill sign, usual traffic congestion area sign
② Vehicle width limit sign, keep distance sign
③ Tram-only road sign, bypass sign
④ Unprotected left-turn sign, left-turn and U-turn sign
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, crosswind sign (caution sign No. 137), wild animal protection sign (caution sign No. 139). ① The uphill slope sign and the chronic congestion section sign are caution signs. ② The vehicle-width limit sign and the minimum following-distance sign are regulatory signs. ③ The streetcar-exclusive road sign and the detour sign are instruction signs. ④ The unprotected-left-turn sign and the left-turn-and-U-turn sign are instruction signs.

② Sign B is placed on the right side of the road between 30  and 120 m from a roundabout.
③ Sign C is placed on the right side of the road between 30  and 200 m from the starting point of a downhill.
④ Sign D is placed on the right side of the road between 30  and 200 m from where the road becomes narrower. A B C D
정답: 4
해설: A. Streetcar caution sign - center or right side of the road 50 to 120 meters before a streetcar intersection (caution sign No. 110-2). B. Roundabout sign - right side of the road 30 to 120 meters before the intersection (caution sign No. 109). C. Downhill slope sign - right side of the road 30 to 200 meters before the point where the downhill slope begins (caution sign No. 117). D. Road narrows sign - right side of the road 50 to 200 meters before the point where the road narrows (caution sign No. 118).

===== 문제 921 =====
유형: 표지판형
질문: Which of the following is true about using an  intersection with this road sign?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/sign_0177.png
① A truck apron cannot be situated on the sides of a traffic island.
② You must always turn on the turn signal when entering  and leaving the intersection.
③ You must turn on the turn signal when entering the  intersection but not necessarily when leaving the  intersection.
④ A vehicle entering the intersection has the right of way to  vehicles already making a turn.
정답: 2
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, roundabout sign (instruction sign No. 304), the roundabout sign installed at a roundabout. At a roundabout, when intending to enter, one must yield to vehicles circulating counterclockwise within the intersection, and when entering and exiting, one must always operate the turn signal. And there is a truck apron at the edge of the central traffic island, made so that large vehicles or semi-trailers can drive over it. The distinction between a roundabout and a rotary is installed and operated based on the "Commentary on the Rules on the Structure and Facility Standards of Roads" (Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport) and the "Roundabout Design Guidelines" (Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport). Classification: Roundabout / Traffic Circle. Entry method: Roundabout - entering vehicles yield (circulating vehicles have the right of way over entering vehicles); Traffic Circle - circulating vehicles yield. Entry section: Roundabout - low-speed entry induced; Traffic Circle - high-speed entry. Circulating section: Roundabout - designed to prevent high-speed driving in the circulating lane (avoids large turning radius); Traffic Circle - high-speed driving in a large circulating section. Splitter island: Roundabout - essential installation for deceleration and direction separation; Traffic Circle - optional installation. Central traffic island: Roundabout - the diameter is mostly within 50 m; in urban areas, ultra-small roundabouts with a minimum diameter of 2 m can also be installed; Traffic Circle - no diameter limit. <Differences between a roundabout and a traffic circle>. Source: "Roundabout Design Guidelines" (Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport).

===== 문제 924 =====
유형: 표지판형
질문: Which of the following is true about this road sign?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/sign_0180.png
① It is a regulatory sign that informs the traffic direction and flow.
② It is a sign that directs vehicles turning right or left or  traveling straight.
③ A vehicle that is trying to turn left must use the outermost lane.
④ A vehicle that is trying to turn left must switch to the  leftmost lane and slow down.
정답: 4
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, passage division by direction of travel sign (instruction sign No. 315). It instructs a vehicle to turn left, go straight, or turn right. Road Traffic Act Article 25 (Method of Passing Through Intersections) Paragraph 1. The driver of every vehicle intending to turn right at an intersection must slow down along the right edge of the road in advance and turn right. In this case, the driver turning right must be careful of pedestrians or bicycles, etc., stopping or proceeding in accordance with the signal. Paragraph 2. The driver of every vehicle intending to turn left at an intersection must slow down along the center line of the road in advance and turn left using the inner side of the center of the intersection.

===== 문제 927 =====
유형: 표지판형
질문: According to the Road Traffic Act and its  Enforcement Decree, which of the following is the  correct way of passing a road where this guide sign is  installed?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q927.png
① A special vehicle is not allowed on this road.
② Trucks are not allowed on this road.
③ Only a two-wheeled vehicle with an emergency purpose  is allowed on this road.
④ Only a motorcycle with an emergency purpose is  allowed on this road.
정답: 3
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, motor-vehicle-exclusive road sign (instruction sign No. 301). It instructs that it is a motor-vehicle-exclusive road or exclusive section. Under the Road Traffic Act, the driver of a vehicle other than a motor vehicle (motorcycles only apply if they are emergency vehicles) or a pedestrian must not travel or cross an expressway or motor-vehicle-exclusive road. Therefore, a motorcycle may travel on the road only if it is an emergency vehicle.

===== 문제 931 =====
유형: 표지판형
질문: Which two statements about A and B among the  following are incorrect? (Choose two)
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q931.png
① A is a guide sign indicating a pedestrian-priority road,  and B is a road marking indicating a pedestrian-priority  road.
② On a pedestrian-priority road, pedestrians can use the  entire road.
③ On a pedestrian-priority road, pedestrians have priority  so they may intentionally obstruct vehicle traffic.

정답: 3, 4

===== 문제 933 =====
유형: 표지판형
질문: Which of the following statements about the  activated left-turn signal shown on the safety sign  is incorrect?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q933.png
① When a vehicle stops within the rectangular marking  (detection line) on the road, the sensor is activated  and detects the vehicle.
② When there are no left-turning vehicles, extra time can  be allocated to the straight-through signal.
③ It helps improve traffic flow, and air pollution is  expected to be reduced.
④ To operate it, the driver must stop and press a switch  installed on the left-hand side of the road, and then  wait briefly.
정답: 4
해설: This traffic sign is a "left-turn responsive signal" sign and falls under an instruction sign (Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] No. 335). With a left-turn responsive signal, when a vehicle stops within the rectangular marking (detection line) on the road, a sensor operates and detects the vehicle, and begins to grant the left-turn signal. When there is no left-turning vehicle, more time can be granted to the straight-ahead signal, which is expected to smooth the flow of traffic and reduce air pollution. Also, as idling time decreases, economic effects such as reduced fuel costs for drivers can also be expected (Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport press release, Korea Road Traffic Authority promotional material).

===== 문제 936 =====
유형: 표지판형
질문: Which of the following statements regarding road  safety facilities installed on this centerline mark is  NOT correct?
이미지: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/evergreenedu-collab/quiz/main/images/q936.png

④ It is installed to separate the same and opposite traffic  flow spatially.
정답: 1
해설: ① The road safety facility in the problem is a delineator post. Guidelines for the Installation and Management of Road Safety Facilities (Ministry of Land, Infrastructure and Transport Established Rule No. 318) - Part 1, Delineation Facilities. A delineator post is a facility installed, in a place with a high risk of traffic accidents where the driver's attention is markedly required, to supplement road markings by spatially separating same-direction and opposite-direction traffic flows and to give advance warning of a hazardous section.

===== 문제 940 =====

Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 6, no-parking sign (regulatory sign No. 219). It prohibits vehicle parking. Tow-away zone sign (supplementary sign No. 428).

===== 문제 965 =====

===== 문제 966 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following describes the most dangerous situation that may arise in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① The large vehicle waiting on the right is about to move.
② The yellow vehicle on the other side is attempting to commit a traffic signal violation.
③ The black vehicle turning right on the right is entering the first lane.
④ A situation wherein a white passenger car in the opposite direction turns right without considering the outer wheel  clearance.
정답: 3
해설: When a vehicle intending to turn right presumes the speed of a straight-going vehicle to be slow, there are many cases where it enters the first lane in one move, avoiding the parked cars. Therefore, when there is a vehicle turning right, one must refrain from the absolute judgment that going straight has priority, and needs to drive while anticipating the case where the right-turning vehicle's driver forces its way in.

===== 문제 967 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following describes the most dangerous situation that may arise in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① The gray vehicle in front may abruptly stop.
② The yellow vehicle on the other side is trying to make an illegal U-turn.
③ The bus on the left-turn lane may suddenly change lanes to go straight.
④ The white vehicle on the right is trying to overtake your vehicle.
정답: 4
해설: The driver of a vehicle that has entered and is traveling through an intersection must pass through the intersection safely, following the traveling path for exiting based on the position where it entered. However, as in the video in the problem, when there is no guide line at the intersection, or even when there is a guide line, cases of deviating from the expected path are frequent. Therefore, when passing through an intersection, one needs to drive with attention to the traveling paths not only of the vehicle ahead but also of the vehicle to the side.

===== 문제 968 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following describes the most dangerous situation that may arise in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① The vehicle traveling in lane 1 on the other side is crossing the centerline.
② Pedestrians on the right are suddenly trying to enter the roadway.
③ The vehicle on the other side of the road turned on its headlights to give a warning.
④ The vehicle on the road right to the intersection has violated the traffic signal, and it is driving into the intersection.
정답: 4
해설: This is a situation where the traffic conditions on the left and right of the intersection cannot be checked due to buildings, etc. In this case, the vehicles on the left and right, even when a yellow or red light is confirmed, may fail to stop and lead to a signal and instruction violation, with a strong possibility of causing a traffic accident. Therefore, even if the signal in the direction of travel is green, a driving attitude of decelerating when approaching the intersection is needed.

===== 문제 969 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following vehicles shown in the video complies with the Road Traffic Act to protect children  crossing a road in a school zone? (Refer to the website)
① The red car
② The white truck
③ The blue truck
④ The orange taxi
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians), Road Traffic Act Article 49 (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers, etc.). In a school zone, since one cannot know when or at what moment a child will appear, one must reduce speed and slow down. Because a child appearing suddenly could cause the car ahead to brake suddenly and the following car to rear-end it, one must secure a safe distance. When passing beside a child, one must always slow down while maintaining a sufficient gap.

===== 문제 970 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following describes the most dangerous situation that may arise in the video?
① The vehicle waiting in lane 1 at the intersection is turning left.
② While switching to lane 2, your vehicle is about to collide with a vehicle driving in lane 2.
③ The pedestrian standing behind a billboard suddenly enters the roadway to jaywalk.
④ The pedestrian is about to cross a crosswalk without a traffic signal.
정답: 3
해설: A pedestrian behind a signboard or sign is in a blind spot created by the obstacle, so it is difficult for the driver to see them. At this time, the pedestrian may be uninterested in the presence of a car coming from far away, or even if they have spotted the car, may misjudge that they can cross because it is a considerable distance away and jaywalk. Also, one should remember for defensive driving that jaywalking is common in front of and behind crosswalks.

===== 문제 971 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following describes the most dangerous situation that may arise in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① The illegally parked vehicle is traveling in lane 1 where parking is prohibited.
② The door of the vehicle parked in the opposite direction is about to open.
③ The vehicle is about to collide with a bicycle traveling in the opposite direction of traffic.
④ The pedestrian crossing the crosswalk suddenly falls down.
정답: 3
해설: On a road with one lane each way, due to illegally parked vehicles, one has no choice but to drive over the center line. In this case, the driver must drive with attention to vehicles traveling in the direction of travel or from the opposite direction. In particular, in a school zone, there is a need to be careful of bicycles, etc., ridden by children and adolescents who violate the Road Traffic Act.

===== 문제 972 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following vehicles is driving in compliance with the Road Traffic Act to protect pedestrians  crossing at an Intersection? (Refer to the website)
① The brown SUV car
② The yellow car
③ The orange taxi
④ The black car
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians) Paragraph 2, Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act Table 2 (Types of Signals Displayed by Traffic Lights and Meanings of Signals). One must be careful of pedestrians who do not obey the green pedestrian signal or who start early in anticipation of the signal. One must be careful of pedestrians who suddenly start running to cross when the green pedestrian signal is flashing. When turning right, one must be careful of the phenomenon of pedestrians who have stepped down to wait at the crosswalk being drawn in. When turning right, one must be careful of vehicles going straight from the opposite side.

===== 문제 973 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is the correct action for the driver to take in the video? (Refer to the website)
① Turn on the high-beam headlights to signal to the driver in front.
② Turn on the hazard lights, pull over to the shoulder, and argue with the opponent driver.
③ Activate the horn and turn signal to signal the intention to overtake, and then apply sudden braking.
④ Exit the expressway and go to a safe location before reporting to the police station.
정답: 4
해설: When an unspecified driver drives in a zigzag or commits an act of deliberately braking and blocking the course in the lane one is traveling in, it is advisable not to respond to that driver directly, but to avoid and detour to a road exit, or to enter a rest area, etc., lock the vehicle doors, and immediately report it and respond.

===== 문제 974 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is the correct behavior for the driver in the video? (Refer to the website)
① Drive at low speed behind the police car.
② Immediately report the police car driver’s violation.
③ Overtake if there is a safe space in the left lane.
④ Overtake if there is a safe space in the right lane.
정답: 1
해설: In the video, when a police car drives on the roadway in a zigzag form, it is giving signals and instructions that induce a virtual congestion. This technique is also called a traffic brake. Its purpose is preventing a secondary or tertiary traffic accident when there is a fallen object on the road, follow-up measures after a traffic accident, or other hazardous situations. Therefore, other vehicle drivers must reduce speed and slow down, must not mistake the police car driver's action for a violation, and must follow the signals and instructions.

===== 문제 975 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is the most dangerous situation depicted in the video? (Refer to the website)
① The vehicle that just entered the safety zone suddenly switches to the right lane
② The possibility of a collision with the vehicle going straight on your right side
③ An accident caused by the reversing of the vehicle stopped in the safety zone
④ A minor accident resulting from a lane change in a no-lane change zone
정답: 1
해설: A safety zone is installed at a no-entry place under the Road Traffic Act. Therefore, the driver must not enter a safety zone. However, some drivers, when the route decision is delayed at a junction (JCT: JUNCTION) or interchange (IC: INTERCHANGE), when their momentary judgment changes, or when experiencing distraction such as phone use, sometimes make a dangerous course change belatedly in the direction of travel. At this time, one experiences the violation of encroaching on a safety zone. Therefore, when the driver identifies a car traveling slowly with hazard lights on or stopped in a safety zone, one must anticipate that the car may suddenly enter one's own traveling lane and be careful and prepared.

===== 문제 976 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is the most dangerous situation in the video? (Refer to the website)
① The possibility of a collision with the two-wheeled vehicle intending to turn right from the right edge of the road
② The possibility of a collision with the black vehicle on the right
③ The possibility of a collision with the white vehicle on the opposite side waiting to turn left
④ The possibility of a collision with the pedestrian standing on the left side of the crosswalk
정답: 1
해설: The driver in the video did not turn on the right turn signal when intending to turn right and while turning right. This is a failure to convey information to the traffic participants on the road. In particular, the driver of a vehicle intending to turn right tends to turn somewhat wide due to the difference between the trajectory the front wheels follow and the trajectory the rear wheels follow (inner wheel difference). At this time, if there is space on the right, a motorcycle rider, seeing the vehicle ahead about to turn right without the turn signal on, judges that it will continue going straight, and this can become a direct cause of a traffic accident.

===== 문제 977 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is the most dangerous situation depicted in the video? (Refer to the website)
① The possibility of a collision with the vehicle turning right from the left lane while you are turning left
② The possibility of a collision with the vehicle approaching from the opposite side while you are turning left
③ The possibility of a collision with the pedestrian crossing the crosswalk
④ The possibility of a minor accident with the black vehicle proceeding straight on the right
정답: 3
해설: In a situation of intending to turn left on the green light, since the traffic light of the crosswalk encountered on the left was green, one should have stopped before entering the crosswalk. At this time, the act of turning left was compliant with the signal because it was a green light under the Road Traffic Act. However, it was an act that did not protect the crosswalk pedestrian. It is important for a driver intending to make an unprotected left turn at an intersection to have the mindset of recognizing that there is a need to be especially careful of the crosswalk encountered on the left, and to drive accordingly.

===== 문제 978 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is NOT a dangerous situation found in the video? (Refer to the website) (Video of bus  slowing down and crossing the centerline)
① Vehicle traffic in the opposite direction
② Limited visibility with the road ahead obscured
③ Pedestrians entering the crosswalk
④ Passengers on the sidewalk after exiting the bus
정답: 4
해설: A pedestrian waiting to cross on the sidewalk connected to a crosswalk without a traffic light may, contrary to the driver's judgment, suddenly enter, so when approaching the crosswalk one must slow down, and when there is a pedestrian crossing or intending to cross the crosswalk, one must stop. Crossing the center line because the car ahead is decelerating on a road curving to the right violates Road Traffic Act Article 13, and this act becomes a direct cause of a traffic accident. Therefore, where the center line is installed as a yellow solid line, one must travel on the right while maintaining a safe distance from the car ahead. A driver following behind a tall large bus or large truck cannot check the road situation ahead, so one must have the attitude of slowing down and keeping a wide following distance so as to maintain space to brake or stop. In the video in the problem, up to the sidewalk passage of the passengers who will alight from the bus cannot be said to be a dangerous situation.

===== 문제 979 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: In the video, which of the following is least expected by the driver to take place? (Refer to the website)
① A child may run out from between the parked cars in the alleyway.
② The driver of the blue vehicle may open the door and exit the vehicle.
③ The personal mobility device driver coming from the opposite direction may suddenly stop.
④ The driver of the two-wheeled vehicle ahead may fall down while yielding to the passenger vehicle coming from the  opposite direction.
정답: 3
해설: One needs a driving attitude that always prepares for the possibility that there is a hazard in an unseen place. It is not simply thinking of it as a clue to a hazard, but an attitude of preparing for the worst situation. When a fully predictable hazard appears, a driver can easily prepare for it, but the more a driver is unlearned or inexperienced with a hazard, the more they tend to neglect it. In particular, traffic accidents in residential areas occur when hazards are neglected.

===== 문제 980 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: In the video, which of the following situations poses the highest risk? (Refer to the website)
① The truck ahead may speed up.
② The vehicles in lanes 1 and 3 may overtake the truck, forcing it to hit the brakes hard.
③ The vehicle in lane 4 may switch to lane 5 to enter the exit ramp.
④ The vehicle in lane 3 may switch to lane 4.
정답: 2
해설: If one has a vigilance about hazards and pays attention to accident prevention, one will observe what hazards there are on the road. To prevent hazards, one must, along with such observation, acquire through experience knowledge of unusual traffic behavior that occurs on the road and be sufficiently equipped with the ability to prepare for it. However, what one must be careful about is that experience is limited and one can obtain only limited knowledge, so the possibility of being exposed to unpredicted hazards always exists.

===== 문제 981 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following driver behaviors constitutes a violation of the Road Traffic Act? (Refer to the website)
① The driver slows down after checking the advance pavement markings of the crosswalk.
② The driver stops to protect a pedestrian who is about to cross the crosswalk.
③ The driver turns on the indicator in the right-turn lane.
④ The driver quickly enters the left-hand lane while turning right.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians). Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] Types, Manufacturing Methods, and Installation/Management Standards of Traffic Signs. Road marking No. 529. Crosswalk advance-warning marking. Article 38 (Signals by Vehicles) (1) The driver of every vehicle must, when making a left turn, right turn, crossing, U-turn, slowing down, stopping, or reversing, or when changing lanes while proceeding in the same direction, and when entering or exiting a roundabout, signal with a hand, turn signal, or lights until the action is completed. The main character driver confirmed the crosswalk advance-warning marking and slowed down, and came to a complete stop before reaching the stop line installed just before the crosswalk. Also, under Road Traffic Act Article 38, the driver signaled using the turn signal.

===== 문제 982 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following driver behaviors constitutes a violation of the Road Traffic Act? (Refer to the website)
① The driver turns on the turn signal to indicate the direction.
② The driver approaches the right edge of the road in advance to turn right.
③ The driver exercises caution to avoid colliding with the car in front.
④ The driver turns right in accordance with the traffic signal ahead.
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians). Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] Types, Manufacturing Methods, and Installation/Management Standards of Traffic Signs. Road marking No. 529. Crosswalk advance-warning marking. Article 38 (Signals by Vehicles) (1) The driver of every vehicle must, when making a left turn, right turn, crossing, U-turn, slowing down, stopping, or reversing, or when changing lanes while proceeding in the same direction, and when entering or exiting a roundabout, signal with a hand, turn signal, or lights until the action is completed. The main character driver confirmed the crosswalk advance-warning marking and slowed down, and came to a complete stop before reaching the stop line installed just before the crosswalk. Also, under Road Traffic Act Article 38, the driver signaled using the turn signal. 982. Which of the driver's actions violates the Road Traffic Act? (refer to the website) ① Turned on the turn signal to indicate the direction of travel. ② Traveled along the right edge of the road in advance to enter the right turn. ③ Was careful to avoid a rear-end collision with the vehicle ahead. ④ Turned right in accordance with the traffic light ahead.

===== 문제 983 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following driver behaviors is a violation of the Road Traffic Act? (Refer to the website)
① The driver observes the maximum speed limit of the road section.
② The driver safely changes lanes at a location where changing lane is permitted.
③ The driver stops to protect pedestrians crossing the crosswalk.
④ The driver stops at the stop line just before the intersection when the crosswalk signal turns red.
정답: 4
해설: The main character driver carried out the direction-change signal under the Road Traffic Act. Also, in accordance with the method of passing through intersections, the driver traveled along the right lane of the road in advance in order to turn right. Of course, to prevent a rear-end collision with the car ahead, the driver slowed down while securing an appropriate following distance, and did not collide. However, the main character driver should have stopped at the stop line just before the crosswalk on the vehicle's red light, but simply followed the car turning right ahead. This violated the duty to obey signals and instructions under Road Traffic Act Article 5. As for whether, when there are consecutive vehicles intending to turn right, the second vehicle must also come to a complete stop when the first vehicle has come to a complete stop, the answer is as follows. According to the Korean National Police Agency's reply to inquiry (2023.6.8.), under Road Traffic Act Article 27 Paragraph 1 and the Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 2], all vehicles have a duty to stop when the front is a red light. Therefore, after the first vehicle (A) comes to a complete stop, the second vehicle (B) coming immediately after must also come to a complete stop. 983. Which of the driver's actions violates the Road Traffic Act? (refer to the website) ① Complied with the maximum speed limit of the road section. ② Changed course safely at a place where a course change is possible. ③ Stopped to protect a pedestrian passing on the crosswalk. ④ Moved to the stop line just before the intersection and stopped because the crosswalk traffic light changed to red.

===== 문제 984 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following pairs of vehicles complies with the Road Traffic Act? (Refer to the website)
① The black motorcycle and white passenger car
② The driver's vehicle and the white passenger car
③ The black motorcycle and the black passenger car
④ The driver's vehicle and the black motorcycle
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 5. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] Types, Manufacturing Methods, and Installation/Management Standards of Traffic Signs. When, while approaching an intersection, one confirms a green light and the desire to enter the intersection leads to acceleration and one reaches high speed, one cannot stop just before the intersection even if one confirms a yellow light. This leads to a serious violation of Road Traffic Act Article 5 (Duty to Obey Signals and Instructions). Therefore, the driver must anticipate that the longer the green light has been lit and maintained, the sooner it will change to yellow, and reduce speed when approaching the intersection and prepare to be able to stop on the yellow light.

===== 문제 985 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following correctly matches the Road Traffic Act violations committed by the main driver shown in  the video? (Refer to the website)
① Violations of the pedestrian protection duty, signal, designated lane, and no parking and stopping area
② Violation of the duty to protect pedestrians, signal violation, lane designation violation, illegal merging into traffic.
③ Violations of the no-lane changing zone, improper method of overtaking, pedestrian protection duty, and signal
④ Violations of the no-lane changing zone, no parking and stopping area, pedestrian protection duty, and signal
정답: 4
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 5 Paragraph 1. A pedestrian, vehicle, or streetcar driver traveling on the road must obey the signal or instruction indicated by traffic safety facilities and the signal or instruction given by any of the following persons (subparagraphs omitted). Road Traffic Act Article 27 Paragraph 1. The driver of every vehicle or streetcar must, when a pedestrian (including the rider of a bicycle, etc., who is dismounting and pushing or carrying the bicycle, etc., under Article 13-2 Paragraph 6) is crossing or intending to cross a crosswalk, come to a complete stop before that crosswalk (where a stop line is installed, that stop line) so as not to obstruct or endanger the pedestrian's crossing. Article 19 (Securing Safe Distance, etc.) Paragraph 3. The driver of every vehicle must not change course when, in changing the vehicle's course, there is a risk of obstructing the normal passage of another vehicle coming in the direction of the intended change. Road Traffic Act Article 2. "Parking" means keeping a vehicle continuously in a stationary state while the driver waits for a passenger, loads cargo, the vehicle breaks down, or for other reasons, or keeping a vehicle in a state where the driver has left it and cannot immediately drive it. "Stopping" means the driver keeping a vehicle stationary for not more than 5 minutes, being a stationary state other than parking. Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act [Table 6] Types, Manufacturing Methods, and Installation/Management Standards of Traffic Signs, road marking 516-2 no-parking/no-stopping marking. The place where the main character car in the video stopped is a road section installed with a yellow double-line road marking, so it is a section where parking and stopping are not allowed.

===== 문제 986 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: You are passing through a residential area. Which of the following is the LEAST related to things or places to be  cautious of while driving? (Refer to the website)
① Illegally parked vehicle
② Vehicle passing on the opposite side of the road
③ Crosswalk with no traffic lights
④ Pedestrians talking on the sidewalk on the left side of the road
정답: 4
해설: When driving at night, there may be a sudden entry of a pedestrian or bicycle in front of or behind an illegally parked car. Since one may momentarily lose vision due to the headlights of a car traveling on the opposite road, there is a need to be careful. At a crosswalk without a traffic light, a crossing pedestrian may be invisible due to the light of an oncoming car, so when approaching a crosswalk without a traffic light one must decelerate, confirm the crosswalk before entering, and stop when there is a pedestrian crossing or intending to cross. The pedestrian on the left sidewalk identified in the video cannot be said to be a large hazard factor.

===== 문제 987 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: In the video, which of the following is the correct way to drive?
① The passenger vehicle in lane 1 can travel straight ahead.
② The truck in lane 2 can turn left.
③ The drivers of passenger vehicles in the third lane must come to a complete stop before turning right, and they should  be prepared for risks while ch
④ The passenger vehicle in lane 3 may turn right while pedestrians are still on the crosswalk.
정답: 3
해설: Drivers who have excessive confidence throughout their driving behavior or who recklessly violate traffic rules are mostly drivers with insufficient hazard-prediction ability. The more a driver does not well understand what hazards there are when they violate traffic rules or do not fully make efforts at safe driving, the more easily they violate the rules and drive dangerously. The hazards visible on the road are not all of them; one must always try to see latent hazards and consider what driving behavior one can do for others in order to reduce the possibility of an accident.

===== 문제 988 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is least related to preventing traffic accidents as shown in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① Slow down in advance and stop just before the stop line.
② Slow down in advance and approach the intersection to yield to vehicles entering from the right.
③ Since the road surface is icy, press the brake pedal firmly.
④ It is advisable to drive with snow chains on the tires when it is snowing.
정답: 3
해설: Engine braking means, in a vehicle, decelerating using the engine's compression resistance or the friction resistance of the engine and transmission; while driving, when one takes one's foot off the accelerator pedal, the speed drops even without pressing the brake, and the effect tends to be greater in a lower gear. "Gigwan jedong" (engine braking) implies the same meaning as engine braking. That is, they are synonyms. A driver traveling on a snowy road must, when approaching a place where they may have to stop, prevent skidding when operating the brake pedal. Therefore, when approaching a place where a stop is necessary, one must slow down in advance by the engine braking method and prevent skidding when operating the brake. Road Traffic Act Article 26 Paragraph 3. At an intersection where traffic is not being controlled, the driver of a vehicle intending to enter at the same time must yield the way to the vehicle on the right road.

===== 문제 989 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: The driver is approaching and passing through an intersection. Which of the following is a violation of the Road  Traffic Act? (Refer to the website) (Promotional issue_Passing through roundabout)
① Upon exiting, the driver turned on the correct turn signal.
② Upon entering, the driver turned on the correct turn signal.
③ Upon exiting, the driver proceeded from the inner lane without changing lanes within the intersection.
④ Upon entering, the driver immediately entered the inner lane without changing lanes within the intersection.
정답: 2
해설: The intersection in the video is a type of roundabout entered by two lanes. Under Road Traffic Act Article 25-2, when intending to enter or exit a roundabout, one must operate the turn signal in advance. The lights to be turned on at this time are, according to the table of the Enforcement Decree of the Road Traffic Act, the left when entering and the right when exiting. When entering a roundabout operated with two lanes, in accordance with the road markings, from the left lane (first lane) one must enter and circulate in the inner lane inside the intersection, and from the right lane (second lane) one must enter and circulate in the outer lane inside the intersection.

===== 문제 990 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: When entering and passing through an intersection by turning left, which of the following descriptions matches  the situation shown in the video? (Refer to the website)
① The driver turns left according to the intersection signal.
② The driver’s slow driving causes another driver to overtake.
③ The overtaking driver yields priority when entering the intersection.
④ The overtaking driver enters the intersection according to the flashing red light of the signal.
정답: 1
해설: Enforcement Rule of the Road Traffic Act, Table 2, flashing red light. A vehicle may, where there is a stop line or crosswalk, come to a complete stop just before it, or just before the intersection, and then proceed with attention to other traffic. Road Traffic Act Article 13 Paragraph 3. The driver of a vehicle must travel on the right portion of the road (on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, the roadway) with respect to its center (where a center line is installed, the center line; the same applies hereinafter). Paragraph 5. The driver of a vehicle must not enter a place where entry is prohibited by a traffic sign, such as a safety zone. Road Traffic Act Article 22 (Time and Places of Overtaking Prohibition) Paragraph 3. The driver of every vehicle must not overtake another vehicle at any of the following places: 1. An intersection; 2. Inside a tunnel; 3. On a bridge; 4. A place designated with a traffic sign as a place the Commissioner of the City/Provincial Police Agency deems necessary to prevent danger on the road and secure traffic safety and smooth flow, such as a curve of the road, near the crest of a hill on a slope, or a steep downhill slope.

===== 문제 991 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is NOT true about driving on the hi-pass lane as shown in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① This is a single hi-pass lane, and vehicles must travel at 30 km/h or below.
② Drivers who fail to pay a toll may be charged 5 times the regular toll.
③ If you have insufficient balance in your hi-pass card, you can pay via the Korea Expressway Corporation website.
④ Any military vehicles traveling on a hi-pass lane are exempted from all toll fees.
정답: 2
해설: A single-lane hi-pass restricts the passing speed to 30 kilometers per hour or less, and a multi-lane hi-pass restricts it to 80 kilometers per hour or less. Also, when passing a toll road without paying the toll, an additional toll equivalent to 10 times the toll can be imposed and collected (Enforcement Decree of the Toll Road Act Article 14 Subparagraph 5).

===== 문제 992 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following should you pay attention to the most when anticipating danger on a back road? (Refer  to the website)
① Children who might suddenly come out from between stopped cars
② Left turn of the blue truck as seen in the rear-view mirror
③ Right turn of the cyclist on the right
④ Sudden braking of the car ahead
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians), Road Traffic Act Article 49 (Matters to Be Observed by All Drivers, etc.). When passing along a back road, one must be careful of pedestrians who may suddenly emerge from between cars. Since back roads mostly have no center line, one must be careful of cars that at any time suddenly change lanes and enter without turning on the left turn signal. When turning right or left, one must be careful of hazard factors such as being unable to see well surrounding vehicles or pedestrians due to the blind spots of the vehicle body's pillars.

===== 문제 993 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: The driver is traveling on a road with one lane in one direction. Which of the following is a dangerous situation?  (Refer to the website)
① The driver caused the trailing car to overtake by maintaining a safe distance from the agricultural machine ahead.
② The driver caused the trailing car to overtake by quickly decelerating and driving slowly.
③ On local roads, the detection of agricultural machines passing or crossing roads may be delayed.
④ The driver obstructed the trailing car from overtaking safely.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 13 Paragraph 3 states, "The driver of a vehicle must travel on the right portion of the road (on a road where the sidewalk and roadway are separated, the roadway) with respect to its center (where a center line is installed, the center line; the same applies hereinafter)." In the video, the driver in question is traveling on the right of the center line, and the vehicles following behind must also comply with this. Therefore, since the driver is traveling in compliance with the Road Traffic Act, it cannot be said that that driver's securing of a safe distance and slowing down induced the following vehicles' encroachment on the center line. The following vehicle drivers did not comply with Road Traffic Act Article 13, encroached on the center line, and committed an overtaking act that is prohibited in principle. When traveling on a local road with places such as a curved road or the crest of a hill, since farm machinery may not be easily spotted, one must have the attitude of slowing down.

===== 문제 994 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: In the video, which of the following situations poses the highest risk when overtaking a cultivator that is about  to turn right? (Refer to the website)
① The truck on the right may slow down to enter the intersection.
② The red passenger vehicle on the left may slow down to turn right.
③ The white passenger vehicle on the right may try to overtake the truck at the intersection while the cultivator is turning  right.
④ The cultivator may stop at the stop line to turn right.
정답: 3
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 19 (Securing Safe Distance, etc.), Road Traffic Act Article 20 (Duty to Yield the Way). Near an entrance ramp, one must spot an entering vehicle early and, taking into account the distance from and speed difference with that vehicle, judge whether one's own car will go first or will let it enter ahead of one. In this case, when there is a vehicle intending to enter from the right and the vehicle behind is already about to change course into the overtaking lane, changing lanes to the left is dangerous. Drive while maintaining a constant speed so that the vehicle in the acceleration lane can enter easily.

===== 문제 995 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: You want to exit from an expressway. What is the most appropriate way to do so? (Refer to the website)
① Cross lanes continuously without delay for a quick exit.
② Change lanes quickly by sharply reducing your speed.
③ Reduce your speed from the deceleration lane while checking the speedometer.
④ Pass through the exit road quickly by accelerating when there is no car in front in the deceleration lane.
정답: 3
해설: Expressway driving inevitably involves tense operation due to the high speed. Therefore, when exiting from the main lane to the exit ramp, since there is a vehicle following behind, decelerating suddenly risks a rear-end collision with the vehicle behind, so one must be careful. After leaving the main lane and entering the deceleration lane, one must reduce speed reliably by looking at the speedometer rather than relying on feel.

===== 문제 996 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is NOT considered a hazardous factor/situation as shown in the video? (Refer to the  website)
① Poor visibility when entering the tunnel
② Poor visibility when exiting the tunnel
③ Thin black ice at the tunnel entrance/exit or on a bridge
④ Vehicles violating the overtaking rules inside the tunnel
정답: 4
해설: Light adaptation refers to the process by which the visual system's sensitivity sharply decreases when one suddenly moves from a dark place to a bright place. Dark adaptation can be said to be the process opposite to light adaptation. Due to these characteristics, when entering or exiting an underpass or tunnel, etc., the traffic situation ahead is not easily checked. Therefore, when intending to enter or exit an underpass or tunnel, etc., the driver must have the driving attitude of reducing the vehicle's speed in advance to prepare for the traffic situation ahead. In particular, in sub-zero temperatures, (fine) dust, sand, and water on the road become entangled to form a thin sheet of ice, and this thin ice is not easy for the driver to identify with the naked eye while driving. This thin ice (so-called black ice) is likely to form on bridge-type roadways (bridges) with no geothermal heat or at tunnel entrances and exits. Therefore, the driver needs to be careful of this. In the video, there was no vehicle that committed an overtaking violation in the tunnel and bridge sections.

===== 문제 997 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following correctly describes the driver's law-abiding driving behavior as seen in the video? (Refer  to the website)
① The driver turned on the headlights.
② The driver safely overtook the other vehicle.
③ The driver observed the speed ​reduction standards for abnormal weather conditions.
④ The driver drove slowly to avoid splashing water onto the pedestrians on the side.
정답: 1
해설: A driver must, when driving on a rainy road, comply with the speed under Road Traffic Act Article 17, but in abnormal weather, one must reduce speed in accordance with the following: 1. Cases where one must drive at a speed reduced by 20/100 of the maximum speed (when it is raining and the road surface is wet); 2. Cases where one must drive at a speed reduced by 50/100 of the maximum speed (when visibility is within 100 meters due to heavy rain, heavy snow, fog, etc.). However, the driver drove at excessive speed. Also, under Road Traffic Act Article 13 one must travel on the right, but the driver traveled on the left of the center line. In addition, on a rainy road one should have observed the driver's obligations so that puddle water does not splash onto pedestrians, but a violating behavior appeared.

===== 문제 998 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Glare may occur when driving on a curved road at night. What should the driver do? (Refer to the website)
① The driver should look at the right edge of the road.
② The driver should increase his/her speed in order to get out of the glare.
③ The driver should decelerate by slamming on the brakes.
④ The driver should look at the left edge of the road.
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 19 (Securing Safe Distance, etc.), Road Traffic Act Article 20 (Duty to Yield the Way), Road Traffic Act Article 31 (Places to Slow Down or Come to a Complete Stop), Road Traffic Act Article 37 (Lights of Vehicles and Streetcars). At night, one can only see within the range the headlights illuminate, so there is a limit to judging hazards; it is important to reduce speed and to secure sufficient space and safe distance so as to be able to respond to sudden movements of other traffic.

===== 문제 999 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following vehicles is driving in compliance with the road traffic regulations to protect pedestrians  traversing a crosswalk without traffic signals? (Refer to the website)
① The white car
② The white truck
③ The brown car
④ The red car
정답: 1
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians). At a crosswalk without a traffic light, one must be careful of vehicles that pass between pedestrians despite the presence of pedestrians. Since one may fail to spot a pedestrian due to a vehicle illegally parked within the crosswalk, one must be careful. One must be careful of a pedestrian who suddenly starts running to cross. One must be careful of a pedestrian who crosses assuming, as a matter of course, that the vehicle will come to a complete stop when they cross the crosswalk.

===== 문제 1000 =====
유형: 동영상형
질문: Which of the following is a violation of the Road Traffic Act as shown in the video? (Refer to the website)  (Including outdoor mobile robots)
① Violating the duty to protect pedestrians, ignoring traffic lights and disobeying instructions, and crossing the centerline
② Violating the duty to protect pedestrians, ignoring traffic lights and disobeying instructions, and exceeding the speed  limit
③ Ignoring traffic lights and disobeying instructions, violating the duty of special protection of school buses for children,  and exceeding the speed l
④ Ignoring traffic lights and disobeying instructions, violating the duty of special protection of school buses for children,  and violating the duty to
정답: 2
해설: Road Traffic Act Article 5 (Duty to Obey Signals and Instructions), Article 27 (Protection of Pedestrians), Article 17 (Speed of Vehicles, etc., and Streetcars). In the video presented in the problem, the driver is confirmed to have violated the above Road Traffic Act.
